kaṟpuk kālattē, ōtal - nāṭukāval - tūtu - paki - poruḷ tēṭal - eṉṉumivaṟṟiṉ nimittam talivaṉ taliviyip pirintu vikum kālelli ōr yāṇṭē (188 - 130). pācaṟikkaṇ taṅkum pirivu tavira ēṉiya pirivukaḷiṭit talivi taṉatu pirivi āṟṟāḷ eṉṟu, avaḷi avaṉ uṭaṉkoṇṭu cēṟalumuṇṭu (175). taliviyip pirintu viṉivayiṉ ceṉṟōṉ, avviṉi muṭiyuṅkāṟum appiriviṉi niṉintu varuntutalilaṉ. kuṟitta paruvam kaṭanta viṭattum, talivi taṉpāl viṭutta tūtu varakkaṇṭa viṭattum, eṭutta viṉi veṟṟiyoṭu muṭintaviṭattum avaḷi niṉintu avaṉ pulampuṟuvāṉ (186). talivaṉ taṉṉip piriyaluṟum pōtellām appirivu kuṟittut talivi varuntuvāḷ (184). avaḷi amitiyuṟutta vēṇṭiyē avaḷōṭu avaṉ ciṉṉāḷ maṉikkaṇ taṅkuvāṉēyaṉṟi, tāṉ muṉṉarc cūḻntavāṟē, avaḷip pirintu viṉimuṭittē mīṭal avaṉukkuk kaṭami (185); tāṉ piriyumuṉṉar avaḷpāl koṇṭuḷḷa aṉpiṉi veḷippaṭuttu mukattāṉ avaḷi avaṉ mikap pukaḻvāṉ (232); tāṉ ceyyum naṟkāriyaṅkaḷ paṟṟi avaḷiṭam taṉṉip pukaḻntu kūṟikkoḷḷutalumuṇṭu (181). cēyiṭi viṉivayiṉ ceṉṟōṉ, neṭuñcēymittāyiṉum iṭiyē yāṇṭum taṅkāmal tērūrntu taṉ illam viriviṉ mīḷvāṉ (194).
iṅṅaṉam illaṟam pēṇi vāḻum kiḻavōṉ, āṇṭu mūppavē, kāmiṉpattil paṟṟaṟṟu, aṟam mutal mūṉṟiṉum ciṟantatākiya vīṭṭiṉpam eytavēṇṭi, taṉ cuṟṟattoṭu, vēṇṭuvaṉa mēṟkoḷḷa muyalvāṉ (192).
aṭiyōr, viṉivala pāṅkiṉōr eṉpavarkaḷum kikkiḷi peruntiṇikkaṇ talivarāvār (23). tam maṉattu nikaḻpavaṟṟi maṟittut tam taliviyariṭam pēcutalum ivarkaḷ iyalpu (211).
tolkāppiyam kuṟippiṭum talivi paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ -
(eṇkaḷ, poruḷatikārattu nacciṉārkkiṉiya muṟiyil aminta nūṟpā eṇkaḷ)
talivi maṉivi (225, 227) eṉavum, kiḻatti (93) eṉavum, kiḻavi (232) eṉavum, kiḻavōḷ (234) eṉavum, maṉiyōḷ (151) eṉavum cuṭṭap peṟuvāḷ. ivaḷ peṇṇiṟku aminta peruvaṉappum naṟpaṇpukaḷum oruṅkē amiyap peṟṟamiyāl oppaṟṟut tikaḻpavaḷ (209); accamum nāṇum maṭaṉum kāraṇamākat talivaṉoṭu mutaṟkaṇ meyyuṟu puṇarcci mēvātavaḷ (99), ‘pālatu āṇiyiṉ’ avaṉōṭu iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāḷ avviṭattēyē talip peytu maruvuvāḷ (102). innāḷkaḷil avaḷpāl viḷinta putumi avaḷuṭiya kaṇ mutaliya uṟuppukkaḷil kuṟippāṉ nikaḻum (108).
tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṉkaṇ, talivi taṉ pāṅkiyiṭam koṇṭa ceṟinta naṭpiṉāṉ nāṇum maṭaṉum nīṅkat taṉ kātal niliyik kūṟutalumuṇṭu (110); talivaṉoṭu kaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻttutaṟku avaṉiṭam tāṉē nērā kavō, tōḻivāyilāka vō kuṟiyiṭattik kuṟippāḷ (120). mutal iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāḷ tāṉē talivaṉ varumiṭatti etirppaṭṭuk kūṭiyavaḷ (119), piṉ perumpāṉmiyum tōḻivāyilākavē pakaṟkuṟi iravuk kuṟiyiṭaṅkaḷic ceṉṟeytuvāḷ (121).
kaḷavukkālattil, pūppu nikaḻum munnāḷum talivaṉoṭu kūṭṭam nikaḻa vāykkumāṟilli; eñciya nāḷkaḷilum allakuṟippaṭutal, varum toḻiṟku arumi eṉṉumivaṟ ṟāṉ orunāḷō irunāḷō toṭarntu avaṉi maruvum vāyppiṉi avaḷ peṟātu pōtalum kūṭum (122). tali vaṉ iṭṭup piritalāṉum arumi ceytu ayarttalāṉum cilanāḷ avaḷukku vāḷā kaḻiyum (111). avaṉ variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ piriyumiṭattē palanāḷ avaṉik kāṇātu avaḷ pulampuṟuvāḷ (112); taṉṉuṭiya ceṟivu niṟi mutaliya paṇpuṭimi kāraṇamākavē avaḷ taṉatu kaḷaviṉit tāyarkkum pulaṉākātavāṟu karantoḻukuvāḷ (202).
ikkaḷavoḻukkattāl varum iṭiyūṟukaḷi eṇṇu mavaḷ, virivil talivaṉ taṉṉi varintukoṇṭu illaṟam pēṇavēṇṭum kaṭamiyiṉi avaṉiṭam nērāka vum tōḻivāyilākavum kūṟi vaṟpuṟuttuvāḷ. atupōtu avaḷ kūṟṟukkaḷ yāvum tāṉ avaṉiyē niṉintu koṇṭiruppatākavum, avaṉ taṉ niṉiviṉṟi vēṟṟuc ceytiyiyē eṇṇuvatākavum ‘taṉvayiṉ urimiyum avaṉvayiṉ parattiyum’ pulappaṭuttum (111). iravuk kuṟikkaṇ avaṉ taṉṉik karuti varumiṭattum mīḷumiṭat tum vaḻiyiṭi avaṉukku viḷiyakkūṭum ētattiyum, avaṉip pirintu tāṉ taṉittu iruttaliyalāta pulampuṟum āṟṟāmiyiyum avaṉukkup palavāṟṟāṉum vaṟpuṟutti, virivil taṉṉi avaṉ varintu koḷḷa āvaṉa muyalu māṟu avaṉi ūkkutaṟkaṇṇēyē taliviyatu āḷ viṉi muḻumiyum tōṉṟum (210).
uṭampum uyirum vāṭiyapōtum, talivaṉatu iruppiṭam nāṭiccēṟal taṉakku muṟiyaṉṟu (203) eṉpataṉiyum kaṭantu, uyiriṉum ciṟanta nāṇiṉi viṭuttu, ataṉiṉum ciṟanta kaṟpukkāraṇamākat tāṉ avaṉiṭam cellutalit tuṇintatākat talivi kūṟalum uṇṭu (113); varital vēṭki kāraṇattāṉ, aṉpu aṟaṉ iṉpam nāṇam ivaṟṟi viṭuttu, perumporuḷ koṇṭuvantu paricap poruḷākat taṉ kuravariṭam koṭuttut taṉṉi maṇantu koṇṭu illaṟam āṟṟumāṟu avaṉi vēṇṭum niliyumuṇṭu (215, 214). (tōḻi ivvāṟu avaṉi vaṟpuṟut taṟkut tāṉ uṭaṉpaṭuvaḷ eṉpatu). talivaṉ variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirinta kāli, appirivuttuyar taṉakku mikumiṭattē avaḷ taṉ neñcoṭum piṟa aḵṟiṇip poruḷkaḷoṭum, taṉ kaṇkāl mutaliya uṟuppukkaḷoṭum uriyāṭutalum avaṟṟit taṉ ēvalic ceyaṟpaṭuttumāṟu vēṇṭutalum (196), avaṉi niṉintu kaṉavukāṇṭalum (197) avaḷpāl nikaḻvaṉa.
nāṇattāl uṟaviṉariṭattē taṉ kaḷavoḻukkattiṉik karantu vantavaḷ, talivaṉ variviṭi vittup poruḷ vayiṉ pirintaviṭattē āṟṟāmi mītūrum kāliyum, talivaṉ varukiyit tōḻiyarō ceviliyō aṟinta kāliyum, tōḻiyiṭam taṉ kaḷavoḻukkattit tāyariṭam iṭaṉaṟintu kūṟit tirumaṇattiṟku āvaṉa muyalumāṟu vēṇṭum cevvi avaḷukku nikaḻtalumuṇṭu (112).
ceviliyum naṟṟāyum taṉṉik kūrntu nōkkit taṉ meyvēṟupāṭu, maṉamāṟṟam eṉa ivaṟṟi yuṇarnta viṭat tum, talivi iṉit taṉ kaḷaviṉi maṟittal takkataṉṟu eṉṟu tōḻivāyilākac cevilikku aṟattoṭuniṟpāḷ (123); ivvāṟu ētam ētēṉum nikaḻvuḻiyaṉṟip piṟa kālattē, taṉatu nāṇkāraṇamākat taṉ kaḷaviṉit tamarkkup pulappaṭuttak kūcuvāḷ (112).
talivaṉi maṇantu illaṟam pēṇum talivi, avaṉ poruḷ tēṭap pirintu vēṟṟūr cellak karutumvaḻi kaṭattaṟkariya curameṉṟu kūṟit taṭuttalumuṇṭu. pāliyiṉ koṭumi kūṟit talivaṉ avaḷi uṭaṉaḻittuc cellātu illattil viṭuttuc cēṟalumuṇṭu (216). avaṉ kūṟic ceṉṟa nalluriyi maṉaṅkoṇṭu, avaḷ illil iruntu nallaṟam ceytu āṟṟiyiruppāḷ.
avaṉuṭiya parattiyartoṭarpiṉip poṟukkamāṭṭā tāḷ, avaṉ ūṭaluṇarttavum uṇarātu pulantaviṭattē, tōḻi, avaṉi “aṉpaṟṟavaṉ koṭiyaṉ” eṉṟu iyaṟpaḻittalip poṟāmal, talivaṉ tamakku iṉṟiyami yāta uṟaviṉaṉ eṉavum, avaṉē tampāl aṉpilvaḻit tam pulavi avaṉukkut tuṉpam payavātu eṉavum kūṟuvāḷ (159). avaṉi eppoḻutum vaḻipaṭṭu vāḻumiyalpiṉaḷākiya avaḷ pulavikkaṇṇum ūṭaṟkaṇṇum avaṉ paṇivi ēṟṟuk koḷvāḷ (160, 227); ūṭal tīrumpōtu, tāṉ avaṉi virumpāviṭiṉum taṉ neñcu avaṉiṭamē ceṉṟu viṭuvatākak kūṟi ūṭal uṇarvāḷ (161); avaṉ varinta piṉmuṟi vatuvip perumpeyark kiḻattiyiyum iṉmukattoṭu varavēṟpāḷ; taṉ putalvaṉik kōlam ceytu avaḷpāl cīrāṭṭutaṟku viṭuppāḷ (172); talivaṉ taṉ parattimikku varuntik kalaṅkiya kālattē, taṉ ūṭali nīkki avaṉukku naṉmi payappaṉavaṟṟi eṭuttuk kūṟum tāy pōla aṟivuri kūṟi, avaṉit tuyar nīkkit taḻuvikkoḷvāḷ (173). putalvaṟpēṟu vāykkap peṟṟi lāt talivi, appēṟṟiṉāṉ ciṟappuṭiyavaḷākiya piṉmuṟi vatuvik kiḻattiyiyum taṉṉi oppa matittut talivaṟku oruvāṟṟāṉum maṉakkavali nērātavāṟu avaḷōṭu oṟṟumiyuṟa maṉiyaṟam pēṇuvāḷ (174); talivaṉatu parattamipaṟṟi ūṭal koṇṭaviṭattum, vaḻimuṟi maṉiviyoṭu tāṉ ciṟappāka oṟṟumi maṉam koṇṭu illaṟam ceytalik kūṟumiṭattum aṉṟip piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilellām talivaṉ muṉṉiliyil taṉṉip paṟṟip perumippaṭap pēcikkoḷḷamāṭṭāḷ; kaṟpuk kālattē ūṭal nikaḻumiṭattuk kōpattāṉ talivaṉip pukaḻntu kūṟutalumuṇṭu (228). talivi taṉṉip pukaḻva tōṭamiyātu parattiyip pukaḻntu kūṟiṉum, avaḷ ūṭalāl kūṟum kūṟṟu avi eṉpataṉit talivaṉ uṇarntu koḷvāṉ (233). talivi vēṟoru taliviyiṉ kuṇaṅkaḷi eṭuttukkūṟi, avaṟṟik kēṭkumiṭattē avaṉatu mukattil nikaḻum vēṟupāṭukaḷik koṇṭu, avaṉ kuṟippiṉi nuṉittu uṇartalumuṇṭu (234).
parattiyar talivaṉāṉ tamakku nikaḻntuḷḷa tuyaraṅkaḷit taliviyiṭam vantu kūṟalumuṇṭu. avar kūṟuvaṉa kēṭṭu avaḷ cilapōtu talivaṉ taṉṉip pōlavē paratti yariyum tuyaruṟac ceytuḷḷamikku makiḻtalum, cilapōtu taṉ piriviṟkup parattiyar urimi koṇṭāṭi varuntum vaki avaṉ avarkaḷatu toṭarpiṉic ceṟivākak koṇṭuḷḷamikkuc ciṉattalum uriyāḷ; perumpāṉ miyum, parattiyar uṭaṉirukkumiṭattē, “parattiyari varuntaviṭṭu iṅku vantu taṅkutal vēṇṭā; āṇṭēyē ceṉṟu uṟital takum!” eṉṟal mutalāka avaṉik kaṭintu kūṟutalum orōvaḻi uṇṭu (235); taṉ parattiyiṟ pirivāl (talivikku) ūṭal nikaḻntami yaṟintu ataṉip pōkkat talivaṉ taṉpāl viṭukkum vāyilōriṭam talivi avaṉ paḻikaḷi veḷippaṭak kūṟutaṟkuk kūcamāṭṭāḷ (247); orōvaḻit talivaṉ aḻittuc cellat taṉṉūri akaṉṟu puṟanakarp pakutiyil yāṟukuḷaṉkaḷil nīrāṭiyum, poḻilil viḷiyāṭiyum, makiḻntu mīṭalumuṇṭu (191).
talivaṉ poruḷ mutaliyaṉa kuṟittup pirintavaḻi, muṉpu avaṉ pirintu ceṉṟa vaḻiyil uṭaṉpōkkil avaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟiruntavaḷ, aṅkuḷḷa māvum puḷḷum tuṇiyoṭu viki iṉpuṟṟiruntami kaṇṭaṉaḷātaliṉ, avaṟṟit talivaṉum kaṇṭu piritaltuṉpattiṉi añcit tāṉ mēṟkoṇṭa viṉiyi muṟṟa muṭiyātu taṉ niṉivāṉ ceyali iṭiyil viṭuttu mīṇṭuviṭuvāṉō eṉṟañci varuntutalu muṇṭu (148).
talivi maṭaṉuṭiyaḷāyiṉum, talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirintuvantu taṉakkup puṟattoḻukkam yātumiṉṟu eṉṟu valiyuṟuttik kūṟumiṭattum, aṅṅaṉam poyyākat taṉ tavaṟṟi maṟittuk kūṟum avaṉuṭaṉ tāṉ kūṭṭatti virumpumiṭattum, avaḷ taṉ maṭaṉ nīṅki, avaṉatu kaḷa voḻukkattic cāṉṟukāṭṭip pulappaṭuttuvāḷ (305).
talivaṉtaliviyar iruvarkkum ākā eṉa nīkkappaṭṭa meyppāṭukaḷuḷ ciṉam, pētimi, (kuṟṟaṅkaḷākiya mikikaḷip poṟuttuk koḷḷum taṉmiyiṉmi ākiya) nimpiri, vaṟumi eṉpaṉa nāṉkum talivi talivaṉ mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷa uṇmik kātalic ciṟappittuk kūṟumiṭattē avaḷiṭattē nikaḻum (245).
talivi taṉatu cuṟṟu vaṭṭāram tavirap piṟa iṭaṅkaḷil payiṉṟaṟiyātavaḷ (301); marutam neytal eṉṟa īriṭattē uḷḷuṟi uvamam kūṟuvāḷ. ettuṇip payiṉṟu vittum taṉ vēṭkiyit talivaṉ muṉ nērē kūṟāḷ. putumaṭkalattu nīr puṟam pocintu kāṭṭumāṟu pōla, avaḷakattu vēṭki talivaṉukkup pulappaṭum (118).
kaṟpiyaluḷ, parattimiyuṭiyaṉāyum talivaṉ ōḻukivara, taliviyō, illiliruntu nallaṟam ceytu, teyvamañci, aruḷum aṉpum koṇṭu, kaṇavaṉ piriviṉiyum avaṉip paṟṟi ayalavar kūṟum paḻimoḻikaḷiyum añciyē vāḻpavaḷ; avaṉ pirinta kālattē, iṉpatti veṟuttup paci aṭa niṉṟu pacali pāyntu uṇṭiyiṟ kuṟintu uṭampunaṉi curuṅkik kaṇtuyil maṟuttuk kaṉavoṭu mayaṅkit taṭumāṟupavaḷ (270).
ivvāṟu illaṟam pēṇi vāḻumavaḷ, āṇṭu mūppavē, kāmiṉpattiṟ paṟṟaṟṟu aṟamutal mūṉṟiṉum mikka vīṭu pēṟu karutit talivaṉ mēṟkoḷḷum virata oḻukkattiṟkut tāṉ peritum tuṇiyāka amivāḷ (192).
piṟarkkuk kuṟṟēval ceyvōrām aṭiyōr, piṟar ēviya toḻilic ceyvōrām viṉivalar iṭiyē, kik kiḷi peruntiṇikaḷil, taliviyar uḷar (23). ivarkaḷ tammaṉattu nikaḻpavaṟṟi maṟittut tam talivarpāl pēcum iyalpiṉar (211).
tolkāppiyam kuṟippiṭum tōḻi paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ -
(eṇkaḷ, poruḷatikārattu nacciṉārkkiṉiya muṟiyil aminta nūṟpā eṇkaḷ.)
tōḻi eṉpāḷ ceviliyiṉ makaḷāvāḷ (125). talivi nalaṉ kuṟittu ārāytalōṭu avaḷukku uriyāṭum tuṇiyākavum tōḻi iruppāḷ (126). talivi vēṟu, tāṉ vēṟu eṉṉum vēṟṟumi yiṉṟi oṉṟupaṭat tōṉṟum iyalpiṉaḷ ātaliṉ ‘oṉṟittōṉṟum tōḻi’ (39) eṉappaṭṭāḷ. talivikkut tōḻiyarāy uḷḷār palaruḷḷum taliviyāṉ peritum pēṇappaṭṭāḷāyt talivikku vāyilāntaṉmi uṭimiyiṉ avaḷ ‘peṭṭa vāyil’ eṉavum kūṟappaṭuvāḷ (102). tōḻi taliviyiṉ uṟuppukkaḷik kūṭat taṉṉuṭiya uṟuppukkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟum aḷaviṟkut taliviyiṭam mika urimi uṭiyavaḷ (221). talivikkut talivaṉ toṭarpuṇmiyit tōḻi avaḷiṭam putiyaṉavāka ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa nāṟṟam, tōṟṟam, oḻukkam, uṇṭiveṟuttal, taṉittiruttal, cevilipakkal uṟaṅkutali viṭuttut taṉittut tuyiṟal mutaliyavaṟṟāṉ aiyamiṉṟi uṇarnta piṉṉum, taṉ karutti uṟuti ceytu koḷḷat tāṉ ‘kuṟṟēval niliyiṉaḷ’ eṉṟa elliyik kaṭavāmal taliviyiṭam meycceytikaḷiyum poyc ceytikaḷiyum uriyāṭi avaḷ uḷḷattu niliyi ārāyvāḷ. (114). talivikku nikaḻum varuttattip parikarittal taṉakkuk kaṭamiyāka amitaliṉ, tōḻi kūrtta matiyuṭiyaḷākavē iruppāḷ (239). ulaka vaḻakkiṟkup poruttamillāta kūṟṟukkaḷ āyiṉum akapporuḷukkup payaṉpaṭa varumāyiṉ talivikku nāṇam tōṉṟāta vakiyil akkūṟṟukkaḷāṉ taliviyiṭam tōḻi uri yāṭutalum uṇṭu (218, 219). taṉṉiṭam irantu kuṟiyuṟavanta talivaṉit tōḻi taliviyiṉ arumiyi avaṉ uṇartaṟku avaḷi neruṅka viṭāmal, tāṉ avartam kaḷavoḻukkiṉi aṟintāḷākavum. aṟiyāḷākavum meyyum poyyum kalantum parikacittum uriyāṭutalum uṇṭu (237). tōḻi talivaṉi ‘ellā’ eṉavum (220), aruki ‘eṉṉi’ eṉavum (246) muṟiyē viḷittalum cuṭṭalum uṇṭu. tōḻi talivaṉit taliviyoṭu kūṭṭuvitta piṉṉar muṉṉart taṉṉip paṇintu niṉṟa talivaṉit tāṉē paṇintoḻuki avaṉ viruppiṉi niṟivēṟṟutaṟkaṇ īṭupaṭuvāḷ (114). taliviyiṉ kuṟip paṟintu tōḻiyum pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟikaḷit talivaṉukkuc cuṭṭutalum uṇṭu (121). tōḻi allakuṟippaṭutalāṉ kuṟi iṭiyīṭupaṭutalum uṇṭu (133). talivaṉ iṭṭuppiritali virumpiyiṭattum, talivi talivaṉit taṉ illattukku viruntiṉaṉākak kōṭal virumpiyiṭattum tōḻi talivi nalam karuti uriyāṭuvāḷ (114); talivaṉiṭam taliviyi iṟutivari pātukākkumāṟu vēṇṭuvāḷ (114). talivi yiṉ varital vēṭkiyiyum, avaḷ talivaṉpirivu kuṟittu añcum accattiyum, kaḷavoḻukkam ampalum alarum āṉa tiṟattiyum, piṟar aṟintu nōkkutalāṉ pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟikkaṇ etirppaṭal iyalātu eṉpata ṉiyum tōḻi talivaṉukkuk kūṟi varivu kaṭāvuvāḷ (225); talivaṉ iravukkuṟikkaṇ varum vaḻiyiṉ ētatti kuṟittu añcik kuṟivilakki varivu kaṭāvuvāḷ; talivaṟku nāṭu ūr il kuṭippiṟappu ciṟappu mutaliyavaṟṟi eṭuttuk kūṟik kaḷavoḻukkam tavirttu varintukoḷḷumāṟu vaṟpuṟuttuvāḷ (114). taliviyiṉ uḷḷakkiṭakkiyi uṇarntu avaḷ virumpiṉālaṉṟi tōḻi aṟattoṭu niṟṟali niṉiyāḷ (206). talivikku veṟiyāṭṭālō piṟar varivu nērtalālō ētam uṟṟa kālattut tōḻi eḷittal mutaliya eḻuvakiyuḷ oṉṟaṉi uṭkoṇṭu cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟpāḷ (207, 208). akkālattuk kaṭṭuvicci vēlaṉ eṉpāriṭamum talivi yiṭamum, murukaṉ peyari viḷittum tamar kēṭkumāṟum, tōḻi kūṟṟu nikaḻum (114). tamar varivu nērntavaḻit talivaṉ kālat tāḻppiṉṟit taliviyi maṇantukōṭali vaṟpuṟuttu vāḷ. avaḷ (114). ‘tāṅkaruñ ciṟappiṉ tōḻi’ (114) eṉṟa pārāṭṭukkut takkavaḷ. tamar varivu nērātavaḻi ataṟkuk kāraṇam tamar mikuporuḷ vēṇṭutalē eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟum vāyppum uṇṭu (214). iṅṅaṉam kūṟumiṭattu, talivikkut talivaṉoṭu virivil tirumaṇam nikaḻtal vēṇṭum eṉṟa peruviruppāl, tāṉ talivaṉiṭam koṇṭa viruppu - kuṭippiṟantār oḻukum aṟam - tamakku iṉṟiyamiyāta iṉpam - makaḷirukku iṉṟiyamiyāta nāṇ - eṉpaṉavaṟṟi viṭuttu, taliva ṉiṭam mikuporuḷ koṭuttut taliviyi maṇattal vēṇṭum eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟutal paḻiyuṭittākātu vaḻuvamitiyākum (215).
kaḷavuk kālattil tamar varivukku uṭaṉpaṭāvaḻit tōḻi talivaṉukku uṭaṉpōkkiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyi eṭuttiyampi avaṉi uṭaṉpōkkukku oruppaṭuttut taliviyiyum uṭaṉpōkkukku iciyacceytu talivi yit talivaṉoṭu kūṭṭi uṭaṉpōkkukku aṉuppu miṭattut taliviyit talivaṉiṭam ōmpaṭi cārttik kūṟi aṉuppuvāḷ (39); tāyariyum taṉṉiyum nīttuc cellutaṟkaṇ varuttamuṟum taliviyit tēṟṟi aṉupputa lum uṇṭu; uṭaṉpōkkuc ceṉṟa makaḷi mīṭkap puṟappaṭum tāyic cāṉṟōruṭiya upatēcaṅkaḷ palavaṟ ṟiyum kaṟpaṉi kalantu kūṟi avarkaḷi mīṭkac cellāta vāṟu tōḻi ceytuviṭuvāḷ. taliviyatu pirivāl varuntum tāyi avaḷ uḻuvalaṉpu kāraṇamākap pirinta taliyāya aṟacceytiyi eṭuttuk kūṟi amitiyuṟac ceyvāḷ (39).
tirumaṇam nikaḻntapiṉ talivaṉ ataṟkup peritum muyaṉṟa tōḻiyi vāyārap pukaḻntaviṭattut tōḻi pukaḻukkuriya vaṉ talivaṉē eṉṟāṟpōla maṟumoḻi kūṟum uraṉuṭiyavaḷ (150). kaṟpukkālattil talivaṉ aṟam ceyaṟ kum, poruḷ ceyaṟkum, iciyum kūttumākiya iṉpam nukartaṟkum, taliviyi maṟantu oḻukiyavaḻit taliviyiṉ maṉaniliyit tōḻi eṭuttukkūṟi, avarkaḷ ilvāḻkki iraṅkutaṟkuriyatu eṉpāḷ (226, 150); talivaṉ puṟatto ḻukkam karuti maṉam vēṟupaṭṭa taliviyiṭam talivaṉip paṟṟi iyaṟpaṭa moḻivāḷ (150); puṟattut taṅki vanta tali vaṉik kaḻaṟit taliviyiṉ ūṭalip pōkkik kūṭṭu vippāḷ; talivaṉ puṟattoḻukkam taṉakku iṉṟu eṉṟu poyccūḷ uṟṟavaḻi avaṉi iṭitturippāḷ; talivi uṇarppuvayiṉ vārā ūṭal uṟṟavaḻit talivaṉpakkal niṉṟu avaḷi vekuḷi nīkkuvippāḷ (150). talivaṉ taliviyi maṟantavaḻit taliviyiṉ kaṟpu, kāmam, naṟpāl oḻukkam, melliyal poṟi, niṟi, viruntu puṟantarutal, cuṟṟam ōmpal, aṭiciṟṟoḻil, kuṭi nīrmikku ēṟṟa vakiyāṉ talivi ēṉiya taliviyariyum maṉam makiḻ vuṟuttal, kāmakkiḻattiyarālum naṉku naṇpu ceytu matikkappaṭutal mutaliya mēmpaṭṭa paṇpukaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟi, talivaṉip paṇṭu pōl taliviyip piriyātu vāḻumāṟu kūṟit tiruttum vāyilkaḷil tōḻikkē mutaliṭamuṇṭu (152). uṇarppuvayiṉ vārā ūṭaṟkaṇ talivaṉ pulantavaḻit tōḻi avaṉi iṭittuk kūṟi, avaṉ ūṭali nīkkuvāḷ (157). talivaṉuṭiya parattimi yiyum, talivi avaṉ parattimi aṟintum avaṉ poyyurikaḷi nampit taṉ maṭamiyāl cīṟṟaṅkoḷ ḷātu vāḻum ceyaliyum uṭkoṇṭu, tōḻi, tali vaṉi, “nī, aṉpili koṭiyi” eṉṟu vekuṇṭu kūṟalu muṇṭu (158). talivaṉatu parattimi ūraṟiya alarām ceytiyiyum tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟuvāḷ (162). talivaṉ poruḷvayiṉ piriyak karutiyavaḻi avaḷ cella virunta curattiṉ kaṭumi kūṟit tōḻi avaṉatu celaviṉi vilakkutalum uṇṭu (216). talivaṉ pirintu varavu nīṭṭitta vaḻit tōḻi taliviyiṭam talivaṉ ceṉṟa iṭattu ‘aṉpuṟu takuna iṟicci’kaḷic cuṭṭi avaṉ virivil mīḷvāṉ eṉa vaṟpuṟuttutalum uṇṭu (231). tōḻi talivaṉum taliviyum nikaḻttum ciṟappāṉa illaṟatti nōkki avar iruvariyum pukaḻntu kūṟutalum, kaḷaviṉ kaṇ talivaṉi uyarttuk kūṟutalum uṇṭu (240). tōḻi uḷḷuṟi uvamam kūṟuṅkāl taṉ iruppiṭattiṟku aruki luḷḷa karupporuḷkaḷiyē aṉṟi annilattiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟiyum koṇṭu uḷḷuṟi uvamam kūṟuvāḷ (301 pērā.) taṉ uḷḷak karuttit teḷḷitiṉ viḷḷātu maṟittuk kuṟippiṉāṉ pulappaṭutta vēṇṭiya tēvi ēṟpaṭum iṭattilēyē avaḷ uḷḷuṟi uvamam kūṟuvāḷ (306 pērā.) talivaṉtaliviyartam kūṟṟik kēṭpōruḷḷum, talivaṉtaliviyariṭam kūṟṟu nikaḻttu vōruḷḷum tōḻikku mikacciṟanta iṭam uṇṭu.			(501 pērā.)
ivvāṟu akattiṇi vāḻvil tōḻi mikacciṟantatōri ṭattip peṟṟuḷḷāḷ ātaliṉ, ‘tāṅkaruñ ciṟappiṉ tōḻi’ (114) eṉappaṭṭāḷ.
‘tollōr kiḷavi pulliya neñcam’ -
“ellāvaṟṟiṉum ciṟanta uyiriṉum makaḷirukku nāṇam ciṟantatu; anta nāṇattiviṭak kuṟṟam tīrnta kaṟpiṉic ciṟantatu eṉṟu maṉattāl kāṇutal ciṟantatu” eṉṟu muṉṉuḷḷōr kūṟiya kūṟṟiṉit taliviyiṉ maṉam taṉṉuḷ koḷḷutal. 	(to. po. 113 nac.).
toḻutu irantu kūṟal -
“pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ vāraṟka!” eṉṟu talivaṉip pakalvaral vilakkiya tōḻi, talivaṉ iraviṭi varutal kūṭum eṉak karuti, “ciṅkattiṟkum yāḷikkum añci yāṉikaḷ iṭam peyarātirukkum mikka iruḷil koṭiya kāṭṭuvaḻiyil nī vārātirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu niṉṉaṭi toḻukiṟēṉ” eṉa, avaṉi virivil taliviyi maṇantu koḷḷumāṟu kuṟippiṉāl terivittut toḻutu vēṇṭik kūṟuvatu. 
itu ‘varivu muṭukkam’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 255)
toṉṉūl viḷakkam -
ittāli nāṭṭavarāṉa peski eṉṟa vīramāmuṉivarāl 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa aintilakkaṇanūl; 370 nūṟpākkaḷiyuṭiyatu. innūṟku nūlāciriyarē uri varintār. itaṉ akapporuṭ pakutiyil uḷanūṟceyti kaḷik kāṇalām.
toṉṉūlviḷakkam kuṟippiṭum akattiṇicceytikaḷ -
(eṇkaḷ: cūttireṇkaḷik kuṟippaṉa)
colluvataṟku eṭuttukkoṇṭa poruḷik kāṭṭum vaḻi akattiṇiyum puṟattiṇiyum eṉa iraṇṭām (150). potu, ciṟappu, uvami, puṟanili, etirnili, karuvi, kāriyam, kārakam, muṉṉavi, piṉṉavi, eṉa akattiṇi 12 vakippaṭum (151). poruḷkaḷiṉ iyalpiṉik koṇṭu apporuḷkaḷi viḷakkuvatu iyalpuakattiṇiyām (152).
vāṭivīcutal, intirakōpamum mayilum kēkayappuḷḷum tōṉṟi makiḻtal, veṇkāntaḷ - ceṅkāntaḷ - koṉṟi - kūtāḷam - vēṅkimaram - kākkaṇañceṭi - mulli - kampu - kāyā - kuruntu - eṉa ivi malartal, aṉṉam - kiḷi - kuyil - eṉṉum ivi nīṅkutal, tāmari mutaliya nīrmalar oḷit tal eṉṉumivi kārpparuvattu urimi eṉappaṭum (167).
kūtirkkāṟṟu vīcutal, kuruku - aṉṉam - kokku - cakōram - eṉṟum paṟavikaḷum caṅku - naṇṭu - natti - eṉpaṉavum  makiḻtal, nīrteḷital, mīṉiṉam tōṉṟal, mēkam cūl koḷḷutal, pāricātam - cantaṉam - ciṟuceṇpakam - cem paratti - nāṇal - eṉivi malartal, piṟa paṟavikaḷum vilaṅkukaḷum makkaḷum oruṅkuṭaṉ varuntal eṉṉumivi kūtirpparuvattu urimi eṉappaṭum (168).
koṇṭalkāṟṟu vīcutal, tūkkaṇaṅkuruvi kūki ānti eṉumivi makiḻtal, māmaramum civantiyum malartal,  ilanti paruttal, kuṉṟi kāyttal, cennel viḷital, karumpu mutirtal eṉṉumivi muṉpaṉipparuvattu urimi eṉappaṭum (169).
ulavikkāṟṟu vīcutal, pala puṟāiṉaṅkaḷ - kāṉāṅkōḻi - eṉumivi makiḻtal, kōṅku - ilavu - eṉumivi pūttal, pērīntu - paṉi - eṉumivi paḻuttal, parutti veṭittal eṉiviyellām piṉpaṉipparuvattu urimi eṉappaṭum (170).
teṉṟal vīcutal, pala vaṇṭiṉam - kiḷi - pūvi - aṉṟil - kuyil - eṉumivi makiḻtal, māṅkaṉi utirtal, nīrmalarē yaṉṟi makiḻ - tāḻi - puṉṉi - ceṇpakam - eṉap palavum malartal, kārpparuvattē makiḻum kōpam - kēkayappaṟavi - mayil - eṉumivi melital eṉa iviyellām iḷavēṉiṟparuvattu urimi eṉappaṭum (171).
kōṭikkāṟṟu vīcutal, kāṉal tōṉṟutal, kāṭi - valiyāṉ - vāṉampāṭi - kākam - kavutāri - eṉumip paṟavikaḷ makiḻtal, malliki - puḷi - pātiri eṉumivi pūttal, pāli - eṭṭi - nāval - iluppi - eṉum maraṅkaḷ kāyttal, nīrnili vaṟṟutal, iṅkukkūṟiyavi tavirap piṟavuyiriṉaṅkaḷ kuḻintu cōrtal eṉivi mutuvēṉiṟ paruvattu urimi eṉappaṭum (172).
eṉavē, avvapparuvattukku ōtiya muṟi vaḻuvātu ceyyuḷ pāṭutal paruvatturimiyām; piṟaḻtal paruvamali vām eṉṟavāṟu. piṉvarum ciṟupoḻutukaṭkum itu poruntum.
iṉic ciṟu poḻutu āṟāvaṉa māli, yāmam, vikaṟi, viṭiyal, uccippakal, eṟpāṭu eṉpaṉa. pakaloḷipōy iravu pukum poḻutu mutaṟkoṇṭu pappattu nāḻikiyāka ivvāṟaṟkum nēram koḷḷappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ, kuvaḷi malartal, puḷḷiḷam cōlicērntu kalakaleṉap peritum olittal, pacumutalāya kaṟavikaḷ tam kaṉṟi niṉintu varutal, tāmarikūmputal, kaṉṉaṭam kāmpōti eṉum irākaṅkaḷip pāṭutal eṉa iviyellām mālip poḻutiṟku urimiyām;
ākari rākam pāṭutal, kūkiyum cakōramum makiḻtal, kaṭal poṅkutal, kāmam - nicaptam - kaḷavu - eṉumivi mikutal eṉa iviyellām yāmappoḻutiṟku urimiyām;
kōḻikūvutal, naṉavupōlak kaṉavu tōṉṟutal, viṇmīṉka ḷatu oḷikuṟital, veḷḷi muḷittal, mātavar tuyi luṇarntu iṟivaṉi vāḻttutal, irāmakavi intōḷam eṉumiraṇṭu irākaṅkaḷ pāṭutal eṉa iviyellām vikaṟip poḻutiṟku urimiyām;
vilaṅku paṟavi makkaḷ mutaliya ellāuyirum mukamalarntu makiḻtal, pūñcōliyil pūṇṭuppū koṭippū kōṭ ṭuppū aṉṟi nīrppūkkaḷuḷ kamalam mutaliyaṉa malartal, veṇpaṉi tuḷittal, pūpāḷam tēcāṭci eṉum iraṇṭi rākaṅkaḷ pāṭutal eṉa iviyellām viṭiyaṟpoḻu tiṟkuurimiyām; cakkiravākappaṟavi makiḻtal,pēyttēr ōṭutal, erumikaḷ nīriṟ pāyntukiṭattal, cāraṅkarākam pāṭutal eṉa iviyellām uccippoḻutiṟku urimi yākum; 
mali mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ niḻalellām nīḷutal, vāṉam civattal, palavaki yāṭṭuk kuṭṭikaḷ kutittal, kāpi kaliyāṇi eṉumiraṇṭu irākaṅkaḷ pāṭutal eṉa ivi yellām eṟpāṭṭup poḻutiṟku urimiyām (173).
veṟuppu, makiḻcci, irakkam, vekuḷi, nāṇam, tuṇivu, accam, mayakkam, āci eṉpaṉavum piṟavum makkaḷiṭattut tōṉṟum paṟṟutal ākum. ātaliṉ, kēṭpōriṭattuk kavitāṉ appaṟṟiṉik kiḷappumtaṉmiyum paṟṟuṭi yārkku aḵtu iyalum taṉmiyum aṟintu ammuṟi piṟaḻātu urippatu paṟṟutal urimi eṉappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ, kēṭpōrmaṉattil oṉṟaṉmēl veṟuppum, oṉṟaṉmēl āciyum, ōriṭattē ūkkamum, ōriṭattē accamum, orucārāriṭam irakkamum orucāriṭam vekuḷiyum eṉa ivi mutalākiya paṟṟutalik kiḷapputal kaṟṟōrtoḻil. avvavaṟṟukkuriya poruḷām aṇiyum tērntu urippatu aṟivuṭiyōr kaṭaṉ eṉka. aṉṟiyum, pala vakippaṟṟutal eḻumpiya taṉmiyāl maṉitarum pala kuṇamuṭiyār āvar. avaruḷ, kaṭuñciṉamuṭiyāṉ niṉivum moḻiyum oḻukka mum vēṟāki, avaṉē ciṉam āṟappeṟṟu nāṇam koṇ ṭāṉāyiṉ anniliyil avaṉuṭiya niṉivum moḻiyum oḻukkamum vēṟākumaṉṟō? piṟa paṟṟutalkaḷum at taṉmiya ātaliṉ makkaḷ maṉattē eḻumpiṉa paṟṟutaṟkuriya vaṟṟic ceppalum urimiyē eṉṟavāṟu. (188).
caturam mutal palavaṭivum, eṇ mutal aḷavum, vemmi mutal ūṟum eṉa ivi mutalākiya kuṇaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟ kuriyavaṟṟi maṟṟoṉṟaṟku uriyavāka urippatu kuṇavaḻu vām. pala poruḷkaṭkup potuppaṭaniṟkum kuṇaṅkaḷic collutal vaḻuvaṉṟāyiṉum, potuppaṭātataṟku uriya vaṟṟic collutal ciṟappu ākik kuṇavurimiyām. pintiṉi eṉpuḻi, pacumi piṅkūḻukkellām potuk kuṇam. ceṟikuraltiṉi, tāṟṟuk katirvaraku eṉpuḻi ceṟikuralkaḷiyuṭimiyum tāṟṟukkatirkaḷiyuṭimi yum tiṉikkum varakukkum muṟiyē ciṟappuṭik kuṇavurimi yām. ivvāṟē pacuṅkatali, uyarteṅku eṉpuḻip pacumiyum uyarcciyum potukkuṇam; viritalikkatali, īrkkilit teṅku eṉpuḻi, viritali yuṭimiyum, īrkku iliyām ōliyuṭimiyum ciṟappuṭik kuṇavurimiyām. ivvuri miyi aṇinūlār taṉmiyaṇi eṉpa(189).
tammuḷ ovvā muṟikaḷit tōṟṟāmal ēṟṟa muṟi koṇṭu ulakanaṭi piṟaḻātu urippatu oḻukkavurimi yām (190).
caṉukkirakam, caṅkatam, avappirañcaṉam, pākatam eṉṟu colvaki nāṉkāka vakuttaṉar pulavar. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉiya iraṇṭum tēvarmoḻi, avappirañcaṉam iḻi caṉarmoḻi, ēṉiyatu ellā nāṭṭārum vaḻaṅkumoḻi yām. pākatam, taṟpavam - taṟcamam - tēcikam -eṉa mūvakittu. ivimūṉṟaṟkum muṟiyē eṭuttukkāṭṭāvaṉa: cuki pōki cupāvam nitiyam eṉavum, amalam kamalam kāraṇam kuṅkumam eṉavum, nilam nīr tī vaḷi eṉavum kaṇṭukoḷka (191).
tēcikaccolloṭu cila taṟpavaccollum kalantu vanta ceyyuḷ kēṭpōrkku iṉpamāka vaḻaṅkum uṟuppuc ceyyuḷām.
e-ṭu:  ‘tarātarat(tu) oppat tarātalat(tu) ōṅki
          varātarat(tu) antarattil vāḻpa - parākac
          ciracaraṇā cuttiravi tiṅkaḷmīṉ pūṇṭāṉ
          karacara ṇātitoḻiṟ kaṇṭu.’
ivveṇpāvil, oppa ōṅki vāḻpa tiṅkaḷ mīṉ pūṇṭāṉ toḻil kaṇṭu eṉpaṉa eṭṭum tēcikamākiya (piṟa moḻiyi nōkkātu avvannāṭṭār collēyāy varuvaṉa) tamiḻnāṭṭuc coṟkaḷ; piṟavellām taṟpavam (192).
toṉṉūlviḷakkattuḷ, cūttireṇ 150 mutal 199 muṭiya niṉṟa 50 nūṟpāvum poruḷatikāram paṟṟiyavi, ivaṟṟuḷ 167-173 muṭiya niṉṟavi perumpoḻutu ciṟupoḻutu paṟṟiyavi; 188-192 muṭiyaniṉṟavi, akam puṟam eṉum irutiṇikkum potuvākac ceyyuḷpuṉital, marapu paṟṟiyavi. iṉi 199ām nūṟpā akattiṇipākupāṭu urikkiṟatu.
akapporuḷāvatu kāmam. ataṉuḷḷum, orutalikkāmam kikkiḷi; poruntākkāmam peruntiṇi; aṉpuṭik kāmam  kuṟiñci pāli mulli marutam neytal eṉa ain nilattiṟkum uriyatāy varum aintiṇi. ivvāṟu akap poruḷ ēḻutiṇiyām. ivaṟṟiṉ oḻukkamuṟiyāṉa kikōḷ, kaḷaveṉavum kaṟpeṉavum iraṇṭu. varivataṉ muṉṉar nikaḻvatu kaḷavu; varinteytiya piṉṉar nikaḻvatu kaṟpu. ivviraṇṭu tiṟattiṟkumuriya palavaki naṭiyi yuṭiyatu akapporuḷ. vatuvi nikaḻvataṟkuriya muṟiyik kāṭṭutal, vatuvi purintukoṇṭapiṉṉart talivaṉ taliviyartam vāḻkki muṟikaḷi vakuttal, talivaṉatu piriviṉkaṇ talivi pulampuri eṉṟiṉṉōraṉṉa pala ceytikaḷiyum pāritturippatu akattiṇicceyti. (199).
tōḻi ‘añci accuṟuttaliṉ’ kaṇ kūṟiyatu -
talivaṉ aṅku niṟpatāl avaṉukkum tamakkum tuṉpam ēṟpaṭum eṉṟu tāṉ añciṉa taṉmiyit talivaṉiṭam kūṟi, avaṉiyum accamuṟacceytu, tōḻikūṟutal;
atu ‘yāy varuvaḷ’ eṉṟāṉum, ‘tamiyaṉmār varuvar’ eṉṟāṉum, ‘kāvalar varuvar’ eṉṟāṉum kūṟutal. 			(to. po. 112 iḷa.)
aṅṅaṉam āṟṟuvittum, kaṭitu kuṟi muṭiyāmiyik karutum tōḻi, kuravarit tāṉ añcuvatu pōlat talivi yum añcuvaḷ eṉak kūṟal. ivvaccam kūṟavē, avaṉ āṟṟum.
“aiya! karumpu pōṉṟu parutta taṇṭiṉiyuṭiya tiṉi vaḷarnta puṉattil yāṅkaḷ kiḷi kaṭiyac celkiṟōm nī maṇam kamaḻum mārpōṭu appakkam vāraṟka. tāy aṅku varuvāḷ” (kuṟun.198) eṉpatu yāyi añci urittatu.
“yāṉikaḷ tiriyum maliyilē vāḻum kuṟamakaḷirākiya yām kākkum tiṉikkollippakkam varuvataṉik kaṇṭālum em uṟaviṉar uṉṉiyum eṅkaḷiyum kōpippar.” (tiṇi. aim. 6) eṉpatu tōḻi tamari añci urittatu.  	(to. po. 114nac.)
tōḻi ‘aṭaṅkā oḻukkattu avaṉvayiṉ aḻintōḷi, aṭaṅkak kāṭṭutal poruḷiṉkaṇ’ kūṟal -
aṭaṅkā oḻukkattiyuṭiya talivaṉmāṭṭu maṉam aḻinta taliviyi aṭaṅkak kāṭṭuvataṟku ētuvāṉa poruṭ pakkattil tōḻi avaṭkuk kūṟutal.
“erumi taṉ kaṉṟiviṭṭu nīṅkātu pakkattiluḷḷa paciya payiri uṇṇum talivaṉatu illattil pala kaṭami kaḷiyum koṇṭu āṇṭu mūttu aṉupavaaṟivu ciṟanta peṇkaḷākiya emakkut talivaṉ puṟattoḻukkattāṉ ēṟpaṭṭa pulavikkālattē, ‘ talivaṉ ittaṉmiyaṉ’ eṉṟu paḻittukkūṟum coṟkaḷāl uṇṭākum payaṉ yātu?’ (kuṟun. 181) eṉṟu tōḻi taliviyit tēṟṟiyurit tal.  	(to. po. 148 iḷa.)
puṟattoḻukkatti yuṭivaṉākiya talivaṉmāṭṭu maṉam vēṟupaṭṭa taliviyi, “puṟattoḻukkamiṉṟi avar niṉmēl aṉpuṭiyavar” eṉa, avvēṟupāṭu nīṅka neruṅ kik kūṟutali yuṭittākiya poruḷiṉkaṇ tōḻi kūṟutal.
“neṟpayiriṉ katirkaḷik koṇṭu naṇṭu nīri oṭṭiyuḷḷa taṉmaṇvaḷikkuc celvatupōl, vēṇṭiya poruḷ kaḷic cērttukkoṇṭu nam maṉikku varum talivaṉik kuṟittu, vaḷikaḷ nekiḻumāṟu uṭal melintu tuṉpuṟu vatu etaṟkāka?” (aiṅ. 27) eṉṟu tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟutal.  		(150 nac.)
tōḻi, ‘arumikkālattup perumi kāṭṭiya, eṇmik kālattu irakkattiṉkaṇ’ kūṟal -
tām ariyarākak kaḷavukkālattut tamatu perumiyik kāṭṭiya tām eḷiyarākiya kaṟpukkālattu irakkattiṉkaṇ tōḻi talivaṟkuk kūṟutal. atu vāḷātē iraṅkutalaṉṟi, “paṇṭu ivvāṟu ceytaṉi; ippoḻutu ivvāṟu ceyyāniṉṟaṉi” eṉat tamatu uyarcciyum talimakaṉatu aṉpu nili yiṉmiyum tōṉṟa iraṅkutal. itu talivaṉ nīṅkiyoḻukum oḻukkam mikkavaḻik kūṟuvatu.
“aiya! muṉpu talivi vēppaṅkāyik koṭuttālum vellak kaṭṭiyip pōla iṉippatākak kūṟiṉīr. ip poḻutō, pārivaḷḷalatu paṟampumalic cuṉiyiṉ iṉiya taṇṇiya nīrit tariṉum atu vevvitāy uvarkkiṉṟatu eṉkiṉṟīr. aṉpunili ivvaḷavil ivvāṟu tirintu viṭṭatu!”(kuṟun- 196) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.			(to. po. 148 iḷa, 150 nac)
tōḻi ‘arumiyiṉ akaṟci’ yiṉkaṇ kūṟal - 
tōḻi, talivaṉ taliviyik kiṭṭutaṟku arumi kūṟi māṟṟutal 	(to. po. 112 iḷa.)
avikēṭṭup piṟṟi ñāṉṟum vantavaṉmāṭṭuc ciṟitu neñcu nekiḻnta tōḻi aṅṅaṉam kūṟātu, “ivaḷ ariyaḷ” eṉṟu kūṟutal. iruvarum uḷvaḻi avaṉvaravu uṇartaliṉ iruvar uḷḷamum uṇarntu aṅṅaṉam kūṟiṉāḷ.
“taliva!  neytalnilattut tāḻampūkkaḷoṭu kuṟiñcinilap pūkkaḷik kaṭṭi muṭimāli amittal iyalumō?  kulattiyē peritāka matikkum eṅkaḷ taliviyi vēṟṟuk kulattavaṉākiya nī maṇattal iyalumō?” eṉṟu tōḻi viṉavutal. 	114 nac.
tōḻi allakuṟippaṭṭami ciṟippuṟamākak kūṟal - 
“talivi! tāḻampū maṇam kamaḻum kaṭaṟkarit tuṟivaṉākiya namtalivaṉ nammāl teḷintukoḷḷa muṭiyāta kuṟi yiṉic ceytuviṭṭāṉ. ātaliṉ nām iyaṟkiyil nikaḻvaṉavaṟṟi avaṉ kuṟi ceytatāka mayaṅki alla kuṟippaṭṭu mīṇṭu viṭṭōm” (ain. aim. 49) eṉṟu talivaṉ ciṟippuṟamākat tōḻikūṟal.  	(to. po. 133 nac.)
tōḻi, ‘avvayiṉ uṟutaki illāp pulaviyuḷ mūḻkiya, kiḻavōḷpāl niṉṟu keṭuttaṟkaṇ’ kūṟutal - 
talivaṉuṭiya puṟattoḻukkattāṉ avaṉpirivu karutip pulaviyil muḻukiya talivipakkam iruntu avaḷpulaviyi nīkkutaṟkaṇ tōḻi kūṟutal. 	(to.po. 150 nac.)
talivaṉ puṟattoḻukkam nikaḻttiyaviṭattu, avaṉ taliviyi vantaṭiyum vāyppiṉip peṟa muṭiyāta taliviyiṉ pulaviyi nōkki avaḷpakkam iruntu pēcu vatupōla avaḷpulaviyit tīrttaṟkaṇ tōḻi kūṟutal. 			(148 iḷa.)
“talivi! nī varuntumāṟu nītta talivaṉ ciṟitum taṉ puṟattoḻukkam paṟṟi nāṇamillātavaṉāyiṉ, avaṉi ṭattē ūṭuvatāl payaṉ eṉ?” (kali 87) eṉṟu tōḻi taliviyiṭam kūṟal.
‘uppamin taṟṟāl ................... nīḷa viṭal.’ (kuṟaḷ 1302)(iḷa., nac.)
“ ‘kāliyil eḻuntavuṭaṉ parattiyarit tērēṟṟivarat tēri āyattam ceytu avarkaḷi aḻittuvantu kūṭiya viḷakkattoṭu talivaṉ illattukku varukiṟāṉē’ eṉṟu talivi varuntukiṟāḷ. uyarkuṭippiṟappu, mikavum cakipput taṉmiyik koṇṭirukkac ceykiṟatu” (kuṟun 45) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟit tōḻi taliviyatu pulavi tīrttal.
tōḻi, ‘avaḷ aṟivuṟuttup piṉvara eṉṟal’ - 
‘ avaṉ aṟivuṟuttu’ eṉpatu iḷampūraṇar pāṭam (112)
taṉṉiṭam, taliviyi aṭiyavēṇṭum kuṟiyi muṉ ṉiṭṭu vanta talivaṉi nōkki, “niṉṉāl kātalikkap paṭṭa talivikku niṉ eṇṇatti mutalil aṟivittu viṭṭup piṉ eṉṉiṭam varuka” eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟutal.
“taliva! talivi taṉṉuḷḷēyē ētō niṉittu nāṇuṟ ṟukkoṇṭiruppavaḷ. itaṉic collappōṉāl avaḷ eṉṉiyum pārttu nāṇuvāḷ. ātaliṉ, taṉiyāka orunāḷ pārttu aṉṟu nī niṉippati avaḷiṭam veḷip paṭiyākak kūṟi avaḷi niṉvaḻippaṭuttu” eṉavum,
“nīyum vēlēntiyuḷḷāy. em tamiyaṉmārkaḷum vēle ṭuttup pōr ceypavarkaḷ. puli pōṉṟuḷḷa nummiṭip pōr vārāmal iruppataṟkākavē,  nāṉ nī koṭukkum taḻiyāṭiyi iṉṟu ēṟkamuṭiyavilli. nāṉ nī taliviyic cantittuk kūṟi uṭaṉpaṭuvittapiṉ nāḷi niṉ kiyuṟiyip peṟṟukkōṭal eṉakku eḷitu” (tiṇi māli. 20) eṉavum kūṟutal.  	(to.po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi avaṉmēlmēl kuṟiceykiṉṟami talivikkuk kūṟal - 
“talivi! cuṟāmīṉkaḷ tiriya iṟāmīṉkaḷ ulavum kaṭaṟkarit tuṟivaṉākiya nam talivaṉ ceyta iravukkuṟi aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ palavāka iruppiṉum, nammi ēmāṟṟi iyaṟkiyil avvaṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ nikaḻntu viṭuvatāl, avi payaṉaṟṟup pōkiṉṟaṉa” (tiṇi. aim. 43) eṉṟu kūṟuvāḷ pōlat talivaṉ iravukkuṟi, mēṉmēlum ceyvataṉit tōḻi talivikku eṭuttukkūṟutal.  			(to. po. 133 nac.)
tōḻi ‘avaṉvarivu maṟuttaṟ’ kaṇ kūṟal - 
talivaṉukkut taliviyi maṇam ceytu koṭuttalit talivicuṟṟattār maṟuttavaḻiyum, tōḻi aṟattoṭu niliyāl kūṟutal.
“aṉṉiyē!  maḻi peytu aruvi nīrāl mūṅkil vaḷarn tuḷḷa maliccarivu vaḻiyē iṟaṅkum talivaṉuṭiya maliyi otta mēmpaṭṭamārpiṉit taḻuvāta nāḷkaḷi lellām ittaliviyiṉ kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷ kalaṅkik kaṇṇīr vaṭikkum.” (aiṅ. 220) eṉavum,
“ittaliviyip periya malināṭaṉākiya talivaṉ varivu vēṇṭivariṉ maṟukkāmal koṭuttāltāṉ ivaḷukku naṉmi ceytatākum. talivaṉ varivu vēṇṭit tamari viṭuttatik kaṇṭapiṉṉum, ittaliviyiṉ tuyaram iṉṉum kuṟiyavilli.” (aiṅ. 258) eṉavum varum tōḻikūṟṟu. 			(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, avaṉ vilaṅkuṟutaṟkaṇ kūṟal -
kāppu mikutiyāṉ iravukkuṟi iṭiyīṭupaṭat talivaṉ vārātu pōyavaḻit talivi varuntumiṭattut tōḻi kūṟutal. 			(to. po. 112 iḷa.)
tōḻiyāṉum taliviyāṉum iṭiyīṭupaṭutaliṉṟit taliva ṉāl kūṭṭattiṟku iṭiyīṭu tōṉṟiṉum tōḻi kūṟṟu nikaḻum. iṭiyūṟu - variviṭip poruṭpirivum, vēntaṟ kuṟṟuḻip pirivum, kāvaṟpirivum ām. 114 nac.
“taliva! nī eṅkaḷiṭam aḻakāṉa cuvimikka coṟkaḷik kūṟit taliviyi meṉmiyākat taḻuvic ceyta taṇṇaḷi yellām uṉ uṇmi aṉpiṉ ceyalkaḷ eṉṟu yāṉ niṉittiruntēṉ. ippoḻututāṉ avi poy eṉpatu pulappaṭukiṟatu. ūravar alar tūṟṟumāṟu emmit taṉittirukka viṭṭuviṭṭu nī pālivaḻiyē cellat tiṭṭa miṭṭiruppati ippoḻutē aṟikiṟōm. nī niccayamāka uyarnta makaṉ alli” eṉṟu tam pētimiyik kātalāl ciṟappittut tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟutal. (kali 19)
“paratavar mukantu vanta mīṉkaḷ kāyavikkappaṭumiṭattup paṟavikaḷ avaṟṟik kavarntu cellum tuṟiyi uṭiya talivaṉi maṟupaṭiyum oru muṟikāṇum vāyppu nēriṉ, ‘nī kavarnta em aḻakiṉit taruka’ eṉṟu vaṟpuṟutti vēṇṭalām” (ain. eḻu. 66). eṉṟu tōḻi kaḷavukkālap piriviṭit taliviyiṭam kūṟal.	(nac)
tōḻi, ‘aḷavumikat tōṉṟiya’ vaḻic cevili karuttik koṇṭu kūṟal - 
kaṇṇum tōḷum muliyum piṟavum puṇarcciyāṉ katirttuk kāriki nīravāyt taliviyiṭattu avimikak kāṭṭiyavaḻic cevili tāṉē kūṟutalum, avaḷ kūṟṟiṉit tōḻi koṇṭu kūṟutalum ām.
e-ṭu:	‘kaṇṇiṟinta kārikik kāmpērttōṭ pētikkup
	     peṇṇiṟinta nīrmi peritu’.  (kuṟaḷ - 1272)
itu katirppuk kaṇṭu cevili tāṉē kūṟiyatu. tōḻi talivaṉiṭam, “taliva! nī taliviyi viṭuttup pōyiṉa piṉ, cevili, avaḷ curuṇṭakūntal, poṉpōla mārpil paranta tēmal, kacciṭiyē mukaṅkoṇṭeḻunta nakilkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi nōkki, ‘ivaḷ paruvam uṟṟatāl mika aḻakiya ḷāka uḷḷāḷ’ eṉṟu paṉmuṟi kūṟi avaḷit taḻuvi avaḷ etirkālattip paṟṟi neṭitu niṉittu avaḷi iṟceṟittuviṭṭāḷ” eṉṟu cevili karuttik koṇṭu kūṟiyatu. (akanā 50) 	(to.po. 115 nac.)
tōḻi, ‘aṟṟam aḻivu urippataṉkaṇ kūṟal - 
kaḷavukkālattuppaṭṭa varuttam nīṅkiyami paṟṟit taliviyiṭa mum talivaṉiṭamum tōḻi kūṟutal.
“maṇalmēṭṭil ñāḻalpūkkaḷ maṇam vīcum tuṟivaṉi nī makiḻvoṭu taḻuvikkoḷka” (aiṅ. 148). eṉṟu kaṟpuk kālattē tōḻi taliviyiṭam kūṟiyami.
“nāṭa! nī ivaḷi maṇantu nalla ceyal ceytāy. niṉtanti vāḻka! em illattē ivaḷukkut tirumaṇam nikaḻa, atupōtu ivaḷ kūntalil malariccūṭṭi nāṭaṟiya ivaḷi niṉ tuṇivi ākkik koṇṭāyē!” (aiṅ.294) eṉṟu vatuvikuṟittut tōḻi talivaṉiṭam makiḻntukūṟal. 			(to.po. 150 nac.)
tōḻi, ‘aṟṟam illāk, kiḻavōṟ cuṭṭiya teyvak kaṭattiṉ’ kaṇ kūṟal - 
kuṟṟamillāta talivaṉik karutit teyvattiṟku vaḻipāṭāṟ ṟumvaḻit tōḻi taliviyiṭamum talivaṉiṭamum kūṟutal. 		(to. po. 148 iḷa.)
“‘ayalār alaruri kēṭṭuc cuṭumoḻi kūṟi accam tōṉṟap pārkkum tāy paṭuttum tuyar tīrvatāka! ’ eṉṟu teyvam taṅkiya maliyi maṟintu niṉṟu vaṇaṅkiyataṉ payaṉi, virivil talivaṉi maṇantukoṇṭataṉmūlam peṟṟuviṭṭōm” eṉṟu tōḻi taliviyiṭam kūṟiyatu. 
“‘em talivaṉ vāḻka! aracaṉ pakitaṇika! amitiyākap pallāṇṭu kaḻika!’ eṉṟu talivi virumpiṉāḷ. yāṅkaḷ, ‘talivaṉ virivil taliviyi varital vēṇṭum; entiyum avaṟkut taliviyi  maṟātu koṭuttal vēṇṭum’ eṉṟu virumpiṉōm” (aiṅ. 6) eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟutal.
“taliva! eṉ talivi niṉ pirivāl tōḷ nekiḻntu varuntiya tuṉpattip pōkka viruntiṉaṉāka nī varumāṟu karinta kākkikku, naḷḷiyiṉ kāṭṭiluḷḷa pacukkaḷiṉ neyyōṭu, toṇṭi eṉṟa ūril viḷinta veṇṇel cōṟṟi eḻukalaṅkaḷil uṇavākap paṭippiṉum, kimmāṟu ākātu” (kuṟun 210) eṉṟu, pirintuvanta taliva ṉiṭam tōḻi kūṟal.  (iḷa.) 
tōḻi, ‘aṟṟam  illāk kiḻavōṭcuṭṭiya’ eṉṟu pāṭaṅ koṇṭār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. (150) avar uri:
kaḷavu veḷippaṭāta vakiyil talivaṉ taliviyi maṇakkumāṟu, tāṉ muṉ vēṇṭiya teyvattiṟku, tirumaṇam muṭintapiṉ paravukkaṭaṉ koṭuttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṟkuk kūṟutal.
‘kiḻavōṟ cuṭṭiya’ eṉpatu iḷampūraṇar pāṭam. avar uri:
“ ‘tāy varuttuvataṉit tīrppāyāka!’ eṉṟu talivi mali uṟi teyvatti vaḻipaṭṭataṉ payaṉāka, niṉvatuvi virivil kūṭiṟṟu ātaliṉ ammaliyuṟi teyvattiṟkup paravukkaṭaṉ koṭuttal vēṇṭum” eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟiyatu.
tōḻi aṟattoṭu niṟṟal - 
tāy tuñcāmi, nāy tuñcāmi mutaliya ciṟikāvalāṉ talivi talivaṉi iravukkuṟikkaṇ aṭiyamuṭiyātu pōka avaḷukku vēṭki mikkavaḻiyum, ayalār peṇ kēṭkat toṭaṅkiyavaḻiyum, talivaṉtamar pārppār cāṉṟōr ākiyōri muṉṉiṭṭu, aruṅkalaṉkaḷoṭu taliviyi makaṭpēca vantavaḻip pariyam ciṟitu eṉṟō, nāḷum puḷḷum aṉṟu takkaṉa alla eṉṟō oru kāraṇam kāṭṭit talivitamar makaṭkoṭi maṟuttavaḻiyum, talivaṉ iravukkuṟikku varum vaḻi, eṇkum veṇ kōṭṭi yāṉiyum aravum urumum puliyum vāṉara makaḷirum variaramakaḷirum uṭittātaliṉ ataṉkaṇ talivaṟku ētam nikaḻuṅkollō eṉṟu talivi añciyavaḻiyum, 
ākiya nāṉ kiṭattum, talivi taṉ nāṇatti viṭṭut tōḻikku aṟattoṭu niṟpat tōḻi kālam tāḻttātu, cevilikku māṟukōḷ illā moḻiyiṉāṉ aṟattoṭu niṟkum.  	(iṟi. a 29 uri.)
tōḻi ‘aṟaṉ eṉappaṭutal iruvakip, puritīr (muṉṉilik) kiḷavi (eṉṟu) tāyiṭip pukuppiṉ’ kaṇ kūṟal - 
aṟaṉ eṉṟu collappaṭum taṉmi iruvarkaṇṇum kuṟṟam tīrnta etirppāṭu(cantippu) eṉṟu ceviliyiṭattē kūṟi, akkiḷaviyi naṟṟāyiṭattuc celuttutaṟkaṇ tōḻi kūṟutal; eṉṟatu, puṉaltaru puṇarcciyum, pūttaru puṇarcci yum, kaḷiṟu tarupuṇarcciyum pōlvaṉa cevilikkuk kūṟi avaḷ naṟṟāykkuk kūṟutali nikaḻvittalām. avaḷ tantikkum taṉṉiyarkkum uṇarttutalum, tōḻi ataṉi mīṇṭuvantu talivikku uṇarttutalum peṟṟām.
‘kāmar kaṭumpuṉal’ (kali. 39) eṉṉum kalippāṭṭiṉuḷ, tōḻiyoṭu, virintucellum nīril nīrāṭiya taliviyi nīr īrttuc ceṉṟavaḻi, aruḷiṉāl nīriṟkutittu avaḷit taḻuvik kari cērtta kāṉakanāṭaṉmakaṉ ceyalit tōḻi cevilikku uṇartta, cevili naṟṟāykku uṇartta, naṟṟāy tanti taṉṉiyarkku uṇartta, avarkaḷ orunāḷ muḻutum cūḻntu, taliviyi attalivaṉoṭu maṇaviṉiyāṉ kūṭṭuvikka muṭivuceytamiyit tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟiyatu. 	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi aṟiyāḷ pōlak kūṟal -
“iṅkuḷḷa aḻakiya peṇkaḷ palaruḷḷum nī virumpik kuṟiyuṟukiṉṟatu yāriṭattu eṉpatu eṉakkup pulappaṭa villi” eṉṟu tōḻi aṟiyātavaḷ pōlat talivaṉiṭam kūṟutal. 
iḵtu ‘eṇṇarum palnaki kaṇṇiya vakiyiṉum’ eṉṟa tāl koḷḷappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ orukiḷavi. (to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, āṟṟatu tīmi aṟivuṟu kalakkattiṉkaṇ kūṟal - 
talivaṉ varuneṟiyiṉatu tīmiyit tāṅkaḷ aṟivuṟṟata ṉāṉ eytiya kalakkattiṉkaṇ tōḻi kūṟutal.
“ūravar uṟaṅkiviṭṭaṉar. pēykaḷ ulavat toṭaṅki viṭṭaṉa. ūrkkāvalar kuṟiñcippaṇ pāṭikkoṇṭu uṟaṅkāmal ūrik kāttuvarukiṉṟaṉar. yāṉiyoṭu pakalil poruta vēṅki uṟumukiṟatu. innaḷḷiravil miṉṉaloṭu maliccāralil maḻi poḻikiṟatu. pāmpukaḷ tām kakkiya maṇikaḷit tēṭi uḻalumāṟu iṭi iṭikkiṟatu. attakiya kāṭṭuvaḻiyē iṉṟu talivaṉ vārātiruppi ṉum naṉṟu. nām piriviṉāṉ tōḷ nekiḻntu varuntiṉum, nam tuyarip poruṭpaṭuttal vēṇṭā. avaṉ ēmamē iṉṟiyamiyātatu” eṉṟu tōḻi taliviyiṭam kūṟiyatu.  			(naṟ. 255)
maḻipeytal, pāmpuulāval, puliuṟumutal - āṟṟatutīmi;
ūrkkāvalar uṟaṅkāmi - variṉum, kūṭutalarumi.
eṉpaṉa kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
“vēṅkiyiṭam akappaṭṭu uyntu āṇyāṉi accat tuṭaṉ mella naṭakkum vaḻiyilē talivaṉ naḷḷiruḷil varutal kuṟittut talivikaṇkaḷ kavaliyāl uṟaṅkāvā yiṉa” (ain. aim. 16) itu tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟiyatu.
“taliva! kāṭṭu vaḻiyil yāṉikaḷ iyaṅkum. vāṉattil iṭi olikkum. iṭiyiṭiyē añcattakka pāmpum puliyum kāṇappaṭum. cellum vaḻiyum kuṟukiyatu. nīyum taṉittu varukiṟāy. nī virivil ivaḷi maṇantu koḷvataṟku ēṟpāṭu ceykiṟēṉ. iṉṟumutal iravukkuṟikkum vārātē. avvāṟu variṉum, tuyarattoṭu niṉcelavi niṉittu ēṅkiniṟkum yāṅkaḷ amitiyuṟumāṟu eṅkaḷik kaṇṭu mīṇṭu nī uṉ iruppiṭatti nalamāka eytiyati yāṅkaḷ aṟiyumāṟu caṅkiṉi ūtit terivippāyāka” (akanā 318) eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟiyatu. 	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, ‘āṟṟatu tīmi aṟivuṟu kalakkam’ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉkaṇ kūṟal - 
talivaṉ varuneṟiyiṉatu tīmiyit tāṅkaḷ aṟivuṟṟata ṉāṉ eytiya kalakkattāṉum kāvaṟkaṭumi variyiṟantata ṉāṉum kuṟiyiṭamum kālamumākat tāṅkaḷ varinta nili miyi vilakki, talivi kātal mikutal uḷappaṭap piṟavum avaṉuṭiya nāṭum ūrum illum kuṭiyum piṟappum ciṟappum eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ mikutiyi nōkki avaṉmāṭṭuk kiḷakkum kiḷaviyoṭu kūṭi attaṉmittākiya vakiyi ṉāṉē varital vēṇṭiyum tōḻikūṟṟu nikaḻum. 			(to. po. 112 iḷa.)
tōḻi, “āṟṟāta taliviyik kaṭitiṉ varivēṉ” eṉṟu talivaṉ teḷivikkap pukkuḻit teḷiviṭi vilakkik kūṟiyatu -
“taliva! ekkālattum karuṇi ceytaluṭiya mēmpaṭṭa naṭpiṉiyuṭiya uṉ pōṉṟa cāṉṟōrkaḷum aṟiya uṟutimoḻi kūṟi, empōṉṟōr ‘cūḷ niṟivēṟavil liyē!’ eṉṟu vāṭi varuntumpaṭi uṟutimoḻiyi niṟi vēṟṟātiruppiṉ, empōṉṟār uyirvāḻtal evvāṟu? uṉ teḷivuri aḻintoḻika! talivi tāṉ paṭavēṇṭum tuṉpamellām nukarntu aḻika!” eṉṟu tōḻi, talivaṉ uṟutiyākat teḷivitta coṟkaḷit taṭuttut talivi nili kūṟutal. (naṟ 345).
iccūttirattup ‘piṟavum’ (aṭi. 32) eṉṟataṉāṉ koṇṭa kūṟṟu itu. 	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi āṟu iṉṉāmi kūṟal -
“tiṉippuṉattuk katirkaḷik kiḷikaḷ uṇṇātavāṟu koṭicci avaṟṟi ōṭṭavum, avaḷiyum maṟittuk kiḷikaḷ tiṉikkatirik kavarntuṇṇum nāṭa! iruḷ perukiviṭṭatu. yāṉikaḷ iyaṅkum kāṭṭuvaḻiyil iravukkuṟi karuti vāraṟka”(aiṅ 282) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
iḵtu ‘āṟṟatu tīmi aṟivuṟu kalakkam’ eṉpataṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum.	(to.po. 112 iḷa.)
tōḻi iyaṟpaḻittal (1) -
kaṟpukkālattil talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirintu avaḷ illattāṉāka, avaṉ taliviillattē iruppatākak karuti viṭiyaṟkāliyil tuyileḻumaṅkalam pāṭa vanta pāṇaṉiṭam tōḻi, ” em talivi varuntat talivaṉ ayalāriṭattut taṉ iṉpatti nalkutalāl, avaṉ eṅkaṭkut takutiyuṭiyaṉ allaṉ” eṉat talimakaṉ paṇpiṉip paḻittuk kūṟiyatu. itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi 376.)
tōḻi iyaṟpaḻittal (2) -
pirintu ceṉṟa talivaṉ vārātatāl varuntiya taliviyiṉ nili kaṇṭa tōḻi talivaṉik kuṟṟam cāṟṟip pēcutal.
“talivi! ‘niṉṉiṟpiriyēṉ’ eṉṟu cūḷuṟavu kūṟiyapiṉṉum nī aḻumāṟu pirintu ceṉṟa talivar nallarnallar! ” eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉ paṇpukaḷi iḻittup pēcutal. 			(tañci kō 292)
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘talivi aṟattoṭu niṟṟal’ eṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. (aṟattoṭunili, oruvaḻit taṇantatum variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirintatum paṟṟi nikaḻum).	(na. a. 176.)
tōḻi iravukkuṟi ēṟpittu, “innilattiṉkaṇ nī varuṅkāl iṉṉa peṟṟiyāṉ varuvāyāka” eṉṟatu - 
“taliva! neytalnilattalivaṉ makaḷākiyatalivi, āmpaṟ pūkkaḷ iṭiyē toṭukkappaṭṭa cantaṉattaḻiyālākiya āṭiyi aṇintu nīlam cūṭi varuvāḷ. ivaḷ tamiyaṉ mār cūṭumpū tāmarippū ākum. ātaliṉ nīyum tāmarippūc cūṭi variṉ ivvūrār niṉṉip putiyavaṉ eṉṟu aiyuṟār” (tiṇimoḻi. 40) eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṟku iravukkuṟikku ivvāṟu varuka eṉṟu aṟivuṟuttal.
iccūttirattup ‘puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu itu.			(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, iravu kuṟikkaṇ talivaṉ vantami talivikkuk kūṟal - 
“talivi! cuṟāmīṉ piṟaḻum uppaṅkaḻikaḷi nīnti  nāḷ tōṟum varum talivaṉ ippoḻutu iravukkuṟi nayantu vantuḷḷāṉ” (ciṟṟeṭṭakam) eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟutal.
‘puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ koḷḷappaṭuva toru kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, iravukkuṟi nayanta talivaṉ ciṟippuṟamāka, pakaṟkuṟi nēr vāḷ pōla, iravukkāppu mikuti kūṟal - 
“talivi! uyarnta maliyiṉ arukil iṉiya oliyiyuṭiya aruviyil yām nīrāṭiyapōtu aruvi niṉṉi īrttuc cellātapaṭi pātukātta talivaṉ mārpiyē teppa mākak koṇṭu aruviyil āṭiyatu mika iṉimiyāka iruntatu. āyiṉ iravil uṟakkamiṉṟi viḷakkoḷiyil aṉṉi nam mutukiṉit taḻuvikkoṇṭu tūṅkavum, uṟakkam vārāmal nām uṟaṅkamuyalvatu tuṉpamāka uḷḷatu” (kuṟun. 353) eṉṟu talivaṉ ciṟippuṟamākat tōḻi iraviṟ kāval mikkuḷḷami paṟṟik kūṟutal.
‘puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ koḷḷappaṭuva toru kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi,  iravukkuṟi nērnta vāypaṭṭāṉ talivikkuk kūṟal - 
“talivi! nam talivaṉ, kaḷiṟu puliyit tākki valicōrum malippakkavaḻiyē naḷḷiravil namporuṭṭu varuvāṉ. avaṉ varukiyāl namakku uṇṭākum paḻikku nām nāṇōm” (kuṟun. 88) eṉṟu iravukkuṟi paṟṟit tōḻi, taliviṭam kūṟutal.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ, ‘puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum’ eṉṟa taṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po.  112 iḷa.)
tōḻi, iravukkuṟiyiṭam kāṭṭikkūṟal - 
“kāṉaṟ cērppa! em illattu muṉṉiṭam, uppaṅkaḻiyiṉatu neṭiya kaṭaṟkaric cōliyiṉiṭattē puṉṉimaraṅkaḷ taḻikkappeṟṟu, maṭalkaḷil aṉṟiṟpaṟavikaḷ kūppiṭum neṭiya paṉikaḷiyum uṭiyatu. (avviṭam emtaliviyi nī iravil maruvutaṟkēṟṟa kuṟiyiṭam ām.) (tiṇimāli. 56) eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṟkuk kūṟutal.
‘puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kūṟṟu itu.   	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, “iravikkuṟiyiṭit talivaṉ varukiṉṟāṉ” eṉat taliviyiṭam kūṟal - 
“nilavum maṟintuviṭṭatu; iruḷum paraviviṭṭatu. nammi veḷiyē cellātu pātukākkum aṉṉiyum uṟaṅki viṭṭāḷ. kāppōri nīttut taṉittuvarum yāṉi pōlat talivaṉ ippaṉiyil naṭuṅkiyavāṟu kuṟikkaṇ vantuḷḷāṉ. kāṇāmaṟpōṉa nalla aṇikalam mīṇṭum kiṭikkap peṟuvatāl viḷiyum makiḻcci pōṉṟa makiḻvuṭaṉ, iṟceṟippāl aṭiyamuṭiyātupōṉa talivaṉ mārpiṉi ippoḻutu peṟṟu, ācitīrat taḻuvi mīḷka.” (naṟ. 182) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
‘puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kūṟṟu itu.  	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, iravukkuṟi vanta talivaṉ ciṟippuṟamāka, kūkikku urippāḷāyk kūṟal -
“emmūr vāyiṟkaṇ nīrttuṟiyil paruttōṅkiya ālamarat tōṭu  uṭaṉuṟivu payiṉṟa, tēyāmal vaḷinta vāyiṉi yum kūriya nakaṅkaḷiyum uṭiyiyāy, vāyākiya paṟi yōciyālē piṟari varuttum kūkiyē! emmik kāṇa vēṇṭum kuṟiyāk koḷkiyarāy emkātalar empāl varutali virumpi yām tūṅkātu maṉam cuḻalum ivviṭi yāmattilē, yām añcumāṟu niṉ kaṭuṅkuralāl oliyā toḻivāyāka! ataṟkup paricilāka nī uṇṇutaṟporuṭṭu āṭṭiṟicciyi neyyil puḻukkiya ataṉōṭu eliyic cuṭṭa avveṇmiyāṉa cūṭṭiṉiyum niṟiyak koṭup pōm.” (naṟ. 83) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
‘puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kūṟṟu itu.  	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi iravukkuṟi vilakkiyatu - 
“taliva! niṉ vēliyē niṉakkut tuṇiyākakkoṇṭu, mika virintu iṟaṅkum veṇṇiṟaaruviyi nīnti, naḷḷira vil tīṅku payakkum vaḻiyil nī varutali niṉittut talivi yiṉ uḷḷam eṉṉa tuyaruṟumō eṉṟu eṉ neñcu varuntukiṉṟatu. ātaliṉ iravukkuṟikkaṇ vāraṟka” (ain. aim. 19) eṉṟatu.	(to. po. 114 nac. )
itu ‘kaḷaṉum poḻutum varinili vilakki’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi iravukkuṟi vēṇṭiya talivaṉukku uṭaṉpaṭṭuk kūṟiyatu - 
“katiravaṉ mēṟṟiciyil maṟikiyālē kaṭaltuṟiyum polivu iḻantuviṭṭatu. nārikaḷum iṟāmīṉkaḷi aruntip puṉṉi marattil taṅkiviṭṭaṉa. pūkkaḷ maṟiyumāṟu veḷḷatti mikuvittu nīrttuṟiyil cuṟāmīṉkaḷ iyaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. viḷakkukaḷik koḷuttik koṇṭu emcuṟṟat tārum mīṉvēṭṭikkup paṭakukaḷil ceṉṟuviṭṭaṉar. kaṭaṟkariyi aṭuttu yāṅkaḷ vāḻum ūril iṉṟiravu taṅkic celvatāl niṉakku iḻappu ētum uṇṭō?” (naṟ. 67) eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟi iravukkuṟi ēṟpittatu.
‘puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kūṟṟu itu.  	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi iravu nīṭṭam kūṟic celavu aḻuṅkuvittal - 
nī illāta iravu talivikku ūḻikkālam pōl nīṇṭu tuyartarum, ātaliṉ avaḷip piriyaṟka” eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṟku urittal.
“taliva!  niṉṉip pirintāl talivikku iraviṉ ovvoru nāḻikiyum pallūḻikaḷākak kaḻiyum. attakiya iraviṉ muppatunāḻikikaḷiyum varuttattuṭaṉ kaḻittup piḻit tiruntu ivaḷ katiravaṉik kāṇpatu eṅṅaṉam?” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘poruḷvayiṟ pirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(ampikā 544.)
tōḻi illuḷiruntu ciṟippuṟamākak kūṟiyatu - 
“talivi! nam talivaṉ nam aḻakiya nalaṉi nukarntu nammit taṉittu varuntaviṭṭup pirintu ceṉṟuviṭṭāṉē eṉṟu pōlum, nammēluḷḷa karuṇiyāṉ nammip pōla uṟakkamiṉṟi maṇalmēṭukaḷil mōti iravumuḻutum kaṭal uṟaṅkātirukkiṉṟatu!” (ain. eḻu. 60) eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 133 nac.)
tōḻi, “ivvoḻukkattiṉi vēṅki nīkkiṟṟu” eṉat talivikkuk kūṟal -
“talivi!  palanāḷum kaḷavoḻukkattil amintu paṉi yaḷavu iṉpatti nām nukaravēṇṭum eṉṟu tiṭṭamiṭ ṭiruppavum, nalviṉi payaṉ taruvatāl celvam perukuvatu pōlap pāttikaḷil tiṉikkatir mikutiyākat tōṉṟa, vēṅkippūt tōṉṟum kālam tiṉimutir kālamātaliṉ, vēṅki pūttut tiṉiyi ariyum nāḷik kuṟippiṭṭu nam tiṭṭam vīṇāmpaṭi nammi ippuṉattiṉkaṇ kūṭavarum talivaṉiṭa miruntu pirittuviṭṭatē! itaṉi niṉittup pār” (tiṇi māli 5) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
‘kāppiṉ kaṭumi kiyaṟa variṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ koḷḷap paṭṭatoru kūṟṟu itu.	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, “ivaḷ iṉi āṇṭu variṉ cuṟṟattār aṟivar” eṉal - 
“taliva! nēṟṟiravu talivi uṉ tērkkutiriyiṉ maṇi yōci eṉṟu paṟavikaḷ eḻuppiya oliyit tavaṟupaṭak karutip paṭukkiyiṉiṉṟu eḻuntāḷ. acceyti emarāl aṟiyappaṭa vāyppuṇṭu. ātaliṉ iṉi iravukkuṟikkaṇ vāraṟka” (ain. eḻu. 59) eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu. 			 (to. po. 133 nac. uri.)
tōḻi iṉṟiyamiyāmi kūṟic celavu aḻuṅkuvittal - 
talivi uyirvāḻat talivaṉ avaḷip piriyātiruttal iṉṟiyamiyātatu eṉat tōḻi talivaṟkuc colli avaṉ cellutalit taṭuttal.
“kāmanukarvum, nīṅkāta iḷamiyum, piriyāta iṉṉuyir pōṉṟa taliviyum peṟṟirukkiyil, vēṟu virumpappaṭum poruḷ etaṟku? ātalāl, taliva! talivi melintu varunta nī poruḷukkākap piriya oruppaṭṭatu eṟṟukku?”	(ampikā. 541)
itu ‘poruḷvayiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
tōḻi, ‘uṇarppuvayiṉ vārā ūṭal uṟṟōḷvayiṉ, uṇarttal vēṇṭiya kiḻavōṉpāl niṉṟu, tāṉ vekuṇṭu ākkiya takutikkaṇ’ kūṟutal - 
talivaṉ ūṭaltīrkkavum ataṉvaḻi vārāta ūṭaluṟ ṟōḷvayiṉ avvūṭalit tīrttal vēṇṭiya talivaṉ pakkattaḷāki niṉṟu, taliviyi vekuṇṭu niṉṟu uṇṭākkiya takutikkaṇ tōḻi kūṟal.(to. po. 148 iḷa.)
“pulavi, kalaviiṉpam ceytaṟku vēṇṭumaḷaviṉatu ātal, uppāṉatu tuyppaṉavaṟṟi iṉcuviyiṉa ākkutaṟku vēṇṭum aḷaviṉatu ātalpōlum; iṉi, appulaviyi avvaḷaviṉum ciṟitu mikumāṟu nīṭṭutal, avvuppu avvaḷaviṉum mikkāṟ pōlum” (kuṟaḷ 1302) iḷa.
talivaṉ ūṭalippōkka muṟpaṭavum ūṭal taṇiyāta taliviyiṉ ūṭalip piṉṉum pōkka virumpiya avaṉatu pakkattaḷāki niṉṟu, tāṉ taliviyik kaḻaṟi avaḷ ūṭal nīṅkum taṉmiyi uṇṭākkiya takutikkaṇ tōḻi kūṟal. (150 nac.)
“maṉivāḻkkiyi mēviya kaṟpuṭimaṭantiyākiya talivi! talivaṉ ivvūr tāṅkāta aḷavu palarākiya parattiyarit tērēṟṟik koṇṭuvantu kuṭiyēṟṟi yuḷḷāṉ eṉṟu orupayaṉum illāmal avaṉpāl ūṭutal poruttamiṉṟu. talivar parattimikkāka avaroṭu pulantu niliyākap pirintu vāḻum maṉa valimiyuṭiyavarkaḷ avarāl muṟṟum puṟakkaṇikkappaṭu vatāl, celvavāḻkkiyi iḻantu ciṟitaḷavu ariciyit tāmē camittuṇṭu putalvarukku koṭukkap pālum curakkāta nakilōṭu uṭal melintu vaṟiya vāḻkkiyil vikum niliyiṉarāyiruppati nī aṟiyāyō?” (akanā 316) eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōḻi kaḻaṟṟuri. (nac).
tōḻi, ‘urittuḻik kūṭṭa’ttiṉkaṇ kūṟal - 
talivaṉ tāṉ virumpiya talivi yāvaḷ eṉpataṉit tōḻiyiṭam kūṟiyavaḻi, “avaḷum niṉṉip pōlavē taṭumāṟṟamuṟṟu niṟkiṟāḷ” eṉṟu tōḻi avaṉōṭu avaḷik kūṭṭik kūṟutal. 	(to. po. 112 iḷa.)
“uppaṅkaḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu paṟittuvanta nīlamalarkaḷic cūṭāma lum, naṇṭuvaḷikaḷik kōliṭṭu alikkāmalum, neṟṟiyil viyarvi tuḷikkac cintaṉiyil āḻntirukkum taliviyiṭam nāṉ ecceytiyi uriyāṭikkoṇṭu neruṅkic colvatu eṉpatē eṉakku viḷaṅkavilli!” eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.	(iḷa)
āyattiṉ nīṅkit taṉṉoṭu niṉṟa taliviyit taliva ṉoṭu kūṭṭavēṇṭi avaḷi viṭuttu avaṉiṭam vantu iṉṉa viṭattu ivaḷic cantippāyāka eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟal. 114 nac.
“koṇka! neytaṟpūvip parappi ataṉmēl eṉ pāviyi vittuviṭṭu nī irukkumiṭattukku yāṉvantuḷḷēṉ. nāri eṉ pāviyi mitittuviṭumō eṉṟañci yām virivil cēravēṇṭum. virivil nīyē avaḷip pōkaviṭu” (kuṟun 114) eṉṟu tōḻi kuṟippāṉ talivi varavu kūṟi, avaḷi iṭattu uyttavāṟu kūṟutal. (nac.)
tōḻi uri māṟupaṭṭatu -
talivaṉuṭiya pirivāl talivi varuntiyirunta kaṟpuk kālattu avaḷatu tuṉpam nīṅkumāṟu tēṟṟutaṟku uri eṭutta tōḻi, taṉ kūṟṟiṉiyē cevimaṭukkāmal talivi āṟṟiyiruppataṉi aṟintu, “maṭavāy! talivaṉ kūṟiya paruvam vantuviṭṭatu eṉpataṟku aṟikuṟiyāka maḻi peyyat toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu. talivaṉ ipparuvattum mīṇṭu varavilliyē eṉṟu nī eṉṉiṭam oru vārttikūṭa āṟṟāmiyoṭu kūṟavilliyē! iḵtu eṉṉa viyappu?” eṉṟu māṟupaṭak kūṟutal. 	(naṟ. 237.)
tōḻi ‘ulaku urittu oḻittaṟ’ kaṇ kūṟal - 
ulakattār makaṭkoḷḷumāṟupōlat taliviyi talivaṉ maṇantukoṇṭu piṉ kūṭumāṟu tōḻi kūṟutal. “uppaṅkaḻikaḷil iṟāamīṉkaḷi akappaṭuttum cērppa! ittaliviyi nī aṭitalvēṇṭiṉ, maṇantukoṇṭu eytuvāyāka!” (aiṅ. 196) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.			(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, ‘eṇṇarum paṉṉaki kaṇṇiya vakiyiṉ’ kūṟal - 
tōḻi, talivaṉiṭam kaḷavoḻukkam puṟattārāl alar kūṟip palavāṟu nakikkappaṭum nilikku vantuviṭṭatākak kūṟutal. 	(to. po. 112 iḷa.)
“‘ippakalil makaḷir tamakku iyaṟkiyāṉa aṭakkap paṇpiṉik kākkum iyalpiṉarallātu irukkiṉṟaṉar; ivarkaḷ irakkappaṭutaṟkuriyar’ eṉṉātu, kāmam irakaciya māyirukkum elliyik kaṭantu ūrppotuviṭam vari ceṉṟuviṭṭatu!” 	(kuṟaḷ 1138.)
“taliva! nam kaḷavoḻukkattit tāyum aṟintuviṭṭāḷ. ūrmuḻutum icceyti paraviviṭṭatu. uṉṉip pirintiruk kum taṉimiyil eṅkaṭku illattil iruppatum veṟuttu viṭṭatu. koṭiya vāṭikkāṟṟum taṉittirukkum eṅkaḷi varuttukiṟatu. nāṅkaḷum uṉṉōṭu uṅkaḷūrkkup puṟappaṭukiṟōm, eḻuka!” (aiṅ238) eṉpatu pōṉṟu tōḻi talivaṟku alaraccam cāṟṟutal. (iḷa). 
talivaṉ iḷivantoḻukutaṟkup poṟāta tōḻi avaṉ iḷivaravu uṇarttum karuttiṉaḷāyc ceyaṟkiyāka ārāytaṟku ariyavāka varum oṉṟallāp palavakic ceytikaḷi nakiccuvi tōṉṟak kuṟittuk kūṟutal. 114 nac. (iḷivantu oḻukutal - taṉṉit tāḻttikkoṇṭu paḻakutal).
avi ‘eṉṉi maṟittal evaṉākiyar’ eṉṟalum, aṟiyāḷ pōṟalum, kuṟiyāḷ kūṟalum, paṭittumoḻi kiḷaviyum, kuṟippu vēṟu koḷalum piṟavumām.(nac). 
‘tōḻi eṉpu nekap pirintōṉvaḻic ceṉṟukaṭii, aṉputali yaṭutta vaṉpuṟikkaṇ’ kūṟal -
eṉpu urukumāṟu talivaṉāṉ piriyappaṭṭa talivikku vaḻi pāṭāṟṟic ceṉṟu tāṉ kūṟum moḻiyi avaḷmaṉattē celuttit talivaṉ aṉpiṉi avaḷiṭattē cērttuk kūṟiya vaṟpuṟuttutaṟkaṇ tōḻi kūṟutal.
appirivu varintukōṭaṟkup poruḷvayiṉ piritalum, vēntarkku uṟṟuḻip piritalum, kāvaṟkup piritalum ām. avviṭattē vaṟpuṟuttuṅkāl tōḻi taliviyi āṟṟi yiruttal ceyyum poruṭṭāka, talivaṉi iyaṟpaḻittum, iyaṟpaṭa moḻintum palavāṟāka avaṉatu meyyaṉpiṉi avaḷukku viḷakkik kūṟuvāḷ. “ceṅkaṭu moḻiyāṉ” iyaṟpaḻittaṉavum vaṟpuṟuttal payaṉām.  (to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, ‘eṉṉi maṟittal evaṉ ākiyar?’ (iṟi.a.12 ) eṉal -
eṉṉi maṟittu nīṅkaḷ iruvarum ecceyalum ceytal iyalātu eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟal.
“malināṭa!  maṟikkattakāta eṉṉi maṟittukkoṇṭu nī uṉ viruppam pōlak kaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻttak karutiṉāy āyiṉ, iṉit taliviyiṭam eṉṉi viṭuttu nī taṉittuc ceyyak kūṭiya ceyal oṉṟumilli” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
iḵtu ‘eṇṇarum palnaki kaṇṇiya vakiyiṉum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ orukūṟṟu.			(to. po. 114nac.)
tōḻi, ‘aiyacceyki tāykku etirmaṟuttup poy eṉa māṟṟi mey vaḻik koṭuttal’ -
talivikkup piṟaroṭu kūṭṭamuṇṭu eṉṟu collic cevili aiyuṟṟavaḻi, ataṉi maṟuttut talivi ceyta ceykiyip poyyeṉa nīkkip piṟitōrāṟṟāṉ tāy nampumāṟu tōḻi vēṟoru ceyti kūṟutal.
“tāyē! iṭiyoṭu maḻi peytu ōynta naḷḷiravil taṉ kātaṇikaḷ oḷivīca maliyiṉiṉṟu iṟaṅkum mayil pōlat talivi oru mēṭṭiṉiṉṟu kīḻē iṟaṅkiyatik kaṇṭatākak karuti eṅkaḷi vekuḷātē. nam maliyiluḷḷa teyvaṅkaḷ aḻakiya pūkkaḷic cūṭit tām virumpiya vaṭivattil nam maṉippakkal varutaluṇṭu. cilapōtu, kaṉavukaḷ kūṭa naṉavu pōla neñcil nilipeṟutal kūṭum. nam taliviyō maṉikkaṇ viḷakkillāta iṭattil kūṭat taṉiyē irukka naṭuṅkum iyalpiṉaḷ; marāmarattil kōṭṭāṉ olittā lum neñcu naṭuṅkip pātukāvalāṉa iṭatti nōkki ōṭi viṭupavaḷ. ivitavira, tanti maṉikkaṇ uḷḷapōtu talivi ittaku ceyal puriya añcamāṭṭāḷō?” (akanā.158) eṉṟu tāy nampumāṟu tōḻi tam kaḷavoḻukkat tiṉi maṟittup pēcutal.
“tāyē! talivikkuk kaṇkaḷ ēṉ civantuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu viṉavukiṟāy. maliyil vēṅkimalarkaḷip paṟittatāl eṅkaḷ uṭal viyarttuviṭṭatu. viyarvi tīra aruviyil pāyntu nīrāṭiṉōm. ataṉāl talivikkuk kaṇkaḷ civantuviṭṭaṉa” (ain. aim. 15) eṉṟu kaḷaviṉi maṟuttut tōḻi viṉaviya tāykkup poykkāraṇam kūṟal. 			(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, oruvaḻit taṇanta talivaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat talivikkuk kūṟal - 
“nilavik kuvittāṟ pōṉṟa veṇmaṇaliṉ oru pakkattil iruḷit tokuttu vittāṟpōṉṟa taṇṇiya ceṟinta niḻal tarum puṉṉi maram poruntiya poḻil taṉimit tuyaram tantu nammi varuttavum, mīṉvēṭṭikkuc ceṉṟa tamiyaṉmāruṭiya mīṉpiṭipaṭakukaḷ tirumpi varuvatu teriyavum, iṉṉum talivaṉ varavilliyē” (kuṟun. 123.) eṉṟu āṟṟāmi tōṉṟat tōḻi kūṟal. itu kēṭṭa talivaṉ ciṟippuṟattiṉiṉṟu veḷivantu taliviyi aḷavaḷāvutal payaṉ. 	(to. po. 112 iḷa.)
talivaṉ tāṉ puṇarcciyi virumpātu pirivi virumpu miṭattum tōḻi kūṟṟu nikaḻum. appirivu, taṇṭātu irat tali muṉinta maṟṟiyavaḻi iṭṭuppirivum arumiceytu ayarttalum ām. āṇṭut talivaṟkum talivikkum tōḻi kūṟuvaṉa koḷḷappaṭum. 	(114 nac)
oruvaḻittaṇappatākak kūṟiya talivaṉiṭam tōḻi, “vaḷinta aṭimarattiyuṭiya puṉṉiyiṉmēl kaṭal alikaḷ mōtum cērppa! pūṅkoṭi pōṉṟa talivikku niṉmārpu eṉṟum urimiyākumāṟu aruḷ ceyka!” 			(tiṇi. aim. 42).
eṉṟu kūṟutal pōlvaṉa (nac. 114). (naṟ. 19. aiṅ.214) iviyum īṇṭuk koḷḷappaṭum. 	(112 iḷa.)
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ, “vēṇṭāp piriviṉum” eṉṟa taṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.
tōḻi ōtattiṉ mēl vittuk kūṟutal -
“kaṭal veḷḷamē! niṉ alikaḷāl puṉṉimaraṅkaḷi naṉittālum, ataṉpūkkaḷ nirampiya kiḷikaḷi naṉit tālum, eṉ taliviyiṉ naṭiyiṉi otta naṭiyaḻakiṉi yuṭiya aṉṉappaṟavikaḷi naṉikkātē” eṉṟu tōḻi kaṭalveḷḷatti nōkkik kūṟal.   	(to.po.133 nac.)
tōḻi ‘ōmpaṭikkiḷavip pāṅkiṉkaṇ’ urittal-
talivaṉ oruvaḻit taṇantaviṭattum, variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirintaviṭattum tōḻi, “taliviyi ennāḷum pātukāttuk koḷ” eṉṟu  talivaṉiṭam kūṟum pakutikkaṇ eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu.
“ivaḷi nī pātukāttuk koḷ” eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟum pakutikkaṇṇum eṉṟu poruḷurittu, variviṭip pirikiṉṟāṉ, “āṟṟuvittuk koṇṭiru” eṉṟāṟkut tōḻi kūṟutalum uṭaṉkoḷḷappaṭum.  	(to.po. 114 nac).
“naṉmali nāṭa!  oruvar tamakkup periya naṉmiceyyiṉ, aṅṅaṉam ceytavarip pōṟṟātār ulakil irār. ataṉkaṇ ciṟappuilli. ippoḻutu niṉakkup pēriṉpam tarum talivi mūppu eytiyavaḻiyum, ciṟitaḷavāvatu naṉmi payappāḷ. appoḻutum ivaḷiṭattu viruppam māṟātu ivaḷmaṉam nōvātavāṟu ivaḷip pātukāppāyāka! ivaḷ niṉṉiyaṉṟi vēṟu paṟṟukkōṭillātavaḷ” (kuṟun 115) eṉṟu oruvaḻittaṇattaṟkaṇ tōḻi talivaṉiṭam ōmpaṭuttatu. (iḷa. 112)  
“malināṭa!  yām kiḷikaṭitaṟkākat tiṉippuṉam vantu taṅkiṉōm. emar tiṉiyi virivil arintuviṭuvar ātaliṉ tiṉippuṉattiṟkuk kiḷiyiṉam vārā. yāmum varuvataṟku vāyppilli. eṅkaḷ naṭpiṉi maṟantu viṭātē” (ain. aim. 18) eṉṟu oruvaḻit taṇakkum taliva ṉiṭam avaṉ tirumpi varutaṟkuḷ tiṉi koytalic cuṭṭit tām puṉattu vara iyalāmiyi aṟivuṟuttit taliviyi maṟavātu maṉaṅkoḷḷumāṟu kūṟiyatu. (iḷa).
“cērppa! tāy vekuṇṭu oṟukkuṅ kālattum ‘ammā!’ eṉṟu avaḷ peyariyē colli aḻum kuḻanti pōl, nī naṉmi tīmi etu ceyyiṉum, talivi niṉṉiyē elliyāka uṭiyavaḷ. avaḷ tuyariṉip pōkkuvār niṉṉi yaṉṟip piṟar illi.” (kuṟun. 397)	(nac.)
cevvāyp pacuṅkiḷikaḷ irakkattiṟku uriyaṉa. tiṉiyik kuṟavarkaḷ arintu pōkat tiṉippuṉattil aritāḷkaḷē niṟpataṉik kaṇṭuvittum, ippuṉattiṉi viṭṭu nīṅkic cellātapaṭi itaṉkaṇ pēraṉpu vittuḷḷaṉa!” (jaṅ. 284)		(nac.)
iviyiraṇṭum muṟiyē variviṭip piriviṭattum, puṉattiṭip puṇarcciyiṉṟi nīṅkumiṭattum tōḻi talivaṟkuk kūṟiyaṉa. piṟaviṭattuk kūṟuvaṉavum uḷa.
“taliva! nī ivaḷiyum uṭaṉ aḻittukkoṇṭu cēṟal naṉṟu. ivaḷ kaṇṇīr tuḷikkumāṟu, peṇmāṉi viṭuttuccellum āṇmāṉ pōla, nī pirintu celliṉ, kārkālattu mālinērattil ivaḷtuyarattiṉi āṟṟuvittal eṉ āṟṟalukku appāṟpaṭṭatākum!” (naṟ. 37) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu. itu variviṭip pirikiṉṟāṉ, taṉṉi “ivaḷi āṟṟuvittukkoṇṭiru” eṉṟāṉukkuk kūṟiyatu. 	(nac. 114)
tōḻikku viṟali kūṟal
periyōrkaḷ tammoṭu paḻakiya ellōriyum taram nōkkātu koṇṭāṭum iyalpiṉālō, palakālamākap paḻakiya kaṇṇōṭṭattālō, eḷiyōrkkum ciṟappuc ceytalip pōla, aṭiyāḷukkum talivi iṟciṟappuc ceytāḷ” (tiṇimāli . 134) eṉṟu, palar vāyilākavum ūṭal tīrāta talivi tāṉ vāyilākap pukkavaḻi ūṭal tīrntatu kaṇṭu makiḻntu tōḻiyiṭam viṟali kūṟiyatu.			(to. po. 152 nac)
tōḻi kaṭinakarpukku, “nalam toliyāmē naṉku āṟṟiṉāy” eṉṟāṭkuttalivi kūṟiyatu - 
“talivaṉ kaḷavukkālamākiya ceṉṟa mātattil neṭiya veḷḷiya nilāviṉkaṇ eṉ nīṇṭa tōḷkaḷit taḻuvi ṉāṉ. ataṉāl ikkālattum avaṉ mēṉiyiṉatu mulli maṇam eṉtōḷkaḷil kamaḻkiṟatu” eṉṟu tirumaṇam nikaḻum vari tāṉ āṟṟiyiruntami talivaṉuṭiya karuṇiyi ṉālēyē eṉpataṉittalivi tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu. (kuṟun. 193)
tōḻi kaḻaṅku pārttuḻik kūṟiyatu - 
taliviyatu melivu kaṇṭu, tāy, kaṭṭum kaḻaṅkum iṭṭut taliviyatu uṭal meliviṉ kāraṇam paṟṟi aṟiya ēṟpāṭu ceyyavē, “poyyākac ceytiyit terivittali aṟiyāta kaḻaṅkē! ivaḷmēṉiyi varuntacceytavaṉ uṇmiyil murukapperumāṉākiya āṇṭaki allaṉ: malikaḷil mayilkaḷil āṭutalum vaḷḷikkiḻaṅku naṉku vīḻtalum poruntiya kāṭṭuppakutikaḷukku urimiyuṭiya talivaṉē ivaḷit tuṉpuṟuttiyavaṉ”(aiṅ 250) eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟiyatu. 	(to. po. 114 nac)
‘kaḷam peṟak kāṭṭiṉum’ eṉṟataṉkaṇ koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kūṟṟu.
tōḻi, ‘kaḷam peṟakkāṭṭal’ -
kāppu mikutiyāṉum kātal mikutiyāṉum tamar varivu maṟuttataṉāṉum talivi āṟṟāḷāyavaḻi, “iḵtu eṟṟiṉāṉ āyiṟṟu?” eṉṟu cevili aṟivari viṉava, avar kaḷattip peṟāniṟkat taliviyi avarkku veḷippaṭak kāṭṭu miṭattut tōḻi kūṟṟu nikaḻum. kaḷamāvatu kaṭṭum kaḻaṅkum iṭṭurikkum iṭamum veṟiyāṭṭiṭamum ām. 			(to. po. 114. nac)
tōḻi, ‘kaḷaṉum poḻutum varinili vilakki, kātal mikuti uḷappaṭap piṟavum nōkki avaṉvayiṉ kūṟal -
kātalmikuti talivaṉtaliviyar uḷḷattē nikaḻāniṟpa, iruvakik kuṟiyiṭaṅkaḷiyum avaṟṟukkuriya iruvakik kālattiyum avaṉ varintukūṟum niliyit tavirttu, avaṉuṭiya palatiṟattavākiya kūṟṟiṉiyum ārāyntu tōḻi avaṉiṭam kūṟutal. tōḻi, talivaṉ tāṉē iruvakik kuṟiyiṭaṅkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṟkuriya iruvakik kālattiyum variyaṟuttuk kūṟum talimippāṭṭiṉit tavirkkiṟāḷ; taliviyik kuṟikkaṇ kaṇṭu maruvutalum aṉṉapiṟavum paṟṟi avaṉ kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟi ārāykiṟāḷ. piṉṉarē avaṉi nōkki avaḷatu kūṟṟu nikaḻtaluṟukiṟatu.
ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ tōḻi, “pakaṟkuṟi vēṇṭā” eṉa vilakki yum, “iravukkuṟivēṇṭā” eṉa vilakkiyum, iravukkuṟi vēṇṭāmiyi vaṟpuṟuttuvāḷpōlap pakaṟkuṟinērn tum, taliviyatu āṟṟamāṭṭāk kāmamikuti cuṭṭiyum palatiṟamākak kūṟṟu nikaḻttuvāḷ.  	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, kaḷiṟṟiṭit talivaṉ utavi kūṟal - 
“aṉṉiyē! paraṇilē iruntu eṅkaḷik kāttuk koḷḷa muṭiyāta niliyil eṅkaḷi nōkki vanta mata yāṉiyi, eṅkaḷik kākkavēṇṭum eṉṟa aruḷuḷḷat tāl, ampu eytu ōṭṭi, aṉiccamalar nīlamalar oḷimikka acōkamalar ivaṟṟi ārāyntu toṭuttut talivi kūntalil vittuc ceṉṟa talivaṉoruvaṉ uḷaṉ” (tiṇi māli 2) eṉṟu ceviliyiṭam tōḻi kaḷiṟutaru puṇarcci yāṉ aṟattoṭu niṟṟal.
itu ‘puritīr kiḷavi tāyiṭip pukuttaṟ’ kaṇ kūṟiyatu.  			(to. po. 114 nac)
tōḻi, kaḷiṟutaru puṇarcciyāṉ aṟattoṭu niṟṟal - 
āṇyāṉiyit toṭarpuṟuttit tōḻi talivikkum talivaṟku miṭiyē iṇippu ēṟpaṭṭa cūḻniliyic ceviliyiṭam kūṟutal.
“talivaṉ, yāṉiyik kaṇṭu añcik kaṇṇīr perukkiya taliviyi iṭakkiyāl aṇittukkoṇṭu, valakki yāl yāṉiyoṭu pōriṭṭu, ataṉ kuruti taṉmēl valappuṟam paṭiyat taliviyiṉ miyik karitta kaṇṇīr taṉ iṭappuṟam paṭiya avaḷōṭu ammiyappaṉ pōlak kāṭciyaḷittāṉ. talivi avaṉniṉivāka uḷḷāḷ” (tañci. kō. 302) eṉpatu pōṉṟu tōḻi cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟṟal.  	(na. a. 177)
tōḻi kāppu vari iṟantami kūṟal - 
vari iṟattal - elli kaṭantu mikutal.
“taliva! palarum uṟaṅkum naḷḷiravil valiya āṇyāṉi yip pōla vantu iṟkaṭiyiṉ katavit tiṟakkacceyya nī kuṟitta oliyi nāṅkaḷ kēḷāmalilli; kēṭṭōm. eṉ ceyvatu? talikkoṇṭi citiyat tōki meliya mayil valikkaṇ akappaṭṭāṟpōla, yām varuntum tōṟum tāy eṅkaḷi iṟukat taḻuvikkoṇṭamiyāṉ, niṉ kuṟippaṟintum yāṅkaḷ iravukkuṟikku varutal iyala villi” (kuṟun. 244) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
taṉ kaṇavaṉākiya kuraṅku iṟantupaṭṭatākak kimmi nili tāṅka iyalāta manti taṉ kuṭṭiyip pātukākkum poṟuppit taṉ kiḷiyiṭiyē oppaṭittuviṭṭut tāṉ maliccarivil pāyntu uyir nīkkum malippakkatti yuṭiya nāṭa! karuṇi kūrntu naḷḷiravil vāraṟka. niṉakku nikaḻakkūṭum ētatti niṉittu añcukiṉṟōm” (kuṟun. 69) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
‘kāppiṉkaṭumi kiyaṟa varutaṟ’kaṇ varum kūṟṟu itu. 			(to. po. 112 iḷa.; 114 nac)
tōḻi, kuravar iyalpuṇartti varika eṉal - 
“taliva! ittaliviyiṉ iṉiya kural kiḷiyiṉ kurali ottuḷḷatu. ivaḷ kiḷikaṭiyum olikēṭṭuk kiḷikaḷ ataṉit tam iṉattiṉ oli eṉṟu karutit tiṉikkatiri nōkki vantuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉik kuṟavarkaḷ aṟintu ivaḷit tiṉikkāvaliṉiṉṟu nīkkiviṭuvar ātaliṉ, ivaḷi nī aṭiya virumpiṉ varintu ēṟṟukkoḷ” (aiṅ. 289) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
‘kāppiṉ kaṭumi kiyaṟa varutaṟ’kaṇ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 112 iḷa.)
tōḻi, kuṟitta paruvattut talivaṉ vārātavaḻit tūtāy vanta pāṇaṟkuk kūṟal - 
“pāṇa! uṅkaḷ talivar mīṇṭu varuvatākak kūṟicceṉṟa paruvam vantuviṭṭatu. avar emmi niṉittuppārkkā viṭiṉum, tām coṉṉa col poyyākumē eṉṟu nāṇiyāvatu ikkālattē avar mīṇṭuvarutal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamum avar ceyyāmikku yāṉ varuntukiṉṟēṉ” (aiṅ. 472) eṉṟu, avaṉ talivaṉiṭam tūtu ceṉṟurip pāṉ eṉpatu karuti, tōḻi kūṟiyatu.
iccūttirattuḷ ‘piṟa’ eṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟu itu.  	(to. pā. 150 nac)
tōḻi kuṟiyiṭam kūṟal -
talivi, talivaṉ viruppiṟku iṇaṅkiyami kūṟiya tōḻi, avaṟkup pakaṟkuṟiyiṭam kuṟippiṭutal.
“kaṭaliṉ pulālnāṟṟattiṉip puṉṉimalariṉ maṇam nīkkum kaṭal tuṟivaṉē! mīṉuṇakkalik kavaravarum paṟavikaḷi ōṭṭutaṟkut talivi irukkum iṭam tāḻi maraṅkaḷum ñāḻalmaraṅkaḷum ceṟinta poḻilē yākum” (tiṇimāli 44) eṉṟu talivaṟkut tōḻi pakaṟkuṟiyiṭam kūṟal.
‘naṉṉayam peṟṟuḻi nayampuri yiṭattiṉum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ oru kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi ‘kuṟi avaṭcārtti meyyuṟa’t talivaṉ kūṟal -
tōḻi,  “ivaṉ kūṟum kuṟi taliviyiṭattatāka vuḷḷatu” eṉṟu ataṉit talivimēl cērtti ataṉi uṇmi eṉṟuṇarumāṟu, “kaṭalil valivīci aṇṇaṉmār piṭittuk kāyavittuḷḷa mīṉ vaṟṟalik kavara varum paṟavi kaḷi veruṭṭum ippeṇṇē eṉakku mikkatuṉpam tarupavaḷā yuḷḷāḷ” eṉavum, 
“nallāy! immalar cūṭiya taḷirmēṉit tiyal eṉ paṇpiṉiyum uṟakkattiṉiyum kippaṟṟik koḷvataṟku yāṉ ceyta tavaṟu yātu eṉak kūṟuvāyāka” (aiṅ. 176.) eṉavum talivaṉ tōḻiyiṭam uriyāṭutal.  			(to. po. 102. nac.)
tōḻi, ‘kuṟintu avaṭpaṭartaliṉ’ kaṇ kūṟal - 
talivaṉ puṇarcci yuṇmi aṟintu tāḻaniṉṟa tōḻi tāṉum kuṟiyuṟṟut talivimāṭṭuc cellutaṟkaṇ kūṟutal. 	(to. po. 112. iḷa.)
irantu piṉniṉṟa talivaṉ uḷḷappuṇarcci uḷvaḻiyum kuṟiyuṟṟu meyyuṟu puṇarcci vēṇṭit talivimāṭṭuc cellumiṭattum tōḻi kūṟṟu nikaḻum. (iḷa.)
talivaṉ irantu piṉ niṉṟami kaṇṭu tōḻi maṉam nekiḻntu tāṉ kuṟintu taliviyiṭattē ceṉṟu avaṉ kuṟi kūṟutal. (114. nac.)
“makaḷir viḷiyāṭum kaṭaṟkaric cōliyilē ciṟiya nīrt tuṟiyi viṉaviniṉṟa neṭuntēriṉaṉākiya talivaṉi nām kāṇaccelvōm” (aiṅ. 198) eṉṟāṟ pōlat tōḻi taliviyiṭam kūṟutal. (nac.) 	(to. po.)
tōḻi, kuṟiyuṟaṟku etiriya kiḻavaṉi maṟiyuṟap perumiyiṉ peyartta’liṉkaṇ kūṟal -
kaḷavoḻukkattiṉkaṇṇē uṟutaṟkākat taṉatu kuṟiyic colla vēṇṭi etirppaṭṭa talivaṉi avaṉatu perumiyiṉ nīkkuvataṉkaṇ tōḻi kūṟutal. 			(to. po. 112 iḷa.)
irattal valiyuṟṟuk kuṟikūṟat toṭaṅkiya talivaṉit tōḻi tāṉ ivvoḻukalāṟu aṟintum aṟintilaḷpōla avaṉperumi kūṟi avaṉkuṟippiṉi māṟṟutaṟkaṇ kūṟutal. tāṉ aṟintataṉi maṟittukkūṟutalaṉṟit tōḻi aṉpiṉmi orutaliyāka uṭiyaḷ allaḷ.	(114. nac.)
“cāṉṟīr! malippakutiyil tiṉikkolliyik kāttu varum ēḻikaḷi virumpi vantu, mēmpaṭṭa numatu celvak kuṭikkup paḻitēṭutal takkatō?” eṉavum, 
“malināṭa! iṅkuḷḷa ciṟukuṭiyil vāḻmakkaḷ tamakku akkaḷaṅkam tarum ceytiyic ceyyakkarutār. mūttamāṭ ṭiṉ mutukil iṭappaṭṭa cūṭu, ataṉ mutumiyāṉ appakutiyil mayir tōṉṟāvāṟu iṟutivari taṉ vaṭuvik kāṭṭi niṟpatu pōla, emakku iṟutivari ippaḻi niṟkum. ātaliṉ niṉ ceyal takkataṉṟu” eṉavum,
“taliva! nī cāṉṟōri otta māṇpuṭiyi. immali vāḻnar cilar, piṉṉikaḻvu karutāmal apapōtiya iṉpatti virumpi, makaḷiric cuṟṟit tiriyalām. āyiṉ niṉ pōṉṟōrkku icceyal takkataṉṟu” eṉavum, 
“taliva! ittaliviyō, kaṭalil mīṉpiṭikkum paratavar illattuppeṇ. nīyō, pērūril virintu cellum tēril ivarum celvaṉuṭiya aṉpu makaṉ. mīṉ uṇakka liṭiyē puḷ ōppum emakku yātuḷatu ciṟappu? yām mīṉvāṭi nāṟukiṟōm. caṟṟu vilaka niṉṟu pēcu. eṅkaḷ eḷiya vāḻkki celvarām nummaṉōrkku ottataṉṟu. eṅkaḷuḷḷum ciṟitaḷavu celvam paṭittavariyē it talivi maṇakkattakkavaḷ” (naṟ. 45) eṉavum varum tōḻi kūṟṟu.	(nac. 114)
tōḻi, kuṉṟattiṟkuk kūṟuvāḷākac ceṟippu aṟivuṟīiyatu - 
“kuṉṟē! tāy, ‘tiṉippuṉattiṉ pakkam ōr āṇyāṉi aṭikkaṭi varukiṟatu. aḵtu ētam tarum ātaliṉ nīṅkaḷ tiṉikkāvaṟkuc cēṟal vēṇṭā’ eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭāḷ. iṉit talivi uṉṉiṭattiṉ iḻiyum aruviyil āṭu taṟkō tiṉippuṉam kāttaṟkō, uṉṉiṭattuḷḷa iṉiya taḻikaḷik koytaṟkō āṇṭu varutaṟku vāyppu illi” eṉṟu talivi iṟceṟikkappaṭṭa ceytiyit tōḻi kuṉṟattiṭam kūṟutal. (itaṉ payaṉ, ciṟippuṟamāka niṉṟa talivaṉi varivu kaṭāvutal.)
itu ‘kāppiṉ kaṭumi kiyaṟa varutaṟ’kaṇ koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi kūṟum uḷḷuṟi uvamam -
tōḻi kālattiṟkum iṭattiṟkum poruntumāṟṟāl avvan nilaṅkaṭkuriya karupporuḷkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum koṇṭu marutam neytalkaḷil mikutiyākavum, kuṟiñciyil ciṟitu kuṟivākavum, ēṉiya pālimullikaḷil mēlum kuṟivākavum uḷḷuṟiuvamam kūṟuvāḷ.
itu cevilikkum okkum. 	(to. po. 301, 306 pērā.)
tōḻi caṅkiṉi vāḻttutal -
kaṟpukkālattut talivaṉ cēyiṭippirintu cella, avaṉ kuṟitta paruvattoṭakkattēyē talivi avaṉpirivu kuṟittu āṟṟāḷāka, tōḻi aritiṉ āṟṟuvittukkoṇ ṭirukka, talivaṉ tēroṭu virivil mīṇṭuvarukiyil avaṉ taṉ varukiyik kuṟikkac cēymikkaṇṇēyē ūtiya caṅkoli taliviyatu āṟṟāmiyi nīkkiyatāl, tōḻi caṅkiṉi vāḻttutal.
“oḷi cūḻnta valamākac curinta mukattiṉiyuṭiya caṅkē! nī talivaṉatu varukiyit terivittaṉiyātaliṉ, immalar tali ulakil nī neṭitu vāḻka!”
itu kaṟpiyaluḷ, ‘cēyiṭippirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu. 	(ta. ne. vi. pak. 52)
tōḻi cantiraṉi nōkki iraṅkik kūṟal -
“cantiraṉē! nī ōriṭattil nilittaliṉṟik kalitēyntu, veḷuttu, uṭal pātikkum kuṟintu, irāppakal kuḷircci mikkuk kaḷaṅkattuṭaṉ cērntu irukkiṟāy. eṉtaliviyum, ōriṭattil niṟkāmal tirintuḻaṉṟu, āṭicōrntataṉiyum aṟiyāmal, uṭal veḷuttup pātiyaḷaviṉum iḷittu, iravum pakalum kaṇṇīr perukkit tariyil puraḷutalāl mācu cērntu irukkiṟāḷ. iruvīrniliyum ottuḷḷami yāl, nīyum tiruvēṅkaṭattup perumāṉmītu koṇṭa kāta lāl ivvāṟu ākappeṟṟaṉiyō?” eṉpatu akappuṟat taliviyiṉuṭiya tōḻi kūṟṟu. 	(tiruvēṅkaṭat. 19)
tōḻi, ciṟippuṟamākac ceṟippu aṟivuṟuttal - 
talivaṉ tiṉippuṉattiṉ veḷiyē, vēliyarukil varumpōtu, tōḻi, “tiṉi muṟṟiviṭṭatu; iṉi nām puṉam kākka varutal nērātu; tīttīṇṭukiyār (vēṅkiyār) nammip pirivittuviṭṭār” (tiṇimāli. 5) eṉṟu avaṉ kēṭkumāṟu kūṟutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘orucār pakaṟkuṟi’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(na. a. 154)
tōḻi, ciṟippuṟamākavum aḵtu aṉṟākavum taliviyiṉ āṟṟā miyit talivaṉiṭam kūṟi varivu kaṭāvutal - 
“kaṭaṟkariccōli nilavupōla veḷḷoḷi vīcukiṟatu. uppaṅkaḻi iruḷ pōlak karuniṟamāyuḷḷatu. ivi tam iyaṟkiyiṉiṉṟum ciṟitum tirintila; āyiṉ taliviyiṉ nutalum tōḷkaḷum tam paṇṭi nalloḷiyi iḻantu pacali niṟam eytiviṭṭaṉa” eṉṟāṟ pōlat tōḻi ciṟippuṟamākat talivaṟkut taliviyiṉ āṟṟāmi kūṟi varivu kaṭātal.
‘aṉinili vaki’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu itu. 	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, “cuṟṟattār poruḷ vēṇṭi maṟuttār” eṉat talivikkuk kūṟal - 
“talivi! nam cuṟṟattār, talivaṉ varivu kuṟittut tamari viṭuttuḻi, avar taruvatākak kūṟiya poruḷiṉum mikka poruḷi vēṇṭukiṉṟaṉar. namar varivip poruḷ karuti ēṟṟukkoḷḷātatāl, ivariṭiyē amar nērumō eṉṟañciṉēṉ. āyiṉ avarkaḷ amitiyāka mīṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar. namar kēṭṭa poruḷuṭaṉ avarkaḷ viriviṉ mīṇṭum varuvar eṉak karutukiṉṟēṉ” eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟiyatu.
iccūttirattu ‘vaki’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu itu. 	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, ‘ceṅkaṭumoḻiyāṉ citivuṭittāyiṉum, eṉpu nekap pirintōṉvaḻic ceṉṟu kaṭii, aṉpu taliyaṭutta vaṉpuṟikkaṇ’ kūṟal - 
cevviya kaṭiya colliṉāṉē, talivaṉ aṉpu citi vuṭit tāyiṉum, eṉpu urukumāṟu piriyappaṭṭavaḷiṭat tuc ceṉṟu talivaṉ aṉpuṭimiyiṉ aḷippaṉ eṉṟu āṟṟuvitta vaṟpuṟuttaṟkaṇ tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟutal.  			(to. po. 112 iḷa.)
‘ceṅkaṭumoḻi’ eṉṟatu, koṭiya kaṭumoḻiyaṉṟi maṉattiṉāṉ iṉiyaḷākik kūṟum kaṭumoḻi; aḵtāvatu iyaṟpaḻittal; avvāṟu iyaṟpaḻittavaḻit talivaṉ aṉpu citivuṭit tāyiṉum eṉṟavāṟu. ‘aṉpu tali yaṭutta vaṉpuṟi’ yāvatu talivaṉ iṉṟiyamiyātavaṉ eṉa āṟṟuvittal. (iḷa.)
	1. iyaṟpaḻittal -
“pāṟiyilē tuṇiyip pirinta varuttattiyuṭiya āṇmāṉ taṅkum nāṭaṉāyiruntuvittum, niṉ tuyar kaḷiyāmal niṉakku nōy tantataṉāl niṉ kuvaḷimalar pōṉṟa kaṇkaḷ pacali pāyntuviṭṭaṉa,” (kuṟun. 73.) eṉat tōḻi taliviyiṭam talivaṉi iyaṟpaḻittal.
	2. vaṉpuṟi - 
“talivi! talivaṉatu mārpiyē nī virumpi vāḻkiṟāy. aḵtu uṇmiyē. āyiṉ, paḻakumpōtu ciṟitu kaṭumi yoṭu kūṭiya ārāycciyum vēṇṭum. avaṉ viḻiyum pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟi ivaṟṟiṟku nī uṭaṉpaṭutalāl avaṉ kaḷavi nīṭṭikkiṟāṉ. kaḷavil avaṉ piriyumpōtu nī varuntukiṟāy. uḷḷattil aṉpu mikiṉum, puṟattē pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟikaḷi maṟuppiṉ, talivaṉ virivil maṇappāṉ; piṉ piriviṉṟi vāḻalām” (kuṟun. 73.) eṉṟu tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyurittal.
“talivi! oruvarmey maṟṟavar meyyiliruntu piritaṟku virumpāta mikka kāmattāl nī kaṭamiyi aṟiyātiruppi ṉum, ataṉik kūṟal vēṇṭuvatu eṉ kaṭami. tiṉik katirkaḷip paṟippataṟku muṉpē palatiṉikkatirkaḷik kiḷikaḷ veḷavic ceṉṟu viṭṭaṉa.  nīyō, talivaṉōṭu iṉpam nukartalilēyē kālam kaḻikkiṉṟāy. nī aṭikkaṭit tiṉiyiṟ kiḷikaḷik kaṭitaṟkaṇ muyalātuviṭiṉ, uṉakkuk kiḷi kaṭital teriyavilli eṉṟu aṉṉi vēṟoru varit tiṉikāvalukku amarttiviṭiṉ, talivaṉ mārpu uṉakkuk kiṭṭutaṟku aritākappōyviṭum” (akanā. 28.) eṉṟāṟ pōlat tōḻi taliviyiṭam avaḷ kaṭamikaḷi vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟutal.
	3. āṟṟuvittal -
“āṇpuli maṭappiṭi īṉṟa kuḻaviyiṉik kavartaṟkup palāmara niḻalil patuṅkiyirukkum malināṭaṉākiya talivaṉi niṉittu, paṟittup pōṭappaṭṭa taḷirip pōla nī vāṭi uṭal melintu pacalipāyvatu etaṉporuṭṭu?” (aiṅ. 216.) eṉṟu, kuṟivayiṉ taliviyit tavaṟātu kōṭaṟkākavē talivaṉ cevvi pārttu varupavaṉ ātaliṉ, avaṉ varukiyiṉ kālattāḻppuk karuti varuntutal vēṇṭā eṉṟu tōḻi taliviyi āṟṟuvittal. 
“talivi! varuntaṟka. nam talivaṉ nammiṭam pēraṉpiṉaṉ; taṉṉāl namakku varakkaṭava paḻikku añcupavaṉ. ulakil aḻiyāta pukaḻi virumpupavaṉatu celvam virivil avaṉi viṭṭu nīṅkumāṟu pōla, uṉ aḻaku oḻukum mēṉiyil paraviya pacaliyum virivil nīṅkiviṭum” (kuṟun. 143.) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟum atu.
“kaḷiṟu malinelli uṇṭu marāmara niḻalil ilikaḷip parappit tuyilum cōlikaḷiyuṭiya malināṭaṉ naṭpu, avaṉip poruntiyavarukkup pātukāvalit tarum” (ain. eḻu. 12) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟum atu.
immēṟkōḷ pāṭalkaḷ palavum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum kāṭṭuvaṉa.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar itaṉi iraṇṭākak koḷvar:
ceṅkaṭu moḻiyāl citivuṭittāyiṉum:
tōḻi cevvaṉaṅ kūṟum kaṭuñcoṟkaḷāṉ talivi neñcu citivuṭittāyiṉum āṇṭut tōḻi kūṟṟu nikaḻum. avi talivaṉi iyaṟpaḻittalum, taliviyik kaḻaṟalumām.
“ūravar paḻi tūṟṟa aḻakiḻantu neṟṟi pacali pāya nammit tuṉpuṟa vittup pirinta talivaṉiviṭa avaṉuṭiya mali, mēkattāl maṟikkappaṭṭu nam kāṭciyit taṭuttata ṉāl koṭiyatupōlum!” eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉi iyaṟ paḻittal. itu variviṭip paruvaṅkaṇṭaḻintavaḷ kūṟṟu.
“paṉṟi uḻuta kolliyuḷ vaḷarnta muṟṟiya vāḻikkāy kaḷik kaṭuvaṉ paḻuppataṟkākap putittuvittup piṉ putitta iṭam teriyāmal varuntum malināṭaṉ tāṉ teḷivittuc colliya uṟutimoḻikaḷ taṉ taliviyiṉ neñcil ampukaḷip pōlat tittut tuṉpuṟuttumāṟu avaṟṟic ceyaṟpaṭuttāmal irukkiṟāṉ.” (ain. eḻu. 11) eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉ cūḷ poyttatu eṉa iyaṟpaḻittal. 		(114. nac.)
1. tōḻi, celavukkuṟippu aṟintu vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaṭku 	urittatu -
talivaṉatu poruṭpirivu eṇṇi varuntiya taliviyiṭam tōḻi, “māntaḷiri otta niṉ mēṉi pacappa, nammiṉum talivaṟku mēmpaṭṭatākat tōṉṟum poruḷit tēṭik koṇara, mūṅkil uyarnta nīraṟṟa koṭiya iṭattē maṟavar koḷḷiyaṭittuṇṇum kāṭṭikkaṭantu poruḷ ceyya avar niṉiyār” eṉṟu kūṟal. 	(kuṟun. 331.).
2. tōḻi, cevili aiyuṟavu aṟintu kūṟal - 
aṟattoṭunili piṟanta piṉṉum varivu nīṭṭittatāka, “maṟṟoru kulamakaḷi avaṉ variyuṅkol?” eṉṟu aiyuṟṟa ceviliyatu kuṟippaṟintu tōḻi avaṭkuc colliyatu.
“aṉṉāy! talivaṉ em tōḷkaḷit tuṟantāṉāyiṉ, avaṉāl putiyarāka virumpappaṭupavar tōḷkaḷum virivil tuṟakkappaṭum allavō?” (aiṅ. 108.) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘pāṅkuṟa vanta vakiyum’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kūṟṟu.
3. tōḻi cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟṟal -
talivaṉ kaḷavukkālattē vari poruṭkup pirintāṉāka avaṉpirivāl talivi uḷḷamum uṭalum melivuṟavē, ak kāraṇatti uḷḷavāṟu uṇaramāṭṭāta cevili talivikku melivu murukaṉ aṇaṅkiyatāl vantatu eṉṟu murukaṉ uvappaṉa ceyyumāṟu vēlaṉikkoṇṭu veṟi yāṭṭu nikaḻttat toṭaṅka, ataṉit tōḻi vilakka muṟpaṭa, atu pōtu cevili tōḻiyi veṟivilakkaluṟu vataṉ kāraṇatti viṉavat tōḻi, “aṉṉāy! yāṅkaḷ paṇṭorunāḷ niṉ āṇippaṭi tiṉippuṉaṅ kāval ceytiruntapōtu emmit tākka vanta yāṉiyi aruḷuṭiyāṉ oruvaṉ etirpārā  vakiyāl āṅku vantu ōṭṭi niṉmakaḷ uyirik kāttāṉ. taṉṉuyir kātta apperumakaṉi niṉintu ivaḷ melikiṟāḷ. ituvē eṉṉāl aṟiyappaṭṭa ceyti. iṉi nī virumpiyavāṟu ceyka” eṉṟu aṟattoṭu niṉṟatu.
itu variporuṭpirital eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(kōvi. 293.)
tōḻi, cevili taliviyi uṟṟu nōkki vēṟupāṭṭiṉ kāraṇam viṉaviyatākat talivaṉiṭam kūṟutal - 
“ōṅkumalināṭa! nī palakālum iravukkuṟiyiṭit talivi yic cērtaliṉ ataṉāl uṇṭākum putumaṇattik karuti ivaḷuṭiya tōḷic cārntu vaṇṭukaḷ palavāka moykkaluṟa, em aṉṉi taṉ kaṇkaḷāl emmik kolvāḷip pōla nōkki, ‘ nī itaṉ muṉṉum ippaṭi vaṇṭukaḷāl moykkappaṭṭa tōḷiṇikaḷi uṭiyi yō?’ eṉṟu viṉava, ataṟku viṭi kūṟa iyalātu talivi mayaṅka, yāṉ ‘iṅkuḷḷa cantaṉakkaṭṭikaḷi viṟakāka aṭuppil māṭṭutaliṉ avaṟṟilirunta curumpukaḷ iva ḷuṭiya tōḷkaḷil moykkiṉṟaṉa’ eṉṟu maṟittuk kūṟiṉēṉ. virivil ivaḷi maṇattaṟku muyaluka” (naṟ. 55.) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
iccūttirattuḷ ‘aṉṉavi piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ ik kūṟṟut taḻuvappaṭṭatu. 	(to. po. 115. nac. uri.)
tōḻi, cevili taliviyik kōlam ceytu ivaḷ nalattiṟku oppāṉ oruvaṉip peṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟāṭkuk kūṟiyatu -
“aṉṉāy! kaṭaṟkariyil ittalivikku oppaṟṟa utavi ceyta cērppaṉi oppāṉoruvaṉi viṟpōril valla vīrarkaḷ kūṭi naṭattum vilviḻāk kūṭṭattilō, peṇkaḷ kūṭum maṇaviḻā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilō yāṅkaḷ pārtta tilli. orukāl tēvarulakil iruttal kūṭum!” (tiṇi māli. 62.) eṉṟu kūṟit tōḻi cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟṟal.
itu ‘puritīr kiḷavi tāyiṭip pukuttaṟ’kaṇ varuvatoru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi ceviliyi aruku aṭuttatu - 
kaḷaviṉvaḻi vantu oḻukum talivaṉ iṭiyīṭu mutaliya vaṟṟāl cilanāḷ vārātoḻintāṉāka, talimakaḷatu mēṉi melintu vāṭiyatu kaṇṭa aṉṉi, atu murukaṉāl ēṟpaṭṭatu pōlum eṉṟu karuti murukaṉukku veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻttat toṭaṅka, atu kaṇṭa tōḻi aṉṉiyiṉ aruku ceṉṟu, “veṟiyāṭṭiṉāl payaṉ illi; taliviyi iṉṉum oru muṟi tiṉikāvalukku aṉuppi ṉāl, avaḷ iḻanta nalaṉip peṟuvāḷ” eṉṟu ārāyntu kūṟutal. 	(naṟ. 351.)
tōḻi, tamar varivu maṟuttuḻi āṟṟāta talivikku, tīyakuṟi nīṅki naṟkuṟi taṉakkuc ceyyakkaṇṭu, “kaṭitiṉ niṉṉi varivar” eṉṟu kūṟutal -
taṉakku nalla nimittaṅkaḷē tōṉṟuvataṉit tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟit talivaṉ virivil avaḷi maṇappāṉ eṉṟu kūṟutal.
“talivi! eṉ kaṇkaḷil iṭakkaṇ tuṭikkiṟatu. eṉ muṉ kiyiluḷḷa vaḷikaḷum nekiḻāmal ceṟikiṉṟaṉa. kaḷiṟ ṟiṉ tākkutaliṉiṉṟu tappiya ciṉam ciṟanta puli iṭipōla muḻaṅkum malināṭaṉākiya talivaṉ virivil variya varuvāṉ” (aiṅ. 218) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
‘avaṉ varivu maṟuppiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu itu. 	(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi, tamar varivu maṟupparō eṉak kavaṉṟāṭku (- kavaliyuṟṟa talivikku)k kūṟal -
“talivi! namcuṟṟattār, kiyil taṇṭu ūṉṟit taliyil talippāki aṇintu, “naṉṟu naṉṟu” eṉṟu vanta talivaṉ tamaroṭu mukamaṉ kūṟi aḷavaḷāviniṉṟaṉar. ātaliṉ, nammūril pirintavarkaḷic cērttu vippavarkaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu koḷ” (kuṟun. 146.) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.

‘avaṉ varivu maṟuppiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu itu. 	(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi, ‘talippeytu kaṇṭa’vaḻic cevili viṉaviyatik kūṟal -
talivaṉ talivi eṉṉum iruvarkkum kūṭṭam nikaḻta lāṉ, talivaṉ, tamatu maṉiyatu ellippakkam varutalik kāṇiṉum, talivi illakkaṭṭaṭa elliyik kaṭantu veḷiyē cellutalik kāṇiṉum, cevili viṉaviyatit tōḻi taliviyiṭam kūṟal.
“talivi! talivaṉ naḷḷiravil aruviyik kaṭantu vēlē oḷi ceyya iruḷik kaṭantu taṉiyē vantu, kuḷiriyum poruṭ paṭuttāmal nammūri yaṭintu, pāṟiyiṉmītu miḷakukkoṭi paṭarntirukkum nammaṉik kolliyik kaṭantu, kuṟukiya tāḻvārattiyuṭiya nam illiṉuḷ nuḻiya, nam tāy avaṉik kaṇṭu mikka uvakiyōṭu avaṉi murukaṉ eṉṟu koṇṭu, upacāramoḻi kūṟic centiṉiyi nīroṭu vittu vaḻipaṭat toṭaṅkiṉāḷ. iṉit talivaṉoṭu nām koṇṭa naṭpu yātākumō?” (akanā. 272.) eṉṟu talivaṉ varaviṉit tāy aṟintami yit tōḻi kūṟiyatu. 	(to. po. 115. nac.)
tōḻi, talimakaḷ kuṟi nayantami talimakaṟkuk kūṟal - 
talivi tōḻi kūṟṟiṟku iṇaṅkit talivaṉ tēviyi muṭittaṟku uṭaṉpaṭṭavaḻi, ataṉit talivaṉukkut tōḻi kūṟutal.
“taliva! neytal pūppaṭukkiyil yām ceyta piñcāyk kōriyālākiya pāviyik kiṭatti uṉ kuṟiyiṭattu vantuḷḷēm. oru kāl nāri appāviyi mitittuc citittalum kūṭum. ātaliṉ yāṅkaḷ virintu tirumpu māṟu, taliviyi virivil tiruppi aṉuppuka” (kuṟun. 114.) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘naṉṉayam peṟṟuḻi nayampuri yiṭattiṉum’ eṉṟa taṉkaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 112. iḷa.)
tōḻi talimakaṉ vāyilkaṭku urittatu - 
“ciṟuvarkaḷ, periyōr ūrum periya tēriṉi ūrntu iṉpuṟāviṭiṉum, attēri niṉintu paṇṇiya ciṟu tēriṉi iḻuttuc celvatilēyē, avareytum iṉpat tiṉit tāmum aṭivatu pōla, yām parattiyar peṟum meyyuṟu puṇarcciyip peṟṟu iṉpuṟēmāyiṉum, talivaṉi niṉittu uḷḷattē naṭpip perukkutalāl, apparattiyar peṟum iṉpatti yām peṟṟōm. ataṉāl emmuṭal meliyavilli; vaḷikaḷ nekiḻa villi. ātaliṉ talivaṉ vantu emakkuc ceyyavēṇṭiya taliyaḷi vēṇṭā” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu. (kuṟun. 61.)
tōḻi talimakaṉatu varitaṟ ceytiyit tamarkku urittami kūṟal -
taliviyi aḻittukkoṇṭu uṭaṉpōy avaḷi varintukoṇṭu piṉ ūrukku mīṇṭa talivaṉ, tāṉ taliviyi varintukoṇṭa ceytiyit taliviyiṉ tamarkku urikkumāṟu tōḻiyi vēṇṭiṉāṉāka, tāṉ ataṉi muṉṉamēyē terivittu viṭṭatākat tōḻi kūṟal.
iḵtu iccūttirattu ‘iyalpuṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷap paṭṭa kiḷavi.
itaṉi, nampi akapporuḷ ‘tōḻi tāṉ atu muṉṉē cāṟṟiyatu urittal’ eṉṉum. (194.)
kuraṅkukkuṭṭi periya mūṅkilmēl ēṟi mūṅkiṟkōlāl cantiraṉip puṭippatu pōlak kāṭcitarum nāṭaṉ nī. (ātaliṉ, nī tīṅku ceyvārip pōlat tōṉṟuvāyē aṉṟi, uṇmiyiṟ ceyyāy eṉpataṉi yāṉ aṟivēṉ ātaliṉ) nī taliviyik karaṇavakiyāṉ maṇantukoṇṭa ceytiyip piṟarvāyilākak kēḷviyuṟṟu yāṉ muṉṉarē eṉ tāykkuk kūṟiviṭṭēṉ”(aiṅ. 280.) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.  		(to. po. 39. nac.)
tōḻi talimakaṉit tuṇital -
“taliviyiṉ uḷḷam kavarntavaṉ ivaṉē” eṉṟu tōḻi taṉmaṉattu uṟuti ceytu koṇṭatu.
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyilum pāṅkaṟkūṭṭattilum taliviyi nukarnta talivaṉ, taliviyum tōḻiyum uṭaṉ irunta iṭam vantu vaḻi mutaliyaṉa viṉaviyaviṭattē, muṉpē nāṟṟam tōṟṟam mutaliyavaṟṟāṉ taliviyiṭam ētō vēṟupāṭu tāṉ aṟiyāvaki nikaḻntuḷḷatu eṉṟa aiyuṟa vōṭu ārāyum tōḻi, avaṉ ceyalkaḷi ārāyntu, “vēṅkikkiḷiyil ēṟi mayil akavum nāṭa ṉākiya ittalivaṉē taliviyiṉ maṉattil iṭam peṟṟavaṉ” eṉṟu tuṇintu muṭivu ceytal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(ta. ne. vi. pak. 18.)
tōḻi, ‘talivarum viḻuma nili eṭutturi’ttal -
talivaṉ taliviyik koṇṭu uṭaṉpōkkup pōkā viṭiṉ, talivi taṉ mēṉi eḻil vāṭap peritum varuntum nili ēṟpaṭum eṉpataṉiyum, ataṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa notumalar varivu muyalvataṉiyum, īramillā maṉattava rāṉa ayalār paḻi moḻitaliyum, accoṟkaḷik kēṭṭu aṉṉi kaṭiya coṟkaḷ kūṟutaliyum, taliviyi iṟceṟittaliyum tōḻi eṭuttuk kūṟit talivaṉi uṭaṉpōkkiṟku oruppaṭac ceytal.
uṭaṉpōtali viḻiyātu, nāṇ iḻattaṟku añci illattil ceṟippuṇṭiruppiṉ, cērippeṇṭir tūṟṟum paḻimoḻikaḷiliruntum, avaṟṟik kēṭṭu aṉṉi kūṟum kaṭiya coṟkaḷiliruntum uyyum vaḻi iṉṟātaliṉ, talivaṉōṭu uṭaṉpōtalukkut taliviyit tōḻi oruppaṭac ceytal. (akanā. 65.)
“taliva! nī piriyiṉ, niṉtalivi taṉatu aḻakiya niṟam vāṭak kuḻaliviṭap peritum iraṅki ēṅkuvāḷ” (aiṅ. 306) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 39 nac.)
itaṉi nampiakapporuḷ, ‘pāṅki talivaṟku uṭaṉpōkku uṇarttal’ (182) eṉṉum.
tōḻi talivaṟku uṭaṉpōkku nērntu ataṉit talivikku urittal -
“talivi! oru kāraṇamumiṉṟi nammi iṟceṟittuk kaṭuñcoṟ kūṟit tuṉpuṟuttum nam tāy, ivvillattil tāṉ oruttiyākavē irukka! ūravar alar tūṟṟuka! cēriyil āravāram perukuka! nī malikaḷiṭiyiṭiyē kuṟukkiṭṭa pāli nila vaḻiyil iṭiyiṭiyē kāṇap paṭum nelli marattiṉiṉṟu paḻuttu utirntu kiṭakkum kāykaḷit tiṉṟu, maliyaṭivārattil yāṉikaḷ kāl kaḷi aḻunta vittuc ceṉṟataṉāl uṇṭāṉa karumpup pātti pōṉṟa paḷḷaṅkaḷil taṅkiyirukkum nīrikkuṭittu, kaḷipput tīrntu uṭaṉ pōtalit takkatu eṉṟu niṉittut talivaṉiṭam kūṟiyuḷḷēṉ” (kuṟun. 262) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
iḵtu iccūttirattu ‘iyalpuṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷap paṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 39. nac.)
ituvum ‘pāṅki talivaṟku uṭaṉpōkku uṇarttal’. 			(na. a. 182)
tōḻi, “talivaṟkuk kūṟumiṉ” eṉṟu (aḵṟiṇip poruḷkaḷiṭam) kūṟal -
“kaṇṭal ceṭikaḷē! uppaṅkaḻiyarukē paṭarntuḷḷa muṇṭa kaṅkaḷē! muṉpu talivaṉik kūṭṭuvitta nalviṉippayaṉ emakkut toṭarntu illāmiyāl, kaḻikkariyil puḷḷōpputal toḻili viṭuttu ūrukkut tirumpic celkiṟōm. iṉi, ittoḻiṟku mīṇṭum vara vāyppu iṉṟu. emar iṟceṟitta ceyalic colvataṟku nāṇa muṟāmal talivaṉiṭam nīvir kūṟumiṉ!” eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟutal. (kaṇṭal - tāḻi; muṇṭakam - muḷḷi.)
