itu ‘kāppiṉ kaṭumi kiyaṟa varutaṟ’kaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi, talivaṟkup piṟṟiñāṉṟu kūṟiyatu - 
“taliva! nī ‘allakuṟip paṭṭēṉ’ eṉa varuntukiṟāy. tavaṟu emmēlatu illi. maḻi peyyum iravilē nī kuṟiceyyum ōci kēṭkappaṭukiṟatō eṉṟu cevikaḷic cāyttuk koṇṭu anniṉivākavē irukkum eṉ talivi, niṉṉi aṭiya muṭiyāta varuttattāl kaṭiya kāvaluṭiya aṉṉiyiṉ muṉṉart taṉ vaḷiyalkaḷ nekiḻntu kaḻaṉṟa niliyil kāṭciyaḷikkiṟāḷ. ittakiya nilikku emmit toṭarntu āḷākkāmal viriviṉ varintukoḷvāy” (kali. 48) eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟiyatu. 	(to. po. 133. nac.)
tōḻi, talivaṉ āṟṟāmi kaṇṭu kiyuṟi etirtal -
“taliva! ippoṉṉiṟa mēṉiyāḷiṉ parutta koṅki kaḷiṉ vaṉappiṉi niṉittu nī varunti vāṭutal vēṇṭā. nī toṭuttu vantuḷḷa kuṟuṅkaṇṇi vāṭippōvataṉ muṉ avaḷ ataṉi ēṟṟukkoḷḷumāṟu ceyval” (tiṇi māli 21.) eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kiyuṟiyip peṟṟatu.
itu ‘naṉṉayam peṟṟuḻi nayampuri iṭattiṉ’kaṇ varuva toru kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi, “talivaṉ iravukkuṟikku vantāṉ” eṉṟu talivikkuk kūṟal -
“nilavum maṟintuviṭṭatu. iruḷum paraviviṭṭatu. nammi veḷiyē cellātu pātukākkum aṉṉiyum uṟaṅki viṭṭāḷ. kāppōri nīttut taṉittu varum yāṉi pōlat talivaṉ ippaṉiyil naṭuṅkiyavāṟu kuṟikkaṇ vantuḷḷāṉ. kāṇāmaṟpōṉa aṇikalam mīṇṭum kiṭip pataṉāṉ ēṟpaṭum makiḻviṉi otta makiḻvuṭaṉ iṟceṟip pāṉ aṭiya muṭiyātupōṉa talivaṉ mārpiṉi ippoḻutu peṟṟu āci tīrat taḻuvi mīḷka!” (naṟ. 182.) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘naṉṉayam peṟṟuḻi nayampuri iṭattiṉ’kaṇ varuva toru kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 112. iḷa.)
tōḻi, talivaṉ iravukkuṟi nayantavaṉi maṟuttatu - 
“taliva! pakivarkku makiḻcci uṇṭākumāṟum emakkut tuyaram uṇṭākumāṟum ivvāṟu aṟiñar varuvārō? uṉṉāl piriyappaṭṭa talivi, uṉ tērccakkaraṅkaḷāṉ citivuṇṭa neytalmalarkaḷip pōla nī iraviṭi varuvataṉ ētam kuṟittup peritum varuntukiṟāḷ. ava ḷuṭiya iraṅkattakka niliyiṉi niṉittuppār” (kuṟun. 336) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟit talivaṉukkut tōḻi iravuk kuṟi maṟuttal.
itu ‘puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, talivaṉ iravukkuṟi nayappak kūṟal - 
“taliva! emmūrt terukkaḷil nilāviṉip pōṉṟa veṇmaṇal paraviyirukkum. emmūr ciṟiya ūrtāṉ. maṉikaḷ pul vēyappeṟṟa kuṭicikaḷtām. nāṟpuṟamum nīr cūḻntatutāṉ emmūr. āyiṉ, maṉattukku iṉpam taruvatil itaṟku nikar vēṟu ūr illi. eṅkaḷ ūril orunāḷ taṅkiyavarkaḷ maṟunāḷ taṅkaḷ contavūri maṟantuviṭu vārkaḷ. maraṅkaḷ ceṟinta pakkattil pakaṟpoḻuti eṅkaḷoṭu kaḻittu māliyil tēriṉip pūṭṭic celka. atuvaṉṟi, iravil empatiyil taṅkic celliṉ, yāmum emmāl iyaṉṟa aḷavu upacāram ceyvōm. uṉ karuttu yātu? kūṟuka” (akanā. 200) eṉṟu, talivaṉ iravukkuṟiyi virumpumāṟu tōḻi kūṟiyatu.
itu ‘naṉṉayam peṟṟuḻi nayampuri iṭattiṉum’ eṉṟa taṉkaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi, “talivaṉ ēṟu taḻuviyē uṉṉi aṭital iyalum eṉpatik kuravi pāṭi āṭit teyvam paravit talivaṟku uṇarttap pōtuvāyāka” eṉṟu kūṟiyatu -
“mayiṟkaḻuttup pōlum kāyāmpūkkaṇṇikaḷ pavaḷam pōṉṟa cenniṟak kurutiyuṭiya nilattilē māṟupaṭṭuk kiṭakkum potuvarum, pulikkūṭṭamum yāṉittiraḷum tammuḷ māṟumāṟāyp pōriṭṭāṟpōlap porutu, ēṟṟit taḻuvikkoṇṭu cērat toḻuvi viṭṭup pōyiṉar. avar pōṉa appōtē malaruṇkaṇ kātal makaḷirum avarkaḷ kaṇavarum mintuṟṟut tātākiya eruviṉiyuṭiya maṉṟattilē kuravik kūttāṭuvar. āṇṭu yāmum ceṉṟu akkuravik kūttiṉkaṇṇē kūṭi,
‘kollum ēṟṟiṉuṭiya kompiṟku añci ataṉi aṭarkkātu pōyiṉa potuvaṉi āymakaḷ maṟupiṟappi lum taḻuvāḷ;
‘āyarmakaḷ tōḷkaḷ, añcātu koliyēṟṟiṉi aṭarkkavalla āṭavar taḻuvataṟkup poruntuvaṉavēyaṉṟi, varuntutaluṟṟu avvēṟṟiṉit taḻuvutaṟku neñcillā tavar ceṟitaṟku ariyaṉa;
‘āyarmakaḷ tōḷkaḷ, uyir oru kāṟṟē eṉṟu uṇarātu ataṉuṭaṉē oru ceṟivāy ataṉik kāval koṇṭu kollēṟṟiṉatu kompiṉi añcum neñciṉāṉ tōytaṟku eḷiyaṉa ākumō?
‘koliēṟṟiṉatu kompukaḷiṭiyē, tām virumpum makaḷirtam mārpil muliyiṭip pukumāṟu pōla, vīḻntu avvēṟṟit taḻuviṉa āṭavariṭam emmiṉattu āyar tam makaḷirkku mulivili vēṇṭār.’
eṉṟu yām em cuṟṟattār kūṟum muṟimiyip pāṭik kuravikkūttāṭi, nam pāṇṭiyaṉip pāṭi, cīriya pukaḻi yuṭiya teyvattip paravuvōm. nīyum aṅṅaṉam pāṭu taṟkup pōtuvāyāka!” eṉṟu tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟuvatu.  		(to. po. 45. nac) (kali. 103.)
tōḻi, talivaṉ oruvaḻit taṇattalit talivikku urittal - 
“talivi! maliccariviluḷḷa palāmarattiṉ paḻutta paḻam kāmpu kaḻaṉṟu kaṟkaḷiṭiyē paḷḷattil tēṉkūṭṭiyum citittukkoṇṭu viḻum nāṭṭut talivaṉ uṉkaṇkaḷ aḻutu kaṇṇīr viṭumāṟu ciṟappuṭiya taṉ nalla nāṭṭiṟ kuc celvatākak kūṟukiṟāṉ” (aiṅ. 214.) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘vēṇṭāp piriviṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 112. iḷa.)
tōḻi, talivaṉ kuṟṟēval makaṉāl, varivu malintamiyit talivikkuk kūṟal - 
‘aṅkataṉ taṉmiyiṉ vaṉpuṟi’ eṉpataṉkaṇ iḵtu aṭaṅkum. aḵtāvatu, tōḻi talivaṉi varivu kaṭāya vaḻit talivaṉ taliviyi maṇantukoḷḷutal mey yāyiṉamiyiṉ, tirumaṇam niṟivuṟumvari talivaṉ talivi iruvarum ciṟupirivi āṟṟiyirukkumāṟu vaṟpuṟut tutal. ataṉkaṇ, itu, tōḻi talivaṉuṭiya ēvalāḷāl talivaṉ tirumaṇa ēṟpāṭu ceytuviṭṭa ceytiyi aṟintu talivikkuk kūṟutal.
“talivi! talivaṉ varivukkuriya muyaṟcikaḷi mēṟ koṇṭāṉāka, avaṉtaṉ ēvalmakaṉi nōkki yāṉ, ‘ceṉṟa ceyal nalamā?’ eṉṟu viṉava, avaṉ ‘nalamē’ eṉṟāṉ. avaṉ neymika ūṟiya kuṟumpūḻ camitta kaṟiyōṭu, uṇavu peṟuvāṉāka!” (kuṟun. 389) eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉuṭiya kuṟṟēvalmakaṉāl aṟinta ceytiyit talivikkuk kūṟutal. 	(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi, talivaṉ kuṟiyātatu kūṟal - 
talivaṉ terintukoḷḷa vēṇṭāta toṉṟit tōḻi kūṟutal.
taliviyi iyaṟkip puṇarcciyilum iṭantalippāṭā kiya pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattilum kūṭiya talivaṉ tōḻiyi irantu kuṟiniṉṟavaḻi, taliviyi eḷiyaḷākat talivaṉ niṉittal kūṭātu eṉṟa karuttāṉ avaṉoṭu palavaki yāka uriyāṭik kālam tāḻkkum tōḻi, “tūymi mikka paḻaṅkuṭiyil tōṉṟiya, coṉṉa col tavaṟāta nāviṉi yuṭiya matitaraṉ eṉṟa vaḷḷal pōlap perumi porun tiya maliyil ciṟanta tava muṉivarkaḷum vantu cārumāṟu nilipeṟṟa avvūr viḷaṅkit tōṟṟamaḷikkiṟatu” eṉṟāṟ pōl avaṉukkut tēviyillāta oṉṟaṉik kūṟutal.
itu ‘tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṟa tokutiyiṉkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ta. ne. vi. pak. 19)
tōḻi, talivaṉ kuṟi maṟātavāṟṟāl kūṟiyatu -
talivaṉuṭiya kuṟiyi maṟukka muṭiyāta vakiyil tōḻi taliviyiṭam kūṟiyatu.
“aviḻttu viṭappaṭṭa kutiri tuḷḷiyeḻum eḻucciyip pōla, vaḷittup piṉ viṭṭamiyāl nimirntu vāṉaḷāva ōṅkum mūṅkiliyuṭiya malināṭaṉ talivaṉ(am mūṅ kilip pōlat talivaṉ namakkākap paṇintu tōṉṟiṉum iyalpil mika mēmpaṭṭavaṉ.) nāmum avaṉi niṉittu uḷḷam melikiṉṟōm. ataṉi avaṉ aṟiyāṉ.  vēṉiṟ kālattu vemmiyi āṟṟāta kāḷiyip pōla, nam nalatti virumpi avaṉ melintuḷḷāṉ.  avaṉukku viri vil aruḷutal vēṇṭum” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.	(114. nac)
tōḻi, talivaṉ kiyuṟi maṟuttatu - 
“taliva! emmoṭu vantu kaṭalāṭum makaḷirum neytal taḻiyik koṇṭu tām viḷiyāṭum pāvikkum āṭipuṉiyār; tāmum attaḻiyi āṭiyāka aṇiyār; mālikkākac cila neytaṟpūkkaḷiyē paṟittuk koḷvar. ātaliṉ uṉṉit tavira ippūviyō taḻiyiyō paṟippār illi. ākavē, itaṉi yām ēlēm” (aiṅ. 187.) eṉṟu kūṟit tōḻi talivaṉ talivi pāl cērppikkumāṟu koṭutta kiyuṟiyi ēṟka maṟuttatu.
iccūttirattu (‘pāṅkuṟa vanta’) vaki eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kūṟṟu itu. 	(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi, talivaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉāka, veṟiaccuṟuttik kūṟutal - 
“maṇam kamaḻum malināṭaṉ taliviyiṉ meyyaḻakik koḷḷi koṇṭati aṟiyāta tāy, taliviyit teyvam tīṇṭiyatāl avaḷ aḻaku kuṟivatākak karuti vēlaṉi aḻittu vantu āṭṭukkuṭṭiyi aṟuttu ataṉ kurutiyit tūvi veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻttak karuti varuntukiṟāḷ” (ain. aim. 20) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
‘piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ (aṭi 32) koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu itu. 	(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi, talivaṉ ‘cīruṭip perum poruḷ vittavaḻi maṟappiṉ’kaṇ kūṟal -
cīruṭip perumporuḷāvatu illaṟam. ataṉit talivaṉ talimakaḷiṭattu vittaviṭattē avaḷi maṟantu oḻukiṉavaḻit tōḻi kūṟutal; aḵtu aṟattiṉāṉāvatu, poruḷiṉāṉāvatu, ici kūttu mutaliya iṉpattiṉā ṉāvatu taliviyit talivaṉ pirintu maṟantavaḻit tōḻi kūṟutal.	(to. po. 148. iḷa.)
“taliva! nam taliviyatu aḻaku muṉpellām ivvāṟu tēyntiruntatā? nī pakkattē yiruntu aṉpu ceyyumvaḻiyum, ciṟitu niṉ muyakkam nekiḻntataṉāṉ viḷinta aḻaku vēṟu pāṭā itu? allatu kaḷḷuṇpārkkuk kaḷkōppiyil kaḷ tīrntu pōṅkālattup piṟanta vēṟupāṭu pōla, niṉ aṉpu kuṟintu nīṅkiya kālattu viḷinta vēṟupāṭā ippacali? itaṉi aṟintu ceyaṟpaṭuka” (naṟ. 35.) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu. (iḷa.)
talimiyiyuṭiya illaṟattit talivimāṭṭu vitta kālattut talivaṉ aṟam ceyaṟkum, poruḷ ceyaṟkum, iciyum kūttumākiya iṉpam nukartaṟkum taliviyi maṟantoḻukumiṭattē tōḻi kūṟutal. 	(150. nac.)
“tēṉūrip pōṉṟa iyaṟkiyaḻakuṭiya ivaḷatu nalla aḻakiṉi orukālattu nī virumpi nukarntu ippoḻutu ivaḷit tuṟantoḻukuvatāl, ivaḷ neṟṟi palarum nī pirintiruttali aṟiyumāṟu pacali pāyntuḷḷatu” (aiṅ. 55) eṉṟu talivaṉpirivāl talivi vaṉappiḻattalit tōḻi kūṟutal. (nac.)
tōḻi, talivaṉ cēṇiṭip pirintu iraviṉ vantuḻik kūṟutal -
talivaṉ iravil mīṇṭu vantami kaṇṭu makiḻnta tōḻi avaṉi nōkki, “pūntār mārpa! parukkikkaṟkaḷi mikavumuṭiya kuṟukalāṉa piḷavupaṭṭa vaḻikaḷil yāṉi kaḷāl ūṟu nērumē eṉavum karutāmal, eṅkaḷukku aruḷ ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṟu uṉ karuṇiyuḷḷam uṉṉic celuttutalāl iṭiyē yāṇṭum taṅkāmal, taṉ karumiyāl vaḻiccelavi vilakkum ivvariya iruḷil evvāṟu mīṇṭu vantāyō?” (aiṅ.  362) eṉṟu kūṟutal.
itu ‘piṟavum’ eṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kūṟṟu.			(to. po. 150 nac.)
tōḻi, talivaṉ taḻi tantami kūṟal -
“aṉṉiyē! talivaṉ taṉ maliccariviluḷḷatoru marattiṉ poṉ pōṉṟa malarkaḷiyum, cemmaṇi pōṉṟa arumpu kaḷiyum toṭuttut tāṉ māliyāka aḷittāṉ. ataṉ taḷirkaḷik koṇṭu eṅkaḷukkut taḻiyāṭi amittuk koṭuttāṉ.  ivvāṟu poṉ pōṉṟa malar, cemmaṇi pōṉṟa arumpu, āṭikku utavum taḻi ivaṟṟik koṇṭa maram yātāka irukkum?”(aiṅ. 201) eṉṟu kūṟuvāḷ pōlat tōḻi talivaṉ taḻi tantami kūṟic cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟṟal.
itu ‘puritīr kiḷavi tāyiṭip pukuttaṟ’ kaṇ varuvatoru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, talivaṉ (kiḻavōṉ) tuyarnili kiḷattal - 
tōḻi talivaṉatu tuyar mikka uḷḷaniliyit talivik kuk kūṟutal.
“talivaṉ mey naṭuṅkikkoṇṭu kiyil taḻiyi ēntit taṉ ampu paṭṭa yāṉiyi viṉavik kiyil vil iṉṟip pittaṉ pōlap pēcukiṟāṉ. avaṉnili mikavum iraṅkat takkatāka uḷḷatu” (kōvi. 102) eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu kōviyāruḷ ‘ irakkattoṭu maṟuttal’ (102) pōlvatu.
ikkūṟṟu, kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatu. 	(na. a. 148.)
tōḻi, talivaṉ pirivu vēṇṭiyavaḻik kūṟiyatu - 
talivaṉ taliviyi maṉattāl piriyāṉ eṉpataṉi uṟutiyāka aṟinta tōḻi, ūril alar aṭaṅkum vari kaḷa vil īṭupaṭātu talivaṉ oruvaḻit taṇattali virumpiya viṭattē kūṟutal. (oruvaḻittaṇattal - mikka aṇimik kaṇ pirital.)
“taliva! niṉ tērip pākaṉ celutta emmip pirintu celka. āyiṉ oru ceytiyi maṉamkoṇṭu celka.  nī ciṉṉāḷ kaḻittut tirumpi varumpōtu talivi uyiruṭaṉ irukka māṭṭāḷ!” (naṟ. 19.) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘vēṇṭāp piriviṉum’ eṉṟataṉkaṇ koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kūṟṟu. 	(to. po.  112 iḷa., 114. nac.)
tōḻi, talivaṉmēl tavaṟu ēṟṟal - 
“malippakutiyilē taṉ uṭalpārattic ciṟiya marakkiḷi tāṅkumā eṉpataṉi aṟiyātu, āṇ kuraṅku acciṟu kiḷimītu vēkamākap pāyntatāl kiḷi muṟintupōṉatu pōla, talivaṉ nām aṟiyum vaṇṇam kuṟi ceyyāta tavaṟṟāl nam tōḷkaḷ pacantaṉa” (kuṟun. 121.) eṉpatu pōlat talivaṉ ciṟippuṟamāka niṟpat tōḻi avaṉmītu tavaṟṟiṉi ēṟṟik kūṟutal.
tōḻi, talivaṉ vantami kūṟal - 
“kaṇṇīr vaṭittuk kaṇavaṉ varavu nōkkiyirukkum talivī! ñāyiṟu pōṉṟa pēroḷiyoṭu nāṭu kākkum nam talivar vantuviṭṭār” eṉṟāṟpōla, pirintu ceṉṟa talivaṉatu varukiyit tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟutal. 
‘piṟavum’ eṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kūṟṟu itu. 			(to. po. 148. iḷa.)
tōḻi,“talivaṉ varuvāṉ” eṉal - 
“paṉṟi keṇṭi veḷippaṭuttiya cemmaṇikaḷi nerup pākak karutik kuḷir tīrak kuṟavarkaḷ iraviṭit tam kikaḷi av veppattil kāyccikkoḷḷum malināṭṭut talivaṉ nam mēṉiyil paraviya pacappuk keṭat tavaṟātu varuvāṉ” eṉpatu pōṉṟu tōḻi taliviyiṭam kūṟutal.
‘puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ varuvatoru kūṟṟu itu. 	(to. po.  114.  nac.)
tōḻi, talivaṉ varivu vēṇṭiviṭat tamar maṟuttuḻi avar kēṭkumāṟṟāṉ talimakaṭkuc colluvāḷāy aṟattoṭu niṉṟatu -
“tōḻi! nam ūril variciyāka viḷaṅki vīḻum aruviyi uṭiya nam malināṭaṉām talivaṉ taṉṉic cērntā riṭam iṭiyaṟāta naṭpuṭiyavaṉ eṉiṉum, nam tanti taṉṉiyari irantāyiṉum niṉṉi maṉiviyākak koḷ ḷātu, aṅṅaṉam irattal taṉ perumikku iḻukku eṉṟu viṭuttāṉāyiṉ, nām uyir vāḻtal yāṅkaṉam iyalum?” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.	(aiṅ. 228.)
tōḻi, talivaṉatu ‘cūḷvayiṉ tiṟattāl cōrvu kaṇṭu aḻi’yum vaḻik kūṟal - 
talivaṉ cūḷuṟṟa cūḷuriyil cōrvuṟṟami kaṇṭu, tōḻi tāṉ maṉam aḻintaviṭattē kūṟutal 	(to. po. 148. iḷa.)
“taliva! emmūr nīrttuṟiyilē taliviyiṉ muṉki kaḷip paṟṟi, ‘niṉṉiṟ piriyēṉ; piriyiṉ āṟṟēṉ; piriyiṉ aṟaṉallatu ceytēṉāvēṉ’ eṉṟu, añcattakka teyva makaḷir cāṉṟāka nī kūṟiya uṟuticcoṟkaḷik kāvāmi yāl, attavaṟṟukku atteyvaṅkaḷ niṉakku yātu taṇṭaṉi vaḻaṅkumō eṉṟa eṇṇam emmi varuttu kiṟatu” (kuṟun. 53.) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu. (iḷa.)
kūṭutal vēṭkik kūṟupāṭṭāṉ talivaṉ tāṉ cūḷuṟak karutiya cūḷuṟaviṉatu poymmiyik karutit talivi varuntu miṭattē tōḻi kūṟutal. 	(150. nac.) 
(“cūḷnayat tiṟattāṉ” eṉpatu nac. pāṭam.)
talivaṉ puṟattoḻukkattiṉi aṟintu talivi melin tuḻi, aḵtu illi eṉṟu tēṟṟat talivaṉ cūḷ urittuḻit tōḻi, ” ivaḷ neṟṟi vaṉappu iḻakkumāṟu  nī tēṟṟiya col payaṉ oṉṟum tārātu; poyccūḷāl payaṉ iṉṟu” (aiṅ. 56.) eṉṟu kūṟutal; akanā. 266. ām pāṭalum atu. (nac.)
tōḻi, talivaṉiṭam avaṉūr cuṭṭik kūṟal -
“taliva! nī palakālum eṅkaḷip pirintu uṟikiṟāy. nī pirintirukkum nēraṅkaḷil kāmam elli kaṭantu em uḷḷattik tuṉpuṟuttiṉ yām nēril vantu niṉṉik kāṇa vēṇṭum nili nikaḻtalum kūṭum.  atupōtu niṉṉi vantu kāṇa, ōṅkit tōṉṟum maliyil num ūr yāṅkuḷatu eṉpataṉi emakkut terivippāyāka” (aiṅ. 237) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
iḵtu ūri iṟappak kūṟiyatu; ‘nāṭum ūrum....  kiḷavi yoṭu’ eṉṟatāl koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟu itu. 			(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi talivaṉiṭam avaṉ ūṟu añcutaṟkaṇ kūṟal -
“kariyi mōti veḷḷam perukkeṭuttōṭum kāṭṭāṟ ṟiṉ karivaḻiyē eṅkaḷi niṉittu naḷḷiravil varukiṉ ṟāyē! pulikaḷ uṉakku añci akaṉṟu pōvaṉavāka! iṭi uṉ kivēlukku añci vilakuka! āyiṉ uṉṉit tīṇṭi varuttum teyvamakaḷir niṉṉik kavarntu ceṉṟuviṭuvār kaḷō eṉṟu añcukiṟōm.  ātaliṉ naḷḷiravil vāraṟka” eṉat talivaṉ varu vaḻiyiṉatu iṭiyūṟṟiṉik kaṇṭu añcit tōḻi kūṟutal.
‘āṟṟatu tīmi aṟivuṟu kalakkam’ eṉpataṉkaṇ varuvatoru kūṟṟu itu. 	(to. po. 112. iḷa.)
tōḻi talivaṉiṭam avaṉkuṭimi cuṭṭik kūṟal - 
“maṇam vīcum puṉṉimara niḻalil taṉ aḻaki niṉṉiṭam tōṟṟa taliviyik tōḷvaḷi nekiḻumāṟu kiviṭ ṭirukkum ceyal, uṉ kuṭippiṟappiṟkum periyatoru kuṟṟam taruvatāy amiyātō?” (kali. 135.) eṉat tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kuṭimi cuṭṭik kūṟi varivu kaṭāvutal.
‘kuṭiyi iṟappak kūṟiyatu’ (aṭi 33, 34.) itu.  			(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi talivaṉiṭam avaṉnāṭu cuṭṭik kūṟal -
“taliva! tām etirpārāta iṉpatti niṉ nāṭṭiluḷḷa kuraṅku mutaliya palvēṟu vilaṅkukaḷum eytukiṉṟaṉavē.  etirpārtta iṉpatti nī aṭitaṟkaṇ taṭi ētu uḷatu? ātaliṉ virivil taliviyi maṇantu taṭiyillā iṉpam tuyppāyāka” (akanā. 2) eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu. 
‘nāṭum ūrum . . . .  kiḷaviyoṭu’ eṉṟatāṉ vanta kūṟṟu. itu  	(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi talivaṉiṭam avaṉpukaḻmi kūṟal - 
“taliva! puṉṉi niḻalil niṉṉiṭam  taṉ aḻakiya nalam tōṟṟa taliviyi, muṉpu avaḷiṭam tōṟṟa malarkaḷ itupōtu ikaḻumāṟu, poliviḻanta kaṇṇiṉaḷ āku māṟu kiviṭṭirukkum ceyal, niṉ pukaḻiṭattil periya tōr ikaḻcciyāka amiyātō?” (kali.135.) eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉiṭam pukaḻmi cuṭṭik kūṟi varivu kaṭāvutal.
‘nāṭum ūrum ... kiḷaviyoṭu’ eṉṟatāṉ koṇṭa kūṟṟu itu. 	(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi talivaṉiṭam avaṉvāymi kūṟal -
“taliva! puṉṉi niḻalil niṉṉiṭam aṇinalam tōṟṟa taliviyik kāmanōy mikka niliyaḷāka vittu nī kiviṭṭirukkum ceyal, niṉ mey kūṟiyoḻukum paṇ piṉkaṇ periyatoru poyyākat taṅkātō? ” (kali. 135.) eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉiṭam vāymi cuṭṭik kūṟi varivu kaṭāvutal. 
‘nāṭum ūrum . . . .  kiḷaviyoṭu’ eṉṟatāṉ koṇṭa kūṟṟu itu. 	(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi, talivaṉiṭam talimakaḷ taṉṉi aḻintami kūṟal - 
taṉṉi aḻital - taṉ maṉattuḷḷēyē maṟittuvittuk koṇṭu tuṉpuṟutal.
“taliva! talivikku niṉ piriviṉāl tuṉpam mikkavaḻiyum ataṉi avaḷ eṉṉiṭam kūṟiṉ, yāṉ niṉṉip piṟar muṉṉarp paḻitturippēṉō eṉṟu nāṇit taṉ tuyari eṉṉiṭam kūṟāmal taṉṉuḷḷēyē koṇṭu varuntu kiṟāḷ” (kali. 44.) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
iḵtu ‘eṇṇarum pal naki kaṇṇiya vakiyiṉ’kaṇ nikaḻva toru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 112. iḷa.)
tōḻi, talivaṉiṭam, “paricapporuḷ mikak koṭuttal vēṇṭum” eṉal - 
“taliva! mutalil oru cilarāl pēcappaṭṭu vanta kaḷa voḻukkam itupōtu alarākip parava, atu kēṭṭut taliviyit tāy iṟceṟitta ceytiyi aṟiyātu pala nāḷum taṭumāṟum maṉattōṭu eṉvayiṉ varum niṉṉi, ‘ivviṭatti viṭṭu akalka’ eṉṟu yāṉ yāṅkaṉam kūṟutal iyalum? taliviyiṉ tamarō avaḷukkup paricap poruḷāka mikka peruntokiyi etirpārkkiṉṟaṉar. ataṟku āvaṉa ceytu virivil taliviyi varika” (akanā. 90.) eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟutal.
itu ‘piṟavum’ (aṭi 32) eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kūṟṟu  	(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi, talivaṉiṭam, “puṉaṅkāval iṉi iṉṟu” eṉal - 
“taliva! tiṉikkolliyil katirkaḷ muṟṟa viḷintu - viṭṭaṉa. iṉit tiṉikkatir aṟukkappaṭṭuviṭum. tiṉi yiṟ kiḷiyik kaṭiyat talivi iṉi varum vāyppu illi. ātaliṉ avaḷi aṭital aritu. ākavē avaḷi virivil varika” (aiṅ. 260.) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
‘piṟavum’ (aṭi 32) eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu itu.  	(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi, talivaṉukku muṉṉilip puṟamoḻi moḻintu ceṟippu aṟivuṟuttal - 
tōḻi talivaṉi muṉṉilippaṭuttāmalum, potup paṭak kūṟuvatu pōlavum, “talivi iṉip puṉaṅkākka varumāṟu illi; avaḷ aṉṉiyatu pātukāvalil illiṉkaṇṇēyē iruppāḷ” eṉpataṉi avaṉukku aṟivittal.
“yāṉiyatu kiyip pōla vaḷintuḷḷa tiṉiyiṉ katirkaḷic cuṟṟattoṭu cērntu uṇṇum vaḷivāyp pacuṅkiḷikāḷ! talivaṉ taliviyit taḻuvip pirinta piṉṉar, kāvaliṉṟiyuḷḷa tiṉippuṉattik kāval ceyvati nīkkumāṟu, aṟaṉillāta aṉṉi paṇittuviṭ ṭamiyi nīṅkaḷ aṟintilirō? talivaṉik kāṇpīrēl, talivi iṟceṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa ceytiyic ciṟitāyiṉum avaṉukku naṉku aṟivuṟuppīr!” (naṟ. 376.) eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘orucār pakaṟkuṟi’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
tōḻi talivaṉi aruku aṭuttatu - 
kaḷaviṉvaḻi vantoḻukum talivaṉ cilanāḷ vārātu viṭuttu varutalik kaṇṭa tōḻi avaṉ aruku ceṉṟu, “taliviyi nī taḻuvi iṉpuṟāyāyiṉum, eṉpāl nī koṇṭuḷḷa aṉpu mikutiyālāvatu avaḷik ki cōra viṭātu aṇintu taliyaḷi ceyvāyāka! uṉṉit tavira avaḷukkup paṟṟukkōṭāvār piṟar ilar” eṉpatupaṭak kūṟutal. 	(naṟ. 355.)
tōḻi, talivaṉi “aruḷal vēṇṭum” eṉal - 
“cērppa! naṟpaṇpukaḷ ciṟitum kuṉṟāta koṭipōl vāḷākiya taliviyi virivil maṇantukoḷḷum ceyali niṉiyāmal, nī kaḷavoḻukkattil kālam nīṭṭuvatu takkatō?” (tiṇimoḻi. 44.) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
‘piṟavum’ (aṭi 32.) eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu itu. 	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi talivaṉi iṭitturittal - 
talivaṉ kaḷavukkālattuk kuṟi tapputaṟkut tāṉ kāraṇa mākiyavaḻiyum, kaṟpukkālattut taṉṉōṭu ūṭiya talivi tāṉ evvaḷavu teḷivippiṉum maṉam māṟāmal ūṭali nīṭṭittavaḻiyum, pulaviyum ūṭalum koḷvāṉ. appo ḻutu talivaṉi amitippaṭuttum poṟuppiṉit talivi ēṟṟukkoḷḷāḷ. talivaṉiṭam paṇinta moḻi kūṟi avaṉuṭiya pulaviyiyum ūṭaliyum tīrkkum poṟupput tōḻiyiṉatu. talivaṉuṭiya parattimik kuṇattiyum, talivaṉ kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟiyellām nampi avaṉiṭam cīṟṟam koḷḷātu maṭappattoṭu talivi irukkum paṇpu nalattiyum cuṭṭikkāṭṭit tōḻi avaṉi aṉpaṟṟavaṉ, koṭiyaṉ eṉṟu iṭittuk kūṟutalum uṇṭu.  	(to. po. 158. nac.)
tōḻi talivaṉip paḻittuk kūṟal -
“maliyiluḷḷa varuṭimāṉik kaṇṭu tiṉikkatirik koḷḷa varum kiḷikaḷ añcum nāṭa! nī poy kūṟutaṟkaṇ āṟṟaluṭiyāy; colvataṉicceyaṟpaṭuttuvatil āṟṟaluṭiyi alli” (aiṅ.287) eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘piṟavum’ (aṭi 32) eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 114 nac)
tōḻi, talivaṉi maṟuttaṟku arumi māṭṭal -
talivaṉit tāṉ evvakiyālum maṟutturikka iyalāmiyit tōḻi taliviyiṭam kūṟutal.
“talivi! avar mikavum paṇintu irantu kūṟum coṟkaḷik kēṭkumpōtu eṉ maṉam mikavum varuntukiṟatu. eṉṉāl poy kūṟi maṟuttalum iyalāta tākiṟatu. iṉiyum avar variṉ eṉṉāl maṟukka oṇṇātu”  (tañci. kō.124) eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘viravik kūṟal’ eṉṉum (84)
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum kiḷavik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(na. a. 148)
tōḻi talivaṉoṭu collāṭal -
talivaṉ tōḻiyiṉ karuttit taṉvaḻippaṭuttip piṉṉum taḻiyum kaṇṇiyum koṇṭu piṉ niṟkum.  attali vaṉuṭiya kuṟiyi muṭikkak karutum tōḻi taṉkaṇ nāṇam keṭum variyil kuṟippāka avaṉōṭu uriyāṭu vāḷ.  ivaḷ uriyāṭa uriyāṭa, avaṉukku āṟṟāmi perukum.  avaṉukku āṟṟāmi perukap peruka, ivaḷukku nāṇam keṭum.  avaṉ āṟṟāmiyum muḻu elliyi aṭiya, ivaḷ nāṇamum iṟuti ellikkuc curuṅka, talivaṉ, “uṉṉāl eṉ kāriyam muṭiyātati aṟintēṉ; maṭalēṟiyō, varipāyntō eṉ viruppiṉi muṭittuk koḷkiṟēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟuvāṉ.  tōḻi atu kaṇṭu taṉ nāṇiṉi nīkki, “ariyaṉa tāṅkuvataṉṟō, periyōr ceyki! nīr varuntal vēṇṭā.  yāṉ avaḷtiṟattu eṉṉiṉ āvatuṇṭēl kāṇpaṉ” eṉṟu kūṟi, avaṉ āṟṟāmiyi nīkkutal.  	(iṟi.a.9.uri)
tōḻi, talivi āṟṟāmi kaṇṭu, “varivu kaṭāvavō?” eṉṟu avaḷi viṉaval -
kaṭitiṉ taliviyi maṇakkumāṟu talivaṉi vaṟpuṟuttu taṟkut tōḻi taliviyatu icivu vēṇṭutal.
“talivi! uṉakkum talivaṟkum iṭiyē uḷḷa mikac ciṟanta naṭpurimiyi eṭuttuc cuṭṭi, avaṉi, ‘niṉṉi viriviṉ maṇantu eṉṟum piritalaṟiyā illaṟattit toṭaṅkuka’ eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟalāmā?” eṉṟu tōḻi taliviyi viṉavutal.
iḵtu  ‘aṉinili vaki’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kiḷavi	(to. po. 114 nac)
tōḻi, talivi āṟṟāmi kaṇṭuḻi, “pirinta talivaṉ mīṇṭāṉ” eṉakkūṟal -
talivaṉ taliviyip pirintu pala malikaḷik kaṭantu cēyiṭip pirintāṉāka, avaṉpirivu kuṟittut talivi varuntikkoṇṭirunta niliyil, talivaṉ virivāka viṉimuṟṟi vara, atu kaṇṭa tōḻi taliviyi nōkki, talivaṉ, cikaraṅkaḷ uyarnta malikaḷ palavaṟṟik kaṭantu vēṟṟunāṭu ceṉṟāṉāyiṉum, pirivip peritāka niṉittu, tuṭikkat tuṭikkak kuṟiyātu perukiya uṉ kaṇṇīrpperukki niṉittu, viṉiyi virivil muṭittu vantuviṭṭāṉ” (aiṅ.358) eṉṟu avaḷatu āṟṟāmi nīṅkat talivaṉ varukiyik kuṟippiṭutal. 			(to. po. 45 nac.)
tōḻi, talivi uṭaṉpōkat tāṉ taṅkutal -
taliviyi uṭaṉpōkkukku oruppaṭuttut talivaṉiṭam oppaṭittu aṉuppiya tōḻi taliviyip pirintu tāṉ iruppataṟkum, piṉ tāyum piṟarum kūṟum ēccukkaḷik kēṭṭup poṟuttiruppataṟkum varuntuvāḷāyiṉum, kaṟpukku ūṟu nikaḻumiṭattu nāṇattiyum viṭuttu uṭaṉpōkkil pōyāvatu talivaṉit talivi maṇat talē paṇpu eṉpati maṟṟavarukkut teḷiyac collit taliviyiṉ māṇpiṉi niliniṟuttuvataṟkākavē talivi yoṭu tāṉum uṭaṉ pōkātu illattut taṅkuvāḷ.  			(iṟi. a. 23)
tōḻi, talivikku ārāyntu kūṟiyatu -
aṉṉi veṟiyeṭukkat tuṇintuviṭṭāḷ eṉṟu paṭittu moḻintu varivu uṭaṉpaṭukkaluṟum tōḻi, talimakaḷi neruṅki, “aṉṉi eṭukkum veṟikkaḷat tukku varum vēlaṉi, nām nam kātalaṉiṭattu aḷavu kaṭanta viruppam uṭiyēmāy iruppavum, avaṉāl virumpap paṭāmal iruppataṟku uriya kāraṇam yātu eṉṟu viṉavu vōmā?” eṉṟu ārāyntu kūṟiyatu.  	(naṟ. 268)
tōḻi, talivikkut talivaṉvaravu aṟivuṟuttal -
talivaṉ kuṟiyiṭattil vantiruppatit tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟutal. ‘iṟivikku iṟivaṉ varavaṟivuṟuttal’ eṉṉum kiḷavi.
“talivi! nam cōliyil āṇmayilkaḷ tamakku ēṟpaṭṭa iṉpattip peṭimayilkaḷiṭam colluvaṉa pōlat tuyil iṉṟi olittukkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa” eṉṟal (kōvi. 160) pōṉṟu, talivaṉ maṉippuṟattē vantu puṭkaḷi eḻuppit taṉ varukiyit terivittuḷḷa ceytiyit tōḻi talivikkuk kuṟippākak kūṟutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu. 	 (na. a. 158)
tōḻi, talivikku varavu malintatu -
“talivi! talivaṉpirivāl varunti uṭalmeliya, uṉ kiyiluḷḷa toṭi uṭpaṭak kaḻalat toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu.  niṉ uṭalmeliviyum maṉavaruttattiyum kuṟippāka aṟintu uṉputalvaṉ uṉṉit taḻuvikkoṇṭu aṉpi ṉāl aḻukiṟāṉ. avaṉkuralik kēṭkuntōṟum uṉakku avaṉiṭam āci mika, avaṉit taḻuvit talivaṉatu pirivuttuṉpattit tāṅkikkoṇṭirukkiṟāy.  uṉakku maṉamakiḻvu uṇṭākumāṟu, vaṭukar irukkum neṭiya pala kuṉṟaṅkaḷik kaṭantu talivaṉ mīṇṭuvantu viṭṭāṉ.  iṉi nī avaṉuṭaṉ neṭuṅkālam piritaliṉṟi illaṟam nikaḻttuvāyāka!”  (naṟ. 212) eṉṟu, talivaṉ varuki aṟinta tōḻi taliviyiṭam kūṟutal.
itu ‘piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.	(to. po. 150 nac.)
tōḻi, talivikku varivu malivu kūṟutal -
“talivi! nam talivaṉi nām virumpukiṟōm. nam tāyum avaṉiṭam viruppuṭiyaḷāyuḷḷāḷ. nam tantiyum avaṉukku uṉṉi makaṭkoṭukka virumpukiṟāṉ.  ūril paḻi tūṟṟum makkaḷum uṉṉōṭu avaṉic cērttē colkiṉṟaṉar” (kuṟun. 51)
“kuḷaṅkaḷ vaṟaṇṭu pōṉatāl nīr peṟātu pacumiyaṟṟa niliyil karuvoṭu vāṭiya neṟpayirkku iravil peyta maḻi pōla, nam tuyar tīrat talivaṉ ippoḻutu vari voṭu vantāṉ”  (naṟ. 22)  eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu. varivu malivu-tirumaṇa nikaḻcci paṟṟiya makiḻvu.	(114. nac)
iḵtu ‘āṅkataṉ taṉmiyiṉ vaṉpuṟi’ eṉpataṉkaṇ varuva toru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, talivikuṟippu aṟintu pulaviyum ūṭalum kūṟal -
“tāy uyirōṭiruttali vēṇṭāta naṇṭu tāṉiyaṅ kaḷit tiṉṟu avaṟṟi virumpum ūra! ittaṉi nāḷka ḷāka illāmal iṉṟu evvāṟu taliviyik kāṇa vēṇṭum eṉṟa taṉitta aṉpu niṉakku vantatu?”
“tammip peṟāmal pulanta makaḷirip pulavi nīkkik kalavāmal āṭavar cellutal, muṉṉarēyē tuṉpamuṟṟu maṉam aḻintavarkaḷi ataṉmēlum mikka tuṉpattiṉic ceytāṟ pōlum”  	(kuṟaḷ 1303)
ittōḻi kūṟṟukkaḷ kaṟpukkālattē nikaḻntavi.
“kaṉipeyal naṭunāḷ yāṉ” eṉṟu talivi colleṭup pat tōḻi, avaḷ kuṟippiṉāṉ “eṉvayiṉ pulantāyum nīyākiṉ poyyāṉē velkuvi” eṉṟu talivaṉiṭam kūṟutal.  (kali.46) itu kaḷavukkālattatu.
“yāṉ ūṭat tāṉ uṇartta, yāṉ uṇarāta aḷavil tāṉ ūṭa yāṉ uṇarttat tāṉ uṇarāṉāka, vaḻutiyiṉ mārpip pullāmalēyē iravu kaḻintatu.  (muttoḷ. 104)
itu pāṭāṇ kikkiḷi.  	(to. po. 157 nac)
tōḻi “talivi kuṟippu vēṟu koṇṭāḷ” eṉal -
tōḻi, talivitaṉ maṉam aṉṟu vēṟuvitamāka māṟupaṭ ṭiruppatāl talivaṉatu kuṟiyit tāṉ eṭuttuk kūṟiṉ avaḷ ataṉic cevimaṭukkum niliyil illi eṉpataṉi avaṉukkuk kūṟutal.
“cērppa! uppaṅkaḻikaḷil malarnta nīlappūkkaḷic cūṭāma lum, naṇṭuvaḷikaḷi alittu viḷiyāṭāmalum, neṟṟiyil viyarvi tuḷikkac cintaṉiyil mūḻkiyirukkum avaḷmaṉatti eṉṉiṭam tiruppip piṉ avaḷiṭam uṉ kuṟikaḷik kūṟutal eṉṉāl evvāṟu iyalum?” eṉṟa tōḻikūṟṟu.
iḵtu ‘eṇṇarum palnaki kaṇṇiya vakiyiṉ’kaṇ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu,	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, talivi kuṟiyātatoṉṟik kūṟal -
talivi talivaṉ ceyta koṭumiyit taṉṉiṭam kūṟiṉāl, tāṉ piṟariṭam akkuṟiyik tūṟṟak kūṭum eṉṟu karutit taṉṉiyum maṟittatākat tōḻi talivaṟkuk kūṟutal.
“taliva! uṉṉāl taṉakkut tuyar mikavum, nī ceytuḷḷa piritaltuṉpamākiya aruḷaṟṟa ceyali, nāṉ niṉṉip paṟṟip piṟariṭam kuṟikūṟuvēṉ eṉṟu karuti ataṟku nāṇit talivi eṉṉiṭamum collāmal maṟittu viṭṭāḷ” (kali. 44) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
iḵtu ‘eṇṇarum pal naki kaṇṇiya vakiyiṉ’kaṇ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu. tōḻi, talivaṉiṭam taṉṉi aḻintami kūṟal’ kāṇka.  	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, talivi taṉ ‘ciṟanta putalvaṉi nērātu 
pulampaṟkaṇ’ kūṟal -
talivaṉ talivi iruvarkkum ciṟanta putalvaṉi niṉi yāmiyāl talimakaṉ taṉimiyuṟutaṟkaṇ tōḻi kūṟutal.		(to. po. 148 iḷa)
talivi makaṟpeṟṟāḷāka,avaḷkuḻanti ceviliyōṭu uṟaṅka, talivi vālāmi nīṅka neyyāṭi melliya paṭukkiyil kaṇṇayarntiruppa, taṉakku makaṉ piṟanta makiḻcciyāṉ taliviyiyum makaṉiyum kāṇṭaṟkut talivaṉ parattiyar illattiṉiṉṟu ceṟinta naṭuiravil kaḷvaṉip pōl vantāṉ.  avaṉiṭam ūṭal koḷḷātu avaṉi etirkoḷḷumāṟu taliviyit tōḻi vēṇṭutal. e-ṭu : naṟ. 40	(iḷa.)
yāriṉum ciṟanta putalvaṉi vāyilākakkoṇṭu ceṉṟuḻi, talivi avaṟku vāyil nērāḷākat talivaṉ varuntu miṭattē tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟutal.	(40 nac)
tōḻi taliviyi nōkki, “nam talivaṉ taṉ putalvaṉit tūkkikkoṇṭu niṉṉik kāṇa vantuḷḷāṉ.  aimpaṭit tāliyiyum taṉ eccilāl naṉittukkoṇṭu tanti pūciya cantaṉattiyum naṉikkumāṟu eccili oḻuka viṭṭukkoṇṭu uṉ putalvaṉ maḻali moḻi pēcum aḻakik kāṇātiruppatu koṭitu.  taṉṉi viṭṭu otuṅki niṟkum niṉṉi vāyil nērvikka, taṉ tantiyoṭu varupavaṉip pōlat taṉ tantiyiṉ peyarik koṇ ṭuḷḷa mintaṉoṭu vantuḷḷāṉ talivaṉ.  avaṉatu mārpiṉip puṟakkaṇikkātē” eṉṟu kūṟi vāyil nēr vittal  	(nac.)
tōḻi, talivi tiṉikkolliyip pātukāvāmi karutic cevili piṟari acceyaṟku viṭuttuviṭuvāḷ eṉṟu kūṟal -
“cantaṉa maraṅkaḷiyaḻittu uḻuta maliccāralil viḷiyum tiṉippuṉattiṉi, cantaṉamarattāṟ ceyta paraṇ mītiruntu cantaṉam taṉ mēṉiyiṟ kamaḻak kiḷikaḷiyōṭṭik kāval ceyyum taliviyiṉ kuralik kēṭṭuk kiḷikaḷ āravā rittut tiṉikkatiriṉiṉṟu pōkamāṭṭāvāy aṅkēyē vikiviṭum.  ātaliṉ avaḷi aṉṉi kiḷi kaṭiya iṉi aṉuppātu piṟariyē tiṉippuṉam kākka iṭuvāḷ” eṉṟu, tōḻi, tiṉippuṉam kāval oḻintapiṉ taliviyi maṇappatākak kūṟiya talivaṟkuk kūṟutal (tiṇi. nūṟ. 3) 			(to. po. 114 nac).
itu ‘kāppiṉ kaṭumi kiyaṟa variṉum’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koṇṭatoru kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi talivituyar kiḷantu viṭuttal -
iravil vaḻiyil talivaṉukku yātu nērumō eṉṟu niṉittu niṉittut talivi tuyaramuṟuvatit tōḻi eṭutturittu avaṉi vaḻi aṉupputal. ‘tōḻi talimakaḷ tuyar kiḷantu viṭuttal’ eṉpatu nūṟpā aṭi.
“taliva! yāḷikaḷ yāṉikaḷit tēṭittiriyum maliccāra lil nī tirumpip pōm vaḻi karuti varuntum taliviyiṉ tuyartīra, nī niṉṉūr cērntavuṭaṉ ūtukompiṉi ūti emakku nī ētamiṉṟic cērntataṉi aṟivippāyāka” (tañci. kō. 188) eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 158)
tōḻi, talivi nalam tolivurittut talivaṉi varivu kaṭāvutal -
“taliva! nī yāri naṭpāka uṭiyi? nī emmaḷavilnaṭantu koḷḷum ceyal naṇparceyalāka illi. kuṭṭuvaṉ eṉṟa maṉṉaṉukku urimiyāvatum kaṭalvaḷam pacuvaḷam mikkatumāṉa māntinakar pōṉṟa iyaṟki vaṉappip peṟṟirunta emmi virumpuvāyalli ātaliṉ, niṉṉāl iḻanta em nalaṉit tiruppik koṭuttuviṭṭu appāṟ celvāyāka” (naṟ. 395) eṉṟu, talivinalaṉ talivaṉ kaḷaviṭip pirivatāl tolivataṉic cuṭṭi, appirivu nikaḻātavāṟu tōḻi varivu kaṭāyatu.
itu ‘varivuṭaṉ paṭṭōṟ kaṭāval vēṇṭiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu.  	(to.po. 114 nac)
tōḻi, talivinili kūṟi viṭuttal -
poruḷvayiṉ piriya oruppaṭṭa talivaṉit tōḻi, taliviyatu tuṉpaniliyik kuṟippāl uṇarttip pōka viṭitarutal.
“taliva! nī poruḷporuṭṭākap pirintu cella virumpiṉāl nāṅkaḷ atit taṭukkavilli.  āyiṉ puṟattil varukiṉṟa yāriyāvatu taliviyip paṟṟi yātum kēṭkāmal puṟappaṭṭuc cel.  appaṭik kēṭpiṉ uṉ kāriyam muṭintu veṟṟi tārātu” (yāriyāvatu kēṭpiṉ, talivaṉ kēḷvippaṭum ceyti ‘talivi iṟantupaṭṭāḷ’ eṉpatāka irukkum eṉpatu kuṟippu) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu‘poruḷvayiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(ampikā. 543)
tōḻi, talivi maṇapporuṭṭāka aṇaṅkip parānili kāṭṭal -
talivi taṉ tirumaṇam iṉitu niṟivēṟutaṟporuṭṭut teyvatti vaṇaṅkutalit tōḻi talivaṉukkuk kāṭṭutal.
“nāṭa! talivi uṉṉōṭu taṉ tirumaṇam muṭṭiṉṟi nilipeṟap pūkkaḷiṉ tiraḷit tūvi naṟumaṇappuki yeṭuttut teyvatti vaḻipaṭum ceyalik kāṇpāyāka!  (tañci. kō.286) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘varivu malital’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(na. a. 174).
tōḻi talivi mīṇṭu vantuḻi ūratu nilimi kūṟal -
talivaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟa talivi uṭaṉpōkkiṉ piṉṉar ūrkku mīṇṭuvantakālit taṉpirivu kuṟittu ūravar koṇṭa uṇarvu paṟṟi viṉāvat tōḻi, “talivi! nī ceṉṟa pāli nilam, paṟavikaḷ kaṇakkiṭṭu aṟiyāta aḷavu paḻaṅkaḷik koṇṭa maraṅkaḷip peṟṟu, māṉkaḷum kaṇakkiṭṭaṟiyāta aḷavu nīrnilikaḷip peṟṟu iṉimi yāka iruttal vēṇṭum!” eṉṟu teyvatti vēṇṭi, uṉṉi niṉikkuntōṟum kaṇṇīr ukuttukkoṇ ṭirunta eṉṉiviṭa, ivvūr niṉṉip pirinta taṉimiyāl peritum vāṭiṟṟu” (aiṅ. 398) eṉṟu talivi uvakiyuṟu māṟu kūṟutal.
‘iyalpuṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu itu. 		(to. po. 39 nac)
tōḻi, taliviyatu āṟṟāmi kaṇṭu, “em varuttam tīrttaṟku iravukkuṟiyum vēṇṭum” eṉṟu kūṟal -
“talivaṉatu tēr kāliyil vantu nam kāṉalil taṅki māliyiltāṉ puṟappaṭṭup pōkiṟatu. attuṇi nēram taṅkiya piṉṉum attēriṉ pirivāl kaṭaṟkariccōli peritum poliviḻantatu.  nāmum taṉimit tuyaric ciṟitu nukarkiṟōm” eṉṟāṟpōlat taliviyatu āṟṟāmiyit taṇikka iravukkuṟiyum vēṇṭum eṉṉum tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 114 nac)
tōḻi, taliviyiṭam kaḷaviṉ nikaḻvik kaṟpuk kālattuk kūṟal -
“talivi! nam talivaṉ uṉ tirumaṇattiṉ muṉṉar uṉṉi makaṭ pēcac cāṉṟōri aṉuppiyavaḻi, nam uṟaviṉar paricap poruḷākap peruntoki kēṭṭaṉar. antac cikkal kāraṇamāka irutarattārukkumiṭiyē pūcal nikaḻumō eṉṟu kūṭa añciṉēṉ.  āyiṉ, nam nalviṉip payaṉākap paricapporuḷi muṭivu ceyvatil iruvarum porutta māṉa ōr aḷaviṉi icintukoṇṭaṉar” eṉṟāṟ pōlap paṇṭi nikaḻcciyit tōḻi taliviyiṭam kūṟal.
itu‘piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.  			(to. po. 150 nac.)
tōḻi, taliviyiṭam “talivaṉ varivoṭu varukiṉṟami kāṇa vammō!” eṉal -
“tāḻittātu puṉṉimalaroṭu naṟumaṇam kamaḻum kaṭaṟkaric cōliyilē pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ vantu niṉ uṭalaḻaku kuṉṟumāṟu niṉṉip pirintu ceṉṟa talivaṉ, ippoḻutu taṉ mārpil aṇinta māliyil vaṇṭukaḷ ūta, maṇikaḷ olikkum kutiriyic celutti varukiṉṟa aḻakik kuṉṟu pōlak kuvintuḷḷa veḷḷiya maṇalmēl mēṭṭil ēṟik kāṇpōm”  (naṟ. 235) eṉṟu tōḻi taliviyi aḻittal.
‘āṅkataṉ taṉmiyiṉ vaṉpuṟi’ eṉpataṉkaṇ varuvatoru kūṟṟu itu.  	(to. po. 114 nac)
tōḻi, taliviyiṭam, “talivaṉik kaṇṭu muyaṅkukam vammō” eṉṟatu -
“talivi! nilavum maṟintuviṭṭatu.  iruḷum cūḻntu viṭṭatu.  uṉṉip puṟam pōkāmal kākkum aṉṉiyum uṟaṅkiviṭṭāḷ.  talivaṉ ippaṉippoḻutilum taṉitta peruṅkaḷiṟu pōla, iravukkuṟiyiṭattu vantuḷḷāṉ.  ki tavaṟiya poruḷ mīṇṭum kiṭṭuṅkāl viḷiyum makiḻcci pōlac ciṉṉāḷākap pirintirunta talivaṉ vantuḷḷami yāṉ avaṉit taḻuvutalāl ēṟpaṭum makiḻcciyip peṟa varuka!”  (naṟ. 182) eṉṟu iravukkuṟikkut tōḻi taliviyi aḻittatu.
itu ‘puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koṇṭatoru kūṟṟu.	(to. po.114 nac.)
tōḻi, taliviyiṉ ‘kātal mikuti’ kūṟal -
vērppalāmaram ceṟinta malippakkattiyuṭiya taliva! palāmarattiṉ ciṟiya kiḷiyil periya paḻam toṅkiyatu pōlat taliviyiṉ uyir mikac ciṟiyatu.  avaḷ kāmanōy mikap peritu.  anniliyi aṟintavar yār?  oruvaru milli.  ātaliṉ yāṉ eṭuttukkūṟukiṟēṉ.  virivil avaḷi varintukoḷvāyāka”  (kuṟun. 18) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu. ‘kātal mikuti uḷappaṭa’ eṉpatu nūṟpāp pakuti.
ataṉāl peṟappaṭṭa kūṟṟu itu.	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi taliviyi āṟṟuvittatu -
talivaṉ oruvaḻittaṇantaviṭattu avaṉpirivu karuti āṟṟāḷākiya taliviyi nōkkit tōḻi, “kaṭaṟkaric cōliyilē alikaḷ koṇarnta maṇal kuvintirukkum mēṭṭiṉmēl ēṟi, niṉ vaḷiyali nekiḻacceyta talivaṉatu kaṭaṟkari nāṭṭik kāṇpōm, vā”  (aiṅ. 199) eṉṟu talivaṉatu nāṭṭiṉic cuṭṭik kāṭṭit taliviyit tōḻi āṟṟuvittal.
itu ‘vēṇṭāp piriviṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ koḷḷap paṭṭa toru kūṟṟu.	(to.po. 112 iḷa.)
tōḻi taliviyi iṭattu uyttu nīṅkal -
“avuṇarkaḷip pōrkkaḷattē aḻitta civanta ampu kaḷiyum, kurutikkaṟi paṭitalāṉ civanta tantaṅkaḷiyuṭiya yāṉiyiyum uṭiya murukap perumāṉukku urimiyāṉa nam malip pakutiyil cenniṟak kāntaḷkaḷ mikutiyāka uḷḷaṉa.  ivaṟṟiṉ aḻakik kaṇṭu cuvippāyāka”  (kuṟun. 1) eṉṟu tōḻi taliviyik kuṟiyiṭattuc cella viṭuttut tāṉ nīṅkutal.
itu ‘puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ varuvatoru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 114 nac)
tōḻi, taliviyi iravukkuṟi nayappittal
(nayappittal - virumpac ceytal) -
peṇ yāṉiyi virumpiya kaḷiṟu maliyiṭattē taṅkik kuṟavarkaḷ āravārittataṉāṉ ūrmaṉṟattik kaṭantu cellum nāṭaṉ, pakalilē vantu kuvaḷikaḷip paṟittu māli toṭuttut tantum tiṉiyiṟ kiḷikaḷik kaṭintum, mālivarit taṅki, nammiṭam oṉṟi virumpi ataṉi veḷiyiṭavum eḻucciyiṉṟi varunti niṉṟaṉaṉ”  (kuṟun. 346) eṉṟāṟpōlat tōḻi taliviyiṭam kūṟi avaḷi iravuk kuṟiyi nayappittal.
itu ‘puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum ’ eṉpataṉkaṇ varuvatoru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 114 nac)
tōḻi, taliviyip pakaṟkuṟi nayappittal -
vēṅkippū viḻunta pāṟiyip puliyākak karutip peṇ yāṉi añcum nāṭaṉākiya talivaṉ taṉimit tuyarāl taṉ vaṉappuk keṭṭu nāḷum varuntavum, nām avaṉiṭam kaṇṇōṭik karuṇiyoṉṟum ceyyavilli” eṉṟu kūṟit tōḻi taliviyip pakaṟkuṟiyiṉi virumpac ceytal
itu ‘puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ koḷḷap paṭuvatoru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 114 nac)
tōḻi taliviyi muṉital -
palavāṟu kūṟiyum uṭaṉpaṭātu maṟukkum taliviyit tōḻi caṟṟē kaṭintatu pōl kūṟutal.
“avar vantu kalaṅkiya maṉamum kaṇṇumāka irantatik kaṇṭu tāṅkāmal uṉṉiṭam coṉṉēṉ. nīyum irakka muṭiyavaḷtāṉē?  oruvar uyiriyē kākkum ceyal itu eṉṟum coṉṉēṉ.  uṉakku iḵtu ēṟkavilliyāṉāl eṉ piḻiyip poṟuttaruḷvāyāka” eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘pulantu kūṟal’ eṉṉum.  (87)
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(na. a. 142).
tōḻi tavaccelavu arumi -
talivaṉ iravukkuṟikkaṇ vantu mīṇṭu cēṟaṟkaṇ vaḻiyiṭi yuḷḷa ētaṅkaḷ palavāka iruttal paṟṟit tōḻi kūṟi iravukkuṟi vilakkutal.  (kuṟiñcikkaṇ kaḷaviyaluḷ nikaḻaṟpālatām ikkūṟṟu neytal naṭiyiyaluḷ piṟaḻak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.)  	(vī. cō. 996 urimēṟ.)
tōḻi taḷarvaṟinturittal -
kaḷavukkālattē talivaṉukkut talivitamar makaṭkoṭi maṟuppār eṉṟu eṇṇiya tōḻi, taliviyi uṭaṉpōk kiṟ koṇṭu ceṉṟu maṇakkumāṟu avaṉiṭam kuṟippiṭak karuti, “eṉ talivi kaṭaliyum cōliyiyum pārttuk kaṇṇīr vaṭittut taṉ āyattāriyellām taḻuvik koṇṭāḷ.  avaḷ maṉattē eṇṇiyatu iṉṉatu eṉa aṟikilēṉ” eṉat taliviyatu varuttattik kūṟutal.  			(kōvi. 199)
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
tōḻi taṉikaṇṭurittal -
pakaṟkuṟiyiṭattup puṇarcciyiṉ piṉ tōḻi taliviyi ēṉiya tōḻiyaroṭum viḷiyāṭa viṭuttut taliva ṉiṭam vantu tammūril aṉṟu iravu tam illattu viruntiṉa ṉākat taṅkumāṟu avaṉukku ulakiyalmuṟi paṟṟik kūṟal.
itaṉi ‘ulakiyal mēmpaṭa viruntu vilakkal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa. 	(na. a. 149)
itu ‘pakaṟkuṟi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  			(kōvi. 127)
tōḻi, tāy aṟiyāmi kūṟi veṟivilakkiyatu -
tāy makaḷatu nōyiṉ uṇmik kāraṇam aṟiyāmal veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻttat toṭaṅkiṉāḷ eṉpati viḷakkik kūṟit tōḻi veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻtali niṟuttiyatu.
“aṉṉiyē! miḷakukkoṭi vaḷarum maliyil uḷḷa murukapperumāṉtāṉ taliviyatu nōykkuk kāraṇam eṉṟu uṇmi aṟiyāta vēlaṉ kūṟiya kūṟṟi maṉattuk koṇṭu veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻtta nī muyalvatu porutta maṉṟu”  (aiṅ 243) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 114 nac)
‘kaḷam peṟak kāṭṭal’ eṉpataṉkaṇ ikkūṟṟu aṭaṅkum; ‘tōḻi kaḻaṅku pārttuk kūṟiyatu’m atu.
tōḻi tāykkuk kūṟuvāḷāy allakuṟi aṟivittatu -
“aṉṉāy! nam maṉikkolliyil nam talivi viḷi yāṭutaṟku niḻal koṭuttuvarum puṉṉiyiṉ kāykaḷ ceṟinta kiḷiyil kāṟṟu vēkamāka vīcuvatāl akkāykaḷ arukēyuḷḷa kuḷattil vīḻum ōci kēṭkiṟatu eṉa niṉik kiṟēṉ.  nīyum kavaṉittup pārttuc colluvāyāka” eṉṟu tōḻi tāyiṭam kūṟuvāḷ pōlat talivaṉ ciṟip puṟattāṉāka, iyaṟkiyāka nikaḻnta kuṟiyi avaṉ ceyta kuṟiyākat tām karuti allakuṟippaṭṭati aṟivittal.  			(to. po. 133 nac uri)
tōḻi tāyatu tuyiliṉi uṇarntu, talivikkut talivaṉ vantami kūṟik kuṟivayiṉ ceṉṟatu -
tōḻi naḷḷiravil tāyi nōkki,“aṉṉāy! nammaṉik kolliyiluḷḷa paḷḷattil vaḷarnta kūtāḷañceṭi varuntumāṟu ataṉmītu aruvi nīr ōṭukiṟatu.  tāyē! nam maṉik kolliyil acōka marattaṭiyil kaṭṭappaṭṭa ūcalip pāmpu eṉṟu karuti iṭi muḻaṅkukiṟatu.  aṉṉi! iṉṉum kēḷ!” eṉṟu kūṟa, avaḷiṭamiruntu maṟumoḻi vārāmiyāl avaḷ uṟaṅkiviṭṭati aṟintāḷ.  maṉikkaṇ yāvarum uṟaṅkavē, eṅkum amiti nilaviṟṟu.  piṉṉar avaḷ taliviyi nōkki, “talivaṉ ip poḻutu vantāl nallatu eṉṟu niṉikkiṉṟōm alla mō?  attakiya nam niṉippu īṭēṟumāṟu, pakaṟkālattum añcum kāṭṭu vaḻiyil kāṭṭāṟṟu veḷḷattil kaṉṟukaḷ aṭittuc cellappaṭuvatik kaṇṭa peṇ yāṉikaḷiṉ oli kēṭṭu āṇyāṉikaḷ veḷḷattil kaṉṟukaḷit tuḻāvum kāṭṭāṟṟiṉi nīntit talivaṉ iravukkuṟikku vantuviṭṭāṉ”  (akanā. 68) eṉṟu, tāy tuyil uṇarntu, talivaṉ vantamiyik kūṟik kuṟivayiṉ ceṉṟatu.  	(to. po. 114 nac.)
itu ‘puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu.
tōḻi, tāṉ atu muṉṉē cāṟṟiyatu urittal -
uṭaṉpōyt taliviyit taṉ maṉiyakattē maṇantu koṇṭu, piṉ taliviyiṉ ūrkku vanta talivaṉ, tām iruvarum muṟippaṭi varintukoṇṭa ceytiyic cuṟṟat tārkkuc collumāṟu tōḻiyiṭam kūṟa, ataṟku avaḷ ataṉit tāṉ muṉṉarē avarkaḷukkuc colli viṭṭamiyik kūṟutal.
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘taṉmaṉivarital’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(na. a. 194)
tōḻi, tāṉ aṟattoṭu niṉṟami talivikkuk kūṟal -
“nam tantiyum tāyum uṇarumpaṭi aṟivittu nām itukāṟum maṟivāka vittirunta kaḷavoḻukkac ceytiyi veḷip paṭiyāka yāṉ kūṟiyapiṉ, nam talivaṉ vantu varivu vēṇṭa, namar naṉmi ceyyum koḷki yōṭu icintu koṇṭamiyiṉ, nammi itukāṟum alar kūṟivanta ūravar ippoḻutu nammōṭu otta karuttiṉarāki viṭṭaṉar”  (kuṟun. 374) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘puritīr kiḷavi tāyiṭip pukuttal’ eṉpataṉkaṇ varuvatoru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, tiṅkaḷmēl vittuk kūṟal -
“īriya kiraṇaṅkaḷiyuṭiya matiyamē! nī iravil uṟaṅkātu vāṉattil niṉṟukoṇṭirukkiṟāy. mēlēyiruntu pārppatāl, uṉakku nilavulaka nikaḻccikaḷ yāvum pulaṉākum.  ‘talivi tōḷ meliyumāṟu, pirintuuṟiya māṭṭēṉ’ eṉṟu emmiṭam kūṟip pirintu ceṉṟa em talivarik kaṇṭāyā?”  eṉṟāṟpōlat tōḻi matiyattiṭam kūṟutal.  	(to. po. 133 nac. uri)
tōḻi, tiṉi arikiṉṟamiyum cuṟṟattār poruḷ vēṭkiyum kūṟiyatu -
“peruntaṉmiyillāta vēṅki pūttut tiṉi ariyum kālatti uṇarttavē, iṉit tiṉikkatirkaḷ koyyap paṭṭuviṭum.  talivaṉip pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ kāṇa vāyppu ila tākiviṭum.  emar emmi iṟceṟittu viṭuvar. taliviyi maṇam pēca varuvōriṭam emar paricapporuḷāka mikunta poṉṉaṇikalaṉkaḷi etirpārkkiṉṟaṉar.  yām paḻakip payiṉṟa talivaṉnaṭpu iṉi yātāka muṭiyumō?”  (tiṇi māli 18) eṉṟu talivaṉ ciṟippuṟamākat tōḻi avaṉ kēṭpak kūṟiyatu.	(to. po. 114 nac.)
itu ‘piṟavum’ (aṭi 32) eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.
tōḻi, tiṉikkolliyit talivi pātukāvāmi karutic cevili piṟari acceyaṟku viṭuppāḷ eṉṟu avaḷiṭam kūṟal -
1)  “talivi! tiṉikkatirkaḷ muṟṟat toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉa.  namar tiṉikkatirkaḷik koyyaluṟuvataṉ muṉṉamēyē pala katirkaḷ kiḷi mutaliyavaṟṟāl kavarappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉa.  nīyō, talivaṉik kūṭip poḻutupōkkaṟkaṇṇēyē īṭupaṭṭavaḷāy uḷḷāy. aṉṉi ittiṉippuṉatti vantu kāṇiṉ, ciṟukiḷikaḷi ōṭṭum āṟṟal uṉakku illi eṉṟu muṭivuceytu vēṟu oruvarit tiṉippuṉam kāvalukku amarttiṉāl, talivaṉatu mārpu uṉakkuk kiṭṭutaṟku aritākiviṭum”  (akanā.  28) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟu tōḻi taliviyiṭam avaḷkaṭami paṟṟik kūṟutal.  			(to. po. 115 nac.)
iccūttirattuḷ ‘aṉṉavi piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ ik kūṟṟut taḻuvik koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
tōḻi, tiṉi viḷintami kūṟic ceṟippu aṟivuṟīiyatu -
“malināṭa! eṅkaḷ tiṉippuṉattiṟku iṉikkāval vēṇṭuvatiṉṟu. tiṉi muṟṟiviṭṭamiyāṉ aṟukkap paṭṭuviṭum. nī iṉi eṅkaḷik kāṇa varutal aritākum pōlum” (tiṇi moḻi. 2) eṉṟu talivi illil ceṟikkappaṭṭuviṭuvāḷ eṉpataṉit tōḻi tiṉi viḷintami kūṟit talivaṉukku aṟivuṟuttiyatu.  			(to. po. 114 nac)
itu ‘kāppiṉ kaṭumi kiyaṟa varutal’ eṉpataṉāl koḷḷap paṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.
tōḻi tūtu vantami talivikkuk kūṟal -
“talivi! nī talivaṉ pirintami kuṟittu maṉam cuḻaṉṟu varuntaṟka. nam talivaṉ taṉ yāṉippaṭiyāṉ pakivari veṉṟu pōril mēmpaṭṭa veṟṟi koṇṭuḷḷāṉ. avaṉ mīṇṭu varukiṟāṉ eṉṟu tūtu vantuḷḷatu” (kali. 26.) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 150. nac.)
itu‘piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.
tōḻi tēettut talivi kuṟiyuṟu puṇarcci -
tōḻiyiṉ naṭpu nilippataṟkākat talivi talivaṉik kūṭutaṟku uṭaṉpaṭutal. aḵtāvatu tōḻi taliviyi nōkki, “talivaṉ oruvaṉ niṉṉiyaṭiyavēṇṭi eṉṉik kuṟi irakkiṟāṉ. akkuṟiyi nī muṭittaru ḷāyāyiṉ eṉ kuṟṟēvali iḻappāy” eṉṟāṟpōla accuṟutta, tōḻiyiṉ naṭpi iḻattaṟku añcit talivi talivaṉik kūṭa uṭaṉpaṭutal. (itu talivikku orupō tum nikaḻātu eṉpatu.) 	(iṟi. a. 13.).
tōḻi, “namar perumporuḷ vēṇṭa, atu niṉ vatuvikku ūṟāmō eṉṟu yāṉ añciṉēṉ” eṉṟu kaḷaviṉ nikaḻvik kaṟpil talivikkuk kūṟal -
“talivaṉ uṉṉi varitali vilakkuvataṟkāka namar perumporuḷ paricamākak kēṭṭapōtu, vatuviyi ēlātu perumporuḷ kēṭṭa namarkkum talivaṉukku miṭiyē pūcal ēṟpaṭṭuviṭumō eṉṟu añciṉēṉ. nam nalviṉi! talivaṉ perumporuḷ tara icintu piṉ atutantu niṉṉi amitiyāṉa cūḻniliyil maṇantu koṇṭāṉ” eṉṟu piṟṟiñāṉṟu tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟal.
iccūttirattuḷ ‘piṟavum’ eṉṟaṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
tōḻi taliviyiṭam kaḷaviṉ nikaḻvik kaṟpuk kālattuk kūṟal kāṇka. 	(to. po. 150. nac.)
tōḻi, naṟṟāy tanti taṉṉiyarkku aṟattoṭu niṟṟal -
“talivaṉ maṇam muṭikka aṉuppum kuṇakkuṟipāṭu illāta cāṉṟōrkaḷi varavēṟṟu avarkaḷ vēṇṭu kōḷi maṟukkātu nam taliviyi nīrvārttuk koṭut tal nam maṉattukku niṟivutarum ceyal eṉṟu ceyvō mēyaṉṟi, ittalivikkup paricapporuḷ kēṭpatāyiṉ ivvulakamē vili pōtātu” (tiṇimāli. 15.) eṉavum, “talivaṉ viṭutta cāṉṟōrkaḷ eṭuttukkoṇṭa muyaṟci yiyum uṅkaḷatu vāṉaḷāviya pukaḻuṭiya kuṭicciṟappiyum uṭkoṇṭu, ivarkaḷ paricapporuḷākat tarum ivvaḷamāṉa kuṉṟiṉi ēṟṟukkoṇṭu, ittaliviyi maṇamceytuvikkalām. avvāṟaṉṟi ittalivikku mulivilikkuriya paricap poruḷi nīṅkaḷ etir pārppiṉ, cōḻaṉuṭiya paṅkuṉi uttara viḻā naṭipeṟum uṟiyūrum, cēraṉuṭiya uḷḷiviḻā naṭipeṟum vañci nakarum kūṭa ivaḷukku viliyāvataṟku oppākā” (naṟ. 234.) eṉavum varum tōḻi kūṟṟukkaḷ.
itu ‘puritīr kiḷavi tāyiṭip pukuttal’ eṉpataṉkaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi, ‘nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum oḻukkamum uṇṭiyum, ceyviṉi maṟippiṉum celaviṉum payilviṉum, puṇarcci etirppāṭu uḷḷuṟuttu varūum, uṇarcci ēḻiṉum uṇarntu’ kūṟal -
taliviyiṭattup putiyaṉavākat tōṉṟiya nāṟṟam tōṟṟam mutaliyavaṟṟāṉ, talivikkuk koṭuppārum aṭuppārum iṉṟik koḷḷum kaḷavuppuṇarcci uṇṭu eṉṟu tōḻi taṉ maṉattuḷ kūṟikkoḷḷutal.
1. nāṟṟam - pūviṉāṉum cāntiṉāṉum talivaṉmāṭṭu ḷatākiya kalaviyāṉ talivimāṭṭuḷatākiya nāṟutal; ōtiyum nutalum pētipparuvattukkut taka  nāṟātu talivaṉ kūṭṭattāṉ māṉmataccāntu mutaliyaṉavum pala pūkkaḷum viravi nāṟutal.
2. tōṟṟam - puṇarcciyāṉ vanta poṟpu; nīṇṭum piṟaḻntum piḷḷipparuvattu veḷḷinōkku iṉṟi uḷḷoṉṟu koḷḷa nōkkum kaṇṇum, tam nili tirintu tuṇittu melkip paṇittuk kāṭṭum tōḷum muliyum eṉṟu iṉṉōraṉṉa.
3. oḻukkam - āyattāroṭu vēṇṭiyavāṟellām oḻukutaliṉṟit taṉṉip pēṇi oḻukutal; paṇṇi āyattoṭu muṟṟilāṉ maṇaṟkoḻittuc cōṟamittal mutaliyaṉa muṉinta kuṟippiṉaḷāyp peṇṭaṉmikku ēṟpa oḻukutal.
4. uṇṭi - uṇṇum aḷaviṉ kuṟital; paṇṭu pāl mutaliyaṉa koṇṭu oṟuttu ūṭṭa uṇṭu varukiṉṟāḷ, ippoḻutu ācāramum nāṇum kātalum mītūra, ataṉ mēl uvappu āṇṭiṉṟi mika oṟutta uḷḷattaḷātal.
5. ceyviṉi maṟittal - pūkkoytalum puṉalāṭalum pōlum viṉikaḷit tōḻiyi maṟittut taṉittu nikaḻttutal; aṉṟiyum, talivaṉ ceyta puṇarcciyākiya karuttiṉip pulappaṭaviṭātu tōḻiyi maṟittal eṉṟumām. taṉ niṉivum ceyalum piṟarkkup pulaṉākāmi maṟittal; aḵtu āyattoṭu kūṭātu iṭantalippāṭ ṭiṟku ētuvāka nīṅki niṟṟalām.
6. celavu - etticiyiṉum ceṉṟu viḷiyāṭuvāḷ oru ticiyi nōkkic cēṟal; paṇṭu pōl vēṇṭiyavāṟu naṭavātu cīrpeṟa naṭantu ōriṭattuc cēṟal.
7. payilvu - ōriṭattup payilutal; cevilimuliyiṭattut tuyil vēṇṭātu peyarttu vēṟōriṭattup payiṟal.
(ivviraṇṭākac collappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷuḷ, mutalāvatu iḷampūraṇaruri; ēṉiyatu nac. uri.)
puṇarcci etirppāṭu - puṇarcciyuṇmi; ‘etirppāṭṭup puṇarcci’ eṉa māṟuka. atu koṭuppārum aṭuppārum iṉṟi iruvarum tamiyarāy etirppaṭṭup puṇarum puṇarcci. ivvoḻukkam pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattiṟku ētuvākātu.
tōḻi, nāṟṟam mutaliya ēḻāṉum puṇarcciyuṇmi aṟintu taṉ maṉattuḷ, “nam taliviyiṉ kaṇkaḷil cevvari paravi yuḷḷatu. neṟṟiyil viyarvi tuḷippataṉoṭu neṟṟiyi vaṇṭukaḷ cūḻntu varukiṉṟaṉa. ivaḷiṭam ētō māṟutal ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷatu eṉṟu eṉatu uḷḷam karutukiṟatu” (ciṟṟaṭ ṭakam) eṉavum, “taliviyiṉ mūṅkil pōṉṟa tōḷkaḷum, kariya matartta kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷum, parutta iḷiya koṅkikaḷum paṇṭiya niliyiṉ vēṟupaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pakalum iravum ivaḷ uṭalil itaṟku muṉ illāta putiya maṇam vīcukiṟatu. ammaṇatti ivaḷum virumpuvatākat terikiṟatu. ivaṭku ētō putumi nikaḻntuḷḷatu” eṉavum, 
“nam talivi paṇṭiyaḷ allaḷ. innili teyvattāṉ ākiyatō, aṉṟi, makkaḷāṉ ākiyatō eṉpatu pulappaṭa villi” eṉavum,  “nam talivi tiṉikkolli kāppa taṟku vantatākat teriyavilli; iṅku vantu niṟkumavaṉ taṉṉāṉ ampu eyyappaṭṭatākak kūṟum māṉit tēṭi vantāṉākavum teriyavilli. ivarkaḷ maṉattil karuti yuḷḷatoṉṟi maṟittu oḻukukiṉṟaṉar; tammuḷ nāṇa muṭiyavarkaḷip pōla naṭittuk kaṇṇāṉ pēcik koṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷ” (paḻampāṭalkaḷ) eṉavum, 
“kākkikku iru kaṇkaḷukkum karumaṇi oṉṟāka iruppatu pōla, ivariruvaruṭiya uṭampukaḷ iraṇṭaṟkum uyir oṉṟāka amintiruppatu pōlat tōṉṟukiṟatu”(kōvi. 71) eṉavum palavāṟu pēcikkoḷḷutal.	(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi, nikaḻātatu nikaḻvatākap paṭittuk kūṟal -
tōḻi, taliviyiṉ uṟaviṉar koṭiyavar ātaliṉ avarāṉ talivaṟkut tīṅku nikaḻum eṉṟu uṇmiyil nikaḻāta nikaḻcci oṉṟaṉit tāṉē kaṟpaṉi ceytu kūṟal.
“nīṅkaḷ eṅkaḷ pakutikkup putiyavarāyiruttaliṉ eṉṉi viṉavukiṉṟīr. ataṟku maṟumoḻi koṭukka vēṇṭiyataṉi yāṉ takkatākak karutukiṟēṉ. iṅkiruppatāl numakku ētam varum eṉa añcukiṟēṉ. eṉ taliviyiṉ aṇṇaṉmār kaṇṇōṭṭamillāk koṭiyavar ātaliṉ ciṟitum karuṇi kāṭṭātu, niṉittati niṉittapaṭi muṭikkum avarkaḷāṉ umakku iṭar varumō eṉṟu añcukiṟēṉ” eṉṟāṟ pōlat tōḻi nikaḻātati nikaḻntatākat taliva ṉiṭam kūṟutal.
iḵtu ‘eṇṇarum pal naki kaṇṇiya vaki’ eṉpataṉkaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi nimittam kaṇṭu kūṟal -
talivaṉ pirintāṉāka, varuntip pirivi āṟṟiyirukkum taliviyi nōkkit tōḻi,“kāṭṭuppacukkaḷ muḻaṅkum malippuṟattu ariya vaḻiyil villil ampip pūṭṭi eyyum vēṭarkaḷukku añci vilaṅkukaḷ ōṭum irakkamaṟṟa curattu vaḻiyē ceṉṟa talivaṉ varak kaṇṭu, palli naṟcoṟ kūṟukiṟatu.  talivaṉ virivil vantuviṭuvāṉ” (kinnili. 18) eṉṟu kūṟal.
itu ‘piṟavum’ eṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷap paṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.  			(to. po. 150 nac.)
tōḻi nimittam kūṟal -
talivaṉ varutaṟkāṉa naṉṉimittaṅkaḷ tōṉṟak kaṇṭa tōḻi ataṉit talivikku urittal.
“talivi!  pakivari māyttu veṟṟiyoṭu nam talivar tirumpi varukiṟār eṉpataṟku aṟikuṟiyāyp palli oli kāṭṭukiṟatu.  nam iṭakkaṇṇum tuṭikkiṟatu.  ākavē nī kavalal vēṇṭā” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
iḵtu ‘vēntaṟku uṟṟuḻip pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.  	(ampikā. 535)
tōḻi, ‘nītta kiḻavaṉi nikaḻumāṟu paṭīik, kātta taṉmiyiṉ kaṇiṉṟu peyarttaṟ’kaṇ kūṟal -
puṟattoḻukkattāṉ taliviyik kiviṭṭa talivaṉit tāṉ nikaḻtta vēṇṭiya illaṟattil nilipeṟac ceytaṟ kākat tōḻi, puṟattoḻukkattiṉ payaṉiṉmi kūṟik kātta taṉmiyāṉē kaṇṇōṭṭamiṉṟi nīkkutaṟkaṇ kūṟutal. 			(to. po. 150. nac.)
“kāṭṭuppūṉi kavarntuviṭumō eṉṟu añcippeṭṭik kōḻi taṉ kuñcukaḷip pātukāvalāṉa iṭattic cērak kūvi aḻippatu pōla, talivi uṉṉik kavarntu viṭuvāḷō eṉṟa accattōṭu parattiyar kūṟum coṟ kaḷiṉ kūccalōṭu em teruvukku vārātē!” (kuṟun. 139) eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟal. (nac)
‘parattiyar kūṟum paḻiyoṭu vāral’ eṉavē, paṉṉāḷ nīttamiyum kaṇṇiṉṟu peyarttamiyum kūṟiṟṟu. kōḻi pōlat tāyar makaḷirit taḻuvikkoṇṭā rātaliṉ, puṟam pōyum payaṉ iṉṟu eṉak kātta taṉmi kūṟiṟṟu. (nac)
taliviyi nītta talivaṉi avaḷoṭu nikaḻumāṟu paṭuttal vēṇṭi, avaḷip paṇṭu puṟaṅkātta talivaṉ taṉṉi vāyil vēṇṭi vantaviṭattu, avaṉik kaṇ ṇōṭṭamiṉṟi vāyil maṟuttaṟkaṇ tōḻi kūṟutal. 			(148 iḷa)
tōḻi,“nuṅkaṭku avaṉ kūṟiya tiṟam yātu?” eṉṟu kēṭṭa tāykkuk kūṟal -
talivaṉ tirumaṇam ceytukoḷḷak kālam tāmatitta vaḻic cevili tōḻiyi nōkki, “talivaṉ nummit tuṟantāṉ pōlum!  uṅkaḷukku avaṉ kūṟiya ceyti yātu?” eṉṟu viṉavac cevilikkut tōḻi kūṟal.
“aṉṉiyē!  uḷtuḷiyi uṭiya neytaṟpū niṟinta tuṟiyi uṭiya talivaṉ em tōḷit tuṟantu vari poruṭkup pirintiruppatu uṇmiyē.  āyiṉ, avaṉ mutalnāḷ emakkuk karuṇi ceytu ‘piriyēṉ’ eṉṟu uṟutimoḻi kūṟiya nēram, nāṭōṟum avaṉ pirintu ceṉṟuḷḷa nāṭṭilum varumaṉṟō?  appoḻutu tāṉ kūṟiya uṟutimoḻiyi nāṭōṟum niṉikkum vāypput talivaṟku ēṟpaṭum.  ātaliṉ variporuḷ īṭṭit talivaṉ viriviṉ varuvāṉ” eṉṟu, aṟattoṭunili niṉṟa piṉṉar, varivāṉ pirinta talivaṉ kaṭitiṉ vārātavaḻi aiyuṟṟa cevili, “avaṉ nummit tuṟantāṉ pōlum. nuṅkaṭku avaṉ kūṟiya tiṟam yātu?”  eṉṟāṭkut tōḻi colliyatu. (aiṅ. 109)
itu (pāṅkuṟavanta) ‘vaki’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, notumalar varivu pēca vantuḻi, āṟṟātu talivi paci aṭa niṉṟuḻi,“itaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉ?” eṉṟa cevilikkuk kūṟutal -
“kaṭaltiriyi aṭuttu vāḻum kaḻuttu veḷḷiya kākki, tuṟiyil kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum ōṭattiṉ aṭikkaṭṭiyil muṭṭiyiṭum pātukāvaliyuṭiya nīrttuṟikku urimi yuṭiya  talivaṉ karuṇi ceyvāṉāyiṉ, ittalivi pāluṇavu uṇṭu makiḻvāḷ” (aiṅ. 168) eṉṟu talivi paci varutta niṟṟaṟkuk kāraṇam kūṟum vāyilākat tōḻi cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟṟal
itu ‘puritīr kiḷavi tāyiṭip pukuttal’ eṉpataṉkaṇ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 114 nac)
tōḻi, pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ ciṟippuṟamākac ceṟippu aṟivuṟīiyatu -
“nām kaṭalāṭumpōtu tāṉum kaṭalāṭiyum kaṭaṟkaric cōliyil taṅkiyum, nam āyattāroṭu kuravi kōttāṭiyum, ayalāṉip pōla vantu paḻaki niṉṉit talivaṉ taḻuvic celvatāl alar paravi viṭṭatu.  ataṉōṭum amiyātu, nammip piriyātirukkum taḻiyāṭiyip pōl nammi viṭṭuc caṟṟum piriyātu talivaṉ taṅkiya ceyal, ippoḻutu nammit tāy iṟceṟittu vikkum nilikkuk koṇṭu vantuviṭṭatu”  (kuṟun. 294) eṉṟu, talivaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉāka, talivik kuk kūṟuvāḷāyt tōḻi talivi iṟ ceṟikkappaṭṭa miyi aṟivuṟuttiyatu.
itu ‘kāppiṉ kaṭumi kiyaṟa varutal’ eṉpataṉpāṟpaṭuva toru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 114 nac)
tōḻi, pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ talivaṉ nīṅkiyavaḻik kūṟiyatu -
“talivi!  neytalmalarkaḷ kuvintuviṭṭaṉa.  katiravaṉ mēṟṟiciyil maṟiyat toṭaṅka, nilam veppam taṇintu viṭṭatu.  kaṭaṟkaric cōliyiṉ tōṟṟap polivum maṅkiviṭṭatu.  niṉ kāmam muṟṟum taṇiyā niliyil talivaṉ tērēṟic ceṉṟuviṭṭāṉ. talivaṉoṭu kūṭiyirunta kālattu makiḻvit tanta cōli ippoḻutu uṉakku evvāṟākiyuḷḷatō?”  (naṟ. 187) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘naṉṉayam peṟṟuḻi nayampuri iṭattiṉum’ eṉpa- taṉkaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 112 iḷa).
tōḻi pakaṟkuṟi nērtal -
“taliva!  eṅkaḷ ūr arukil poyki uḷḷatu.  poyki arukil kāṭṭāṟu uḷḷatu. cōliyil iri tērum nārikaḷit tavira vēṟu yārum irār. cōlippakkam yāṅkaḷ em kūntalil aṇintu koḷḷac ceṅkaḻunīr koṇṭuvarac celvōm.  aṅkut taliviyum varuvāḷ”  (kuṟun. 113) eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivaṉiṭam tōḻi kūṟip pakaṟkuṟi icital.
itu ‘puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 112 iḷa.)
tōḻi, pakaṟkuṟi nērntu iṭam kāṭṭal -
“talivi!  tirumāliyum palarāmaṉiyum pōlak karuṅkaṭa lum veṇmaṇaṟparappum kāṭci vaḻaṅkum ikkaṭaṟkariyi aṭutta cōliyiṉ pakkaṅkaḷilellām puṉṉi maraṅ kaḷum, naṭuvilellām ñāḻal tāḻi maraṅkaḷum ceṟintuḷa.  ātaliṉ avviṭam pakaṟkuṟikku ēṟṟatu” (tiṇimā. 58) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
“ūrukkup pakkattil poyki uḷḷatu.  poykiyi aṭuttuc ciṟu kāṭṭāṟu uḷḷatu.  aṭuttuḷḷa cōli yil nārikaḷit tavira makkaḷ naṭamāṭṭam irātu.  āṅku yāṅkaḷ eṅkaḷ taliyil aṇintu koḷḷa vēṇṭic ceṅkaḻunīr koyya varukiyil aṅkut taliviyum varuvāḷ”  (kuṟun. 113) eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṟkup pakaṟkuṟi nērntu iṭam kāṭṭutal.
itu ‘puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, pakaṟkuṟi nērvāḷpōl iravukkuṟi vilakkiyatu -
“taliva!  miṉṉal veḷiccattil maḻiyip poruṭpaṭut tātu malikaḷik kaṭantu, tīṇṭi varuttum teyvaṅkaḷukku añcātu, vēl oṉṟaṉiyē tuṇiyākak koṇṭu kaḷiṟukaḷ iyaṅkum vaḻiyil naḷḷiravil vārātē. pūkkaḷ pāṟikaḷil viḻuntu aḻaku ceyyum nammalippakkattil pakalilum ivaḷi nukaralām aṉṟō?”  (kali. 49) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘kaḷaṉum poḻutum varinili vilakku’taṟkaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 114 nac)
tōḻi, pakaṟkuṟiyum iravukkuṟiyum maṟuttu varivu kaṭāyatu -
‘kōḻili vāḻi’ eṉṉum akappāṭṭiṉuḷ (2) “mali nāṭa!  niṉ maliyiluḷḷa palvēṟu vilaṅkukaḷum etir pārāta iṉpaṅkaḷikkūṭat tuykkiṉṟaṉavē! etirpārk kum iṉpattit tuyppatil niṉakkut taṭiyātuḷatu?  taliviyō, niṉ pirivāṟṟātu varuntukiṟāḷ.  nīyō, iravukkuṟiyil vantu tuṉpuṟukiṟāy. vēṅkiyum pūttu viṭṭaṉa.  cantiraṉip parivēṭam cūḻntuḷḷatu” eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟum kūṟṟil, vēṅki malarntata ṉāṉ tiṉiyaṟukkappaṭum ātaliṉ pakaṟkuṟi vāyātu eṉavum, vaḷarpiṟi nilavu mikku varuvatāl iravukkuṟiyum vāyātu eṉavum kuṟippāṟ kūṟi, “tiṭṭamiṭṭa iṉpatti nukarvatil uṉakkut taṭi yātuḷatu?” eṉṟu varivu kaṭāvi, cantiraṉip parivēṭam cūḻntuḷḷatu eṉpataṉāṉ aṇmiyil varum niṟimatināḷē maṇaviṉikku ēṟṟa nāḷ eṉṟu maṇattukkuriya nāḷum variyaṟuttuk kūṟiyatu uṇarappaṭum.
itu‘piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭuvatoru kūṟṟu. 			(to. po. 114 nac).
tōḻi, pakaṟkuṟivantu pōkiṉṟa talivaṉ puṟakkiṭi nōkki āṟṟāta taliviyiṉ kuṟippaṟintu māviṉmēl vittu vaṟpuṟuttal -
puṟakkiṭi-tirumpip pōtal; mā-avaṉtēril pūṭṭiya kutiri.
“pakaṟkuṟiyiṭattut talivaṉoṭu makiḻum nām māli nēram vanta aḷavil mey cōrntu varuntum neñcoṭu tirumpip pōmāṟu talivaṉi aḻittuc ceṉṟa avaṉtēril pūṭṭiya kutirikaḷ, kuḻantikaḷ tam tōḷil kōtta paṟiyiṉ naṭuvil eḻutappaṭṭirukkum kuruviyiṉ uruvam avarāl kōlkoṇṭu aṭikkappaṭutalāṉ citivatu pōla, kōlkoṇṭu tākkappaṭṭu uṭal nalivaṉavākuka!” (naṟ. 58) eṉṟāṟpōlap pakaṟkuṟiyiṟutiyil talivi āṟṟāmiyit tōḻi māviṉmēl vittuk kūṟiyavāṟu.
iḵtu ‘aṉpu taliyaṭutta vaṉpuṟi’ eṉṟa vitappāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi pakaṟkuṟi vilakkiyatu -
“taliva! puṉṉimarattiṉ niḻaliliruntu mīṉuṇaṅkalip puḷ ōṭṭip pātukāttum, kavara varum aṉṉaṅkaḷi ōṭṭiyum, kuḷirnta kaṭaṟkaric cōliyil viḷiyāṭiyum yāmpoḻutu kaḻikkavum, vaḷamāṉa ippērūriṉ teruk kaḷil nāṭōṟum emmip paṟṟip paḻimoḻi tūṟṟappaṭu kiṟatu.  ataṉi nīkkutal vēṇṭi nī pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ vāraṟka” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘kaḷaṉum poḻutum varinili vilakkutaṟ’kaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu 	(to. po. 114. nac.)
tōḻi paṭittumoḻi -
talimakaṉ kuṟiyuṟṟum akkuṟi nīkkappeṟāṉāyc cel kiṉṟāṉi āṟṟuvikkak karutiya tōḻi, putiyaṉavākac cila moḻikaḷip paṭittukkoṇṭu, “cērppaṉē! nī em ciṟukuṭiyiṉkaṇ iṉṟiravu taṅkic celluviyāyiṉ, niṉ parikaḷ uṇavuṇṇa, nīyum taṉiyākat taṅkuvi alli” eṉap puṉinturittal.	(naṟ. 254.)
tōḻi, paruvam aṉṟeṉṟu paṭittu moḻintatu -
“talivi! kal niṟinta vaḻiyik kaṭantu poruḷ īṭṭac ceṉṟa talivaṉ tāṉ kārkālattu mīḷvatākak kūṟic ceṉṟāṉāka, avaṉ kuṟitta paruvam varumuṉ kālamallāk kālattup peyta maḻiyik kārkālap paruvamaḻiyākak karutit taṉ kiḷikaḷil arumpukaḷ arumpiya koṉṟi maraṅkaḷ orutaliyāka aṟiyāmiyuṭiyaṉa!”  (kuṟun. 66) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.  			(to. po. 150 nac)
tōḻi palvakiyāṉum paṭittal -
paṭittal-puṉintu collutal. kaḷavukkālattil taṉṉi irantukoṇṭu taṉ kāriyattik kūṟavanta tali vaṉit tōḻi paṇivukkut kuṟiyillāta coṟkaḷāṉ akaṟṟi niṟuttuvāḷ; “numatu kūṭṭattiṉi yāṉ muṉṉamē aṟival” eṉap poyyākak kūṟuvāḷ; talivaṉ talivi ivarkaḷiṭam taṉakku muṉṉar aṟimukam illiyeṉiṉ, avaṉip ‘poyyaṉ’ eṉṟu kūṟuvāḷ; avaṉ taliviyi virivil varintu kōṭaṟporuṭṭuc cila poykaḷik kalantum pēcuvāḷ; nalla payaṉuṭiya coṟkaḷi ēḷaṉam ceytum pēcuvāḷ; “niṉ kuṟiyi nīyē ceṉṟu uri” eṉpāḷ;  tāṉ taliviyi iṉṉāḷ eṉa aṟiyavilli eṉpāḷ. avaḷ kūṟuvataṉ payaṉ, taliviyiṉ arumiyit talivaṉ uṇarntu kaḷavoḻukkatti nīṭṭikkātu virivil avaḷi maṇantu illaṟam naṭattutal vēṇṭum eṉpatām.  	(to. po. 237 nac.)
tōḻi, ‘pāṇar kūttar viṟaliyar eṉṟivar pēṇic colliya kuṟiviṉikku etir’kūṟal -
pāṇarāyiṉum kūttarāyiṉum viṟaliyarāyiṉum taliva ṉic cārntu virumpic colliya vāyil vēṇṭutalukku etirāka vāyil maṟuttut tōḻi kūṟutal.
“pāṇa! nī kīḻmakaṉ ātaliṉ, uṉ vāyil poyyē varukiṟatu.  nī talivaṉukkākap parintu pēci iṅkē niṟkātē. pakalilē uṉṉi em cēriyiṭattuk kaṇṭāl talivaṉ veṭkuṟak kūṭum.”
itu pāṇaṉi vāyil maṟuttatu.
“ūraṉukku iṉivarukiṉṟa maṟunāḷikkum oru paratti yik koṇarntu koṭuttaṟku nērnta aṟiyāmiyuṭiya viṟaliyē!  uṉ poymoḻikku uṭaṉpaṭṭa apparatti yar tāymāriyum piṟar muyaṅkumāṟu cērkka avarkaḷiṭam niṉ uḷḷīṭillāta coṟkaḷip payaṉpaṭutti yiruppāyē! uṉ coṟkaḷ parattiyarukkum avartāymārukkum ēṟkappaṭalām.  emmiṭam niṉ collāl payaṉilli” (naṟ. 310) eṉṟu tōḻi viṟaliyiṭam kūṟi vāyil maṟuttavāṟu.
maṟuttal pōla orōvaḻi vāyil nērtalum uṇṭu. 			 (to. po. 150 nac)
tōḻi pāṇaṟkuk kūṟal -
“pāṇa! nilavilē kuṟuṅkāl kaṭṭilmītu aminta paṭukki yil perumūcceṟintu koṇṭu talivaṉ makaṉit taḻuvik kiṭakka, talivi avaṉatu mutukit taḻuvi ūṭal oruvāṟ ṟāṉ tīrntu paṭuttirukkiṟāḷ.  itu paṇpuṭittu.  kāṇpā yāka!”  (kuṟun.359) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 150 iḷa.)
tōḻi,‘piriyuṅkāli etirniṉṟu cāṟṟiya, marapuṭi etiriṉ’ kaṇ kūṟal -
talivaṉ cēyiṭip piriyuṅkālattu muṉṉiṉṟu colliya marapuṭiya māṟupāṭṭiṉkaṇ tōḻi kūṟutal  			(to. po. 148 iḷa.)
“taliva! nī taliviyi nīttuc cellum vaḻiyil nīr nītta cuṉiyilē iliyoṭu vāṭiya malarkaḷ niṉ celavit taṭukkum.
“taliva!  tām paṭarnta maram vāṭukiyiṉālē ataṉic cuṟṟippaṭartal nekiḻnta koṭikaḷ niṉ celavit taṭukkum.
“taliva! vāṭiyiṟakkum niliyiluḷḷa vāṭalmalarkaḷ niṉ celavit taṭukkum.
“ivvāṟu aṟivuri kūṟum kēḷirpōla nī pōkum kāṭṭiluḷḷa maramceṭikoṭikaḷē niṉ celavit taṭuttuviṭum”  (kali. 3) eṉṟu talivaṉiṭamum,
“talivar pirivuṇarttiyaviṭattē ataṟku uṭampaṭṭu, piriyumiṭattē nikaḻum allalnōyiṉiyum nīkki, ap pirivutaṉṉiyum āṟṟi, piṉṉum iruntu uyirvāḻum makaḷir ulakattup palar!”  (kuṟaḷ 1160) eṉṟu taliviyiṭamum tōḻi kūṟutal.  	(iḷa.)
talivaṉ kaṟpukkālattup piriyuṅkāl, muṉ niṉṟu iṟanta kāla etirkāla nikaḻccikaḷit tōḻi kūṟutal. 	(150. nac.)
“nī taliviyiṭam poyyākap pēraṉpu kāṭṭi ivaḷik kiviṭṭu ennāḷ puṟappaṭukiṉṟāyō, annāḷ ivaḷ uyir pōyviṭum” (kali. 5) eṉṟu talivaṉi nōkkit tōḻi kūṟuvataṉkaṇ, kiviṭutal-iṟappu, uyir pōtal etirvu eṉa irukāla nikaḻccikaḷum kūṟappaṭṭavāṟu.
“taliva! vēṉilparuvattu veppattil koṭiya pāli yik kaṭantu poruḷ tēṭak karutukiṉṟāyē. uṉṉiyē nampiyirukkum taliviyiṉ mutaṟkaruvil tōṉṟiya putalvaṉatu cirippik kāṇum celvattiviṭa nī tēṭum poruḷ uyarntatō?”  (aiṅ. 309) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu etiratu nōkkiṟṟu.  (aiṅ. 424) ām pāṭalum atu.  	(nac.)
tōḻi, piriviṭi āṟṟāta taliviyi naṉṉimittam kūṟi vaṟpuṟuttal -
“talivi! naṉṉimittaṅkaḷ tōṉṟuvatāl, varuntum nam neñciṉ nōy tīrak talivaṉ virivil vantuviṭalām.  avaṉoṭu nī pulattalum pullutalum kalattalum ceytu makiḻka!” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
iḵtu ‘aṉputaliyaṭutta vaṉpuṟikkaṇ’ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, piriviṭi iyaṟpaḻittu vaṟpuṟuttal -
“talivi!  nam tolviṉippayaṉ nukarcci tarumpōtu ataṉi nukarvataṉkaṇ varuntuvatāl payaṉ eṉ? ātaliṉ varuntaṟka.  uppuppoti maḻiyāṉ karintu aḻivatu pōla nī nāḷtōṟum uruki melivataṉik kaṇṭu uṉ uyirkku ētam varumō eṉṟu añcukiṟēṉ. talivaṉ namakkuc ceyta koṭumiyi niṉittu, nammiṭam aṉpuṭiya avaṉatu paḻamutirmali tuṉpattit tāṅka muṭiyātu kaṇṇīri aruviyākac corikiṟatu.  nām nam tuyari avaṉiṭam nēril ceṉṟu terivikkap puṟappaṭuvā yāka” (naṟ. 88) eṉṟu talivaṉi iyaṟpaḻittu, tuyar āṟṟumāṟu taliviyit tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyavāṟu.
‘tōḷum paḻiya aḻaku iḻantaṉa;  aṉṉiyum tuyar uṟukiṟāḷ;  alarum mikap paravi viṭṭatu’ eṉṟu aḻuta ḻutu uṉ aḻaki iḻakkātē.  talivaṉ cēynāṭṭup pirintu ceṉṟaṉaṉ āyiṉum, uṉṉi niṉiyāmalirāṉ; ātaliṉ virivil vantuviṭuvāṉ”  (akanā. 209) eṉṟu, talivaṉ virivil vantaṇiyumāṟu kūṟit taliviyit tuyarāṟṟumāṟu tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyavāṟu.
iḵtu ‘aṉpu taliyaṭutta vaṉpuṟikkaṇ’ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, ‘ piḻittu vantu irunta kiḻavaṉi neruṅki, iḻittu āṅku ākkik koṭuttaṟkaṇ’ kūṟiyatu -
piḻittu vantu irunta talivaṉi neruṅkit taliyaḷikku māṟu kūṟit talivimāṭṭu avaṉi ākkik koṭuttaṟkaṇ tōḻi kūṟutal. 	(148 iḷa.)
“taliva! tēṉūrip pōṉṟa ivaḷ iyaṟkiyaḻaku taṉittu vāṭa ivaḷip pirintu nī ceṉṟu nukarumāṟu niṉparatti attuṇi aḻakuṭiyavaḷā?”  	(aiṅ. 57)
“taliva!  mayakkam poruntiya uṉ maṉattuyaram tīra uṉakku maruntu pōlap parikāram tēṭum yāṉ ivaḷ pulaviyip pōkkum maruntāka illāmal iruppati nōkka eṉ maṉam varuntukiṟatu”  (aiṅ. 59)  eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟi iruvar maṉavēṟu pāṭum tīrttut taliviyik kūṭṭiyavāṟu.
paratti maṉikkaṇ taṅki vantu maṉikkaṇ pukātu puṟattirunta talivaṉi iṭittuk kūṟi, aṅṅaṉam kūṟuvataṉ vāyilākat taliviyiṉ ūṭalip pōkkit talivaṉuṭaṉ avaḷik kūṭṭumiṭattē tōḻi kūṟutal 	(150 nac.)
“kokkiṉ cēval kayalmīṉ kuṟittu iruppatu pōlap putupparattiyari akappaṭuttaliyē eppoḻutum kuṟik kōḷākak koṇṭa talivaṉē! nī nēṟṟup paratti maṉiyil iruntataṉi maṟukkāmal ēṟṟukkoḷ. nī nēṟṟu niṉ pāṇaṉoṭum maṟṟa cuṟṟattoṭum muḻavu olikkak kaḷḷiṉik kuṭitta kaḷippoṭu emmaṉip pakkam vārā mal niṉparattikkāka amikkappaṭṭa niṉ illatti aṭintapōtu, akkuṟuntoṭi maṭanti peritum makiḻntu niṉṉi ēṟṟukkoṇṭāḷ. nāṅkaḷ atupaṟṟik kavaliyuṟavilli.  puṟattoḻukkam niṉpāl illi eṉṟu māttiram kūṟaṟka.  nī maṟuppatu nakiyākavuḷḷatu”  (akanā. 346) eṉṟu talivaṉpāl talivi koṇṭa ūṭalit tōḻi tīrttatu. 	(nac.)
tōḻi, piṟarik kāttaṟku iṭuvar eṉac ceṟippu aṟivuṟīiyatu -
“taliva!  cantaṉa maraṅkaḷi veṭṭi nīkki uḻuta kolli yil viḷikkappaṭṭa ciṟutiṉiyic cantaṉamaraṅkaḷāl ākkappaṭṭa paraṇmītu iruntu koṇṭu tāṉ pūciya cantaṉam maṇam vīcat talivi tiṉiyil paṭiyum kiḷikaḷit taṉ kuralāl veruṭṭavum, avaḷ kural kiḷiyiṉ kurali ottiruttaliṉ taṉ iṉattiṉatu kuralām eṉak karutik katirkaḷik kavaravarum kiḷikaḷ añci nīṅkuvatilli.  ātaliṉ tiṉiyik kākkap piṟari iṭuvar”  (tiṇi māli 3) eṉṟu tōḻi talivi iṟceṟippiṉit talivaṟku aṟivuṟuttiyatu.  
itu ‘kāppiṉ kaṭumi kiyaṟa varutal’ eṉpataṉkaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu. 	 (to. po. 114 nac)
tōḻi ‘piṟaṉ varivu āytaliṉ’kaṇ talivaṟkuk kūṟal -
ayalavar taliviyi maṇakkak karutiyavaḻi, talivicuṟṟat tār avarukkut taliviyi maṇamceytu koṭuppatu paṟṟi ārāynta viṭattē tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟutal.
“taliva! tāḻiyi vēliyākak koṇṭu muṇṭakac ceṭikaḷāṉ kūri vēyappaṭṭa ciṟiya vīṭukaḷiyuṭiya ik kaṭaṟkaric ciṟṟūril āravāram uṇṭākum vakiyil notumalar taliviyi maṇam pēca neṭuntēril vantuḷḷa ṉar.  mali pōlak kuvinta maṇalmēṭukaḷik kaṭantu vanta avarkaḷ makaṭpēcāmal vāḷā mīḷamāṭṭārpōlum. ipparatavaruṭiya kuṟumakaḷākiya talivi, valiyum tūṇṭilum paṟṟi mīṉpiṭittu vāḻum akkoliñarkaḷ illattiṟku maṇam pēcappaṭṭuviṭuvāḷ.  (ātaliṉ virivil nī maṇaviṉikku muyalka)”  (naṟ. 207) eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṟkuc ciṟippuṟamāka notumalar varivu paṟṟi vantati aṟivuṟutti avaṉi varivu kaṭāayatu.  			(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, ‘piṟaṉ varivu āytaliṉ’kaṇ tāy kēṭpat talivikkuk kūṟal -
“talivi! kuṉṟattil paḻaṅkuḻikaḷi akaḻnta aḷavil vili uyarnta maṇikaḷ kiṭṭum vaḷamāṉa nāṭṭiyuṭiya talivaṉ, paṇṭoru nāḷ uṉ mayirmuṭiyit taṭavik koṭuttavāṟē, ‘ceṟitoṭi!  nī aṟivu mutirum maṅkip paruvat toṭakkattil, eṉ maṉikku illaṟam naṭatta varuvāy’ eṉṟu kūṟic ceṉṟāṉē! avaṉ ippoḻutu eṅku uḷḷāṉō?”  (kuṟun. 379) eṉṟu tāy kēṭpat talivikkuk kūṟum tōḻi aṟattoṭu niṟṟal.  			(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi ‘puṇarcci vēṇṭaṟ’ kaṇ kūṟiyatu -
palavakiyāṉum kūṟit tōḻi talivaṉi akaṟṟa muyaṉṟa vaḻiyum, avaṉ akalātu piṉṉum avaḷi vēṇṭu miṭattuk kūṟutal.
piṉṉum puṇarcci vēṇṭiya talivaṟku iṭam uṇarttutal, pakaṟkuṟi nērtal, iravu varuvāṉip pakal vara eṉṟal, pakal varuvāṉi iravu vara eṉṟal, kuṟipeyarttal mutaliya ellām koḷḷappaṭum.
“ūravar kūṟum paḻimoḻikaḷit tuṇiyākak koṇṭu kāmattip pōkkikkoḷḷa niṉittal, neyyiṉāṉ tī eriyātapaṭi avippatik kūṟum ceytiyi nikarkkum!”  (kuṟaḷ. 1148) eṉṟu tōḻi alaraccam kūṟiya piṉṉum, talivaṉ puṇarcci vēṇṭa, avaṉukku iṭam uṇart taṟkaṇ, “taliva! talivi uṉ coṟkaḷi nampit taṉṉi vaṟpuṟutti nayakkac ceyta eṉcoṟkaḷi ēṟṟu, ñāḻal maraccōliyil niṉṉiṭattil taṉ putumi nalatti iḻantu ippoḻutu taṉimittuṉpattiluḷḷāḷ.  nilāp pōṉṟa maṇaṟparappum iruḷ pōṉṟa kaṭal nīrum ceṟinta neytaṟ parappil paṉi mikka em ciṟiya nallūr atō kāṭci yaḷikkiṟatu.  emmi maṟavātu niṉittu aṭikkaṭi emmi nāṭi varutali vēṇṭukiṟōm”  (kuṟun. 81) eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟal.
“taliva! em ūr arukil poyki uḷḷatu. poyki yarukē kāṭṭāṟu uḷḷatu. cōliyil, iritērum nāri kaḷit tavira vēṟu yārum irār.  cōlippakkam yāṅkaḷ em kūntalil aṇintu koḷḷac ceṅkaḻunīr maṇa malarkaḷip paṟittuvarac celvōm. aṅkut taliviyum varuvāḷ” (kuṟun. 113) eṉṟāṟ pōlat tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟip pakaṟkuṟi nērtal.
“talivi!  nam talivaṉ kaḷiṟu puliyit tākkit taṉ valimi cōrum malippakka vaḻiyē naḷḷiravil nam poruṭṭu varuvāṉ.  avaṉ varukiyāṉ namakku uṇṭākum paḻikku nām nāṇōm”  (kuṟun. 88) eṉṟu iravukkuṟi paṟṟit tōḻi taliviyiṭam kūṟutal.  	(to. po. 112 iḷa.)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttil, ittōḻi kūṟṟiṉkaṇ, talivaṉ pakaṟkuṟiyiyum iravukkuṟiyiyum virumpik kūṟumiṭattē, patiṉintu tiṟappaṭuvaṉa mēlum uḷa. avaṟṟi uriya talippil kāṇalām.  	(114 nac.)
tōḻi, ‘puṇarntapiṉ avaṉvayiṉ vaṇaṅkaṟkaṇ’ kūṟal -
talivaṉ taliviyik kūṭiya piṉ, muṉ taṉṉip paṇintu niṉṟavaṉit tōḻi tāṉē paṇintu oḻukumvaḻik kūṟutal.
“taliva! nī iṅku vantu eṉ talivi tiṉiyiṟ kiḷikaḷik kaṭitali maṟakkumāṟu ceytuviṭṭāy.  iṉi nī nīṇṭa kālam ivaḷi niṉ maṉattiṟ koṇṭu tuṉpuṟātu pātukāttal vēṇṭum. ivaḷi nī ciṉṉāḷ piriyiṉ ivaḷ aḻaku aḻintuviṭum. nī attakiya koṭumiyuṭiyāy alli.  āyiṉum, virintu cellum kutiriyi iṉṉum virivākac celuttum tāṟṟukkōl pōla eṉ coṟkaḷik koḷ.”  	(kali. 50)
“ivaḷ uṉ coṟkaḷi nampi uṉṉit taṉṉoṭu kūṭṭuvitta eṉ coṟkaḷil vitta nampikkiyoṭu taṉ putiya peṇṇalatti niṉṉiṭam iḻantataṉāṉ uṇṭākiya taṉimiyiyuṭiyaḷ. nilaviyum iruḷiyum pōla, maṇal paranta kaṭaṟkaric cōliyum kaṭalum cārnta iṭat til paṉikaḷ uyarntuḷḷa eṅkaḷ ūr atō irukkiṟatu.  eṅkaḷi orupōtum maṟavātu nī niṉital vēṇṭum!”  (kuṟun.81) eṉavarum tōḻi kūṟṟukkaḷ.  	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, puṇarntuṭaṉ pōka niṉitta talimakaḷ oḻiyap pōkaluṟṟa talivaṟkuc colliyatu -
taliviyip pirintu taṉiyē cella virumpiya talivaṉ, “uppu vāṇikar taṅkic cellum pāḻtta ūr pōṉṟu polivaṟṟu ōmimaraṅkaḷ vaḷarntu kāṇappaṭum koṭiya pālinilam ivaḷi uṭaṉkoṇṭu pōtaṟku ēṟṟataṉṟu; mikka iṉṉāmiyuṭiyatu” eṉṟu kūṟak kēṭṭa tōḻi, “aiya! talivarip pirinta makaḷirkku illamtāṉum iṉimi yuṭiyatō?” eṉṟu viṉavumukattāṉ, taliviyi yum uṭaṉkoṇṭu cellumāṟu talivaṉik kuṟippāṉ vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.  	(kuṟun. 124)
tōḻi,‘ puṇarntuḻi uṇarnta aṟimaṭac ciṟappiṉ’ kaṇ kūṟal -
talivaṉoṭu talivi kūṭiya pakaṟkuṟi iravukuṟikkaṇ allakuṟippaṭutalākiya aṟivu maṭampaṭṭa ciṟappiṉkaṇ tōḻi kūṟutal.  	(to. po. 112 iḷa)
“talivaṉ teḷiyāk kuṟiceytāṉ” (ain. aim. 49) eṉavum,  “cērppaṉ ākiya avaṉ tēril pūṭṭiya kutirikaḷiṉ maṇi aravam kēṭpatākak karutit talivi iravukkuṟiyiṭam ceṉṟu atu paṟavikaḷiṉ oli eṉpati aṟintu mīṇṭāḷ”  (ain. eḻu. 59) eṉavum, “tōḻi! nam maṉik kolliyil namakku niḻalāka irukkum puṉṉimarattiṉ kāykaḷ peruṅkāṟṟaṭittaliṉāṉ poykinīril viḻukiṉṟa oliyō, vēṟṟoliyō, kēṭkiṉṟa oli yātu eṉak kūṟu” eṉṟu talivi iravukkuṟi mayaṅkiṉāḷ eṉavum kūṟutal. (iḷa.)
iyaṟkip puṇarcci nikaḻnta kālattu avaṉ tīṅku uṇarātu avaṉi nallaṉāka uṇarnta aṟiviṉatu maṭappam kūṟit tam kātaṟciṟappu urittaviṭattut tōḻi kūṟutal. 	 (114 nac.)
“yām nummoṭu makiḻntu ciritta tūyaveḷḷiya paṟkaḷ, pālinilattē cellum yāṉiyiṉuṭiya, maliyik kuttiya kompu pōla virivāka muṟivaṉa ākuka! em uyir, pāṇartām piṭitta paccimīṉip peyta maṇṭiyip pōla, emakkum periya veṟuppit taruvatāki ummiyum yām peṟa muṭiyāta niliyil iruppati viṭa aḻintupaṭuka!” (kuṟun. 169) eṉṟāṟ pōlat toṭakkattil avaṉi uḷḷavāṟu uṇarāta tam aṟiyāmikku varuntik kūṟu miṭattē, avaṉoṭu nakutaṟkut tōṉṟiya uṇarvu iṉṟiyamiyāmi kūṟit tam kātaṟ ciṟappurittal. 	(nac.)
tōḻi, ‘puṟampaṭu viḷiyāṭṭup pulliya pukaṟcikkaṇ’ kūṟal -
parattiyariṭattē uṇṭākum āṟum kuḷaṉum kāvum āṭip pati ikantu iṉpuṟṟu varum viḷiyāṭṭit talivaṉ poruntiya maṉamakiḻccikkaṇ tōḻi kūṟal 			(to. po. 148 iḷa.)
“kāliyil eḻuntatum, tēri alaṅkarittup paratti yarit tērēṟṟivarac cellum talivaṉ avarkaḷoṭu kūṭiya viḷakkattoṭu mīṇṭu varukiṟāṉē eṉṟu putalvaṟ payanta talivi varuntukiṟāḷ. naṟkuṭiyiṟ piṟantār mikavum poṟumiyik kikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ ik kuṭippiṟappu mikavum varuttukiṟatu.”  (kuṟun. 45) eṉṟu vāyil nērum tōḻi talivaṟkuk kūṟal.  	(iḷa.)
parattiyariṭattē uḷavām viḷiyāṭṭiṉit talivaṉ poruntiya maṉamakiḻccikkaṇ tōḻi kūṟal.  	(150 nac.)
viḷiyāṭṭāvatu pati ikantu yāṟum kuḷaṉum kāvum āṭi nukartal.
“taliva! viyittuṟiyilē tiru marutaccōliyilē nī paratti oruttiyoṭu maṇam ceytaṉi eṉṟu kūṟu kiṉṟaṉar.  appaḻimoḻi neṭuñceḻiyaṉ taliyālaṅ kāṉattil eḻuvar maṉṉari veṉṟapōtu avaṉ vīrar iṭṭa āravārattiṉum mikutiyāka uḷḷatu!”  (akanā. 36) eṉṟu talivaṉ parattami ūr aṟiya veḷippaṭṭa miyit tōḻi cuṭṭutal 	(nac.)
tōḻi, puṉam kiviṭṭup pōkiṉṟuḻic ciṟippuṟamākat talivaṟkuk kūṟiyatu -
“puṉṉimaraṅkaḷ aṭarnta cōliyē! peṇ aṉṉaṅ kaḷē! putaril viḷiyāṭum māṉiṉaṅkaḷē! neṭuṅkaṭalē!  uṅkaḷik kiviṭṭup pōkum eṅkaḷi maṟavātu maṉattuk koḷḷumiṉ” eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘kāppiṉ kaṭumi kiyaṟa varutal’ eṉpataṉkaṇ nikaḻva toru kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, pūttaru puṇarcciyāṉ aṟattoṭu niṟṟal -
ceviliyāṉ viṉavappaṭṭa tōḻi, talivikkum talivaṟ kum pūviṉ toṭarpāṉ kūṭṭam nērntatu eṉṟu kūṟutal.
“aṉṉāy!  yāṉum niṉmakaḷum cōliyil viḷiyāṭiya oru nāḷ murukapperumāṉ pōṉṟa talivaṉ oruvaṉ vantu ‘uṉ kaṇkaḷik kaṇṭu añcit taṇṇīril kiṭakkum nīlappūvum nīyum naṇpar ākuka’ eṉṟu kūṟit talivikku oru nīlappūk koṭuttuc ceṉṟāṉ. pūt tanta avaṉiyē talivi taṉ maṉattil karutikkoṇṭirukkiṟāḷ” (ampikā. 365) eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘aṟattoṭu niṟṟal’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(na. a. 177)
tōḻi ‘periyōr oḻukkam periteṉak kiḷantu peṟutaki illāp piḻippiṉkaṇ’ kūṟal -
cāṉṟōruṭiya oḻukkam ciṟappiṟṟāka irukkum eṉṟu kūṟit taliviyi naṉmuṟiyil vāḻvikkum ciṟappu illāta talivaṉ tavaṟu kaṇṭu tōḻi kūṟutal. (to.po. 148 iḷa.)
“ūra! itaṟku muṉ poruntāta ceytikaḷi virumpāta uḷḷam ippoḻutu virumpumāyiṉum, tām peṟṟa nallupatēcaṅkaḷi niṉittup pārttu, (il) aṟattiṟkum poruḷiṟkum iḻukku vārātapaṭi ārāyntu tamtakutiyi yum nōkki, ataṟku iḻukku vārāvakiyāṉ amiyiṉ, niṉitta kāriyatti muṭittal periyōr oḻukkam.  attakiya periyōruḷ oruvaṉākiya niṉṉiṭattum poy kalanta coṟkaḷ tōṉṟumāyiṉ ulakattil vāymi eṉpatē maṟintuviṭum!”  (akanā. 286) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.  	(iḷa.)
naṉmakkaḷ illaṟattic ciṟappāka nikaḻttuvar eṉavum, naṉmakkaḷ vāḻkkic ceyti mēmpaṭṭatāyirukkum eṉavum collit tōḻi talivaṉi vaḻipaṭutali nīkki urittaṟkaṇ kūṟuvatu. 	 (150 nac.)
muṉṉar nikaḻnta poyccūḷ paṟṟi, “nummaṉōr māṭṭum iṉṉa poyccūḷ piṟakkumāyiṉ, ivvulakattu meyccūḷ iṉi iṉṟām. ataṉāl periyōrit tamatu oḻukkattāṉ tēruṅkāli ariyatāyirukkiṟatu! ” eṉat talivaṉi nōkkit tōḻi kūṟaliṉ (akanā. 286) avaṉi vaḻipāṭu tappiṉāḷāvāḷ.  	(nac.)
tōḻi, ‘peṟaṟku arumporuḷ muṭitta piṉ vanta, teṟaṟku aru marapiṉ ciṟappiṉkaṇ’ kūṟal -
peṟutaṟkariya perumporuḷākiya vatuviyi muṭitta piṟaku aḻala nōkkutaṟkariya marapu kāraṇattāṉ tali vaṉic ciṟappittuk kūṟumiṭattut tōḻi kūṟutal. aḵtā vatu talivaṉik kaṭintu kūṟa muṭiyāta kāraṇattāl avaṉic ciṟappittut tōḻi kūṟutal.  	(to.po. 148 iḷa.)
āmpaṟpūp paṟippavar nīrvēṭkiyuṟṟōm eṉṟu kūṟit taṭumāṟuvatu pōla, ivaḷik kaṟpukkālattut taḻuvik koṇṭirukkumpōtum āṟṟāmiyuṟṟu naṭuṅkukiṉṟīr.  yām mūṉṟām piṟiyip pōla numakkuk kāṇṭaṟku ariyēmāy irunta kālattil mikunta poṟumiyōṭu iruntīrpōlum!”  (kuṟun. 178) eṉṟu talivimāṭṭut tali vaṉ koṇṭuḷḷa tīrāk kātalit tōḻi ciṟappittal.  (iḷa)
talivaṉum taliviyum tōḻiyum peṟutaṟkariyatu eṉa niṉitta perumporuḷākiya vatuvi vēḷviccaṭaṅkāṉ muṭinta piṉpu tōṉṟiya taṉatu teṟutaṟkariya marapu kāraṇattāṉ talivaṉ taṉṉic ciṟappittuk kūṟu miṭattut tōḻi kūṟutal.	(150 nac.)
talivaṉiyum taliviyiyum vaḻipāṭāṟṟutaliṉ ‘teṟaṟkaru marapiṉ’ eṉṟār. teṟutal-aḻaṉṟu nōkkutal;  ciṟappu-“ivaḷi nī āṟṟuvittaliṉ yāṉ uyir tāṅki ṉēṉ”eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa. tōḻi kūṟṟu: “em perumāṉē aritu āṟṟiya tallatu yāṉ āṟṟuvittatu uṇṭō?” eṉṟāṉum, “niṉ aruḷāṉ ivaḷ āṟṟiyatallatu yāṉ āṟṟuvittatu uṇṭō?” eṉṟāṉum kūṟutal.  	(nac)
“taliva!  nī taliviyi maṇanta piṉṉum, kaḷavuk kālattu niṉpirivukku varuntiyatu pōla ippoḻutum ivaḷ varuntiyataṉāṉ kaṇ nīṇṭu pacantatu. kaḷaviṉkaṇ nīṅkātu aḷiyāniṟpavum ciṟitu keṭṭa aḻakiṉ mikutiyō, kaḷ ḷuṇpārkkuk kaḷ uṇṇāk kālattup piṟanta vēṟupāṭu pōlum kāma vēṟupāṭō, avviraṇṭum allavē.  iḵtu ōr amaḷikkaṇ tuyilap peṟṟum, vētaviti paṟṟik kūṭṭam nikaḻāmiyiṉ, piṟanta mikka vēṟupāṭaṉṟō?  itaṉi ivaḷē āṟṟuvataṉṟi yāṉ āṟṟuvikkumāṟu eṉṉi?”  (naṟ. 35) eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟutal. 	(nac.)
tōḻi,‘pēṇā oḻukkam nāṇiya poruḷiṉkaṇ’ kūṟal (1) -
paratti taliviyip pēṇātu oḻukiya oḻukkattiṟkut talivi nāṇiya poruḷiṉkaṇ tōḻi talivaṟkuk kūṟutal.
“taliva! niṉ paratti maṟintu melleṉa vantu mukamaṉ urikaḷ kūṟit taliviyiṭam, ‘nāṉum niṉ teruvil uḷḷavaḷē;  uṉ maṉippakkattu maṉikkaṇ uḷḷēṉ.  uṉakku nāṉ tōḻiyāvēṉ’ eṉṟu kūṟit taṉ mōtiram aṇinta kiviralkaḷāṉ taṇṇeṉṟu poruntat talivi yiṉ neṟṟiyiyum kūntaliyum taṭavip pakaṟpoḻutil vantu mīṇṭa kāṭciyik kaṇṭu talivi nāṇa, nāṉum nāṇamuṟṟēṉ”  (akanā. 386) eṉṟu paratti oḻukiya oḻukkattiṟkut tām nāṇiya ceytiyit talivaṟkut tōḻi kūṟal.	(to. po. 150 nac)
tōḻi, ‘pēṇā oḻukkam nāṇiya poruḷiṉkaṇ’ kūṟal (2) -
talivaṉatu pēṇāta oḻukkattiṉāṉ talivi nāṇiya nili kaṇṭu tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟutal.  pēṇā oḻukkam - puṟattoḻukkam.
parattiyiṟ pukku mīṇṭa talivaṟku vāyil maṟukkum tōḻi, “māmi niṟattiyuṭiya talivi, naṉku mūṭṭuvāy aminta ceppil cūṭappaṭāmal vikkappaṭṭa malar payaṉaṟṟu vataṅkip pōvatu pōla niṉ pirivāṉ mey vāṭi melinta niliyilum, uṉ puṟattoḻukkamākiya koṭumikku nī nāṇāviṭiṉum tāṉ nāṇit taṉ tuyarkkāraṇatti eṅkaḷiṭamkūṭa maṟippataṉāṉ, ciṟanta kaṟpukkaṭam pūṇṭavaḷāvāḷ.  avaḷi nī tuṟantiruttal takka taṉṟu”  (kuṟun. 9) eṉṟu talivaṉatu pēṇā oḻukkattit talivi nāṇiyatit tōḻi kūṟutal.	(to. po. 150 nac)
tōḻi, ‘pētimiyūṭṭaliṉ’ kaṇ kūṟal -
kuṟi nēriṉum avaḷ aṟivāḷorutti allaḷ eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṟkuk kūṟal; tōḻi tāṉ aṟiyāḷ pōlak kūṟal.	(to. po. 112 iḷa.)
aṅṅaṉam “piṉ varuka” eṉṟuḻi, muṉvantāṉit tōḻi aṟiyāmi ēṟṟik kūṟutalum, taliviyiyum aṅṅaṉam aṟiyāmi ēṟṟik kūṟutalum, taliviyi “iḷiyaḷ viḷivu ilaḷ” eṉpatum ām.	(114. nac)
“takaram vakuḷam mutaliya maṇamalarkaḷiyuṭiya maraṅkaḷi vīṇāṉa putarkaḷi veṭṭuvatip pōla veṭṭi nīkki nilatti uḻutu tiṉi vitikku kuṟamakkaḷiṉ kulamakaḷā kiya talivi piṟar tuṉpam kaṇṭu varuntak kūṭiyavaḷā?”  (tiṇi māli. 24) eṉavum (iḷa.),
“aiya!  tēr ēṟivantu taṉiyē niṉṟu, ‘ippakkam vanta yāṉi yip pārttīrkaḷā? eṉṟu viṉavukiṟīr!  tiṉiyiṟ kiḷikaṭi yum makaḷir yāṉi varum vaḻiyil niṟpārkaḷā?” eṉavum (nac),
“vēṅkippū maṇam kamaḻum maliyil uḷḷa kuṟamakaḷir yām. nīr kūṟum akkaḷiṟu kuruti oḻuka ippuṉam arukil cella villi”  (tiṇimoḻi. 8) eṉavum (nac.),
“eṉ talivi tēṉ kalanta pālik kiṇṇattil koṇṭu kiyil vittirunta aḷavil, pakkattu vēṅki maratti lirunta kuraṅku atik kavarntu pōka, attuyarattāṉ aḻutaḻutu avaḷ kaṇkaḷ maḻinīr paṭṭa nīlamalar pōlāki viṭṭaṉa.  tuyarattāṉ avaḷ vayiṟṟil aṭittuk koḷḷa, avaḷ viralkaḷ kāntaḷmoṭṭup pōlac civantuviṭṭaṉa.  ittakiya pētikkuṇattāḷiṭam num karutti evvāṟu colluvatu?”  (naṟ. 379 iḷa., nac.) eṉavum tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟutal.
tōḻi, ‘maṟittu avaḷ arukat, taṉṉoṭum avaḷoṭum mutal mūṉṟu aḷii (muṉṉam muṉ taḷii)p, piṉṉili nikaḻum palvēṟu maruṅkiṉ’ kūṟal -
taliviyiṭam tōḻi kuṟi nayappikkac ceṉṟavaḻit talivi taṉmaṉattil nikaḻvataṉi maṟittu icivillātavaḷip pōl niṉṟavaḻi, ataṉit tōḻi kuṟippiṉāṉ uṇarntu, maṉammoḻimey mūṉṟum oruṅkē aṉpu tōṉṟak kalantu, taliviyip paṇivoṭu vēṇṭumpōtu kūṟutal.		(to. po. 112 iḷa.)
maṟittal-taṉmaṉattu nikaḻcciyi oḷittal; arukutal-icivillātārip pōṉṟu niṟṟal; muṉṉam muṉ taḷitalāvatu - kūṟṟiṉāṉaṉṟik kuṟippiṉāl uṇartal; piṉṉili nikaḻum palvēṟu maruṅkāvatu-vaḻipāṭukoṇṭu varum kūṟṟu vēṟupāṭu.
eṉavē, “talivikkut taḻiyum kaṇṇiyum koṇṭu oruvaṉ nam puṉattu ayal vārāniṉ ṟāṉ” eṉavum,  “avaṉ eṉmāṭṭu oru kuṟiyuṭiyaṉ pōlum” eṉavum,  “aruḷu vārkku iḵtu iṭam” eṉavum,  “avaṉ kuṟi maṟuppiṉ, ‘maṭal ēṟuval’eṉak kūṟip pōntāṉ;  piṉpu varakkaṇṭi lēṉ” eṉavum, innikaraṉa kūṟutal.	(iḷa.)
iṉi, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urippatu (114) varumāṟu:
tōḻi kuṟi nayappittavaḻi, nāṇattāl talivi uṭaṉ paṭātu niṟka, talivaṉoṭum taliviyoṭum nikaḻnta iyaṟkippuṇarcci mutaliya mūṉṟaṉiyum tāṉ aṟin tatik kuṟippāl uṇartti avaḷip palavāka irantu piṉ niṟkumiṭattut tōḻi kūṟutal.
maṟittavaḷ aruka- nāṇ mikutiyāṉ taṉatu vēṭki maṟitta talivi akkūṟṟiṟku uṭaṉpaṭātu niṟṟalāṉ; taṉṉoṭum avaḷoṭum mutal mūṉṟu aḷii - taliva ṉoṭum taliviyoṭum nikaḻnta iyaṟkip puṇarcci mutaliya mūṉṟaṉiyum tāṉ aṟintami kuṟippāṉ uṇartti; piṉṉili palvēṟu nikaḻum maruṅkiṉum - irantu piṉ niṟṟal palavāy vēṟupaṭṭu naṭakkumiṭattum.
avi “peruntakimiyāṉ oruvaṉ yāṉi mutaliyaṉa viṉāyum, taḻiyum kaṇṇiyum koṇṭum ippuṉattu vārāniṉṟāṉ;  avaṉ eṉmāṭṭup peritum kuṟiyuṭi yaṉ;  avaṉ kuṟi muṭiyāmiyiṉ varuntāniṉṟāṉ;  attaḻi nī ēṟṟal vēṇṭum; akkuṟi muṭittaṟku iḵtiṭam; yāṉ kūṟiyatu koḷḷāyāyiṉ, niṉakkuc ceṟintāruṭaṉ ucāvik kuṟi muṭippāy;  maṟuppiṉ, avaṉ maṭal ēṟuvaṉ, varipāyvaṉ ” eṉavum piṟavāṟ ṟāṉum kūṟit tōḻi taliviyik kuṟi nayappittal.(114 nac.)
“talivi!  nām tiṉippuṉam kāttiruntapōtu, tammāl, ampu eyyappaṭṭa vilaṅku appakkam vantatā eṉṟu viṉavi vantavar nammiṭam kuṟippāka viṉavaluṟṟatu vēṟoṉṟu uṇṭu”	(ain. aim. 14 iḷa. nac.)
“talivi! eṇṇey taṭaviya uḻuttamāviṉit tirittāṟ pōṉṟa vayalikkoṭimikka maliyucciyiṉiṉṟu paṟittut toṭukkappaṭṭa acōkantaḷir āṭi vāṭukiṟatu” (aiṅ. 211) (iḷa).
“talivi!  kāntaṭ pū maṇattoṭu varum vāṭik kāṟṟil koṭicci taṉ kūntalik kāyavikkum malināṭaṉākiya talivaṉ peritum kāmakkāyccalāṉ nōkiṟāṉ.  avaṉik kūṭa virumpuvārkku ituvē takka cantaruppam”	(iḷa.)
“taṉ pēṭṭoṭu viḷiyāṭum naṇṭiṉiyum nōkkik kāṉalil eṉṉiyum nōkkit taṉ uṇarvellām kuṟintu ceṉṟa talivaṉ nili ippoḻutu evvā ṟuḷatō?’ (cilap. kāṉal. 31)	(iḷa., nac.).
“talivi! vēṅkiyi otta āṟṟal mikka talivaṉ tēvi itu eṉṟu vāy tiṟantu collāmal, taṉ niṉippil uṉṉiyē koṇṭu, veyil mikka naṇpakalil niḻalukkāka vēṅkimara niḻalilkūṭac ciṟitunēram niṟkāmal taṭumāṟu kiṟāṉ” (tiṇimāli. 31)	(iḷa.)
“talivi! oru nāḷaṉṟu; irunāḷalla;  pala nāḷum eṉṉi nāṭi vantu paṇivāṉa coṟkaḷip pēci, eṉ neñcu nekiḻumāṟu ceyta talivaṉ, maliyil mutirnta tēṉaṭi taṉpāluḷḷa tēṉi oruvarum koḷḷāmiyāl vīḻntu kaḻital pōlat taṉ coṟkaḷi oruvarum ēlāmiyāṉ ivviṭam viṭṭup pōyviṭṭāṉ. avaṉ namakkup paṟṟuk kōṭu āvāṉ eṉa niṉittēṉ. vēṟṟunāṭṭiṟ peyta maḻinīr kalaṅki varuvatu pōla eṉ neñcam kalaṅki yuḷḷatu” (kuṟun. 176)	(iḷa).
“talivi!  ‘malināṭaṉākiya talivaṉ nammi virumpi nam icivu peṟāmiyāṉ varuntukiṟāṉ’ eṉṟa eṉ kūṟṟi nī ēṟkavilli.  atu kiṭakka. nīyum naṉṟāka niṉintu uṉakku aminta ciṟanta tōḻimāroṭum kalantu eṇṇi aṟiya vēṇṭuvataṉi aṟintu ceyaṟpaṭu. avaṉi maṟuttal eḷiya ceyalaṉṟu. ārāyntu piṉṉar naṭpuc ceytal takkatē yaṉṟi naṭpuc ceytu piṉṉar ārāytal takkataṉṟu.” (naṟ.32) 		(iḷa., nac.)
“talivi! tāḻi niṟinta kaṭaṟkaric cōliyil taṉ kuṟi itu eṉṟu veḷippaṭiyākak kūṟāmal, kāliyum māliyum vantu niṉṟa talivaṉ tāṉpuṉinta taḻiāṭi yiyum muṭimāliyiyum iṅkēyē vittuviṭṭuc ceṉṟuviṭṭāṉ.  avaṉ ippoḻutu evvāṟu uḷḷā ṉō?”	(nac.)
“nam āyattār aṟiyumpaṭi talivaṉ tanta taḻiyāṭiyi muṉṉar maṟukkātu peṟṟukkoṇṭa nām, ippoḻutu avaṉnaṭpi ēṟṟuk koḷvatu nam paḻiya kuṭikkup paḻiyākum eṉṟu avaṉi maṟuppatu yāṅkaṉam iyalum?” (nac.)
“talivaṉ teyvam taṅkum maliyiṉiṉṟu paṟittu varuvataṟku mikavum iṭukkaṇ tarum ariya taḻiyip paṟittu vantu taḻiyāṭiyākkit tantuḷḷāṉ. ataṉi uṭup pōm eṉiṉ tāy putumi kaṇṭu vekuḷuvāḷē eṉṟu añcukiṟōm;  mīḷak koṭuttu viṭuvōm eṉṉil, tali vaṉ āṟṟāṉāvāṉē eṉṟu añcukiṟōm.  atteyva malittaḻi vāṭalāmā?  vāṭaviṭiṉ teyvam veku ḷātā?” (naṟ. 359)	(nac.)
“tōḻi! talivaṉ emmōṭu orunāḷ kiḷikaḷi veruṭ ṭiṉavaṉāyt taṅkit taṉ maṉakkarutti veḷiyiṭa vāyppu illāmal pōyviṭṭāṉ.  avaṉ pōya ceyti paṟṟi nāṉ kavalippaṭavilli. tēṉ nukarum vēṭkiyāṉ nalla malar alla malar eṉṟu pakuttup pārātu tēṉ ūtutal oṉṟiyē karutiyirukkum vaṇṭu pōṉṟa, attalivaṉatu tōṟṟap poliviṉik kaṇṭu kaḻalum toṭikaḷi mīṇṭum mēlē ceṟittukkoḷḷum eṉ paṇpaṟṟa ceyti eṉakkuk kavali tarukiṟatu” (naṟ. 25)	(nac.)
“talivi! talivaṉ kaṭalip pōṉṟa nam periya kaṟṟattāri ṭiyē maṭalēṟi vantu uṉṉip paṟṟip pāṭumpōtāvatu nāṉ ippoḻutu kūṟiya ceytikaḷi maṟavaṟka.”	(nac.)
iṉṉōraṉṉa kūṟṟukkaḷ.
tōḻi, ‘māṇnalam tā eṉa vakuttaṟkaṇ’ kūṟal -
talivaṉiṭam,  “nī uṉakku urittākak koṇṭa taliviyiṉ mēmpaṭṭa aḻakiṉi mīḷat tantupō” eṉṟu tōḻi avaṉatu puṟattoḻukkam poṟātu kūṟutal.			(to.po. 148 iḷa)
(“tōḻi! nam talivaṉik kaṇṇāl kaṇṭāl, ‘nī emmiṭam kik koṇṭa em māmi aḻakiṉit tiruppit tantu celka’  eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttalām”  (ain. eḻu. 64) eṉṟu talivi kūṟiyati uṭkoṇṭa) tōḻi, talivaṉi nōkki, “cērppa!  nī taliviyik kiviṭṭuviṭṭāy eṉṟu ellōrum veḷippaṭiyākak kūṟum nāḷ virivil varuvatākuka!  avvāṟu ceyvatu uṉakkum uṭaṉpāṭāyiṉ yām uṉṉit taṭukkavilli. nī kavarntu koṇṭa taliviyiṉ aḻakiṉi mīḷat tantu viṭṭu niṉ viruppam pōlap puṟattoḻukkattil pala kālam kaḻippāyāka!”  (kuṟun. 236) eṉṟu kūṟutal. (iḷa.)
“ivaḷ iḻanta māṭcimippaṭṭa nalattit tantu ikappiṉum ikappāyāka”  eṉṟu talivaṉi vēṟupaṭut taṟkaṇ tōḻi kūṟutal.	(150 nac.)
tōḻi, puṟattoḻukkil ceṉṟu vanta talivaṉiṭam, “eluva!  nī yār?  eṅkaḷukkum uṉakkumiṭiyē yātu uṟavu?  emakku nī evvuṟavum uṭiyāyalli.  nī ayalāṉ pōṉṟu emtiṟattu uḷḷāy.  niṉ viruppam pōlavē naṭantu koḷka.  mānti nakar pōṉṟa iyaṟki vaṉappuṭiya ivaḷiṭam niṉakku viruppamilliyātaliṉ, nī kavarnta ivaḷaḻaki mīḷak koṭuttuviṭṭu niṉ viruppam pōl celka!”  (naṟ. 395) eṉṟu kūṟutal.
tōḻi, muṟkālattu varivu kaṭāvumāṟu pōlaṉṟi varivu kaṭātal -
taliviyiṉ tamar talivaṟku makaḷ koṭukka uṭaṉpaṭat tāṉum varivukku uṭaṉpaṭṭa talivaṉ, variviṭi vittup pirintu nīṭṭittuḻi,  “iṅṅaṉam kālatāmatam ceytal kūṭātu” eṉṟu kaṭuñcol kūṟit tōḻi avaṉi varivu kaṭātal.
