“taliva!  ilvāḻkki naṭattutal eṉpatu vaṟiyavarkku avar vēṇṭiya toṉṟi utavutalām;  oṉṟip pātu kāttal eṉpatu kūṭiṉārip piriyātiruttalām;  makkaṭ paṇpu eṉpatu ulaka oḻukkam aṟintu oḻukutalām; aṉpu eṉpatu taṉ cuṟṟattāri vekuṇṭu nīṅkātiruttal; aṟivu eṉpatu aṟiyātār taṉṉip pārttuc collum collip poṟuttal;  ceṟivu eṉpatu kūṟiyati maṟātu ceytal; niṟi eṉpatu irakaciyattip piṟar aṟiyātavāṟu pātukāttal;  muṟi eṉpatu namar eṉak kaṇṇōṭātu avar ceyta kuṟṟattukku ēṟpa avarit taṇṭittal; poṟi eṉpatu pakivariyum kālam varum vari poṟut tiruttal.  ittakiya aruṅkuṇaṅkaḷi aṟintoḻukum  nī taliviyiṉ nalaṉi uṇṭu avaḷit tuṟantiruttal, iṉiya pālip parukiyavar atu vikkappaṭṭirunta kalattik kaviḻttu viṭuvatu pōlvatām.  ataṉāl, niṉṉāl varuttappaṭuttappaṭṭirukkum talivi tuyar tīra, avaḷi virintu varintukoḷḷa āvaṉa cūḻntu, kutiri pūṭṭiya tēril puṟappaṭuka!”  (kali.133) eṉṟu tōḻi taliva ṉiṭam virivāka nīti kūṟi varivu kaṭāyavāṟu.
itu ‘varivu uṭaṉpaṭṭōṟ kaṭāval vēṇṭiṉum, eṉṟa taṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, muṉpu talivikku nikaḻnta āṟṟāmiyum atu kaṇṭu tāṉ kalaṅkiyavāṟum talivaṟkuk kūṟal -
“taliva!  maḻi peyyak kāṉam taḻippa, uḻavar payir viṉi toṭaṅka, māṉkaḷ pul mēyntu makiḻvoṭu viḷiyāṭum kārkālattum nī mīṇṭu varavilliyeṉil, nili yātāyirukku meṉṟu talivi varuntip pāṇaṉiṭam kūṟi, ‘uṉ eṭutta kāriyam muṭiyāmiyāl nī varuntu kiṉṟāyō?’ eṉṟu ēṅkiyirunta niliyil, avaḷ taṉimit tuyaram tīra vanta nī nīṭu vāḻka!  taliviyum mullip pūkkaḷic cūṭi niṉ malarnta mārpil maṉṉuka!”  (akanā. 314) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.			(to. po. 150 nac.)
tōḻi, ‘muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci muṟi niṟuttu urittal’ -
muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci muṟiyē niṟuttuk kūṟutal, niṟuttuk kūṟalāvatu nīṅkaviṭātu uṭaṉpaṭṭuk kūṟal; “iṉṉum muṉpu kūṭiṉāṟ pōlak kūṭa amiyum” eṉṟu kūṟutal			(to. po. 112 iḷa.)
taliviyi aṭiyat tōḻiyi vēṇṭi vanta taliva ṉiṭam, tōḻi, muṉpu kūṭiṉāṟ pōlavē aṉṟum taliviyi avaṉ kūṭalām eṉṟu avaṉi nīṅkaviṭātu uṭaṉpaṭṭuk kūṟutal eṉṟavāṟu.
tāṉ pētimi ūṭṭiyavaḻi, “ivaḷ ikkuṟi muṭippaḷ eṉṟu irantu oḻukiya eṉakku ivaḷ puṇarcci aṟintilaḷ pōl kūṟiṉāḷ” eṉṟu āṟṟāṉ āya talivaṉit tōḻi, “yāṉ appuṇarcci nikaḻntataṉi aṟival” eṉṟu kūṟi varuttam tīrttal.	(114 nac)
“taliva! talivi eṉakku uyir pōṉṟavaḷ;  taṉ maṉattiṉkaṇ paṭṭa mikka tuṉpatti avaḷ veḷiyiṭātu aṭakki vittuḷḷāḷ. anniliyil ‘talivi icciṟu vayatilēyē mūtaṟivu paṭittu viṭṭāḷ’ eṉṟu uṉ etir kūṟiṉēṉāyiṉ, nī ceṉṟu avaṭku urittiyāyiṉ, aḵtu avaṭkup perunāṇam tarum eṉṟu yāṉ aṟinta ceytiyi uṉṉiṭam kūṟa añcuval ” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟil, avaḷ puṇarcci nikaḻntataṉi aṟinta tiṟam peṟappaṭut tappaṭṭatu.	(nac.)
tōḻi ‘meyyiṉum poyyiṉum vaḻinili piḻiyātu palvēṟu kavar poruḷ nāṭṭattiṉkaṇ’ kūṟal -
meyyiṉālum poyyiṉālum talivi kulattiluḷḷōr perumikku iḻukku vārātu, palavāki vēṟupaṭṭa kavartta poruṇmiyiyuṭiya coṟkaḷik kūṟit (taliviyatu vēṟupaṭṭa nilikkuriya kāraṇattit) tōḻi ārāytal.
nāṟṟam mutaliya ēḻāṉum talivikkup puṇarcciyuṇmiyi aṟinta piṉṉar, tōḻi taliviyuṭaṉ ārāyuṅkālattu, nikaḻntavaṟṟi maṟittuk kūṟa vēṇṭutaliṉ, uṇmip poruḷāṉum poypporuḷāṉum virāvi variṉum, avaṭkuk kuṟṟēval ceyyum taṉmiyiṉ tappātavāṟāka vēṟu pal kavarporuḷpaṭak kūṟi ārāytal. (kavarporuḷ-vēṟupaṭṭa poruḷpaṭum taṉmi) nāṇāṉ talivi iṟantupaṭāmal kūṟutaṟku ‘vaḻinili piḻiyātu’ eṉṟatu.
“talivi!  nām muṉpellām piṟiyit toḻuvōmē!  appiṟi iṉṟum tōṉṟiyuḷḷatu. atu cōḻaṉ aṇikalaṉ pōla oḷi vīcukiṟatu.  ataṉit toḻalām!”  eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟat talivi toḻātatu.
itaṉkaṇ, muṉ kūṟiyavāṟu pōlak kūṟutal mey;  talivi toḻāaḷ eṉṟu aṟintum, toḻavēṇṭuvatu pōlak kūṟutal poy.  vaḻakkam pōlap paṇivākak kūṟaliṉ vaḻinili piḻiyātu, kavar poruḷāyiṟṟu.
“nam tiṉikkolliyil pakalil yāṉ kaṇṭa kaḷiṟu ippoḻutu utiram tōynta kōṭṭoṭu celkiṟatu”  (ciṟṟeṭṭakam) eṉṟu talivi naṭuṅkumāṟu tōḻi kūṟutal kūṭātu.
“talivi!  toyyil eḻutiya uṉ nakilkaḷum tōḷkaḷum putu aḻaku peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  uṉ kūntalilum teyvamaṇam kamaḻkiṟatu.  ivi eppaṭi nikaḻntaṉa eṉpatu eṉakku aiya māka uḷḷatu”  eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟiyavaḻi, talivi,  “cuṉi yāṭiṉēṟku iṅṅaṉam āyiṟṟu”  eṉṟāḷāka, tōḻi,
“yāṉiyāṉ utittut taḷḷappaṭṭa cantaṉamaram viḻuntu kiṭakkum āḻamāṉa cuṉiyil uṉṉip pōlak kuḷittata ṉāṉ āṇmayil pōṉṟu kaṇkavar aḻakiṉip peṟalām eṉil, accuṉikkaṇ yāṉum āṭi avvaḻaku peṟu māṟṟik kāṇpēṉ!”  eṉavum,
“nēṟṟuc celvaṉākiya talivaṉ taṉ perumikku ēlāp paṇivuṭaṉ eṉṉiṭam vantu, yāṉ avaṉi varuttiṉē ṉākak kūṟi eṉ mutukiṉit taḻuva, eṉmaṉam avaṉiṭam nekiḻntuviṭṭatu.  āyiṉ, nāṉ maṉatti uṟuti ceytu koṇṭu karuñcoṟ kūṟi avaṉatu piṇippiṉiṉṟu vilaki, añciya peṇmāṉ pōla akaṉṟu niṟpa, eṉṉiṭam pēca maṉavalimi illāmal piṭiyip pirinta kaḷiṟṟip pōla nēṟṟu varuntic ceṉṟavaṉ iṉṟum nammiṭam pēcit tōṟṟuc celvāṉ.  nam tōḷ avaṉukkē urimi eṉpati aṟiyāmal paṇivuṭaṉ nammi vēṇṭi niṟkum avaṉi ēḷaṉam ceyyac celvōm” (akanā. 32) eṉavum,
“talivi! nammalippakutiyil kūṭṭilirukkum mayilkaḷ, tām tiṉikkatirkaḷik kavarvatik kaṇṭukoṇṭirup pati aṟiyātu, kiḷikaḷ oruvarkkum teriyāmal tām akkatirkaḷi uṇpatākak karutukiṉṟaṉa. atu kiṭakka.  nī tiṉiyiṟ kiḷikaḷik kaṭiya āvaṉa ceyyāviṭiṉum, ayalār varakkūṭiya ivviṭattil aḻāmal iru. aḻutata ṉāṉ uṉ kaṇkaḷ kuruti tōynta ampukaḷ pōlac civantuviṭṭaṉa.” (naṟ.13)  eṉṟāṟ pōlat tāṉ ōraḷavu taliviyatu ceykiyi aṟintuḷḷamiyik kuṟippāṉ aṟivuṟuttit tōḻi avaḷuṭaṉ uriyāṭal. 			(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi,  “yāṉ variyuntuṇiyum āṟṟuvaḷō?”  eṉṟāṟku, āṟṟāḷ eṉṟatu -
“peruṅkal nāṭa!  uṉṉi virumpi niṟkum taliviyiṉ kaṇkaḷ uṉ pirivu āṟṟāmiyāṉ uṟakkatti nīttuk kaṇṇīri ukuttapaṭiyē uḷḷaṉa.  avaḷ aṇintirukkum caṅkuvaḷiyalkaḷum uṭalmelivāṉ nekiḻat toṭaṅki viṭṭaṉa”  (*kuṟun. 365) eṉat tōḻi taliviyatu āṟṟāmi yik kūṟutal.
itu ‘piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.			(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi,  “yāṉ varivukku āvaṉa ceyyavō?” eṉat talivaṉi viṉātal -
“malināṭa! nī ivaḷi virivil varintukoḷḷa niṉ maṉattu muṭivu ceytuviṭṭāl, vēṅkiyum palavum poruntiya ivvūril ittaliviyiṉ uṟaviṉariṭam uṉtamarik koṇṭu nī maṇam pēcuvataṉ muṉṉar, oru peṇṇākiya yāṉ muṉ kūṭṭi maṇam muṭippataṟku āvaṉa ceytaṟkaṇ uṉakkuk karuttu vēṟupāṭu illiyeṉil, ceytuvippēṉ” eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟutal.
iḵtu ‘aṉinilivaki’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻiyiṉ urimi -
oruvarporuḷukku maṟṟavar urimi koṇṭāṭa nāṉku vaḻikaḷ uḷa. avi 1. peṟṟōruṭiya poruḷkaḷāy makkaḷāṉ aṭiyappaṭuvaṉa,  2.  piṟarukkuk koṭuppa avar pāṟ celvaṉa,  3.  vaḷarppup piḷḷikku urimiyāy vantu cērvaṉa,  4. pakivarāṉ kavarntukoḷḷappaṭuvaṉa - eṉpaṉa.
innāṉku vakiyāṉum oruvar poruḷ maṟṟavaruṭiya uṭimiyām.  innāṉkiṉāṉum oruvaruṭiya uṟuppuk kaḷi maṟṟavar tammuṭiyaṉa eṉṟu urimi pārāṭṭal iyalātu, āyiṉum, taliviyoṭu koṇṭa uyariya naṭpuk kāraṇamākat tōḻi taliviuṟuppukkaḷit taṉ uṟuppukkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟikkoḷḷum urimiyuṭiyaḷ.			(to. po. 102 kuḻa.)
tōḻiyiṉ eṇvaki ārāycci -
talivaṉoṭu talivikkut toṭarpuṇṭākiya ceyti yit tōḻi taliviyiṉuṭiya nāṟṟam, tōṟṟam, oḻukkam, uṇṭi, ceyviṉi maṟippu, celavu, payilvu, meyp poruḷāṉum poypporuḷāṉum taṉ kuṟṟēval nilikku ēṟpap pala vakip putiporuḷpaṭak kūṟi ārāy tal eṉṟa eṇvaki ārāycciyāṉum muṭivu ceyvāḷ. ‘tōḻi, nāṟṟamum...... kūṟal’ - kāṇka.			(to. po. 147 kuḻa. uri)
“tōḻiyik kāṭṭu” eṉṟal -
talivaṉ, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattu iṟutiyil taṉ kaḷavoḻuk kattit tōḻiyiṉ tuṇiyāl toṭarak karuti, talivi yiṭam avaḷuṭiya uyirttōḻiyit taṉakkuk kuṟippākak kāṭṭumāṟu vēṇṭutal.
“intaḷūraṉākiya tirumāliṉ malimēluḷḷa mayil pōl vāy!  uṉ uyir pōlpavaḷum,  nī iṭṭa ēvali makiḻ voṭu ceytu muṭippavaḷum, mayil pōlum cāyaluṭiya vaḷum ākiya tōḻiyi uṉ tōḻiyarkūṭṭattiṭiyē yāvaḷ eṉṟu yāṉ uṇarumāṟu uṉ kaṇpārviyāl kuṟippākat terivittu eṉakkuk karuṇi ceyvāyāka!” eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
ikkūṟṟuk kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatu. 	(mā. a. pāṭal 640)
tōḻi, ‘vaṇaṅkiyal moḻiyāṉ vaṇaṅkaṟkaṇ’ kūṟal -
kuṟṟēval niliyiṉaḷ kūṟa vēṇṭiya niliyil, taliva ṉiṭam paṇinta moḻikaḷāṉ taṉ karuttiṉit tōḻi eṭuttuk kūṟi vaṇaṅki moḻital.
“nīrttuṟiyil varāliṉam tiriyum ūra!  nam taliviyik kaṭikkaṇiyāmal maṉiyi nīṅki, apparattiyarcēri celvataṉip periya kāriyamākkik koḷvatu niṉ perumikkut takkatā?”	(ain. eḻu. 54)
“makiḻna!  vēḷvit tīyiṉiyum cevvāmpal pūtta vayal kaḷiyu muṭiya vaḷamikka tēṉūr pōṉṟa ivaḷatu nalam taṉittuk kiṭakkumāṟu nī pirintu cellutaṟku, apparatti attakiya vaṉappu mikkavaḷā, eṉṉa! “(aiṅ. 57 ) eṉa varum tōḻi kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 150 nac.)
tōḻi, vatuvi nikaḻāniṉṟuḻit tāykkuk kāṭṭik kūṟal -
“aṉṉāy!  tuṟivaṉākiya talivaṉē nam talivikku ēṟṟavaṉ!  nam taliviyatu māmi aḻakum avaṉukkē ēṟṟatu!”  (aiṅ. 103) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu (pāṅkuṟa vanta) ‘vaki’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 114 nac.) 
tōḻi ‘vanta kiḻavaṉi māyam ceppip, poṟutta kāraṇam kuṟittakāli’ kūṟal -
“ippakkam yāṉi vantatō?  māṉ vantatō?”  eṉṟāṟ pōlap poycceyti kūṟi vanta talivaṉit talivi vekuḷātu poṟuttukkoṇṭa kāraṇattik kuṟitta viṭattut tōḻi kūṟutal.	(to. po. 112 iḷa.)
“vēṅki maṇam kamaḻum maliyiluḷḷa kuṟavarmakaḷirākiya yāṅkaḷ kuruti cōrum kaḷiṟu ippakkam pōṉatākak kāṇavilli”  (tiṇimoḻi.8) eṉavum,
“tēril vantu taṉiyē niṉṟu, ‘yāṉi ippakkam pōn tatik kaṇṭīrō’ eṉṟu viṉavukiṟīr.  tiṉikkolli yil, kiḷikaḷi veruṭṭum makaḷir yāṉi varum vaḻiyil iruppārā?”  eṉavum talimakaṟkut tōḻi maṟumoḻi kūṟutalum,
“ivaḷum tiṉikkāvalukku vantavaḷākat teriyavilli.  ivaṉum māṉvēṭṭikku vantavaṉākat teriyavilli.  iruvar uḷḷattilum maṟintirukkum irakaciyam oṉ ṟuṇṭu.  eṉ kaṇ muṉ nāṇamuṭiyavarāka naṭikkiṟārkaḷ. maṟittuk kaḷḷi yuṇṭavartam kaḷippu avarik kāṇpava rukkup pulaṉāvatu pōl, ivarkaḷ iruvarum kaṇkaḷāl pēcikkoḷḷum ceyti eṉakkup pulaṉākiṟatu” eṉṟu tōḻi taṉṉuḷ karutikkoḷḷutalum ām. 	(iḷa.)
taṉmuṉ vantu niṉṟa talivaṉit tōḻi etirppaṭṭu niṉṟēyum avaṉ nāḷtōṟum tavaṟātu varutalilli eṉṟu avaṉmītu poyyākak kuṟi kūṟi, ataṉip poṟutta kāraṇam kuṟippiṉāṉ koḷḷak kūṟutal. kāraṇamāvatu, “nī ariyi āyiṉ, ivaḷ āṟṟāḷ āvāḷ eṉṟu niṉṉi etirkoḷkiṉṟēm” eṉṟal. (iḵtu avaṉ varavi virumpiyatu;  varivu kaṭāyataṉṟu)	(114 nac.)
tammāṉ iṭiyūṟeyti varuntum talivaṉit tōḻi aṇuki,  “neytal tuṟiva!  orunāḷ eṅkaḷ kaṭaṟkaric cōliyil muttup pōlap puṉṉikaḷ arumpiyirukkum pakutikku vantu, ‘nīṅkaḷ niṟam pacali yākāmal irukkiṉ ṟīrkaḷā?’ eṉṟu eṅkaḷi viṉaviṉāl uṉ perumi kuṟintuviṭumā?”  (akanā. 30) eṉṟāṟ pōla, nāḷum vantu mīḷum talivaṉi vārātāṉ pōlak kūṟutal. 	(nac.)
tōḻi vantu kūṭal -
pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ taliviyi nīṅkit talivaṉ ceṉṟapiṉ, tōḻi, pirivāṟṟāmiyālum tōḻiyik kāṇpataṉkaṇ nāṇiṉālum moṭṭukkaḷit taṭumāṟip paṟittukkoṇ ṭirunta taliviyi aṭintu tāṉ koṇṭuvanta malar kaḷik kāṭṭi, avaḷ moṭṭukkaḷip paṟittali nīkki avaḷoṭu cērntu puṟappaṭutal.
itaṉip ‘pāṅki melliyaṟ cārntu kiyuṟi kāṭṭal’ 
(na. a. 149) eṉṟum kūṟupa.
itu pakaṟkuṟi eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.			(kōvi. 125)
tōḻi, variviṭip pirintōṉ kuṟitta paruvattukku muṉ varukiṉṟami aṟintu talivikkuk kūṟal -
“talivi!  nam maliyiliruntum arittu varum nīriṉāṉ kāntaḷ ceḻittu vaḷaravum, kātalarip pirintōr ceya laṟṟu varuntavum, kuḷircciyoṭu vīcum vāṭikkāṟṟi yuṭiya kuḷirkālam toṭaṅkuvataṟkumuṉ nam talivaṉ variviṭippirintu mīṇṭu vantuviṭṭāṉ”  (aiṅ. 223) eṉṟa tōḻiyiṉatu makiḻccik kūṟṟu.
itu (pāṅkuṟa vanta) ‘vaki’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi,  “varivu nīṭa āṟṟāta taliviyiṉ vēṟupāṭu puṟattārkkup pulaṉākāmiyum iyiyum, iṅṅaṉam kūṟuvārip peṟiṉ” eṉak kūṟi vaṟpuṟuttal -
“nēṟṟut talivaṉmaliyil maḻi peytuḷḷatu. iṉṟu annīr nammūr āṟṟil ōṭi varum.  avaṉ maliyil peyta nīril kuḷittāl, ōraḷavu avaṉip pirinta āṟṟāmiyip pōkkalām.  tāyō, nammi iṟceṟittuḷḷāḷ. ‘ciṟumi yari āyattāroṭu viḷiyāṭātu maṉiyil aṭit tiruttal aṟaṉumaṉṟu; acceyal celvattiyum aḻik kum’ eṉṟu tāymaṉattil paṭiyumāṟu colluvōr iruppiṉ nammi orukāl apputuppuṉalil nīrāṭa aṉuppu talum kūṭum!” (naṟ. 68) eṉṟu tōḻi taliviyiṭam kūṟip pirivuttuyari āṟṟumāṟu vaṟpuṟuttutal.
iḵtu  ‘aṉpu taliyaṭutta vaṉpuṟi’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷ ḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, veṟiyāṭṭiṭattut tamar kēṭpak kūṟal -
“ivveṟiyāṭṭiṟku nam aṉṉitāṉ ēṟpāṭu ceytuḷ ḷāḷ. nam aḻakāṉa illattil paṭimam oṉṟi yamittu vaḻipaṭṭu vēlaṉ, ‘innōy talivikku murukaṉāṉ vantatu’ eṉṟu kūṟumāyiṉ, ammurukaṉ eṉṟa peyar teyvattiṉ peyarā allatu taliviyoṭu toṭarpu koṇṭuḷḷa malināṭṭut talivaṉ peyarā eṉpatu aiyamāka uḷḷatu”  (aiṅ. 247) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘kaḷam peṟak kāṭṭiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, veṟiyāṭṭiṭattut talivikkuk kūṟal -
“talivi! ivvēlaṉ taṉ veṟiyāṭṭi nikaḻttumpōtu, pala malarkaḷum maṇam vīcum malināṭṭukku urimiyuṭiya nam talivaṉatu maliyip pāṭātupōyiṉ, ivveṟi yāṭṭu avaṉ kuṟippiṭum payaṉit tārātē”  (aiṅ. 244) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘kaḷam peṟak kāṭṭiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi veṟiyāṭṭiṭattu murukaṟkuk kūṟal -
“muruka!  malināṭṭut talivaṉāṉ talivikku viḷin tuḷḷa innōy uṉṉāl vantataṉṟu eṉpataṉi naṉkaṟin tum, kaṭampamāliyic cūṭi vēlaṉ ‘veṟiyāṭṭiṭattu nī varutal vēṇṭum’  eṉṟu vēṇṭiyataṉi niṟivēṟṟa, iv veṟiyāṭukaḷattu vanta nī teyvamāka iruppiṉum, orutaliyāka aṟiyāmiyuṭiyāy āyiṉi!”  (naṟ. 34) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘kaḷam peṟak kāṭṭiṉum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 114 nac)
tōḻi, veṟiyāṭṭiṭattu vēlaṟkuk kūṟal -
“veṟi aṟi vēla!  periya nīrttuṟikkaṇ ittaliviyik kaṇṭu ivaḷ varuntumāṟu ceytavaṉ kaṭavuḷākiya murukaṉ allaṉ; neytalum ceruntiyum toṭuttuk kaṭṭiya naṟu maṇa māliyi mārpil aṇinta āṇmakaṉ oruvaṉē!”  (aiṅ. 182) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu  ‘kaḷampeṟak kāṭṭiṉum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷap paṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 114 nac.)
tōḻi, veṟiyeṉa aṉṉi mayaṅkiyami kūṟal -
tōḻi, taliviyiṭam avaḷatu uṭalmelivip pōkka murukaṉukku veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻttutalē takkatu eṉṟu aṉṉi niṉippatik kūṟutal.
“tōḻi! nam kaṇ pacali pāyumāṟu talivaṉ ceyta nōyi aṟiyāta tāy, ‘itu murukaṉāṉ viḷintatu’ eṉṟu koṇṭu itaṉip pōkkuvataṟku veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻttu talē takkatu eṉṟu niṉikkiṟāḷ”  (aiṅ. 242) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟil, cevili veṟiyāṭṭeṭukka muyalutal peṟappaṭṭatu.
‘aṇaṅkuṭi neṭuvari’ (aka nā.22) eṉṟa pāṭalil “tali vaṉ mārpiṉāṉ viḷinta nōy itu eṉṟu aṟiyātu taṭumāṟiya cevili, ūriluḷḷa mūtaṟivu vāynta makaḷir, ‘murukaṉukku veṟiyāṭṭeṭuppiṉ niṉmakaḷ nalivu tīrum’ eṉṟu kūṟiyatu kēṭṭu, āṭukaḷaṉ amittu mālikaḷ cūṭṭi murukaṉip paṟṟip pāṭip pali koṭuttut tiṉiyik kurutiyoṭu tūvi murukaṉi vaḻippaṭutti veṟiyāṭṭayarntāḷ” (akanā. 22) eṉṟu talivi tōḻi yiṭam cevili veṟiyāṭṭeṭuttami kūṟiṉāḷ.
itu ‘kaṭṭiṉum kaḻaṅkiṉum  . . . . . . .  ceytikkaṇ’ tiṟam eṉ ṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 115 nac.)
tōḻi, vēlaṉ kaḻaṅku pārttami kūṟal -
“muṟattil putumaṇalip parappi, nām ‘ivaṉ uṇmi kūṟāṉ’ eṉṟu pēcuvataṉāl viḷinta kōpattiyuṭiya vēlaṉ, ‘ivaḷukku nōyvanta tiṟaṉ kaḻaṅkiṉāṉ aṟiyap paṭukiṟatu’ eṉṟu kūṟuvataṉic cevili uṭkoṇṭāḷ”  (aiṅ. 248) eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟutal.
itu ‘kaṭṭiṉum kaḻaṅkiṉum . . . .   ceytikkaṇ’ ‘tiṟam’ eṉṟata ṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 115 nac.)
tōḻi, ‘vēḷāṇ peruneṟi vēṇṭiya iṭattu’k kūṟal -
vēḷāṇmi - upakāram. talivaṉit tam illattil viruntāka ēṟṟukkōṭaṟkut tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟal 			(to. po. 112 iḷa.)
“ñāyiṟu maṟintu viṭṭatu. puḷḷellām kuṭampi nōkki uṭaṅku peyarntuviṭṭaṉa. nī piriyappōkiṟāy eṉpataṉi uṭkoṇṭu taliviyum aḻat toṭaṅki viṭṭāḷ.  uṉ kōvēṟukaḻuti uppaṅkaḻikaḷik kaṭantu varumpōtu, ataṉ kālkaḷ cuṟāmīṉāl tākkappaṭavē puṇpaṭṭataṉāl, mīṇṭum naṭantu cella valimiyaṟṟiruk kiṟatu.  uṉ iḷiyarum nīyum ippoḻutē mīṇṭu cellā mal paṉikaḷ ōṅkiya maṉikkollikaḷiyuṭiya em neytal nāṭṭil iṉṟu iravu taṅkic celvatāl uṉakku ētēṉum kāriyam keṭṭuppōy viṭumā?”  (aka nā. 120) eṉavum,
“iṉṟu em ūril taṅkic celvatāyiṉ yāṅkaḷum eṅkaḷ takuti ēṟpa uṅkaḷi viruntiṉarāka ēṟṟu upacarippōm.  ikkaruttu umakku uṭaṉpāṭutāṉā?” (akanā. 200) eṉavum, talivaṉi upacarippataṟkāka aṉṟu iravu taṅkaḷ ūril taṅkumāṟu avaṉi vēṇṭutal. 	(iḷa.)
talivaṟkut tām cila koṭuttalit talivi vēṇṭiya iṭattut tōḻi kūṟal;  atu talivi ‘vēḷāṇ etirum viruntiṉkaṇ ’  (to. po. 107)  tōḻi kūṟal.	(114 nac.)
“pulampa!  pala nāḷāka nī pakaṟpoḻutil vanta puṉṉic cōliyiṉ niḻalil poḻutukaḻittu māliyil tēr ēṟi ūrukkuc ceṉṟuviṭukiṟāy.  nī cellum vaḻiyō, it talivi niṉittu niṉittut taṉ aḻaku aḻiyumāṟu veḷḷakkāṭāka uḷḷatu.  poḻutum iruṇṭuviṭṭatu.  veḷḷattil koṭiya pāmpukaḷum cuṟākkaḷum tiritarum.  inta vaḻiyil innērattil talivi varuntumāṟu tirumpic cellutal vēṇṭā.  iṉṟu iravu emmillattil taṅkic celvataṉāl uṉakkuk kuṟi nērātu. mīṉi viṟṟup paṇṭamāṟṟākap peṟṟa veṇṇelliṉ māviṉit tayiriṭṭu amitta kūḻiṉi niṉkutiri uṇṇuka.  uṉakkup pūcikkoḷḷap potikimalic cantaṉam arittut tarukiṟōm.”	(akanā. 340)
“taliva!  mīṉ uṇakkum tuṟiyil puḷ ōppiya eṅkaḷoṭu pakaṟpoḻutu taṅki, māliyil taliviyiṉ vaḷiyal kaḷit tirutti mayirmuṭiyik kōti, ‘niṉ tōḻiyōṭu illattiṟku ēkuka’ eṉṟu kūṟi nī puṟappaṭa āyattam ceyta aḷavil, talivi peritum varuntukiṟāḷ.  ataṉi niṉit tup pārāmal, nī ayalār pōlap pirintu celvatāl ivaḷukku ētēṉum tīṅku viḷiyumō eṉa añcukiṟēṉ.  uṉakku ivaḷiṭam uṇmiyāṉa viruppam uṇṭāyiṉ, nī neṭuntoliviṉiṉṟu varuvārip pōla niṉiyātu emmūrukku vantāl, uṉṉiyum iḷiyariyum etir koṇṭu iṉcollāṉ viṉavi, ‘poḻutu maṅki viṭṭatu;  alikaḷ perukiviṭṭaṉa; cuṟāviṉ īṭṭam vantuviṭṭatu.  nī ūrukkat tirumpip pōka vēṇṭā’ eṉṟu emar vēṇṭa, uṉṉi iḷiyaroṭu taṅkac ceytu em illattil uṉṉiyum iḷiyariyum kutiriyoṭu viruntāka ēṟṟup pēṇuvōm”  (akanā. 300) eṉṟu talivaṉiṭam taliviyatu tuyar kūṟit tōḻi avaṉi viruntāka ēṟṟavāṟu.	(nac.)
tōṟṟam -
1.  uṟuppu;  atu kaṭpulaṉātaliṉ ‘tōṟṟam’ eṉṟār.  ‘emmeṉa varūum kiḻamit tōṟṟam’( to. po. 221 nac)
2.  nāṭakap piravēcam. 	(ṭu)
tōṉṟal cūḷ collal -
talivaṉ ūṭiya taliviyiṭam tāṉ parattiyar yāriyum aṟiyātavaṉ eṉpataṉi periyavarkaḷmēl āṇi kūṟi uṟutippaṭuttutal.	(pari. 8. 52)
na
nakar kāṭṭal
uṭaṉpōkkil taliviyi aḻittuc ceṉṟa talivaṉ, avaḷi vaḻiyiṭi varuttam tīra niḻaṟpakutikaḷil taṅkac ceytu, maṇaṟ pakutikaḷil viḷiyāṭacceytu iṉpuṟut tik koṇṭu pōy,  “periya māḷikikaḷmēl koṭikaḷ aciya, matil tōṉṟukiṉṟa apperiya nakarē nammuṭiya ūrākum” eṉṟu talivikkut taṉatu ūri aṇimikkaṇ kāṭṭiyatu.  
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉum tokutikaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu 		(kōvi. 222).
nakaraṇimi kūṟal -
talivaṉum taliviyum uṭaṉpōkkil iṉpuṟṟuc cella etirvaruvōr, “iṉṉum ciṟitu tūramceṉṟu akkuṉṟat tik kaṭantāl uṅkaḷ ūr vantuviṭum”  eṉṟāṟpōla, avarkaḷ pōka vēṇṭiya ūr aṇimikkaṇ irukkum ceytiyik kūṟutal
itaṉik ‘kaṇṭōr tampati aṇimi cāṟṟal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa.  	(na. a. 182)
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(kōvi. 221)
nakāturi eṉṟal -
“cirikkāmal kūṟu”  eṉṟa talivaṉ tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.
mēmpaṭṭa talivaṉ iyaṟkip puṇarcciyilum pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattilum taliviyik kūṭit tōḻiyi irantupiṉ niṉṟapōtu, avaṉiṭam palavāṟāka uriyāṭiyavaḷ, “tali makaḷ ariyaḷ”  eṉavum  “niṉkuṟiyi nīyē avaḷiṭam col”  eṉavum avaṉi eḷḷic cirittup palavāṟu pēciyaviṭattu, avaṉ maṉam nontu,  “vaḷiyalaṇinta, naṟumaṇam kamaḻum naṉṉutal tōḻi! nāṉ paṭum tuṉpattiṉiṉṟu viṭutali peṟutaṟkut taliviyi nāṉ aṭiyum vaḻiyi, ēḷaṉa nakiyi viṭuttu, naṉku ārāyntu kūṟu”  eṉṟu vēṇṭutal.
itu ‘tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam’  eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	 (ta. ne. vi. pak. 21)
naki kaṇṭu makiḻtal -
taṉṉi maṟittut talivaṉ etuvum ceyyamuṭiyātu eṉṟu tōḻi nakitturittatik kēṭṭa talivaṉ,  “ivaḷi maṟiyātu ceytiyi veḷippaṭiyākak kūṟiṉ, ivaḷ eṉakkut tavaṟātu utavuvāḷ”  eṉṟu karuti makiḻtal.
piṟar, tōḻi taṉṉi eḷḷi nakiyāṭiyataṟkut talivaṉ varuntuvatākak karuti, itaṉi ‘annaki poṟāatu avaṉ pulampal’ eṉṟum koḷḷupa.  (na.a. 146)
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ,‘cēṭpaṭi’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōivi. 106)
nakitturittal -
talivaṉ kaṟpukkālattil parattiyiṟ pirintoḻukiya kāli, avaṉuṭiya mēmpaṭṭa paratti taṉṉit tāṉē viyantu kūṟiṉāḷ eṉak kēṭṭa talivi, “eṉ taṅkiya rākiya parattiyarukkum iṉi oru taṅki tōṉṟiṉ, avaratu iṟumāppu oḻiyumaṉṟē!  ataṉāl varuvatu aṟiyātu tammit tāmē pukaḻkiṉṟatu eṉṉō?” eṉap parattittaliviyi nōkki nakittuk kūṟiyatu.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(kōvi. 373)
naki naṉi uṟāa(tu) annili aṟital’ -
taṉiyiṭattut taliviyi etirppaṭṭa talivaṉ, taṉ perumiyum aṟivum nīṅki vēṭki mītūrap puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉāṉāyiṉum, taliviyiṉuṭiya accam maṭam nāṇ ivaṟṟi nīkkutaṟku, kaṇṇāṉ kūṟiyati nīkki, moḻiyāṉum kuṟippiṭum eḻuvakic ceytikaḷuḷ itu nāṉkāvatu.
naki naṉi . . . . .  aṟitalāvatu talivaṉ taṉṉip pukaḻntu kūṟak kēṭṭa talivikku maṉattu nikaḻum makiḻcciyāṉ puṟattē puṉmuṟuval tōṉṟa vēṇṭiyataṉi avaḷ maṟittu aṭakkikkoṇṭa anniliyit talivaṉ aṟital.  	(to. po. 98 iḷa)
iyaṟkippuṇarcciyiṉ piṉ piritalum pirital nimittamu mākap poruntuvaṉa talivaṟku uriya eṉṟa ēḻaṉuḷ itu nāṉkāvatu. nakinaṉi . . . .  aṟitalāvatu, talivi makiḻcci mikavum eytāmal varuntumāṟu puṇarccikku iṉamākiya pirivunili kūṟi, avaḷ piriviṉit taṉ maṉattil evvāṟu poṟuttukkoḷḷa muyalkiṟāḷ eṉpataṉit talivaṉ aṟital. “ivaḷip piriyāviṭiṉ, ivvoḻukkam puṟattārkkup pulaṉāka ivaḷ nāṇattāl iṟantupaṭutalum kūṭum;  ivaḷ iṟantāl eṉ uyirum pōyviṭum” eṉ ṟellām karutit talivaṉ pirivu paṟṟik kūṟuvatu ava ḷukku makiḻcci tārātu eṉiṉum pirivu iṉṟiyamiyātatu; itaṉāl talivikkup pirivaccam kūṟiṉār.(101. nac. kuṟun. 57)
nakiyāṭi maṟuttal -
tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattukkaṇ, tōḻi talivaṉ kiyuṟiyākak koṇṭuvanta oḷi poruntiya taḻiyip peṟṟuk koḷ ḷātu avaṉic caṟṟu vilaka niṟuttit taliviyiṉ aru miyi avaṉ aṟiyac ceyvataṟkākap puṉmuṟuvaluṭaṉ iṉimiyākap pēci maṟuttatu.
itu ‘cēṭpaṭi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  			(kōvi. 101)
nakivāyppu uṇartal -
muṟuvaṟkuṟippuṇartal. atu kāṇka. (cāmi.87)
naṭpiṉ naṭakki -
talivaṉ talivi eṉṟa iruvarkaṇṇē naṭpuc ceytu avartam akappuṟacceytikaḷi naṉku aṟintuḷḷa pāṅka ṉum tōḻiyu mākiya iruvaruṭiya naṭpu nili. (taliva ṉoṭu pāṅkaṉ, taliviyoṭu tōḻi naṭpunili)  			(to. po. 200 nac.)
taliviyoṭu tōḻi oḻukum oḻukkam. 	(197 iḷa.)
naṭuṅka nāṭṭam (1) -
.iyaṟkippuṇarcci mutaliya mūṉṟaṉāṉum talivaṉik kūṭiya taliviyiṉ nāṟṟam tōṟṟam mutaliyaṉa kaṇṭu aiyuṟṟa tōḻi avaḷ uḷḷattil uḷḷati aṟivataṟkāka nāṭum tiṟaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu; aḵtāvatu avaḷ naṭuṅkumāṟu kūṟi ārāytal.
“eppoḻutum ittiṉippuṉam vaḻiyē ōryāṉi celvatu vaḻakkam.  iṉṟu ataṉkōṭukaḷ utiram tōyntu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa” eṉṟu tāṉ kūṟat talivi avyāṉi talivaṟku ētēṉum ūṟu ceytuviṭṭatō eṉṟu kalaṅkit taṉṉuḷḷat tē uḷḷatik kūṟuvāḷ eṉṟu tōḻi nāṭiyurittatu itu.  	(na. a. 139 uri)
talivi nāṇumāṟum naṭuṅkumāṟum tōḻi ārāytal kūṭātu.  ātaliṉ ittakiya tōḻi kūṟṟukkaḷ ain tiṇikku ākā eṉpārum uḷar.  	(iṟi. a. 7 uri)
naṭuṅka nāṭṭam (2) -
ikkiḷavi ‘tōḻi taliviyi naṭuṅka nāṭutal’ eṉṉum oru kūṟṟiyē uṭiyatu;  ‘pulimici vittal’ eṉpatu atu.  			(kōvi. 72)
naṭuvaṇ aintiṇi -
kikkiḷi mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytal pāli perun tiṇi eṉa eṇṇum varicikkaṇ,mutalilum īṟṟilumuḷḷa kikkiḷi peruntiṇikaḷip ‘puṟam’ eṉavum  (to.po. 23 nac).  ‘marīiya maruṅku’ (211 nac.) eṉavum kūṟutaliṉ, naṟkāmat tiṟku uriya mulli mutaliya aintiṇikaḷum ‘naṭuvaṇ aintiṇi’ eṉappaṭṭaṉa.  	(2 nac)
naṭuvaṇatu -
mulli kuṟiñci pāli marutam neytal eṉṟa aivaki nilattiṉ naṭuviluḷḷatākiya pālinilam.
mulliyākiya kāṭum, kuṟiñciyākiya maliyum vaṉṉila mām;  marutamākiya vayalveḷiyum, neytalākiya kaṭaṟkarip pakutiyum meṉṉilamām.
mulliyum kuṟiñciyum cila āṇṭukaḷ maḻipeyyā toḻiyiṉ vaṉpāliyām; mīṇṭum maḻivaḷam peṟiṉ paṇṭi muṟiyē mulli kuṟiñci ām.  ivvāṟē maruta mum neytalum maḻivaḷam peṟāvāyiṉ meṉpāli yām; mīṇṭum atu peṟiṉ paṇṭimuṟiyē marutam neytalām.  ātaliṉ pālikkeṉṟu taṉiyē nilam iṉṟu. atu vaṉṉilamākiya mulli kuṟiñcikkum meṉṉilamākiya marutam neytalukkum iṭiyē koḷḷappaṭṭamiyiṉ ‘naṭuvaṇatu’ eṉavum ‘naṭuvu nilittiṇi’ eṉavum peyar vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu.
ulakattip paṭikkuṅ kālattu kāṭum maliyum nāṭum kaṭaṟkariyumākap paṭitta nālvaki nilattiṟkum āciriyaṉ tāṉ paṭitta mulli mutaliya aivaki oḻukkat til pāli oḻintavaṟṟip pakuttuk koṭuttāṉ. ap pāli ēṉiya pōla oru pakutiyuḷ aṭaṅkātu nālvaki nilattiṟkum uriyatākavē pulavarkaḷ ceyyuḷ iyaṟṟi vantuḷḷaṉar. pālikku ‘naṭuvaṇatu’ eṉavum, ‘naṭuvu nilit tiṇi’ eṉavum peyariṭṭatu kuṇam paṟṟiyē ām.  puṇartal iruttal iraṅkal ūṭal eṉpavaṟṟiṟku iṭiyē pirivu nikaḻkiṟatu.  aḵtāvatu puṇarnta talivaṉ pirintāltāṉ talivikkut taṉittiruttalō, avaṉ pirivu kuṟittu iraṅkalō ūṭalō nikaḻum eṉpatu.  nālvaki nilamum maḻiyiṉmi mutaliyavaṟṟāṉ tiritaliṉālēyē pālinilam uṇṭākiṟatu. kālikkum eṟpāṭṭiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa naṇpakalē pālikkuriya ciṟu pōtāka uḷḷatu.  puṇartalukkum iruttalukkum iṭiyē pirivu vikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  ulakiyal poruḷākiya aṟam poruḷ iṉpam eṉpavaṟṟuḷḷē naṭuviluḷḷa poruḷ tēṭaṟku, piritalākiya oḻukkattiṉiyuṭiya pāli kāraṇamāka uḷḷatu.  ātaliṉ pālikku amintuḷḷa appeyar kuṇam paṟṟi vantatām.  	(to. po. 2 nac).
mulliyum kuṟiñciyum aṭutta nilamē kālam paṟṟip pāli nilam ām eṉpatu.  	(iṟi. a. 1 uri).
civañāṉamuṉivar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ‘ naṭuvaṇatu’ eṉṟa peyar kuṇam paṟṟi vantatu eṉpataṟkuk kūṟum viḷakkaṅ kaḷi maṟuttu, mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytal eṉṟu pakukkappaṭṭa nilaṅkaḷum ākātu avaṟṟiṉ vēṟumākātu taṉakkuriya nilam, naṭunikarttatāy niṟṟal paṟṟi ‘naṭuvu nilittiṇi’ eṉavum ‘naṭuvaṇatu’eṉavum pāli kūṟappaṭ ṭatu eṉavum, iṅṅaṉam kuṟiyiṭṭataṉāl pālikku nilam uṇṭu eṉpatum ataṉi ēṉiya nilaṅkaḷiṉ vēṟāka vittup pakutteṇṇutal marapu aṉṟu eṉpatum peṟap paṭum eṉavum kūṟuvar.  	(cū. vi. pak. 37).
ippālikkuriya perumpoḻutu piṉpaṉiyum vēṉilu mām vēṉil eṉavē, iḷavēṉil mutuvēṉil iraṇṭum aṭaṅkum.  ivaṟṟuḷ mutuvēṉilē ciṟantatu. pālikkuriya ciṟupoḻutu naṇpakal.  	(to. po. 9, 10 nac).
naṭuvaṇintiṇikkum kikkiḷiperuntiṇikaṭkum iṭiyē vēṟupāṭu -
naṭuvaṇintiṇi ‘aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi’ eṉak kaḷaviyaluḷ ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭuvatāl, akaṉ ainti ṇikku aṉpu aṭippaṭiyāṉatu eṉpatu pulappaṭukiṟatu. ēṉiya kikkiḷi peruntiṇikaṭku aṉpu iṉṟiyami yātu vēṇṭappaṭuvatiṉṟu; avi kāmatti aṭippaṭi yākak koṇṭavi.  	(to. akat. 2 ca.pāla).
naṭuvu nilittiṇi -
mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytal eṉṟa nālvaki nilaṅka ḷum ākātu, avaṟṟiṉ vēṟum ākātu, maḻipeyyāta kāla māṟupāṭṭāṉ annālvaki nilaṅkaḷilum orō kālattu ēṟpaṭuvatu pālittiṇi.  pālikku mulliyum kuṟiñciyum tirintuvarutalē ciṟappu.
‘naṭuvaṇatu’ kāṇka. 	(to. po. 9 nac.).
naṭi -
iḵtu akapporuḷ uri irupattēḻaṉuḷ oṉṟu. 			(vī.cō. 90)
kuṟiñci mutaliya nilaṅkaḷil naṭakkum oḻukkam paṟṟiya kiḷavi kaḷiṉ variciyāṉa tokuppu ‘naṭiyiyal’ eṉa mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
naṇpaṉ -
kuṟiñci nilattalivaṉ. (tivā. pak. 39)
nayattal -
kikkiḷit talivi taṉṉi nayavāt talivaṉit tāṉ mika virumputal.
“ivaṉ tōḷum mārpum eṉ kaṇṇukku viruntaḷittu makiḻvikkiṉṟaṉa. iṉi ivaṉ mārpiṉi nāṉ ārat taḻuviṉ, aḵtu ettuṇi iṉpam tarumō?” eṉpatu pōṉṟa at talivi kūṟṟu.
itu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāliyil peṇpāṟ kik kiḷiyil oru kūṟṟu. 	(pu. ve. mā. 15-2)
nayappa moḻital -
tōḻi, talivaṉukku iravukkuṟi  nērntu avaṉik kuṟiyiṭattu varaccollit talivi iravukkuṟiyi virumpu māṟu avaḷiṭam uriyāṭi avaḷi iciyac ceytal.
ikkūṟṟu tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattu ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatu.  	(ta. ne. vi. 4.).
nayappu (1) -
talivaṉ tāṉ kaṇṭa talivi māṉuṭamakaḷē eṉṟu tuṇinta piṉ avaḷ pēraḻakiṉi viyantu niṉittal.
itu ‘tuṇintavaḻi viyattal’ eṉavumpaṭum. (i. vi. 491 uri)
iḵtu ‘iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(kōvi. 4)
nayappu (2) -
nayappu - virumputal. iyaṟkippuṇarcci puṇarnta talivaṉ kāraṇamiṉṟit tōṉṟiya iyaṟkiyaṉpiṉāṉum talivi yiṉuṭiya kuṇaṅkaḷāṉ tōṉṟiya ceyaṟkiyaṉpiṉāṉum tūṇṭappaṭṭu avaḷvaṉappiṉi ellikaṭantu pukaḻum vāyilākat tāṉ avaḷiṭattuk koṇṭuḷḷa viruppatti veḷiyiṭuvatu.  	(iṟi. a.2 uri.)
nayappuṟṟu iraṅkal -
kikkiḷit talivaṉ talivimītu koṇṭa kāmam mikuntu kūṭṭam peṟātu tuyaruṟutal.
“eṉ maṉam eṉ vayappaṭṭu nillāmal avaḷiyē cārntu niṟkiṟatē!  eṉ kaṇkaḷum avaḷiyē pārttup pārttu ēṅkukiṉṟaṉavē!  avaḷit taḻuvum eṉ āci niṟi vēṟātō?” eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
iḵtu āṇpāṟkūṟṟuk kikkiḷiyiṉ orukiḷavi. 			(pu.  ve. mā. 14-7)
nalyāḻt tuṇimiyōr iyalpu -
ciṟanta yāḻiṉiyuṭiya piriviṉmiyōriṉ taṉmi. (nac)
kantaruvar patiṉeṇ tēvacātiyāruḷ oruvar. ivarkaḷ eppoḻutum iciyilēyē makiḻvākak kālam kaḻippavar. kantaruvar tām ceyta nalviṉiyiṉ payaṉāka, oruvar koṭuppārum aṭuppārum iṉṟi, (talivaṉum taliviyum ākiya) iruvarum tāmē oru poḻilakattu etirppaṭṭup puṇarvatu kāntaruva maṇamām.  (iṟi. a. 1. uri.)
ikkantaruva maṇattip pōlak koṭuppārum aṭuppā rum iṉṟit talivaṉum taliviyum tāmē etirppaṭṭuk kūṭuvatu iyaṟkippuṇarcci.  	(to. po. 92 nac)
nalam pārāṭṭal (1) -
kikkiḷit talivaṉ taliviyiṉ eḻilnalattip pukaḻn turittal.
“ittaliviyiṉ moḻi kiḷimoḻi, nakilkaḷ kōṅkarumpu, kaṇkaḷ karuviḷi” eṉat taṉ kūṭṭam vēṇṭātāḷip pukaḻnturippatu.
iḵtu āṇpāṟ kūṟṟuk kikkiḷikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  		(pu. ve. mā. 14-6).
nalampārāṭṭal (2) -
iyaṟkippuṇarcciyil talivaṉ taliviyik kūṭiyapiṉ, avaḷuṭiya eḻilnalattiyum uṟuppukkaḷiṉ aḻakiyum pukaḻnturittal.
iḵtu ‘iruvayiṉ ottal’ eṉpataṉkaṇ tirukkōviyā ruḷ (9) aṭakkappaṭum.
iḵtu ‘iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉum tokutiyatu oru pakuti yākiya ‘teyvappuṇacci’kkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  (na. a. 125)
nalam puṉinturittal -
iyaṟkippuṇarccikkaṇ ceviyiṉpam nīṅkalāṉa ēṉiya nālvakip poṟiyiṉpamum tuytta talivaṉ, taliviyiṉ coṟkēṭka virumpik kūṟa, taliviyatu muṟuvalik kaṇṭu ataṉi viyantu puṉinturittal.
itu ‘coṟkēḷāta ayarvu nīṅkiyatu’ eṉavumpaṭum.  			(i. vi. 493 uri).
iḵtu ‘iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatākiya oru kūṟṟu.  	(kōvi. 11)
naṟkāmam (1) -
“cuvarkkattiṉkaṇ ceṉṟu pōkam tuyppal” eṉavum,  “uttarakuruviṉkaṇ ceṉṟu pōkam tuyppal” eṉavum,  “meyññāṉa nūlkaḷ kaṟṟu vīṭu peṟuval” eṉavum,  “teyvatti vaḻipaṭuval” eṉavum eḻum kāmam.   			(iṟi. a. 1 uri)
naṟkāmam (2) -
naṭuvaṇ aintiṇikkuriya ciṟanta kāmamākiya otta kāmam.  			(to. po. 266. pērā.uri)  (ṭu).
naṟparattiyi nayappup paratti paḻittal -
(naṟparatti-cēripparattiyākiya vilimakaḷ;  nayappup paratti-poruḷ kāraṇamāka aṉṟit taṉ kātalē kāraṇamāka oruvaṉukkē urimi pūṇṭu niṟkum paratti (mātavi pōla).
“avaḷ evvaḷavutāṉ aḻakiṉaḷāka iruntu eṉ?  talivaṉ avaḷuṭiya āṭalpāṭalkaḷik kaṇṭu koṇ ṭāṭuvataṉṟi, eṉṉuṭaṉ avaṉ aṉpōṭu ūṭalum kūṭalumākap peṟum iṉpam avaḷiṭattē peṟuvatu illiyē!”  eṉṟa nayappupparatti kūṟṟu.  (ampikā. 482) 
naṟṟāy, ayalārtammoṭu pulampal -
uṭaṉpōkkiṉi aṟinta piṉṉar naṟṟāy maṟṟavarkaḷiṭam ataṉic collit tuyar uṟutal.
“eṉ melliyal ciṟumi, tōḻiyi nīttu, ataṟkumuṉ eṉṉik kiviṭṭu, pakivarmuṉṉē ūrār alar tūṟṟumāṟu, talivaṉuṭaṉ koṭiya pālivaṉattik kaṭakkat tuṇintuviṭṭāḷē!”  (kōvi. 230) eṉṟa naṟṟāy kūṟṟu.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘naṟṟāy varuntal’ eṉṉum (230)
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(na. a. 185)
naṟṟāy (īṉṟāḷ) aṟattoṭu niṟṟaliṉ, tamar piṉcēṟalit talivi kaṇṭu talivaṟku uṇarttal -
talivaṉuṭaṉ pōkum talivi, tāṉ antaṇarvāyilāka viṭutta ceyti kēṭṭut taṉṉi īṉṟa tāyām naṟṟāy taṉ cuṟṟattāriṭam nikaḻntami kūṟiyataṉāl, avarkaḷ piṉ toṭarvatit talivaṟkuk kūṟutal.
ituvariviyaluḷ ‘uṭaṉpōkku iṭiyīṭu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(na. a. 198)
naṟṟāykku (īṉṟāṭku) antaṇar moḻital -
uṭaṉpōkkuc cellum talivi, tāṉ celvatit taṉ tāyiṭam kūṟumāṟu vēṇṭiyataṟkēṟpa, antaṇar (mutali yōr) naṟṟāyiṭam talivi talivaṉuṭaṉ pōṉamiyit terivittal.
“aṉṉiyē! talivaṉ piṉ uṉmakaḷ ceṉṟa kāṉam nīṇṭa niḻaliyuṭiyatu.  nī ēṉ varuntit tavikkiṟāy? uṉ makaḷ pēraṟivukku aruntatiyum nikarākāḷ” (ampikā. 419) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟutal.
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘uṭaṉpōkku iṭiyīṭu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(na. a. 198)
naṟṟāykkuriya kiḷavikaḷ akattiṇiyiyaliḷ ōtappaṭṭa kāraṇam -
talivaṉ mutalāya ēṉiyōrkkuriya kiḷavikaḷ oṉṟaṟku mēṟpaṭṭa uripporuḷiyum nimittaṅkaḷiyum koṇ ṭuḷḷaṉa. naṟṟāykkuriya kiḷavikkaṇ iraṅkalum iraṅkal nimittamumākiya oru kūṟē nikaḻkiṟatu. mēlum koṇṭu talikkaḻital atikārappaṭṭuḷḷatu ātaliṉ naṟṟāykkuriya kiḷavikaḷi mutaṟkaṇ akattiṇiyiyalil eṭuttōti ṉār.  	(to. akat. 38 ca. pāla.)
naṟṟāykkurittal -
talivi talivaṉōṭu uṭaṉpōkku nikaḻttiyavaḻi ataṉi aṟintu taliviyiṉ aṭikaḷatu meṉmittaṉmi karuti iraṅkiya cevili, “yāṉ avaḷit taḻuviya kiyic ciṟitu nekiḻttāl ataṟkē tāṉ eṉṉip pirintu taṉittiruppatākak karuti aḻum eṉpēti, kaṟpu mutirvu tōṉṟa eṉṉi muṟṟum viṭuttu oru kāḷiyiṉ piṉṉē pōṉa ceyal eṉṉi vāṭṭukiṟatu”  eṉa makaḷatu uṭaṉ pōkkiṉi āṟṟātu naṟṟāykku ataṉi uṇarttutal.
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  		(kōvi. 229)
naṟṟāy kūṟṟu -
talivi talivaṉoṭu puṇarntu uṭaṉpōkiya ceytiyi uṇarnta naṟṟāy, antaṇar - teyvam - ayalōr - aṟivar taṉ uḷḷat tuyarattiṉi aṟiyum uṟṟār - cevili - pāṅki - kaṇṭōr ākiyavaroṭu kūṟṟu nikaḻttuvāḷ;  āyiṉ, ekkālattum talivaṉuṭaṉēyō taliviyuṭaṉēyō nērākak kūṟṟu nikaḻttuvāḷallaṉ.  	(na. a. 218, 219)
naṟṟāy kēṭṭu avaṉ (talivaṉ) uḷamkōḷ vēlaṉi viṉātal -
uṭaṉpōkkuc ceṉṟa talivi talivaṉuṭaṉ tammūr varum ceytiyip pāṅkiyar vāyilākak kēṭṭaṟinta naṟṟāy, talivaṉeṇṇam iṉṉateṉa aṟitaṟporuṭṭup pūcāriyik kuṟi kēṭṭal.
“vēla! eṉ makaḷōṭu uṭaṉpōkkiṉ mīṇṭu varuvōṉ eṉmaṉikku varuvāṉā, aṉṟit taṉmaṉikkē cel vāṉā eṉpataṉik kuṟipārttuc col” (tañci. kō. 354) eṉṟu viṉavuvatu.
itu variviyaluḷ ‘mīṭci’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(na. a. 191)
naṟṟāy, tamarkku aṟattuṭaṉ niṟṟal -
talivaṉ pirivāṉ talivikku uṭal meliya, ammelivu teyvattāṉ vantatu eṉṟu karuti ataṉi nīkkac cevili veṟiyāṭṭeṭukkat tuṇintaviṭattē, tōḻi talivi nili kuṟittuc ceviliyiṭam aṟattoṭu niṟpavē, ceviliyum naṟṟāykku aṟattoṭu niṟpa, naṟṟāy tanti taṉṉiyarkkuk kuṟippāl taliviniliyi uṇarttu vāḷ.  aḵtaṉṟic cila pōtu veḷippaṭiyākak kūṟi,
“tūya kuṭiyil piṟanta nīṅkaḷ mayilvākaṉaṉākiya cenniṟa murukaṉip pōṉṟa talivaṟku nammakaḷi maṇam ceytu viyuṅkaḷ” eṉṟāṟ pōla aṟattoṭu niṟṟalum uṇṭu.
iḵtu ‘aṟattoṭu nili’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(ampikā : 361).
naṟṟāy,  “talivaṉ mika aṉpuceyka” eṉṟu teyvattip parāvutal -
“uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ talivaṉ taliviyi aṉpāka naṭatta vēṇṭum” eṉṟu naṟṟāy teyvatti vēṇṭutal.
“eṉ makaḷik kaṭumiyāṉa pāli vaḻiyē aḻittuc ceṉṟa talivaṉ, avaḷiṭam maṉattē aṉpu koṇṭu iṉcol pēci, taṉ mārpiṉiyē taliyaṇiyākak koṭuttu avaḷi uṟaṅkac ceyvāṉāka! ataṟku nī aruḷal vēṇṭum” (akanā. 35) eṉṟu naṟṟāy teyvatti vēṇṭutal.
itu variviyaluḷ ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(i.vi. 538 uri)
naṟṟāy, (talivi) payiliṭam tammoṭu tāṅkalaḷākip pulampal -
uṭaṉpōkkiṉi aṟinta piṉ naṟṟāy, talivi ceṉṟu paḻaki viḷiyāṭi varum maṉiyum malarccōliyum pōlvaṉa pārttuppārttut tuyar uṟutal.
“iḷamāṉkaṉṟē!  maṇippantalē! ceykuṉṟē!  kuvaḷi pūttuḷḷa kuḷamē!  kūvum kuyilē!  umakku nalla ceytikaḷ palavum colli ummiyum eṉṉiyum talivi taliva ṉoṭu kāṇa mīṇṭu varutaṟkuriya nāḷ ennāḷō?”  (ampikā.395) eṉṟāṟ pōla naṟṟāy pulamputal.
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(na. a. 185)
naṟṟāy, talivi mīṇṭu vantuḻic cuṟṟattārkkuk kāṭṭutal -
uṭaṉpōṉa talivi mīṇṭu vantaviṭattē, avaḷip peṟṟa tāy makiḻcciyoṭu, kaṇavaṉōṭu avaḷ vantuṟṟa ceytiyit taṉ uṟaviṉarkku eṭuttuk kūṟi avarkaḷik kāṇac ceytal.
“yāmellām mayaṅkumāṟu tuyarceyta aṉpillāta aṟaṉum niccayamāka aruḷ ceytuḷḷatu.  taṉ taliva ṉōṭu aruñcuram uṭaṉpōy mīṇṭa eṉ kuṟumakaḷik kāṇa vammiṉ!”  (aiṅ.394) eṉṟa naṟṟāy kūṟṟu. 
iḵtu ‘avvaḻi ākiya kiḷavi’kaḷuḷ oṉṟu.  			(to. po. 36 nac).
naṟṟāy, talivivēṟṟumi kaṇṭu ceviliyi viṉātal -
taliviyiṭam tāṉ kaṇṭa vēṟupāṭukaḷāl naṟṟāy ceviliyi veḷippaṭa viṉātal.
“nam makaḷukku ēṟpaṭṭa mayakkam paṟṟi uṉakkut teriyumā?  avaḷmārpil poṉpōl pacali paravat toṭaṅki viṭṭatu;  tōḷvaḷikaḷ kaḻalat toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉa; pēccum taṭumāṟukiṟatu; vēl pōṉṟa kaṇkaḷ pacali pāyntuviṭṭaṉa.  avaḷukku ivvēṟupāṭu ēṟpaṭṭataṉ kāraṇam yātu?” (ampikā. 358) eṉṟu naṟṟāy viṉavutal.
ituvariviyaluḷ, ‘aṟattoṭu niṟṟal’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu  	(na.a. 178)
naṟṟāy, taṉmakaḷ meṉmit taṉmikku iraṅkal -
naṟṟāy, uṭaṉpōkkil melliya taṉ makaḷ aṭi cuṭum poṭimaṇal niṟinta pālivaḻiyil naṭantu cella muṭiyāmal varuntuvāḷē eṉṟu niṉittu varuntutal.
“aṉiccappūmēl naṭantālum varuntum aṭikaḷiyuṭiya eṉmakaḷ neruppil vēli naṭṭu vittāṟpōla veppattāl kotitta paraṟkaṟkaḷ nirampiya pāliyil taṉ aṭikaḷi ūṉṟi naṭattal iyalumō?”  (kōvi. 228) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟu naṟṟāy varuntutal.
itaṉi ‘aṭi niṉintu iraṅkal’ eṉṉum tirukkōvi yār. (228)
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(na. a. 186)
naṟṟāy taṉṉuḷḷē iraṅkal -
talivi uṭaṉpōkkuc ceṉṟatic cevili vāyilāka aṟinta naṟṟāy taṉ maṉattu varuntiyatu.
“makaḷē!  evvaḷavō vacatikaḷiyuṭiya ivviṭatti viṭṭu veḷiyēṟiviṭṭāyē! nī pōkum pālivaḻiyil uṉ tōḻiyarō, viḷiyāṭaṟkuriya pūviyō, kiḷiyō, pantō, pāviyō, illi.  ciṟṟil iḻittu nī viḷi yāṭavum iṭamilli.  inniliyi uṇarātu tuṇintu pālivaḻiyē ceṉṟuviṭṭāyē!”  (ampikā. 391) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟu, naṟṟāy varuntutal.
itu variviyaluḷ ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uriyiṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu. 		(i. vi. 538 uri)
naṟṟāy, pāṅkitaṉṉoṭu pulampal -
cevilivāyilākat taṉmakaḷ talivaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟati aṟinta naṟṟāy, tōḻiyiṭam tuyaruṟṟuk kūṟutal.
“peṇṇē! avaḷ talivaṉuṭaṉ curattil naṭakkavum tuṇintu puṟappaṭuvataṟku muṉṉē, nī eṉṉiṭam colli yiruntāl, muṟiyāka maṇamum muṭittuk koṭuttirup pēṉ.  ataṉic ceyyāmal ippōtu colkiṟāyē!”  (tañci. kō. 329) eṉpatu pōṉṟa naṟṟāy kūṟṟu
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(na. a. 185)
naṟṟāy pāṅkiyartammoṭu pulampal -
uṭaṉpōkku nikaḻntati aṟinta naṟṟāy taliviyiṉ maṟṟa tōḻiyaruṭaṉ talivi tammi viṭṭuc ceṉṟatu paṟṟit tuyaruṟṟuk kūṟutal.
“taṉ pāvikku aṇiya māli oṉṟu tantavaṉē taṉakkut talivaṉ eṉṟu koṇṭu, vēṟu teyvamum toḻa maṉam koḷḷāmal, avaṉpiṉ ceṉṟuviṭṭāḷē! kaṟpuneṟi kaṭavāmal vāḻavēṇṭu meṉṟāl ataṟkut talivaṉpiṉ pālinilattil pōkavēṇṭumā?” (ampikā. 393) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa naṟṟāy kūṟṟu.
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(na. a. 185)
naṟṟāy, maṇaṉ ayar vēṭkiyiṉ ceviliyi viṉātal -
talivaṉum taliviyum uṭaṉpōkkuc ceṉṟapiṉ mīṇṭu tammūr varuvati aṟinta naṟṟāy, avar iruvariyum kaṭimaṇam ceytu kāṇum viruppattuṭaṉ ceviliyiṭam atik kalantukoḷḷutal.
“uṭaṉpōṉa talivaṉ, taliviyoṭu tirumpi vantatum,  nam illattilēyē taliviyiṉ tirumaṇa nikaḻcciyi naṭattat talivaṉuṭiya aṉṉiyiṭam vēṇṭikkoḷ vōmā?”  (tañci kō. 355) eṉṟu naṟṟāy ceviliyi viṉavutal.
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘taṉmaṉi varital’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(na. a. 194)
naṟṟāy, mīṇṭa talivi tirumaṇam paṟṟi ucāvutal -
uṭaṉpōṉa talivi mīṇṭu vantavaḻi avaḷip peṟṟa tāy avaḷ tirumaṇattit tam illattil nikaḻttuvatu paṟṟit talivaṉ karutti aṟiya viṉavutal.
“taṉ poyyāl eṉ makaḷi mayakkic ceṉṟu mīṇṭa ak kāḷiyip payanta tāyiṭam, ‘uṅkaḷ illattil cilampu kaḻi nōṉpi nikaḻttiṉālum eṅkaḷ illattil vatuvi naṉmaṇatti vittukkoḷḷuṅkaḷ ” eṉṟu kūṟuvatil yātu tavaṟu irukkiṟatu?” (aiṅ. 399) eṉṟu talivaṉ illattiṉiṉṟu vantavarkaḷiṭam naṟṟāy kūṟutal pōlvaṉa.
iḵtu ‘avvaḻi ākiya kiḷavi’kaḷuḷ oṉṟu.			(to.po.36 nac.)
naṟṟāy varuntal -
taliviyiṉ uṭaṉpōkkiṉic cevili vāyilāka aṟinta naṟṟāy, “eṉṉiyum ceviliyiyum tōḻiyiyum āyat tiyum nīttu ūrār alar tūṟṟa orukāḷipiṉ eṉmakaḷ pōyiṉaḷē!  yāṉ eṅṅaṉam āṟṟuvēṉ?” eṉpatu paṭa varuntutal.
itaṉi ‘naṟṟāy ayalārtammoṭu pulampal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa.	(na. a. 185)
itu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  		(kōvi. 230)
naṟṟāyiṉ accam -
talivi talivaṉoṭu uṭaṉpōkku nikaḻttiya poḻutē naṟṟāykku accam nikaḻum.  avvaccam iru vakittu.
talivi, talivaṉoṭu cellum pālivaḻiyil iyaṅkum koṭiya vilaṅkum paṟaviyum kaṇṭum, avvaḻiyil irakka miṉṟip poruḷ paṟittut tuṉpuṟuttum vaḻippaṟi ceyyum pālinila makkaḷ ceyal kaṇṭum añci naṭuṅkuvāḷē eṉṟu niṉippatu naṟṟāyiṉ mutalaccam.  taliviyiṉ tantiyum tamiyaṉmārum uṭaṉpōṉa iruvariyum toṭarntu ceṉṟu eyti, avarkaḷ uṭaṉpōṉatu aṟam eṉṟu karutātu, avarkaḷukkut tīṅku iḻipparō eṉpatu avaḷatu iraṇṭām accam.  	(to. po. 36 nac)
naṉmiyum tīmiyum piṟitiṉik kūṟal -
variyātu vantoḻukum talivaṉiṭam tōḻiyō taliviyō kaḷavoḻukkattiṉatu iḻukkiṉi uṇarttu mukattāṉ,
‘kaḻiperuṅ kātalar āyiṉum cāṉṟōr
paḻiyoṭu varūum iṉpam veḵkār’  (akanā. 112)
eṉṟu piṟar ceyalkaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟiṉāṟpōṉṟu aṟivu koḷuttutal.  	(to. po. 210. nac)
naṉṉayam urittal -
taṉiyiṭattut taliviyi etirppaṭṭa talivaṉ taṉ perumiyum aṟivum nīṅki vēṭki mītūrap puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉāṉāyiṉum, taliviyiṉuṭiya accam maṭam nāṇ ivaṟṟi nīkkutaṟkuk kaṇṇāṉ kūṟiyati viṭuttu moḻiyāṉum kuṟippiṭum eḻuvakic ceytikaḷuḷ mūṉṟāvatu.
aḵtāvatu talimakaḷuṭiya ciṟanta aḻakiṉip pukaḻn turittal.  	(to. po. 98 iḷa.)
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉ piṉ piritalum piritalnimittamu mākap poruntuvaṉa talivaṟku uriya eṉṟa ēḻaṉuḷ, mutal mūṉṟu nayappiṉ kūṟu. avaṟṟuḷ mūṉṟāvatu naṉṉayam urittal.  aḵtāvatu vārtti collum iyalpu illāta vaṇṭu neñcu mutaliyaṉa maṟumāṟṟam kūṟuvaṉa pōlak koṇṭu, avaṟṟiṟkut tāṉ taliviyiṭam koṇṭa pēraṉpiṉit tāṉ evariṉum kātalaṉāka uṇarttutal.  naṉṉayam - mikka aṉpu.  iṅṅaṉam kūṟuvataṉ payaṉ, puṇarcci eytiniṉṟa talivikkut talivaṉ evviṭattāṉō, ippuṇarcci iṉṉum kūṭuṅkolyō, ivaṉ aṉpuṭiyaṉkollō eṉa nikaḻum aiyatti nīkkutalām.
e-ṭu :
“makarantattiṉi ārāyum akacciṟikaḷiyuṭiya tumpī!  nī eṉ nilattiṟkuriya vaṇṭātaliṉ atupaṟṟi eṉpāl aṉpu paṟṟi moḻiyātu, nī nukarntu kaṇṭataṉi moḻivāyāka.  payiṉṟa kēṇmi eḻumiyum poruntum naṭpiṉiyum, mayil aṉṉa cāyaliṉiyum, paṟimuṟi nirampiṉa ceṟinta paṟkaḷiṉiyum uṭiya ivaḷatu kūntaliviṭa nī aṟiyum pūkkaḷuḷ naṟumaṇam mikkaṉavum uḷavō?”  (kuṟun. 2) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 101 nac)
‘naṉipakar paḷḷiyiṉ nayantu celavu aḻuṅkal’  -
ōtaṟpirivu mutaliya pirivukaḷil talivi virivil taṉpiri viṟku uṭaṉpaṭāḷ eṉpati uṇarnta talivaṉ, ava ḷoṭu cilanāḷ kūṭi iṉpuṟṟu avaḷ maṉattit taṉ vacappaṭutti uṭaṉpaṭac ceyaṟkut tāṉ avaḷōṭu il lattē taṅkutal. (pāli naṭiyiyal)  	(vī. cō. 93. urimēṟ.)
nākātipaṉ -
nākam - mali. malikku atipaṉ ākiya kuṟiñci nilat talivaṉ. (caṅka. aka.)
nāṭṭam kūṭṭiyurittal -
talivaṉ taṉ uḷḷattu nikaḻnta vēṭkiyit talivikkuc coṟkaḷāṉ kūṟāmal tāṉ pārtta pārviyālē terivikka, taliviyum ataṉi uṇarntu taṉ maṉattu nikaḻum vēṭkiyip peṇmi kāraṇattāṉ coṟkaḷ koṇṭu kūṟātu taṉkaṇkaḷ iraṇṭālum pulappaṭut tutal.
nāṭṭam-pārvi; kūṭṭiurittal-maṉattuppaṭṭa karuttukkaḷit tokuttuk kūṟutal.  (to. po. 96 nac)
nāṭṭāṭci paṟṟiya peyar -
nālvaki nilattum uripporuḷ talimakkaṭku nāṭ ṭāṭci paṟṟiya potuppeyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkappeṟum.
malikiḻāṉ eṉpatu kuṟiñci nāṭṭāṭci paṟṟiya peyar. kuṟumpoṟi nāṭaṉ, kāṉaka nāṭaṉ eṉpaṉa mulli nāṭṭāṭci paṟṟiya peyar. kaḻaṉi nāṭaṉ eṉpatu maruta nāṭṭāṭci paṟṟiya peyar. tuṟikēḻ ūraṉ eṉpatu neytal nāṭṭāṭci paṟṟiya peyar.	(to.po. 20 nac)
nāṭaka vaḻakkam -
aḵtāvatu nikaḻātaṉavaṟṟik kaṟpaṉi ceytu kūṟutal.
cuvipaṭa vantaṉavellām ōriṭattu vantaṉavākat tokut tuk kūṟutal. aḵtāvatu celvattāṉum kulattāṉum oḻukkattāṉum aṉpiṉāṉum ottār iruvar tamariṉ nīṅkit taṉiyiṭattu etirppaṭṭār eṉavum,  avvaḻik koṭuppōrumiṉṟi aṭuppōrumiṉṟi vēṭki mikuti yāṉ puṇarntār eṉavum, piṉṉum avar kaḷavoḻukkam naṭatti ilakkaṇa vakiyāṉ varintu eytiṉār eṉavum, piṟavum innikaraṉavākic cuvipaṭa varuvaṉa ellām oruṅku vantaṉavākak kūṟutal.  	(to. po. 56 iḷa.).
nāṭakavaḻakku eṉpatu, puṇarcci ulakiṟkup potuvāyiṉum malicārntu nikaḻum eṉṟum, kālam varintum, uyarn tōr kāmattiṟku uriyaṉa varintum, meyppāṭu tōṉṟap piṟavāṟum kūṟum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkam.  	(53 nac)
nāṭakavaḻakkāvatu makkaḷ vāḻkkiyiyē uyariya kuṟikkōḷōṭu iyittuc cuvipaṭa naṭittuk kāṭṭum kūttar piṉpaṟṟum marapu.  	(52 pārati)
nāṭakavaḻakkāvatu ulakavaḻakkaṅkaḷil ciṟantaṉavāy uḷḷa vaṟṟi aṟiñarkaḷ oṉṟākat tokuttup piṟkāla ulaka vaḻakkam tiruttamāka naṭipeṟutaṟporuṭṭup pāṭalka ḷākap pāṭivikkum nūl vaḻakkam.  	(42 kuḻa)
nāṭaka vaḻakku eṉappaṭuvatu -
ulakil palvēṟu kālattum iṭattum palvēṟu niliyiṉa rāka vāḻum māntariṭam nikaḻum kāmam kaṇṇiya oḻukalāṟ ṟiṉuḷ cāṉṟōr vēṇṭātavaṟṟik kaḷintu vēṇṭu vaṉavaṟṟit tērntukoṇṭu avaṟṟiṟku iṭamum kālamum variyaṟi ceytu karupporuḷi iṇittu meyppāṭu tōṉṟac cuvipaṭa amittuk kūṟalum, talimakkaḷip piṇi mūppu iṟappu illāta otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyumākat tōṟṟuvittu avarkkup pāṅkarum pākarum iḷiyarum ākiya cuṟṟatti amittup parattiyar kāmakkiḻattiyar eṉpārip puṉintu vāyil āvārip paṭittu vaṉintu kūṟalum, piṟavum ām.	(to. akat.  55 ca.pāla.)
nāṭat tuṇital -
naṟṟāykkut taliviyiṉ uṭaṉpōkkiṉi uritta cevili, naṟṟāy paṭum tuyaram kaṇṭu talivi ceṉṟa iṭam nōkki avaḷit tēṭi aḻittuvarat tuṇital.
itaṉik ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’  eṉum tokutikkaṇ ‘cevili āṟṟāt tāyit tēṟṟal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa.(na.a. 188)
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 234)
nāṭum ūrum kiḷattal
talivi talivaṉiṭam taṉṉuṭiya nāṭu, ūr, tāṉ piṟanta kuṭi ivaṟṟik kuṟippiṭṭut taṉṉip pukaḻntukoṇṭa vaḻi avaḷ uḷḷattil ūṭal iruppatu pulappaṭum.
taliviyiṉ pulaviyit taṇikkat talivaṉ paṇinta moḻi kūṟiyavaḻit talivi, “periya nāṭṭil pērūril periya kuṭumpattil piṟanta peruñciṟappuṭiya numakkuc ciṟiyē mākiya emmiṭam iṅṅaṉam paṇimoḻi kūṟal takkatō?”  (pāṇṭik 295) eṉṟu avaṉuṭiya nāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟic ciṟappittuk kūṟiyaviṭattu avaḷ pulavi nīṅkappeṟṟami pulappaṭum.  	(iṟi. a. 48, 49)
nāṇ tuṟavu urittal -
kaḷavukkālattil pāṅkimatiyuṭaṉpāṭṭiṉpōtu ava ḷāl cēṭpaṭukkappaṭṭu āṟṟāṉākiya talivaṉ, ava ḷiṭam taṉ nāṇam nīṅkiviṭṭa ceytiyik kūṟutalum, kaḷavuk kālattup palavakit tuyar mikutiyāl aṟattoṭu niṟkat tuṇinta talivi, taṉtōḻiyiṭam taṉ nāṇam nīṅkiviṭṭa ceytiyik kūṟutalum ām.
tōḻi talivaṉiṭam taliviyiṉ nāṇ nīṅkiya ceytiyik kūṟutal. (maṇak.)
‘nāṇut tuṟavu urittal’ eṉpatum itu.			(kuṟaḷ ati.114 parimē.)
nāṇ viṭa varuntal -
pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattukkaṇ talivaṉ taliviyip pukaḻntavaḻi avaḷ nāṇit taṉ kaṇkaḷip pottik koṇṭāḷāka, talivaṉ avaḷ kaṇkaḷēyaṉṟi avaḷmēṉimuḻutum taṉṉi varuttuvatākak kūṟa, avaṉuṭiya āṟṟāmimikuti kaṇṭu itaṟkumuṉ taṉṉiviṭṭu nīṅkāta nāṇam taṉṉi viṭṭuk kātalāl nīṅkuvatu kaṇṭu avaḷ varuntutal.
itaṉi ‘iyaṟkip puṇarcci’kkaṇ ‘maṟuttu etirkōṭal’ eṉṟa tuṟiyākak koḷpa.  	(na. a. 127)
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ ‘pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam’ eṉum toku tikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(kōvi. 44)
nāṇa nāṭṭam -
talivi nāṇumpaṭi cila kūṟit tōḻi ārāytal.
iyaṟkippuṇarccikkuppiṉ taliviyiṉ uṭal vēṟupāṭu kaṇṭa tōḻi avvēṟupāṭṭiṉ kāraṇam avaḷi viṉava, ataṟku avaḷ cuṉiyāṭiyamiyik kāraṇamākak kūṟa, tōḻi tāṉum cuṉiyāṭi attakiya vēṟupāṭu peṟa viḻivatik kūṟuvāḷ;  piṉṉum taliviyip piṟiyit toḻumāṟu aḻittu, tāṉ talivaṟku uriyavaḷāyiṉa miyiṉ avaṉi yaṉṟip piṟa teyvamum toḻutal kūṭātu eṉṟa kōṭpāṭṭāṉē avaḷ piṟiyit toḻāmi kaṇṭu, avaḷ vēṟupāṭṭiṉ kāraṇatti oruvāṟu ārāyntu koḷvāḷ.  talivi nāṇumāṟu tōḻi nikaḻttiya ivvuriyāṭalkaḷ ‘nāṇa nāṭṭam’ eṉappaṭum.
tōḻi, talivi nāṇumāṟu avaḷiṭam uriyāṭi ārāy tal tavaṟu eṉpatu orucārār karuttu.  	(iṟi. a. 7)
nāṇanāṭṭam:  kiḷavikaḷ -
piṟi toḻuka eṉṟal, vēṟupaṭuttuk kūṟal, cuṉiyāṭal kūṟi nakittal, puṇarcciyurittal, matiyuṭampaṭutal eṉṟa aintum nāṇanāṭṭattiṉ kūṟṟukkaḷākat tirukkōviyār kuṟikkum.  	(kōvi. 67-71)
nāṇikkaṇ putittal -
pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattiṉkaṇ, talivaṉ taṉ muṉ niṉṟu taṉṉip pukaḻtalik kēṭṭatum, talivi nāṇattiṉāl avaṉmuṉ niṟṟal āṟṟātu oru koṭiyiṉit tuṇi yākak koṇṭu kaṇṇip pottikkoṇṭu varuntutal. itaṉi iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇ aminta ‘vaḻipāṭu maṟuttal’ eṉṟa kūṟṟākat koḷpa. 	(na. a. 127) 
itu tirukkōviyāril ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi. 42)
nāṇiṉam aḻuṅkal -
talivi iyaṟkippuṇarccikkaṇ nāṇam maṭam accam mutaliya peṇmip paṇpukaḷāl kūṭṭattiṟku uṭaṉpaṭu taṟku maṟuttal.  (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal)  (itu ‘vaḻipāṭu maṟuttal’  (na. a. 127)  eṉavum kūṟappaṭum.)  	(vī. cō. 92 uri)
nāṇut talippiriyā nalvaḻi -
nāṇam nīṅkāta nalloḻukkam.
akapporuṭkaṇ kūṟum kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ aṟam mutalāyiṉa vaḻuviṉa āyiṉum, taliviyiṉ nāṇ aḻiya vārāp poruḷē kūṟattakkatu eṉpatu.  	(to. po. 215 iḷa)
tōḻi aṟakkaḻivuṭiyaṉavākiya ceytikaḷik kūṟum pōtum, taliviyatu nāṇ avaḷiṭattiṉiṉṟu nīṅkā mikkuk kāraṇamākiya naṉṉeṟiyākiya poruṭkūṟupāṭu kaḷi uḷḷaṭakkik kūṟutal.  	(219 nac.)
e-ṭu : akanā. 32
nāṇutal kaṇṭu mikutturittal -
kaṟpuk kālattil parattiyiṟ piriviṉkaṇ talivaṉip parattiyarvacam puṉalāṭaviṭṭu, cūṭuvāriṉṟic cep piṉkaṇ iṭṭu aṭittut tamiyē vikum pūppōṉṟa talivi,  “iḵtu avaṉukkut takāta paḻiyām”  eṉak karuti nāṇi avaṉ parattimiyi maṟittāḷāka, atu kaṇṭa tōḻi, “ivaḷuṭiya kaṟpum nalaṉum mika mēmpaṭṭaṉa” eṉat taliviyiṉ nalatti mikuttuk kūṟiyatu.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	 (kōvi. 374) 
“nāṇum niṟiyum aḻiyāmal āṟṟal vēṇṭum” eṉṟa tōḻikkut talivi kūṟiyatu -
“nāṇam eṉum tāḻiṭṭup pūṭṭiya eṉ neñcattu niṟi eṉṉum katavik kāmavēṭki eṉṉum kaṇicci uṭit teṟintuviṭṭatu.  iṉi yāṉ nāṇum niṟiyum kāttal iyalātatoṉṟu”  eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.  	(kuṟaḷ 1251)
nāṇu mika varutal :  poruḷ -
kūṭṭattāṉ piṟanta meyvēṟupāṭu kaṇṭu talivi nāṇam mikutal
ivvoḻukalāṟṟilum taliviyatu kūṟṟu nikaḻum. 			(to. kaḷa. 21 ca.pāla.)
nāṇurittu maṟuttal -
‘tōḻiyiṟkūṭṭattukkaṇ, talivaṉ koṇarnta pala vakit taḻikaḷiyum kāraṇaṅ kāṭṭit tōḻi ēṟka maṟuttavaḻi, avaṉ kaṇṇiyoṉṟu puṉintu vara atu kaṇṭavaḷ, “ceviliyar cūṭṭiya kaṇṇiyiṉmēl vēṟu entak kaṇṇiyic cūṭṭiṉum talivi cūṭikkoḷḷa nāṇuvāḷ.  nīr koṇarnta kaṇṇiyi eṅṅaṉam ēṟṟu avaḷukkuc cūṭṭuvatu?”  eṉat taliviyiṉ nāṇiṉi urittuk kaṇṇiyi ēṟka maṟuttatu.
itu ‘cēṭpaṭi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  			(kōvi. 95)
nāṇuvari iṟattal -
meyyuṟupuṇarcci nikaḻumuṉ talivaṉukkum talivikkum nikaḻum pattuvaki nilikaḷuḷ āṟāvatu.  itu nāṇattiṉ elliyik kaṭappatākum.	(to. po. 97 iḷa)
iyaṟkip puṇarcci mutal kaḷavu veḷippaṭuntuṇiyum talivaṉ talivi eṉṟa iruvarukkum uḷavām ilakkaṇam oṉpataṉuḷ iḵtu aintāvatu.
nāṇuvari iṟattalāvatu poṟukka muṭiyum vari nāṇat tāṉ poṟuttup poṟukka muṭiyāta nili ēṟpaṭumpōtu, nāṇattiṉ elliyik kaṭantu, talivaṉ pāṅkaṟkum tōḻikkum urittalum talivi tōḻikku aṟattoṭu niṟṟalum pōlvaṉa.  	(100 nac.)
meyyuṟupuṇarcci nikaḻumuṉ talivaṉukku nikaḻum pattu vaki nilikaḷuḷ āṟāvatu itu.  	(i. vi. 405)
nāṇoṭu nīṅkal -
talivi nāṇam nīṅkit tōḻi kūṟiyapaṭi talivaṉik kūṭaṟku oruppaṭal.
iyaṟkippuṇarcci pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam ivaṟṟiṉ piṉ talivaṉ tōḻiyik kuṟiyirantu avaḷ ātaravip peṟṟa piṟaku, tōḻi taliviyit talivaṉ viruppam kūṟik kuṟi nayappittavaḻit talivi taṉ kāmavēṭkiyāl nāṇam kavarappaṭṭa ceytiyi nēriṭiyākak kūṟāmal kuṟippāka,  “tōḻi! atō pār.  nam tiṉikkolliyil, puṟaṅ koṭutta maṉṉar celvatti veṉṟavar vīrarkaḷ kip paṟṟic celvatu pōla, tiṉikkatirkaḷik kiḷikaḷ kavarntukoṇṭu celkiṉṟaṉa” eṉpatu pōlak kuṟippāl pulappaṭuttutal.
itu ‘tōḻiyiṟkūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(ta. ne. vi. pak. 23)
valitākavum melitākavum collit tōḻi talivaṉatu kuṟi nayappittaviṭattē, talivi taṉatu āṟṟāmiyāl col piṟavātu niṉṟu, piṉ avvāṟṟāmi nīṅka nāṇu vantaṭiyac collutali ‘nāṇoṭu cūḻal’ eṉṉum kaḷaviyal kāriki.  	(pak. 67)
nāmakkālam -
nām-accam. talivaṉ varum vaḻiyiṉ kaṭumiyiyum;  iṭiyē vilaṅkukaḷāṉ nikaḻak kūṭiya tīṅkiṉiyum; kaḷavoḻukkam ūrār aṟiya veḷippaṭṭuviṭumō eṉa vum, cevili ikkaḷavoḻukkattiṉi aṟintuviṭuvāḷō eṉavum, talivi talivaṉoṭu kaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻttuva taṉi aṟiyāta ayalār taliviyi maṇam pēca vantuviṭu vārkaḷō eṉavum, tamatu uḷḷam kavaṟciyuṟuvatiyum; taliviyum tōḻiyum añcum kaḷavukkālam.			(to. po. 146 nac)
‘nālvarkkum uriya ’ eṉappaṭṭa paṇpukaḷ -
nōyum iṉpamum ākiya nilimikaṭku uriya kāma mikutikkaṇ pulaṉuṇarvum, tuṇitaṟku āṟṟāta nāṇamum, aṟivu ceyaṟ paṭāta maṭamum ākiya mūṉṟum kuṟṟam tīrnta ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya talivaṉ talivi cevili naṟṟāy eṉṉumivar nālvarkkum uriyavām.
īṇṭu nāṇ eṉṟatu viṉipaṟṟi varuvatōr paṇpākiya ‘karumattāl nāṇutal.’ maṭam eṉṟatu, aṟiyāmi; peṇ mikkuṇamākiya maṭattiṉ itu vēṟu.			(to. poruḷiyal 6 ca.pāla.)
nāvāyppaṟi -
neytal nilappaṟi.  	(iṟi. a. 1 uri).
nāḷ eṇṇi varuntal -
kaṟpuk kālattil talivaṉ poruḷvayiṉ piriya avaṉ mīṇṭum vārāmi kuṟittu varuntiya taliviyiṉ varuttam kaṇṭu kavaliyuṟṟa tōḻi, “ittaliviyi varuntumāṟu taṉittu viṭuttuc ceṉṟa talivaṉ ceṉṟa nāḷkaḷi eṇṇum ceyalāl nilaṉum kuḻi viḻuntatu;  nāḷkaḷik kuṟitta eṉ viralum tēyntatu” eṉat talivaṉ ceṉṟa nāḷkaḷi eṇṇi varuntiyatu. nilaṉ - cuvarākiya iṭam.
itu ‘poruḷ vayiṉ pirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi. 345)
nāḷatu ciṉmi oṉṟāmi -
yākki niliyātu eṉṟu uṇarum uṇarcciyip porun tāmi.  	(to. po. 41 nac).
nāḷatu ciṉmiyum iḷamiyatu arumiyum oṉṟāmikkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟal -
vāḻnāḷkaḷ cilavē ātaliṉ virivil illaṟattukkut tēviyāṉa poruḷit tēṭi vantuviṭavēṇṭum eṉṟa uṇarcciyi, accilavākiya vāḻnāḷilum iḷamip paruvam eṉpatu mikak kuṟukiya kālattatu ātaliṉ kāmac cevvi nukartaṟkuriya avviḷamip paruvam vīṇē kaḻita lākātu eṉṟa uṇarcci taṭuttaṟkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟuvatu.  			(to. po. 41. nac.)
e-ṭu: “neñcē! tam āṇ vaṅkāppaṟavi nīṅkappeṟṟami yāl taṉitta ceṅkāl paṟavikaḷ, vallūṟu eṉṉum valiya paṟavi tammi iriyākak koḷḷavēṇṭit tammēl vantu vīḻntatāka, tam āṇpaṟaviyik kāṇātu, vēyṅkuḻalatu ici pōlum kuraluṭiyavāyk kuṟiya pala olikaḷāl āṇ paṟaviyi aḻikkumpaṭiyāṉa kuṉṟu keḻu ciṟuvaḻikaḷ kaṭattaṟku ariyaṉa eṉṟu eṇṇāmal, ‘maṟappu ariya nam kātali iṅkē taṅka nāṉ celvēṉ’ eṉṟu tuṇivatu, iṅkē namatu iḷamipparuvat tiṟku muṭivēyākum.”  (kuṟun. 151) eṉṟu, poruḷ tēṭattuṇinta taṉatu neñciṟkut talivaṉ kūṟuvatu.
nāṟṟattāl aiyamuṟṟu ōrtal -
taliviyiṭam, tōḻi, tammaṉōrpāl illāta putiya māṉmatac cāntiṉ maṇamum piṟa maṇamum kalantu vīcuvatik koṇṭu aiyamuṟṟu avaḷukkut talivaṉoṭu puṇarcci uṇṭeṉṟu tuṇital.  itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkimatiyuṭaṉ pāṭu’ eṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(i. vi. 507)
nāṟṟam peṟṟu nilippuk kāṇac cevili kūṟal -
taliviyiṉ uṭalil putumaṇam vīcuvatu kaṇṭu talivi uḷḷam muṉpu pōlātu vēṟākat tirintuḷḷa ceyti paṟṟit tōḻiyic cevili viṉavutal.  ‘aṉṉavi piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷap paṭuvatoru kūṟṟu itu. 			(to. po. 113 iḷa.)
nikaḻnta aiyattik kaḷintu teḷitaṟkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟal -
payiṉṟataṉmēl allatu cellāta tātu ūtum vaṇṭu, oruvarāṉ ceyaṟkiyākac ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ, muliyiṉum tōḷiṉum eḻutum toyyiṟkoṭi, kikkoṇṭu mōntu uyirkkum pū, maṉitakkaṇkaḷ, muṉpu kaṇṭaṟiyāta vaṭivattik kaṇṭavaḻi maṉattiṟ piṟakkum accattāṉ ēṟpaṭum taṭumāṟṟam, kaṇ imittal, āṇ makaṉit taṉittuk kaṇṭavaḻi maṉattil piṟakkum accam, kāl nilam tōytal, uṭalniḻal tariyil tōṉṟutal, neṟṟiyil viyarvi tōṉṟutal mutaliyavaṟṟik koṇṭu tēvarulaka makaḷiriyum nila ulaka makaḷiriyum vēṟupāṭu kaṇṭukoḷḷalām eṉpataṉiyum nūlaṟivāṉ aṟinta talivaṉ, tāṉ kaṇṭu aiyuṟṟa taliviyiṉ neṟṟiyil viyarvi arumputaliṉāṉum, avaḷ aṭikaḷ nilattil tōytaliṉāṉum, cevvari paranta avaḷ kaṇkaḷ imittaliṉāṉum,  “ivaḷ tēvarulaka makaḷ allaḷ; in nilulaka makaḷē” eṉṟu aiyam teḷintu taṉṉuḷ kūṟikkoḷḷutal. (pu.ve.mā. 14-3)	(*to. po. 95 nac.)
nikaḻntatu kūṟi niliyal -
poruḷ mutaliyaṉa karutip piriya virumpum neñciṟkut talivaṉ taṉ muṉṉiya pirivu aṉupavaṅkaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟit taliviyip piriyātu illiṉkaṇṇēyē iruttal.
e-ṭu :  naṟ. 3.  	(to. po. 47 iḷa.)
talivaṉ kūṟiya kūṟṟiṉit taliviyum tōḻiyum tam maṉattuk koṇṭu piṉ talivaṉ piriyak karutumvaḻi avaṉiṭattil eṭuttuk kūṟal.  itu pālit tiṇiyām.  talivaṉ pirintavaḻi āṟṟāta talivikku avaṉ muṉpu kūṟic ceṉṟa kūṟṟit tāṉ eṭuttuk kūṟit tōḻi āṟṟuvittal pōlvaṉa;  iviyum pālittiṇiyām.
e-ṭu :  kali. 18, akanā. 61  	(44 nac.)
kātalar tammuḷ nikaḻnta ceytiyi eṭuttuk kūṟi ataṉ kaṇ īṭupaṭutal;  iḵtu akattuṟi oḻukkamām.  talivaṉum taliviyum tam maṉattiṟkuk kūṟikkoḷḷutalum, tōḻi yiṭam kūṟutalum uṇṭu.
e-ṭu :  kuṟun. 30,  akanā. 5.  	(43 pārati.)
taliviyum tōḻiyum talivaṉ naṭakkipāl aiyam koṇṭu avaṉiṭam nikaḻntaṉavaṟṟi niṉintu pēcik koṇṭu illattil iruppiṉum, avar uḷḷam piriviyē nāṭutalāl atuvum piriviṉpāṟpaṭum.  	(189 kuḻa.)
nikaḻntatu niṉittaṟku ētuvātal -
muṉṉar nikaḻnta oṉṟu piṉṉar atē kāriyattit toṭaṅku muṉ naṉku eṇṇi muṭivu ceyyak kāraṇa mātal.
e-ṭu : naṟ. 62 	(to.po. 46 iḷa.)
muṉṉart talivaṉkaṇ nikaḻntatoru nikaḻcci piṉṉart talivi niṉittaṟkum (kali.4), muṉṉart taliviyiṉkaṇ nikaḻntatoru nikaḻcci piṉṉart talivaṉ niṉittaṟkum (aka nā. 5 ) nimittam ātal.  (43 nac)
kātalar vāḻkkiyil muṉnikaḻnta nikaḻccikaḷ piṉ niṉip pataṟkum eṭuttuk kūṟuvataṟkum uriyaṉa.
e-ṭu : kuṟun. 25, akanā. 61  	(42 pārati).
talivaṉ taliviyaruḷ, muṉ talivaṉceyalkaḷit taliviyum, talivi ceyta ceyalkaḷit talivaṉum piṉ eṇṇip pārkkumpōtu avaravar uḷḷakkiṭakkiyi aṟitaṟkuk kāraṇamāka avi amiyum. 
e-ṭu: kali. 4, akanā. 5	(188 kuḻa.)
niccamum peṇpāṟkuriyaṉa -
accamum nāṇum maṭaṉum payirppum pētimiyum niṟiyum eṉṟum peṇpāṟkuriya paṇpukaḷām.  			(to. po. 99 nac.)
nitivaravu kūṟāniṟṟal -
talivaṉ variporuṭkup pirintu kālam nīṭṭikka, pirivu kuṟittu āṟṟātirunta taliviyiṭam tōḻi, “naṅkāy! nam uṟaviṉar vēṇṭiyapaṭiyē paricapporuḷkaḷi nam tali vaṉ aṉuppiyuḷḷāṉ.  iṉi, avaṉ niṉṉi virivil varintukoḷvāṉ.  ipparicapporuḷkaḷi nī kāṇpā yāka!”  eṉṟa kūṟutal.
itaṉi ‘varivumalivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇ ‘kātalaṉ mulivili viṭuttami pāṅki kātalikku urittal’ eṉ ṟum kūṟupa.  	(na.a. 174)
itu tirukkōviyāril ‘variporuṭpirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	 (kōvi. 298)
nimittam eṉpataṉ poruḷ -
nimittamāvatu puṇartal mutaliya kāriyaṅkaḷ nikaḻtaṟku ētuvāka avaṟṟic cārntu nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷām.  ātalāl ataṉi pirittu ōtiṉār. 	(to. akat. 16 ca.pāla.)
nimittam pōṟṟal -
uṭaṉpōkki aṟintapiṉ naṟṟāy, ataṟkuk kākam karital pōṉṟa nalla cakuṉaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭumāṟu, talivi mīṇṭu varutal vēṇṭum eṉṟu vēṇṭikkoḷḷutal.
“kākamē!  nī karintāl ūrukkup pōṉavar mīṇṭu varuvar eṉṟu kūṟuvar.  nī iṉṟu eṉ maṉikkaṇ karivāyāka.  eṉ makaḷ tirumpi varumāṟu aruḷiṉāl uṉakku nalla cōṟu pōṉṟavi paliyākattaruvēṉ”  (tañci. kō. 334) eṉpatu pōṉṟa naṟṟāy kūṟṟu.
itu variviyalil ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’k kaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.  (na.a. 186)
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘koṭikkuṟi pārttal’ eṉṉum (235).
nirikōṭpaṟi -
niri kavarumpōtu aṭikkum pāli nilattup paṟivaki.			(iṟi. a. 1 uri)
nilattiṉmi kūṟi maṟuttal -
tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattil tōḻi talivaṉic cēṭpaṭukkat tiṭṭamiṭṭirukkavum, talivaṉ cantaṉattaḻi koṇṭu cella, atu tamakkut takātu eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟiyatu kēṭṭu maṟṟeru taḻiyiṉit talivaṉ koṇṭu cella,  “ikkuṉṟiṉkaṇ kāṇappaṭāta taḻiyi nīr emakkuk koṭuttāl, ‘ittaḻi eppaṭi ivarkaḷukkuk kiṭṭiyatu?’  eṉa ūrmakkaḷiṭiyē ārāycci ēṟpaṭa, em kuṭikkē paḻiyākum;  ātaliṉ ittaḻi eṅkaḷukku ākātu” eṉa maṟuttuk kūṟiyatu.
itu cēṭpaṭi eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 			(kōvi. 92)
nilatteyvam -
1.  aintiṇikaḷukkum muṟiyē iṉṉa iṉṉa teyvam urimiyuṭiyatu eṉṟa muṟiyāl avvan nilattukku uriya teyvam. aḵtāvatu mullikku māyōṉ, kuṟiñcikkuc cēyōṉ, marutattiṟku vēntaṉ (-intiraṉ), neytalukku varuṇaṉ,  (pālikkuk kāḷi) eṉṟu variyaṟuttuk koḷḷap paṭṭa teyvam.
2.	nilamākiya teyvameṉap pūmakaḷiyum kuṟippar.  	(ṭu)
nilamayakkam -
kuṟiñci mulli marutam neytal eṉṉum nālvaki nilaṅkaḷum oṉṟaṉ elliyi maṟṟataṉ elli toṭaṅku miṭattu iru nilattuc cuvaṭukaḷum uṇṭu ātaliṉ appakuti kaḷil kāṇappaṭum karupporuḷkaḷ iru nilattukkum uriyavām niliyē itu 	(cīvaka. 718 nac)
nilayam -
maruta nilattu ūr;  nikamam, nikāyam eṉpatum atu 			(cūṭā. ē āvatu 40)
nilavu kaṇṭu aḻuṅkal -
nilāviṉip pārttup pirivāl varuntutal.  talivaṉ poruḷvayiṉ pirintaviṭattu avaṉpirivāl, paṇṭu makiḻcci tanta nilavu ippoḻutu tuṉpam tantatāka,  “vāṉattil tōṉṟu nilavu kaṇavarāl piriyappaṭṭa peṇkaḷiṉ uyiri vāṭṭum;  āyiṉ kaṇavarik kūṭi yiruppār tōḷkaḷukkup pūrippit tarum” eṉat talivi tāṉ nilavāl tuṉpuṟṟurittal.
itu kaṟpuk kālattup ‘poruḷvayiṉ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(mā. a. pāṭal. 385)
nilavu veḷippaṭa varuntutal -
kaḷavukkālattil iravukkuṟikkaṇ talivaṉukku varum vaḻiyil uṇṭākum ētam kūṟi varivu kaṭāviya tōḻi, maṟunāḷ talivaṉ iravukkuṟikkaṇ vantu niṟka, nilavu veḷippāṭṭāl ceṉṟu etirpaṭamāṭṭāmal tāṅkaḷ varunti niṉṟa ceyali aṟivippāḷāy, avaṉ ciṟippuṟa māka niṟka, nilavoṭu pulantu kūṟutal.
iḵtu ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  			(kōvi. 111)
nilavu veḷippaṭutal -
talivaṉ iravil kuṟiyiṭam varuvataṟku iṭiyūṟāka nilavu tōṉṟi viṭutal.  itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘iravukkuṟi iṭiyīṭu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  itaṉi ‘varun toḻiṟku arumi’ eṉappaṭum kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟākavum koḷḷalām.
itu talivikūṟṟākavum tōḻikūṟṟākavum varum (na. a. 161)
nili kaṇṭurittal -
taṉatu pirivu tāṅkāmal talivi tāṉ ceyalaṟṟu matiyoṭu varunti urippatu kēṭṭa talivaṉ, avaḷ tuyaram pōkka maṉiellikkuḷḷēyē vara, atu kaṇṭa tōḻi,  “nī iṅṅaṉam vantuḷḷati emar kaṇṭāl niṉnili eṉṉa ākum?  talivi uyirvāḻal muṭiyumō?  nī iṉi iṅṅaṉam vāraṟka.  virivil taliviyi varintu koḷka”  eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟal.
itaṉi ‘āṟu pārttuṟṟa accam kūṟal’ eṉpataṉkaṇ aṭakkupa. 	(i. vi. 521)
iḵtu ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 			(i. vi. 521,  kōvi. 178)
nili kūṟu kiḷavi -
taliviniliyic collum coṟkaḷ;  pāṅkaṉ talivaṉ colliyavāṟē kuṟiyiṭattuc ceṉṟu taliviniliyik kaṇṭu,  “puṉattayalē peṇyāṉi pōṉṟa naṭiyiṉi uṭiyāḷ niṟpatik kaṇṭēṉ” (kōvi. 34) eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivaṉiṭam kūṟutal.
ikkūṟṟup ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatu.  			(ka. kā. pak. 33)
nilimi kūṟal -
tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattu iṟutiyil talivaṉ, tāṉum talivi yum maṉam poruntiyirukkum niliyit taṉṉuḷ kūṟik kōṭal.  itu tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattup pakaṟkuṟi eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(ta. ne. vi. pak. 24).
nilimi niṉintu kūṟal -
kaṟpuk kālattil vēntaṟ kuṟṟuḻip piriviṉkaṇ viṉi muṟṟiya piṉ taliviyik kāṇa mīḷa varukiṉṟa talivaṉ,  “puṟākkaḷ tuṇiyoṭu tuyiṉṟu maṉimuṉ viḷiyāṭu vataṉik kaṇṭu, ‘itu namakku aritāyiṟṟu!’ eṉṟu eṉ nilimi niṉintu talivi āṟṟātu iruppāḷ; nī virintu tēric celuttuvāyāka!” eṉṟu talimakaḷatu niliyi maṉaṅkoṇṭu tērppākaṉukkuc colluvatu.
itu ‘vēntaṟku uṟṟuḻip pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.  	(kōvi. 328)
“niṉ kaṇkaḷ kaluḻntu aḻaki iḻakkiṉṟaṉa; nī aḻāmal poṟumi kāṭṭavēṇṭum” eṉṟu kūṟiya tōḻikkut talivi kūṟal - 
“ikkaṇkaḷ kāṭṭiyatāltāṉ nām aṉṟu talivaṉik kaṇṭōm; ikkoṭiya kāmanōyiyum peṟṟōm. iṉṟu akkaṇkaḷē akkātalaṉik kāṭṭumāṟu nammiṭam colli aḻukiṉṟaṉavē, iḵtu ēṉ?” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu. (kuṟaḷ 1171).
“mika virumpi nekiḻntu iṭiviṭāmal aṉṟu avarip pārtta eṉkaṇkaḷ, iṉṟu uṟaṅkāmal varuntum tuyaratti uḻantuuḻantu aḻutu tammiṭamuḷḷa kaṇṇīr aṟṟup pōkuka!” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu. (kuṟaḷ 1177.)			(kuṟaḷ 1171 mutalāka ēḻu.)
“niṉkuṟi nīyē ceṉṟu uri” eṉal -
tōḻi talivaṉi nōkki, “niṉatu iṉṟiyamiyāmiyi nīyē ceṉṟu taliviyiṭam kūṟuka” eṉṟu kūṟutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkiyiṟkūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(na. a. 114).
“niṉ kuṟiyāka itu muṭikkavēṇṭum” eṉṟal -
talivaṉ taṉ pāṅkaṉiṭam, “ic ceyali nī uṉ kāriyam pōla niṉittu muṭittuttaravēṇṭum” eṉṟu kēṭṭuk koḷḷutal.
katiravaṉ veppam tākkum pāṟiyil kiyillāta ūmaṉ oruvaṉit taṉ kaṇkaḷāl kāval kākkumāṟu vikkap paṭṭa veṇṇeyttiraḷ urukip pāṟi muḻutum paraviviṭuvatu pōla, talivi tanta kāmanōy eṉneñcam muḻutum paravi viṭṭatu. eṉṉāl innōy poṟuttuk koḷḷa aritāki viṭṭatu. pāṅka! uṉatu contak kāriyat tik kuṟikkoḷvatu pōlap poṟuppuṭaṉ eṉṉuṭiya innōy tīrum ceyalkaḷic ceyka” (kuṟun. 58.) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattiṉkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. uriyiṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu. 	(i. vi. 505 uri)
“niṉpulavi iṉi yātāṉ nīṅkum?” eṉṟa tōḻikkut talivi kūṟal -
“tōḻi! ippulaviyiṉālēyē, eṉ pōlivaṉmi koṇṭa neñcam iḷakum ceyalkaḷākiya kuḷirppak kūṟutaliyum taḷirppat taḻuvutaliyum talivar nikaḻttuvār. ataṉ piṉ avaric caṟṟum piriyātu taḻuvi iṉpam nukaralām” eṉṟu talivaṉ taṉakkuc ceyya irukkum taṇṇaḷiyit talivi tōḻiyiṭam kuṟippākak kūṟutal. 	(kuṟaḷ 1324)
niṉṟu neñcuṭital -
kaḷavukkālattil iravukkuṟikkaṇ talivaṉ varum vaḻiyatu arumi kūṟi iravukkuṟiyit tōḻi maṟuttāḷāka, atu kēṭṭa talivaṉ, “maṉamē! eytutaṟku ariya taliviyi virumpi nī meliyum icceyalukku yāṉ āṟṟēṉ” eṉat taṉatu iṟantupāṭu tōṉṟa maṉamuṭintu varunti niṟpatu.
iḵtu ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 			(kōvi. 150.)
niṉṟu varuntutal -
pāṅkaṟkūṭṭiṟutikkaṇ talivaṉ taliviyi avaḷ tōḻimārkaḷi aṭiyumāṟu viṭuttut tāṉ av viṭattē niṉṟu appuṉattiṉ ciṟappiṉik kūṟit talivi yatu piriviṉi āṟṟātu varuntutal.
itaṉi iṭantalippāṭṭiṉkaṇ ‘talivaṉ nīṅkaṟkarumi niṉṟu niṉintu iraṅkal’ eṉṟa kūṟṟākak koḷpa. 			(i. vi. 503. uri).
itu ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi. 148)
“niṉṉalatu aṅku avar yāriyum aṟiyēṉ” eṉṟal -
parattiyiṟ pirivu nīṅkit taliviyiṉpāl vanta talivaṉ avaḷatu ūṭal taṇittaṟkāka avaḷ cīṟaṭikaḷit toḻa muṟpaṭṭa pōtu, talivi, “itu ceyal vēṇṭā; itaṉi eṅkiyar (- parattiyar) kāṇiṉ naṉṟākātu” eṉṟu kūṟit taṭuttāḷāka, “niṉṉiyallatu avvāṟu parattiyaril oru variyum nāṉ aṟiyēṉ” eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟutal.
“yāṉ parattiyar yāriyum aṟiyēṉ eṉṟu kūṟuvati nī nampa maṟukkiṟāyā? kaṅkiyil paṭinta aṉṉam ataṉi viṭuttu uvarkkaṭalil paṭiyumā?” (cīvaka. 1017) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaṟpiyalil, ‘parattiyiṟpirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu; ‘uṇartta uṇarā ūṭal’ vakiyatu. 			(na. a. 806)
niṉittami kūṟal -
poruḷvayiṟ pirintu ceṉṟiruntakālit taṉṉi niṉitta tuṇṭā eṉa viṉaviya talivikkut talivaṉ urittal.
“amutaṉiyāy!  pakaṟpoḻutellām uruveḷippā ṭāy nāṉ kāṇum iṭaṅkaḷil ellām eṉakku uṉatu eḻilvaṭivik kāṭṭikkoṇṭiruntāy. ittakiya uṉṉi nāṉ piriyavē illiyē! pirintālaṉṟō mīṇṭum niṉittal uṇṭu?” eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu‘ poruḷvayiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(ampikā. 558)
niṉintavar pulampal -
pulampal - taṉimi eytutal.
talivaṉip pirintirukkum talivi avaṉoṭu  muṉṉark kūṭiyiruntapōtu peṟṟa iṉpatti niṉittu ippo ḻutu taṉittirukkum niliyil varuntutalum, pācaṟiyiliruk kum talivaṉum taliviyoṭu muṉṉark kūṭivāḻnta iṉpatti niṉintu ippoḻutu taṉittirukkum nili yil varuntutalum ām. 	(kuṟaḷ ati. 121. parimē.)
niṉintu viyanturittal -
kaṟpukkālattil parattiyiṟ piriviṉkaṇ taliviyip pirintu parattiyaroṭu putuppuṉalāṭat tiṭṭamiṭṭa talivaṉ, “yāṉ taliviyi niṉiyātu vēṟoṉṟaṉmēl uḷḷattic celuttumpōtum talivi eṉṉi niṉintu eṉ uḷḷam pukukiṟāḷ. yāṉ avaḷi niṉiyuntōṟum, paḷḷattup pukum nīrpōlat taṭukkavum nillātu eṉ maṉattaḷ ākiṉṟāḷ. ātaliṉ taliviyip pirintu parattiyarmāṭṭu iruttal mikavum aritākiṉṟatu” eṉa avaḷi niṉintu viyantu kūṟiyatu.
itu ‘parattiyaṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi. 379.)
niṉiyuṅkāli vāyilar kēṭṭal -
‘niiṉiyuṅkāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, talivaṉum taliviyum taṉittaṉi kūṟa avaṟṟik kēṭṭa vāyilar patiṉmarum tām kēṭṭa kūṟṟiṟkuc ceyyattakuvaṉa tammuḷ ārāyntu kōṭaṟku uriyar. eṉavē, talivaṉ kūṟap paratti kēṭṭa lum, talivi kūṟap paratti kēṭṭalum mutaliyavaṟṟuḷ pulaṉeṟivaḻakkiṟku ēlātaṉa vilakkappaṭṭaṉa. 			(to. cey. 196. nac.)
niṉivaṟivu kūṟi maṟuttal -
talivaṉ tōḻi nilattil makkaḷ vaḻakkamāka aṇiyum taḻiyāṭiyik kiyuṟiyākak koṇṭu varat tōḻi avaṉi nōkki, “eṉakku itaṉi ēṟṟal viruppamē. āyiṉum taliviyiṉ karuttiyum viṉavi aṟintu, avaḷuk kum viruppamuṇṭāyiṉ itaṉip peṟṟukkoḷvēṉ; avaḷ viruppam aṟiyātu yāṉ itaṉip peṟēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟutal.
itaṉi ‘avaḷ āṟṟuvittu  akaṟṟal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa.
itu ‘cēṭpaṭi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. (i. vi. 509) 	(kōvi. 93)
“‘nīṅkaḷ iruvīrum kūṭivāḻum kālattum pulavi nikaḻak kāraṇam yātu?” eṉṟa tōḻikkut talivaṉ kūṟiyatu - 
“nāṉ vēṟṟu nilattup pūvic cūṭiṉāl, yāṉ kātalikkum maṟṟoru peṇṇukku eṉatu pūvaṇi kāṭṭavē itaṉic cūṭiṉēṉ eṉṟu talivi eṉmītu kōpam koḷkiṟāḷ” eṉṟu talivaṉ tōḻikkup pulavikkāraṇam kūṟiyatu.
“nāṉ etu colliṉum ceyyiṉum kuṟṟam kaṇṭu avaḷ ūṭuvatāl, avaḷuṭiya oppaṟṟa uṟuppukkaḷip pārt tuk koṇṭu nāṉ vāḷā iruntālum, eṉ avayavaṅkaḷ num kātalimāruḷ yāryāruṭiya evvev avayavaṅkaḷi ottaṉa eṉpatik karutippōlum, iṅṅaṉam eṉ uṭalik kūrntu nōkkukiṟīr!” eṉṟu talivi pulappatit talivaṉ tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal. (1320) - (kuṟaḷ 1313 mutalāṉa eṭṭum.)
nīṅkaṟku arumi niṉṟu niṉintu iraṅkal -
talivaṉ, “talivi icintu kūṟāviṭil, ivaḷ kākkum tiṉippuṉam yāṉ ivaḷi akaṉṟāl mikka tuyaram ceytu cēyttu ākiṉṟatu; ivaḷit taḻuva varumpōtu pēriṉpam ceytu eṉakku aṇiyatu ākiṟatu” (kōvi. 48) eṉṟu puṉattip paṟṟik kūṟuvatu pōlat taliviyip pirintiruppatu taṉakku iyalātavāṟṟi niṉittu varunti urittal.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘niṉṟu varuntal’ eṉṉum.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘iṭantalippāṭu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uriyiṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu. 			(i. vi. 503 uri)
nīṅkum talivi pāṅkiyartamakkut taṉ celavu uṇartti viṭal -
uṭaṉpōkkuc cellum talivi, vaḻiyiṟ kāṇum anta ṇar mutaliyōr vāyilākat taṉ tōḻiyariṭam taṉatu uṭaṉpōkkiṉi aṟivikkumāṟu colli aṉupputal.
“antaṇīr! yāṉ talivaṉ piṉṉē vaḷamāṉa kāṭṭu vaḻiyil uṭaṉpōkku nikaḻttuvati, eṉpirivi niṉittu varuntum eṉ tōḻiyarkkum, ‘eṉṉēyitu!’ eṉṟu varuntum eṉṉuṭiya aṉṉiyarkkum nīṅkaḷ ceṉṟu col lumiṉ” (ampikā. 418) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘uṭaṉpōkku iṭiyīṭu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(na. a. 198.)
nīṅkum tōḻi talivaṟku uṇarttal -
tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattil taliviyik kuṟiyiṭattu uyttu nīṅkum tōḻi talivaṉiṭam, “talivi taṉittu iruppiṉ aḻuvāḷ.
uṭaṉ uṟiyum tāyum illi. iṉi muḻutum ivaḷ niṉ kāvaliṉaḷ āvāḷ. iṅṅaṉam uṉṉi avaḷik kākkumāṟu paṇippataṟkāka eṉṉi vekuḷaṟka. nāṉ viṭi koḷkiṟēṉ” (ampikā. 153) eṉṟu kūṟutal pōlvaṉa.
itu ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu uriyiṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu, ‘pakaṟkuṟi’ eṉum tokutikkum iḵtu uriya kūṟṟām. 	(i. vi. 509. uri)
nīṅkumiṉ eṉṟal -
pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṉkaṇ tōḻi talivaṉiṭam makaḷir taṉittirukkum iṭattil āṭavar vantu niṉṟu uriyāṭutal aḻakitaṉṟu eṉavum, itaṉi avaḷtanti taṉṉiyar kāṇiṉ talivaṟku ūṟu nikaḻalām eṉavum kūṟit talivaṉi avviṭatti viṭuttuc cellumāṟu kūṟutal (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal) 	(vī. cō. 92. urimēṟ)
itu ‘pāṅki añci accuṟuttal’ eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. 			(na. a. 114).
nīṭu ceṉṟu iraṅkal -
pāṅki talivaṉip pakaṟkuṟi vilakkiya maṟunāḷ, avaṉ akkuṟiyiṭam ceṉṟu talivi varukikku neṭunēram kāttiruntu, “māṉkāḷ! maṭamayilkāḷ! tēṉkāḷ! kiḷi kāḷ! eṉatu iṉṉuyirām talivi, eṉatu maṉam cuḻalum nōyit tāṉ kāṇavēṇṭiyō, taṉṉi illāmal veṟuvitāya iṭatti nāṉ kāṇavēṇ ṭiyō, iṅku eḻuntaruḷātu pōyiṉaḷ?” (tañci. kō. 160)eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟi varuntal.
pakaṟkuṟi iṭiyīṭu eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatu ikkūṟṟu.			(na. a. 156).
nīṭu niṉintu iraṅkal -
talaviyik kūṭutaṟkut tāmatam nērvati niṉittut talivaṉ varuntutal. itu ‘taliviyiṉ eytum iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(na. a. 127)
nīṭēṉ eṉṟu avaṉ nīṅkal -
variviṭi vittup poruḷ tēṭac cellum talivaṉ tōḻiyiṭam, “nāṉ kālam tāḻkka māṭṭēṉ” eṉṟu collip pōtal.
“nāṉ niti tēṭikkoṇṭu virivil mīḷvēṉ. nīṭal ceyyēṉ. itaṉi niṉ talivikku nī colluvatē ēṟpuṭiyatu. avaḷ teḷiyumāṟu colluti” (ampikā. 305) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyalil ‘variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(na. a. 170)
“nī mālippoḻutil peritum tuyaruṟṟu iṅṅaṉam āvatu eṉ paṟṟi?” eṉṟa tōḻikkut talivi kūṟutal -
“tōḻi! eṉ kāmanōyākiya intap pū kālippōtil arumpip pakaṟpōtellām pērarumpāy mutirntu mālippōtil malarkiṟatu. ataṉālēyē māliyil peritum tuyaruṟukiṉṟēṉ” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.			(kuṟaḷ. 1227)
“nīyum āṟṟi niṉ kaṇkaḷum tuyilutal vēṇṭum” eṉṟa tōḻikkut talivi kūṟiyatu -
“tōḻi! kātalar vārāta pōtil avar varavi etir pārttu eṉ kaṇkaḷ uṟaṅkuvatilli; avar vantuviṭiṉō mīṇṭum pirivārō eṉa añcit tuyil koḷvatilli. ākavē eṉ kaṇkaḷ orupoḻutum tuyilutal illi” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu. 	(kuṟaḷ 1179)
‘nīyē kūṟu’ eṉṟal -
taliviyiṉ paricapporuḷ koṭuppataṟkup poruḷ tiraṭṭivarat talivaṉ variporuṭ piritalit tōḻi yiṭam kūṟit talivikkut terivikkumāṟu vēṇṭavē, ” niṉ pirivu niṉittu, nī kūṭiyirukkumpōtum vāṭivaruntum talivikku niṉpirivi nīyē kūṟic celka.” eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟutal.
itaṉi ‘niṉ poruṭpirivu uri nī avaṭku eṉṟal’ eṉavum kūṟupa. 	(na. a. 170)
itu ‘variporuṭpirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi. 269)
nīrkkaṭavuḷ -
varuṇaṉ. ‘katumeṉak kaṇṭa ciṅkanīrkkaṭavuḷi niṉin tāṉ’ 	(upatēcakā. uruttirāk. 227) (ṭu)
nīrmiaṉṟu eṉṟal -
pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṉkaṇ tōḻi talivaṉiṭam, “makaḷir taṉittirukkum iṭattē putiyaṉākiya āṭavaṉ oruvaṉ vantu niṉṟu uriyāṭutal irupālārukkum ēṟṟa taṉṟu” eṉṟu kūṟutal. (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal.) 	(vī. cō. 92 urimēṟ.)
itaṉip ‘pāṅki añci accuṟuttal’ (na. a. 114) eṉṉum kūṟṟiṉkaṇ aṭakkalām; ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatu ikkūṟṟu.
‘neñcattu iraṅki niṉṟu kuṟi yēṟṟal’ -
tōḻi talivaṉ niliyi maṉaṅkoṇṭu tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattil  kuṟi nayappittavaḻi, talivi tōḻikūṟṟi uṭkoṇṭu ceyaṟpaṭuvatāl nāṇ aḻitali maṉattil poṟuttukkoṇṭu avaḷuṭiya coṟkaḷi ēṟṟut talivaṉik kūṭutaṟku uṭaṉpaṭutal.  (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal)  		(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ.)
neñcam mikkatu vāy cōrtal -
pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭattukkup piṉ talivaṉ pirintu ceṉṟa taṉāl taṉ neñcattil mikuntu kiḷarnta tuṉpattit talivi vāyviṭṭuk kūṟutal.
“tāṅka muṭiyāta kātaṟcumiyip poṟukkum eṉ āvi taṉimiyil varuntumpaṭi eṉṉi viṭṭup pirintu, eṉ nalattiyum koṇṭupōṉa eṉ talivaṉatu tēruṭaṉ eṉ maṉamum pōyiṟṟu!”  eṉṟu talivi kūṟutal.
itu ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam ’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(ampikā. 157)
“neñcil tōṉṟiya kāmatti neñcālēyē aṭakkutal vēṇṭum” eṉṟa tōḻikkut talivi kūṟiyatu -
“ulakamē uṟaṅkum naṭuniciyilum eṉ neñcu kāmattukku aṭimiyāki attoḻilil īṭupaṭṭuk kalaṅkukiṟatu.  kaṇṇōṭṭam illāk kāmattukku aṭimippaṭṭa eṉ neñcukkut taṉ taṉmi eṉpatu illāmal pōkiviṭṭatu.  ātaliṉ kāmatti aṭakkum āṟṟal eṉ neñciṟku illi”  eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.  	(kuṟaḷ 1252)
neñcu valiyuṟuttal -
iyaṟkip puṇarccikkup piṉ pirintu cellum talivaṉ, appirivip poṟuttuk koḷḷumāṟu taṉ neñcit tiṇmippaṭuttutal.
“ūḻviṉi tanta ik kūṭṭam nīṭṭittāl, pāṅkiyar itaṉi aṟiya nēralām. atu kāraṇamāka eṉ uyiraṉiya kātali kalaṅkuvāḷ. atik karuti nāṉ pirintālum ivaḷ cōrvuṟuvāḷ.  iti niṉintāl eṉṉuyir naṭuṅkukiṟatu; uḷḷamum urukukiṟatu.  ātaliṉ, neñcē! nī evvakiyilum iṅkēyiruntu varuntutal kūṭātu. pirintu cellat tuṇika!” eṉṟu talivaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟutal.
iḵtu ‘iyaṟkip puṇarcci’t tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ampikā. 18)
neñcoṭu kiḷattal -
talivaṉatu piriviṭattu āṟṟāḷākiya talivi taṉakkup paṟṟukkōṭāka iruntu āṟutal colluvār oruvari yum kāṇāmal taṉ maṉattoṭu ceyyum vakiyaṟiyātu uriyāṭutal. 	(kuṟaḷ ati. 125 parimē.)
neñcoṭu kūṟal -
kaḷavukkālattē talivaṉ variporuḷvayiṉ pirintami kēṭṭu varuntāniṉṟa neñciṉi nōkkit talivi, “namakku ētam payakkum kaḷavoḻukkattiṉit tavirttu maṇam ceytukoṇṭu kuṟṟamaṟṟa vāḻvu vāḻap poruḷtēṭac ceṉṟa nam talivaratu icceyal niṉakku varuttam tarumāyiṉ itaṉi viṭuttu nām varuttamaṟa vāḻum upāyam uḷatō?”  eṉṟu ataṉ varuttam tīrak kūṟutal.
itaṉit ‘talimakaḷ (pūṅkuḻi) iraṅkal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa.  		(na. a. 170)
itu ‘variporuṭpirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi 272)
neñcoṭu nōtal -
 taliviyip pirintu poruḷ tēṭivara muṭivu ceytu poruḷ tēṭap puṟappaṭṭuc ceṉṟa talivaṉ naṭuvaḻiyil mīḷa niṉittup piṉ mīḷamāṭṭāmal poruḷmēl cellāniṉṟa uḷḷattoṭu taṉ neñciṉi nōkki,  “maṉamē!  illattē iruttal, poruḷ tēṭap puṟattē puṟappaṭutal eṉṟa iraṇṭaṉuḷ nī etaṉi uṟutiyākap piṉpaṟṟa muṭivuceytuḷḷāy?”  eṉṟu taṉ neñcoṭu nontu kūṟiyatu.  	(kōvi. 342)
itu ‘poruḷvayiṉ pirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.  
neñcoṭu pulattal (1) -
kaṟpukkālattil poruḷvayiṉ pirivil neñcoṭu nontu iṭiccurattu varuntiya talivaṉ, “maṉamē! pēyoṭu paḻakiṉālum ataṉi viṭuttup pirital aritu eṉpa.  aṅṅaṉa miruppavum, aṉpē vaṭivāṉa taliviyip pirittu eṉṉi ivvaḷavu tūram aḻittuvantuviṭṭa uṉatu vīṇ añcāmi añcat takkatu” eṉap piṉṉum iṭiccurattil taṉ neñcoṭu pulantu kūṟiyatu.
itu ‘poruḷvayiṉ pirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.  	(kōvi. 343)
neñcoṭu pulattal (2) -
kaṟpuk kālattut talivaṉum taliviyum illaṟatti nallaṟamāka naṭatti varuṅkāl, pulattaṟkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷ uḷḷa kālattum pulakkak karutātu puṇarcciyik karuti virikiṉṟa taṉ maṉattuṭaṉ talivi kōpam koḷḷuta lum, ciṟupāṉmi talivaṉ taṉ maṉattuṭaṉ kōpam koḷḷulum ām.  	(kuṟaḷ ati. 130 parimē.)
neñcoṭu maṟuttal -
kaṟpuk kālattup poruḷvayiṉ piriviṉkaṇ iṭiccurat tut talivaṉ neñcoṭu nontu pulantu kūṟip piṉṉum poruḷmēṟ ceṉṟa uḷḷattoṭu taliviyi niṉitta niliyil,  “maṉamē! paṇpunalam cāṉṟa poṉ pōṉṟa taliviyi viṭuttu vēṟu poṉṉit tēṭiyō eṉṉi vāḻac ceyyappōkiṟāy!  itaṟku yāṉ uṭaṉpaṭēṉ.  nīyē celka”  eṉa, mēl toṭarntu pōka eṇṇamiṉṟip poruḷ tēṭac cella vēṇṭum eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttiya neñciṉiṭam maṟuttuk kūṟutal.
itu ‘poruḷvayiṉ pirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.  	(kōvi. 344)
neñcoṭu melital -
kikkiḷit talivi iravil iruḷneṟiyil talivaṉ iruppiṭattic ceṉṟaṭiya virumpiya maṉaniliyāl varuntutal.
“neñcē!  talivaṉik kūṭi makiḻa virumpi avaṉit tēṭik koṇṭu iravil naḷḷiruḷil puṟappaṭṭuc cella niṉitta eṉṉi, ‘atu makaḷirkkut takātu’ eṉṟu taṭutta nīyē avaṉi niṉittukkoṇṭu pōyviṭukiṟāyē!  itu niyāyamāmō?”  eṉṉum talivi kūṟṟu.
itu kikkiḷip peṇpāṟkūṟṟuk kiḷavikaḷuḷ oṉṟu.  			(pu. ve. mā. 15-11)
neñcoṭu melitaliṉ pakkam -
kikkiḷit talivi iravil iruḷ cūḻnta neṟiyil talivaṉatu iruppiṭam kuṟittuc cellum taṉ muṭiviṉi ellōrumē terintukoḷvārāka eṉṟu eṇṇip pēcuvatu.
“neñcē!  iṉiyum eṉṉāl poṟukka iyalātu. eṉatu melivi eṉ talivaṟkuk kāṭṭat tuṇintuviṭṭēṉ.  avaṉi avaṉ iruppiṭattē ceṉṟu kāṇavum tuṇintu puṟappaṭṭuviṭṭēṉ.  poyyāka alar pēcip paḻikkum makaḷir iṉit tammaṉam pōṉa pōkkāl alar tūṟṟuka.” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu kikkiḷip peṇpāṟkūṟṟu.  	(pu. ve. mā. 15-12)
neñcoṭu varuntal -
“muṉṉarp poḻiliṭattē kariya puṉṉimaram poṉpōla malarkiṉṟa, muttup pōlum maṇaliyuṭiyatōr iṭattē eṉṉik kalantu nīṅkiya talivar ceṉṟa tēriṉ piṉṉarc ceṉṟa eṉatu neñcam, avviṭattu iṉṟu ceykiṉṟatu eṉṉō? aṟikiṉṟēṉ ilēṉ” eṉṟu talivi taṉ neñcattuḷḷē kūṟi varuntutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘variporuṭ pirital’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi. 273)
neytal -
kaṭalum kaṭal cārnta iṭamum.
ippakutiyil neytalkoṭi mikutiyāka iruttal paṟṟi innilattiṟku neytal eṉṟa peyar uṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉavum, kaṟpukkālattil paki tūtu mutaliya karutip paruvaṅ kuṟittup pirintu ceṉṟa talivaṉ kuṟitta paruvattu vārāk kālattu irakkam mikkut talivi varuntutal iyalpātaliṉ, iv virakkam eṉṟa uripporuḷ peritum nikaḻum kaṭalum kaṭal cārnta iṭamum ākiya nilapparappiṟku iraṅkal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum neytal eṉpatu peyarāyiṟṟu eṉavum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
neytal naṭiyiyal -
vīracōḻiya uriyil, talivaṉ kaḷavilum kaṟpilum, kuṟitta nērattum nāḷilum paruvattum vārāvaḻit talivi kaṭaṟkariyākiya neytaṟpakutiyi aṭuttu āṇṭuḷḷa kaṭal kāṉal kiti mutaliyavaṟṟoṭu pulampi uriyāṭu talum, pakaṟkuṟi- iravukkuṟi-kuṟivilakkal- kuṟiyiṭiyīṭukaḷ- varital vēṭki-mutaliya ceytikaḷ paṟṟiya kiḷavikaḷ palavum tokuttuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa ceyyuḷ; iraṅkal eṉṟa oḻukkam paṟṟiya naṭappukkaḷi niralpaṭak kūṟal.  			(vī. cō. 96 urimēṟ.)
neytal:  nīrnili, makkaḷ -
nīrnili:  iliñci, paṇṇi, ēlvi, kuṇṭam, alanti, poyki, valiyam, cuṉi, ciṟi, paṭṭam, uṭuvi, payampu, paṭukar, kuṭṭamtāṅkal, kōṭṭakam, ēri, uvaḷakam, maṭu, ōṭi, paṭu, taṭam, vāvi, taṭākam, āvi, cūḻi, kiṭaṅku, calataram, kēṇi, paṇi, kayam, palvaḷam, naḷiṉi, ilanti, mūḻi, kuḻi, kuḷam. 	(piṅ. pak. 87).
makkaḷ:  paratavar,nuḷiyavar, kaṭalar, valiyar, calavar, timilar, aḷavar; nuḷicciyar, parattiyar, kaṭaṟpiṇākkaḷ;  koṇkaṉ, tuṟivaṉ, mellaṉ, pulampaṉ, cērppaṉ - umaṇar, paḵṟiyar, kaḻiyar, kālar, kuḻicciyar - eṉpaṉavum koḷḷappaṭum.  	(tivā. pak. 40,41)
makkaḷ:  paratar, kalavar, kaḷiyar, nuḷiyar, paravar, valiyar, timilar, paratavar, kaṭalar, neytalar; kuḻicci, nuḷicci, kuratti, paratti.  	(nā. ni. 161)
makkaḷ:  kaṭalar, timilar, calavar, nuḷiyar, paratar, valiyar;  parattiyar, nuḷicciyar; mellaṉ, pulampaṉ, cērppaṉ, tuṟivaṉ, koṇkaṉ.  	(piṅ. pak. 87)
neytaṟkarupporuḷ -
1.	teyvam - varuṇaṉ
2.	uyarmakkaḷ- koṇkaṉ, tuṟivaṉ, cērppaṉ, pulampaṉ; paratti, nuḷicci
3.	potumakkaḷ - nuḷiyar, timilar, paratar, aḷavar; nuḷicciyar, parattiyar, aḷattiyar.
4. 	paṟavi - kaṭaṟkākki, aṉṉam, aṉṟil, kampuḷ.
5.	vilaṅku - cuṟāmīṉ, karā (-mutali), umaṇpakaṭu
6.	ūr  -  pākkam, paṭṭiṉam.
7. 	nīr  -  kēṇinīr, kaṭalnīr, maṇaṟkiṇaṟṟu nīr, uvarkkuḻinīr.
8. 	pū   -  neytal, tāḻi, muṇṭakam, aṭampu
9. 	maram  -  kaṇṭal, puṉṉi, ñāḻal.
10. 	uṇavu -  mīṉum uppum viṟṟup peṟum poruḷ.
11. 	paṟi  -  mīṉkōṭpaṟi, nāvāyppampi
12. 	yāḻ -  viḷariyāḻ (-neytal yāḻ)
13.	paṇ  -  cevvaḻi
14.	toḻil  -  mīṉpiṭittal, uppu viḷittal, avaṟṟi viṟṟal, mīṉkaḷik kāya vittal, avaṟṟi uṇṇa varum paṟavikaḷi ōṭṭutal, muttuk kuḷittal, kaṭalāṭal pōlvaṉa.
15.	koṭi - aṭampu.  (to. po. 20 iḷa.;  18 nac; cilap. patikam. aṭi; ta. ne. vi. 6-12; na.a. 24)
	(cāminātam kūṟum) neytaṟ karupporuḷ
	vilaṅku  -  naṇṭu, āmi.
	maram  -  paṉi, paṭṭil, tilli.
	toḻil - veṇcaṅku, muttu, pavaḷam eṭuttal.(cāmi. 79)
neytaṟkuriya poḻutu -
āṇṭu muḻutum neytaṟkuriya perumpoḻutām.
eṟpāṭu neytaṟkuriya ciṟupoḻutu.
eṟpāṭāvatu pakaṟpoḻutiṉ muppatu nāḻikikaḷuḷ iṟutiyil uḷḷa kūṟu eṉṟu iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārk kiṉiyarum koḷpa.  	(to. po. 10 iḷa; 8 nac.)
pāratiyār eṟpāṭu pakaṟpoḻutiṉ piṟkūṟu eṉavum, kuḻanti katiravaṉ puṟappaṭum kālinēram eṉavum koḷvar.  			(akat. 8)
nampi akapporuḷ āciriyarum civañāṉamuṉivarum eṟpāṭu eṉpataṉik kāli nēram eṉṟu koḷvar.  			(na. a. 16; cū.vi. pak. 52)
pakaliṉ piṟkūṟu neytalukkuc ciṟupoḻutātaṟku nacci ṉārkkiṉiyar,  “katiravaṉatu veppam kuṟiyat taṇṇaṟuñ cōli tāḻntu niḻal ceyyavum, uppaṅkaḻikaḷil mēyntu palavakip paṟavikaḷum tattam iruppiṭaṅkaḷi nōkki āṇum peṇṇumāka oruṅku cēravum, puṉṉi mutaliya pūkkaḷiṉ naṟumaṇam vīcat toṭaṅkavum, kaṭalilē matiyam utikkat toṭaṅkavum, kātal kimikkut talivi kaṭaliṭat tum kaṭaṟkaric cōliyiṭattum aṭakkam nīṅki vēṭki pulappaṭa urittaliṉ āṇṭuk kāmakkuṟippu veḷippaṭṭu iraṅkaṟ poruḷ ciṟattaliṉ, eṟpāṭu neytaṟku ēṟṟa tāyiṟṟu”  eṉṟu viḷakkam tantuḷḷār.  	(to. po. 8 nac.)
“paṭutal-uṇṭātal. eṟpāṭu-cūriyaṉ puṟappaṭum nāḷ veyiṟkāli.  viṭiyal pulari vikaṟi eṉpaṉa oru poruṭ kiḷavi ātaliṉ, viṭiyal eṉpatu vikaṟiyik kuṟikkumē yaṉṟik kāli nērattik kuṟikkum col eṟpāṭu eṉpatē.  ūṭal iṟutikkaṇ kūṭal nikaḻvataṟku vikaṟi ēṟṟatē yaṉṟik kāli nēram ēṟṟataṉṟu.  talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirintaviṭattut talivi irākkālam muḻutum vīṇākak kaḻintami paṟṟi iraṅkutaṟkuriya kālam eṟpāṭu eṉappaṭum kālinēramē. ātaliṉ kāli nēramē neytal tiṇikkuc ciṟupoḻutu”  eṉpar civañāṉamuṉivar.  	(cū. vi. pak. 52, 53).
“eṟpāṭu piṟpakal eṉiṉ, pirintu ceṉṟa talivaṉ varum nēram ākiyāl, ‘iṉivaruvāṉ’ eṉṟu talivi āṟṟi yiruppāḷē aṉṟi iraṅkāḷ.  iravumuḻutum taṉittuk kaṇ uṟaṅkātu kaḻitta talivi vikaṟiyum kaḻintu cūriyaṉ utikkum kālippoḻutu varavē, “iṉip pakaṟpōtu kaḻintaṉṟit talivaṉ vārāṉ” eṉṟu eṇṇi iraṅku vāḷ ātaliṉ, neytaṟkuc ciṟu poḻutu kāli āyiṟṟu.”  		(to. po. 8 kuḻa.)
eṟpāṭu eṉpatu piṟpakalē, kālippoḻutaṉṟu eṉpa taṉip pāratiyār takka kāraṇaṅkaḷuṭaṉ niṟuviyuḷḷār.  			(to. po. 8 pārati.)
neyyaṇi -
talivi makappeṟṟa aṇmik kālattil makappēṟṟāl uṭampil uṇṭāya tūymiyaṟṟa kacaṭukaḷ nīṅka eṇṇey tēyttuk kuḷikkum maṅkala nīrāṭṭu. 	(to. po. 147 nac.)
neyyaṇi nayanta talivaṉit talivi neñcu puṇṇuṟīik kūṟiyatu -
makappēṟṟup puṉiṟu tīra eṇṇey nīrāṭi viṭṭu illam pukunta niliyil taṉṉi virumpi vanta talivaṉukkut talivi varuntik kūṟutal. puṇṇuṟīi-puṇṇuṟṟu-varunti-puṇṇuṟutti-varunti eṉpatum ām.
“taliva!  nī parattiyaruṭaṉkūṭiya pōtu uṉmārpil ēṟpaṭṭa kuṟikaḷuṭaṉum, avarkaḷ pūṭṭiya māliyuṭaṉum iṅku vantuḷḷāy. avaṟṟi nīroḻukum vāyuṭaṉ niṉputalvaṉ uṇṇum eṉ tāyppāl citittuviṭum.  ātaliṉ nī eṉṉit taḻuvutal vēṇṭā”  eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(aiṅ. 65)
itu tol. kaṟpiyal 6ām cūttirattuk ‘kayantali tōṉ ṟiya kāmar neyyaṇi, nayanta kiḻavaṉ neñcu puṇṇuṟīi, naḷiyiṉ nīkkiya iḷivaru niliyum,  (to. po. 147 nac.) eṉṟataṉāṟ koṇṭatu.
neruṅkutal -
aṇuki vantu maṉattil naṉku patiyumāṟu kūṟutal.
itu tōḻi taliviyiṭamum talivaṉiṭamum muṟiyē piriviṭit tuyaruṟumpōtum, parattiyiṟ pirital mutaliya vaṟṟik karutumpōtum kūṟum marapākum.  			(to. po. 39, 150 nac.)
neṟkuṟi -
talivaṉpirivāl kaḷavukkālattil, varuntum taliviyiṉ uṭalmelivu kaṇṭu, tāy kaṭṭuvicciyi aḻittut talivi nōy paṟṟi viṉava, avaḷum cuḷakil ciṟitu nel peṟṟu ataṉi eṭuttut tūvi eṇṇippārttu, “in nōy murukaṉāl vantatu” eṉṟu muṭivu ceytu kūṟutal.
‘variporuṭ pirital’ kiḷavikkaṇ, ‘kaṭṭuvitti kūṟal’ eṉum kiḷaviyiṭattu itu paṟṟiya kuṟippu nikaḻkiṟatu.  			(kōvi. 285)
neṟi alippu -
‘vaḻaṅku katikkoṇṭaṉa’ eṉṟu nampi akapporuḷ kūṟuvataṉic (cū.21) cāminātam neṟi alippu eṉṟu kūṟum.  vaḻippaṟi ceytu kavarvaṉa ākiya pālik karup poruḷ; uṇavil aṭaṅkum.  	(cāmi. 75)
neṟi vilakkik kūṟal -
kaḷaviṭit taliviyōṭu uṭaṉpōkkuc ceṉṟa talivaṉ mālinērattil ōrūri aṭintāṉāka,  avvūriluḷ ḷār, “iṉic cellum neṟikkaṇ naṉmakkaḷ illi;  nīyum taṉiyi;  ittaliviyum vāṭip pōyirukkiṟāḷ;  poḻutum ceṉṟu viṭṭatu; iṉṟiravu īṇṭut taṅki nāḷi viṭiyalil puṟappaṭuvāyāka”  eṉṟu mēl toṭarntu naṭattali vilakkik kūṟiyatu.
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṟa tokutikkaṇ aminta kūṟṟuk kaḷuḷ oṉṟu. 	(kōvi. 218)
neṟivilakku -
